dvipalaṃ bhāvite bhāṇḍe ghṛtenāṣṭadinasthitam || 250
siddhaṃ bhavati tacchuktaṃ yadāvatārya gṛhyate
tadā deyaṃ caturjātaṃ pṛthakkarṣatrayonmitam || 251
pañcapallavatoyena gandhānāṃ kṣālanaṃ tathā
śodhanaṃ cāpi saṃskāro viśeṣaścātra vakṣyate || 252
āmrajambūkapitthānāṃ bījapūrakabilvayoḥ
gandhakarmaṇi sarvatra patrāṇi pañcapallavam || 253
caṇḍīgomayatoyena yadi vā tintiḍījalaiḥ
nakhaṃ saṃkvāthayedebhiralābhe mṛṇmayena tu || 254
punaruddhṛtya prakṣālya bharjayitvā niṣecayet
guḍapathyāmbunā hyevaṃ śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 255
No verses of the Cakradatta in this column.
The commentary was not digitized.
46gomūtre cālambuṣake paktvā pañcadalodake
punaḥ surabhitoyena bāṣpasvedena svedayet || 256
gandhogrā śudhyate hyevaṃ rajanī ca viśeṣataḥ
mustakaṃ tu manāk kṣuṇṇaṃ kāñjike tridinoṣitam || 257
pañcapallavapānīyasvinnamātapaśoṣitam
guḍāmbunā sicyamānaṃ bharjayeccūrṇayettataḥ || 258
ājaśaubhāñjanajalairbhāvayecceti śudhyati
kāñjike kvathitaṃ śailaṃ bhṛṣṭāpathyāguḍāmbunā || 259
siñcedevaṃ punaḥ puṣpairvividhairadhivāsayet
yathālābhamapāmārgasnuhyādikṣāralepitam || 260
bāṣpasvedena saṃsvedya pūtiṃ nirlomatāṃ nayet
dolāpakvaṃ pacetpaścāpañcatpallavavāriṇi || 261
khalaḥ sādhumivotpīḍya tato niḥsnehatāṃ nayet
ājaśobhāñjanajalairbhāvayecca punaḥ punaḥ || 262
śigrumūle ca ketakyāḥ puṣpapatrapuṭe ca tam
pacedevaṃ viśuddhaḥ sanmṛganābhisamo bhavet || 263
turuṣkaṃ madhunā bhāvyaṃ kāśmīraṃ cāpi sarpiṣā
rudhireṇāyasaṃ prājñairgomūtrairgranthiparṇakam || 264
madhūdakena madhurīṃ patrakaṃ taṇḍulāmbunā
īṣatkṣārānugandhā tu dagdhā yāti na bhasmatām || 265
pītā ketakagandhā ca laghusnigdhā mṛgottamā
pakvātkarpūrataḥ prāhurapakvaṃ guṇavattaram || 266
tatrāpi syādyadakṣudraṃ sphaṭikābhaṃ taduttamam
pakvaṃ ca sadalaṃ snigdhaṃ haritadyuti cottamam || 267
bhaṅge manāgapi na cennipatanti tataḥ kaṇāḥ
mṛgaśṛṅgopamaṃ kuṣṭaṃ candanaṃ raktapītakam || 268
kācatuṇḍākṛtiḥ snigdho guruścaivottamo guruḥ
snigdhālpakeśaraṃ tvasraṃ śālijo vṛttamāṃsalaḥ || 269
murā pītā varā proktā māṃsī piṅgajaṭākṛtiḥ
reṇukā mudgasaṃsthānā śastamānūpajaṃ ghanam || 270
jātīphalaṃ saśabdaṃ ca snigdhaṃ guru ca śasyate
elā sūkṣmaphalā śreṣṭhā priyaṅguḥ śyāmapāṇḍurā || 271
nakhamaśvakhuraṃ hastikarṇaṃ caivātra śasyate
eteṣāmapareṣāṃ ca navatā pravalo guṇaḥ || 272
47jiṅgīcorakadevadārusaralaṃ
vyāghrīvacācelaka-
tvakpatraiḥ saha gandhapatrakaśaṭī-
pathyākṣadhātrīghanaiḥ
etaiḥ śodhitasaṃskṛtaiḥ palayuge-
tyākhyātayā saṃkhyayā
tailaprasthamavasthitaiḥ sthiramiti
kalkaiḥ pacedgāndhikam || 273
māṃsīmurādamanacampakasundarītvak-
granthyamburuṅmarubakairdvipalaiḥ sapṛkkaiḥ
śrīvāsakundurunakhīnalikāmiṣīṇāṃ
pratyekataḥ palamupāyya punaḥ pacettu || 274
elālavaṅgacalacandanajātipūtiḥ
kakkolakāgurulatāghusṛṇaiḥ palārdhaiḥ
kastūrikākṣasahitāmaladīptiyuktaiḥ
pakvā tu mandaśikhinaiva mahāsugandham || 275
pañcadvikena cārdhena madātkarpūramiṣyate
karpūramadayorardhaṃ patrakalkādiheṣyate || 276
pakvapūte'pyuṣṇa eva samyakpeṣaṇavartitam
dīyate gandhavṛddhyarthaṃ patrakalkaṃ taducyate || 277
prāguktau śuddhasaṃskārau gandhānāmiha taiḥ punaḥ
dviguṇairlakṣmīvilāsaḥ syādayaṃ tailasattamaḥ || 278
pañcapatrāmbunā cādyo dvitīyo gandhavāriṇā
tṛtīyo'pi ca tenaiva pāko vā dhūpitāmbunā || 279
Adhikāra 23
1bāhyaṃ lepābhyaṅgaseko pānāhairvātaśoṇitam
virekasthāpanasnehapānairgambhīramācaret || 1
dvayormuñcedasṛk śṛṅgasūcyalābūjalaukasā
dehāddeśaṃ vrajetsrāvyaṃ śirābhiḥ pucchanena vā || 2
aṅgaglānau ca na srāvyaṃ rukṣe vātottare ca yat
amṛtanāgaradhanyākakarṣatrayeṇa pācanaṃ siddham || 3
jayati saraktaṃ vātaṃ sāmaṃ kuṣṭhānyaśeṣāṇi
vatsādanyudbhavaḥ kvāthaḥ pīto guggulusaṃyutaḥ
samīraṇasamāyuktaṃ śoṇitaṃ saṃprasādhayet || 4
vāsāguḍūcīcaturaṅgulānā-
meraṇḍatailena pibetkaṣāyam
krameṇa sarvāṅgajamapyaśeṣaṃ
jayedasṛgvātabhavaṃ vikāram || 5
līḍḍhā muṇḍitikācūrṇaṃ madhusarpiḥ samanvitam
chinnākvāthaṃ pibanhanti vātaraktaṃ sudustaram || 6
tisro'thavā pañca guḍena pathyā
jagdhvā pibecchinnaruhākaṣāyam
tadvātaraktaṃ śamayatyudīrṇa-
mājānusaṃbhinnamapi hyavaśyam || 7
ghṛtena vātaṃ saguḍā vibaddhaṃ
pittaṃ sitāḍhyā madhunā kaphaṃ ca
vātāsṛgugraṃ rubutailamiśrā
śuṇṭhyāmavātaṃ śamayedguḍūcī || 8
2guḍūcyāḥ svarasaṃ kalkaṃ cūrṇaṃ vā kvāthameva vā
prabhūtakālamāsevya mucyate vātaśoṇitāt || 9
daśamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇam
pariṣeko'nilaprāye tadvatkoṣṇena sarpiṣā || 10
paṭolakaṭukābhīrutriphalāmṛtasādhitam
kvāthaṃ pītvā jayejjantuḥ sadāhaṃ vātaśoṇitam || 11
godhūmacūrṇājapayo ghṛtaṃ ca
sacchāgadugdho rububījakalkaḥ
lepe vidheyaṃ śatadhautasarpiḥ
seke payaścāvikameva śastam || 12
lepaḥ piṣṭāstilāstadvadbhṛṣṭāḥ payasi nirvṛtāḥ
kaṭukāmṛtayaṣṭyāhvaṃ śuṇṭhīkalkaṃ samākṣikam || 13
gomūtrapītaṃ jayati sakaphaṃ vātaśoṇitam
dhātrīharidrāmustānāṃ kaṣāyo vā kaphādhike || 14
kokilākṣāmṛtākvāthe pibetkṛṣṇāṃ kaphādhike
pathyābhojī trisaptāhānmucyate vātaśoṇitāt || 15
kapharaktapraśamanaṃ hṛdyaṃ guḍaghṛtaṃ smṛtam
saṃsargeṣu yathodrekaṃ miśraṃ vā pratikārayet || 16
sarveṣu saguḍāṃ pathyāṃ guḍūcīkvāthameva vā
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā śīlayetsusamāhitaḥ || 17
3triphalānimbamañjiṣṭhāvacākaṭukarohiṇī
vatsādanīdāruniśākaṣāyo navakārṣikaḥ || 18
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ pāmānaṃ raktamaṇḍalam
kuṣṭhaṃ kāpālikākuṣṭhaṃ pānādevāpakarṣati || 19
pañcaraktikamāṣeṇa kāryo'yaṃ nava kārṣikaḥ
kiṃtvevaṃ sādhite kvāthe yogyamātrā pradīyate || 20
4guḍūcīkvāthakalkābhyāṃ sapayaskaṃ śṛtaṃ ghṛtam
hanti vātaṃ tathā raktaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ jayati dustaram || 21
5śatāvarīkalkagarbhaṃ rase tasyāścaturguṇe
kṣīratulyaṃ ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ vātaśoṇitanāśanam || 22
6amṛtā madhukaṃ drākṣā triphalā nāgaraṃ balā
vāsāragvadhavṛścīradevadārutrikaṇṭakam || 23
kaṭukāsavarīkṛṣṇākāśmaryasya phalāni ca
rāsnākṣurakagandharvavṛddhadāraghanotpalaiḥ
kalkairebhiḥ samaiḥ kṛtvā sarpiḥprasthaṃ vipācayet || 24
dhātrīrasasamaṃ dattvā vāri triguṇasaṃyutam
samyak siddhaṃ tu vijñāya bhojyapāne ca śasyate || 25
bahudoṣānvitaṃ bātaṃ raktena saha mūrcchitam
uttānaṃ cāpi gambhīraṃ trikajaṅghorujānujam || 26
kroṣṭuśīrṣe mahāśūle cāmavāte sudāruṇe
vātarogopasṛṣṭasya vedanāṃ cātidustarām || 27
mūtrakṛcchramudāvartapramehaṃ viṣamajvaram
etānsarvānnihantyāśu vātapittakaphotthitān || 28
sarvakālopayogena varṇāyurvalavardhanam
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ghṛtametadanuttamam || 29
7balākaṣāyakalkābhyāṃ tailaṃ kṣīracaturguṇam
daśapākaṃ bhavedetadvātāsṛgvātapittajit || 30
dhanyaṃ puṃsavanaṃ caiva narāṇāṃ śukravardhanam
retoyonivikāraghnametadvātavikāranut || 31
8guḍūcīkvāthadugdhābhyāṃ tailaṃ lākṣārasena vā
siddhaṃ madhukakāśmaryarasairvā vātaraktanut || 32
9padmakośīrayaṣṭyāhvarajanīkvāthasādhitam
syātpiṣṭaiḥ sarjamañjiṣṭhāvīrākākolicandanaiḥ
khujjākapadmakamidaṃ tailaṃ vātāsradoṣanut || 33
10śuddhāṃ pacennāgabalātulāṃ tu
visrāvya tailāḍhakamatra dadyāt
ajāpayastulyavimiśritaṃ tu
na tasya yaṣṭīmadhukasya kalkam || 34
pṛthakpacetpañcapalaṃ vipakvaṃ
tadvātaraktaṃ śamayatyudīrṇam
bastipradānādiha saptarātrā-
tpītaṃ daśāhātprakarotyarogam || 35
tulādravye jaladroṇo dravyatulā droṇe matā || 36
11samadhūcchiṣṭamañjiṣṭhaṃ sasarjarasaśārivam
piṇḍatailaṃ tadabhyaṅgādvātaraktarujāpaham || 37
12śārivāsarjamañjiṣṭhāyaṣṭīsikthaiḥ payo'nvitaiḥ
tailaṃ pakvaṃ vimañjiṣṭhai ruborvā vātaraktanut || 38
13varamahiṣalocanodaraḥ
sannibhavarṇasya gugguloḥ prastham
prakṣipya toyarāśau
triphalāṃ ca yathoktaparimāṇām || 39
dvātriṃśacchinnaruhā-
palāni deyāni yatnena
vipacedapramatto
darvyā saṃghaṭṭayanmuhuryāvat || 40
ardhakṣayitaṃ toyaṃ
jātaṃ jvalanasya samparkāt
avatārya vastrapūtaṃ
punarapi sampādayetpātre || 41
sāndrībhūte tasmi-
nnavatārya himopalaprasthe
triphalācūrṇārdhapalaṃ
trikaṭoścūrṇaṃ ṣaḍakṣaparimāṇam || 42
krimiripucūrṇārdhapalaṃ
karṣaṃ trivṛddantyoḥ
palamekaṃ ca guḍūcyā
datvā saṃmūccharya yatnena || 43
upayujya cānupānaṃ
yūṣaṃ toyaṃ sugandhisalilena
icchāhāravihārī
bheṣajamupayujya sarvakālamidam || 44
tanurodhivātaśoṇita
mekajamatha dvandvajaṃ cirotthaṃ ca
jayati śrutaṃ pariśuṣkaṃ
sphuṭitaṃ cājānujaṃ cāpi || 45
vraṇakāsakuṣṭhagulmaṃ
śvayathūdarapāraṇḍumehāṃśca
mandāgniṃ ca vibaddhaṃ
pramehapiḍakāṃśca nāśayatyāśu || 46
satataṃ niṣevyamāṇaḥ
kālavaśāddhanti sarvagadān
abhibhūya jarādoṣaṃ
yāti kaiśorakaṃ rūpam || 47
pratyekaṃ triphalāprastho jalaṃ tatra ṣaḍāḍhakam
guḍavadgugguloḥ pākaḥ sugandhastu viśeṣataḥ || 48
14prasthamekaṃ guḍūcyāstu ardhaprasthaṃca gugguloḥ
pratyekaṃ triphalāyāśca tatpramāṇaṃ vinirdiśet || 49
sarvamekatra saṃkṣudya sādhayettvarmaṇe'mbhasi
pādaśeṣaṃ parisrāvya punaragnāvadhiśrayet || 50
tāvatpacetkaṣāyaṃ tu yāvatsāndratvamāgatam
dantīvyoṣaviḍaṅgāni guḍūcīphitralātvacaḥ || 51
tataścārdhapalaṃ pūtaṃ gṛhṇīyācca prati prati
karṣaṃ tu trivṛtāyāstu sarvamekatra kārayet || 52
tasminsusiddhaṃ vijñāya kavoṣṇe prakṣipedbudhaḥ
tataścāgnibalaṃ jñātvā tasya mātrāṃ pradāpayet || 53
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ gudajānyagnisādanam
duṣṭavraṇapramehāṃśca sāmavātaṃ bhagandaram || 54
nāḍyāḍhyavātaśvayathūnsarvānetānvyapohati
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaḥ pūrvamamṛtākhyo hi gugguluḥ
ardhaprasthaṃ triphalāyāḥ pratyekamiha gṛhyate || 55
15amṛtāyāśca dviprasthaṃ prasthamekaṃ ca gugguloḥ
pratyekaṃ triphalāprasthaṃ varṣābhūprasthameva ca || 56
sarvametacca saṃkṣudya kvāthayellavaṇāmbhasi
punaḥ pacetpādaśeṣaṃ yāvatsāndratvamāgatam || 57
dantīcitrakamūlānāṃ kaṇāviśvaphalātrikam
guḍūcītvaviḍaṅgānāṃ pratyekārdhapalonmitam || 58
trivṛtā karṣamekaṃ tu sarvamekatra cūrṇayet
siddhe coṣṇe kṣipettatra tvamṛtāgugguloḥ param || 59
yathāvahnibalaṃ khādedamlapittī viśeṣataḥ
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ gudajānyagnisādanam || 60
duṣṭavraṇapramehāṃśca sāmavātaṃ bhagandaram
nāḍyāḍhyavātaśvayathūnhanyātsarvāmayānayam
aśvibhyāṃ nirmito hyeṣo'mṛtākhyo gugguluḥ purā || 61
16śatāvarīnāgabalāvṛddhadārakamuccaṭāḥ
punarnavāmṛtākṛṣṇāvājigandhātrikaṇṭakam || 62
pṛthagdaśapalānyeṣāṃ ślakṣṇaṃ cūrṇāni kārayet
tadardhaśarkarāyuktaṃ cūrṇaṃ saṃmardayedbudhaḥ || 63
sthāpayetsudṛḍhe bhāṇḍe madhvardhāḍhakasaṃyutam
ghṛtaprasthe samāloḍya trisugandhipalena tu || 64
taṃ khādediṣṭaceṣṭānno yathāvahnibalaṃ naraḥ
vātaraktaṃ kṣayaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kārśyaṃ pittāsrasambhavam || 65
vātapittakaphotthāṃśca rogānanyāṃśca tadvidhān
hatvā karoti puruṣaṃ balīpalitavarjitam
yogasārāmṛto nāma lakṣmīkāntivivardhanaḥ || 66
divāsvapnāgnisantāpaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ tathā
kaṭūṣṇagurvabhiṣyandilavaṇāmlāni varjayet || 67
Adhikāra 24
1śleṣmaṇaḥ kṣapaṇaṃ yatsyānna ca mārutakopanam
tatsarvaṃ sarvadā kāryamūrustambhasya bheṣajam || 1
tasya na snehanaṃ kāryaṃ na bastirna ca recanam
sarvo rukṣaḥ kramaḥ kāryastatrādau kaphanāśanaḥ || 2
paścādvātavināśāya kṛtsnaḥ kāryaḥ kriyākramaḥ
śilājatuṃ gugguluṃ vā pippalīmatha nāgaram || 3
ūrustambhe pibenmūtrairdaśamūlīrasena vā
bhallātakāmṛtāśuṇṭhīdārupathyāpunarnavāḥ || 4
pañcamūlīdvayonmiśrā ūrustambhanibarhaṇāḥ
pippalīpippalīmūlabhallātakvātha eva vā || 5
kalko vā samadhurdeya ūrustambhavināśanaḥ
triphalācavyakaṭukagranthikaṃ madhunā lihet || 6
ūrustambhavināśāya puraṃ mūtreṇa vā pibet
lihyādvā triphalācūrṇaṃ kṣaudreṇa kaṭukāyutam || 7
sukhāmbunā pibedvāpi cūrṇaṃ ṣaḍdharaṇaṃ naraḥ
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā mākṣikeṇa guḍena vā || 8
ūrustambhe praśaṃsanti gaṇḍīrāriṣṭameva vā
cavyābhayāgnidārūṇāṃ samadhuḥ syādurugrahe || 9
kalko dihecca mūtrāḍhyaiḥ karañjaphalasarṣapaiḥ
kṣaudrasarṣapavalmīkamṛttikāsaṃyutaṃ bhiṣak || 10
gāḍhamutsādanaṃ kuryādūrustambhe salepanam
kaphakṣayārthaṃ vyāyāmeṣvenaṃ śakyeṣu yojayet || 11
sthalānyākrāmayetkalkaṃ pratisrotonadīstaret
palābhyāṃ pippalīmūlanāgarādaṣṭakaṭvaraḥ || 12
2tailaprasthaḥ samo dadhnā gṛdhrasyūrugrahāpahaḥ
aṣṭakaṭvarataile'tra tailaṃ sārṣapamiṣyate || 13
kuṣṭaśrīveṣṭakodīcyaṃ saralaṃ dārukeśaram
ajagandhāśvagandhā ca tailaṃ taiḥ sārṣapaṃ pacet || 14
sakṣaudraṃ mātrayā tasmādūrustambhārditaḥ pibet
saindhavādyaṃ hitaṃ tailaṃ varṣābhvamṛtagugguluḥ || 15
Adhikāra 25
1laṅghanaṃ svedanaṃ tiktaṃ dīpanāni kaṭūni ca
virecanaṃ snehapānaṃ vastayaścāmamārute || 1
saindhavādye nānuvāsya kṣārabastiḥ praśasyate
āmavāte pañcakolasiddhaṃ pānānnamiṣyate || 2
rukṣaḥ svedo vidhātavyo vālukāpuṭakaistathā
śaṭī śuṇṭhyabhayā cogrā devāhvātiviṣāmṛtā || 3
kaṣāyamāmavātasya pācanaṃ rūkṣabhojanam
śaṭīviśvauṣadhīkalkaṃ varṣābhūkvāthasaṃyutam || 4
saptarātraṃ pibejjanturāmavātavipācanam
daśamūlāmṛtairaṇḍarāsnānāgaradārubhiḥ
kvātho rubūkatailena sāmaṃ hantyanilaṃ gurum || 5
2daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa pibedvā nāgarāmbhasā
kukṣibastikaṭīśūle tailameraṇḍasambhavam || 6
rāsnāṃ guḍūcīmeraṇḍaṃ devadārumahauṣadham
pivetsarvāṅgike vāte sāme sandhyasthimajjage || 7
3rāsnāmṛtāragvadhadevadāru-
trikaṇṭakairaṇḍapunarnavānām
kvāthaṃ pibennāgaracūrṇamiśraṃ
jaṅghorupṛṣṭhatrikapārśvaśūlī || 8
4śuṇṭhīgokṣurakakvāthaḥ prātaḥ prātarniṣevitaḥ
sāmavāte kaṭīśūle pācano rukpraṇāśanaḥ || 9
āmavāte kaṇāyuktaṃ daśamūlarasaṃ pibet
khādedvāpyabhayāviśvaṃ guḍūcīṃ nāgareṇa vā || 10
eraṇḍatailasaṃyuktāṃ harītakīṃ bhakṣayennaro vidhivat
āmānilārtiyukto gṛdhrasīvṛddhyardito nityam || 11
karṣaṃ nāgaracūrṇasya kāñjikena pibetsadā
āmavātapraśamanaṃ kaphavātaharaṃ param || 12
pañcakolakacūrṇaṃ ca pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā
mandāgniśūlagulmāmakaphārocakanāśanam || 13
amṛtānāgaragokṣuramuṇḍatikāvaruṇakaiḥ kṛtaṃ cūrṇam
mastvāraṇālapītamāmānilanāśanaṃ khyātam || 14
māṇimanthasya bhāgau dvau yamānyāstadvadeva tu
bhāgāstrayo'jamodāyā nāgarādbhāgapañcakam || 15
daśa dvau ca harītakyāḥ ślakṣṇacūrṇīkṛtāḥ śubhāḥ
mastvāraṇālatakreṇa sarpiṣoṣṇodakena vā || 16
pītaṃ jayatyāmavātagulmaṃ hṛdvastijāngadān
plīhānaṃ hanti śūlādīnānāhaṃ gudajāni ca || 17
vibandhaṃ jāṭharānrogāṃstathā vai hastapādajān
vātānulomanamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ smṛtam || 18
5alambuṣāṃ gokṣurakaṃ guḍūcīṃ vṛddhadārakam
pippalīṃ trivṛtāṃ mustaṃ varuṇaṃ sapunarnavam || 19
triphalāṃ nāgaraṃ caiva sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet
mastvāraṇālatakreṇa payomāṃsarasena vā
āmavātaṃ nihantyāśu śvayathuṃ sandhisaṃsthitam || 20
6śatapuṣpā viḍaṅgaśca saindhavaṃ maricaṃ samam
cūrṇamuṣṇāmbunā pītamagnisandīpanaṃ param || 21
hiṅgu cavyaṃ biḍaṃ śuṇṭhī kṛṣṇājājīsapauṣkaram
bhāgottaramidaṃ cūrṇaṃ pītaṃ vātāmajidbhavet || 22
7citrakaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ yamānīṃ kāravīṃ tathā
viḍaṅgānyajamodāśca jīrakaṃ suradāru ca || 23
cavyailāsaindhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ rāsnāgokṣuradhānyakam
triphalāmustakaṃ vyoṣaṃ tvaguśīraṃ yavāgrajam || 24
tālīsapatraṃ patraṃ ca sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet
yāvantyetāni cūrṇāni tāvanmātraṃ tu guggulum || 25
saṃmardya sarpiṣā gāḍhaṃ snigdhe bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
tato mātrāṃ prayuñjīta yatheṣṭāhāravānapi || 26
yogarāja iti khyāto yogo'yamamṛtopamaḥ
āmavātāḍhyavātādīnkrimiduṣṭavraṇānapi || 27
plīhagulmodarānāhadurnāmāni vināśayet
agniṃ ca kurute dīptaṃ tejovṛddhiṃ balaṃ tathā
bātarogāñjayatyeṣa sandhimajjagatānapi || 28
8palatrayaṃ kaṣāyasya triphalāyāḥ sucūrṇitam
saugandhikapalaṃ caikaṃ kauśikasya palaṃ tathā || 29
kuḍavaṃ citratailasya sarvamādāya yatnataḥ
pācayetpākavidvaidyaḥ pātre lohamaye dṛḍhe || 30
hanti vātaṃ tathā pittaṃ śleṣmāṇaṃ khañjapaṅgutām
śvāsaṃ sudurjayaṃ hanti kāsaṃ pañcavidhaṃ tathā || 31
kuṣṭhāni vātaraktaṃ ca gulmaśūlodarāṇi ca
āmavātaṃ jayedetadapi vaidyavivarjitam || 32
etadabhyāsayogena jarāpalitanāśanam
sarpistailarasopetamaśnīyācchāliyaṣṭikam || 33
siṃhanāda iti khyāto rogabāraṇadarpahā
vahnivṛddhikaraḥ puṃsāṃ bhāṣito daṇḍapāṇinā || 34
9alambuṣāgokṣurakatriphalānāgarāmṛtāḥ
yathottaraṃ bhāgavṛddhyā śyāmācūrṇaṃ ca tatsamam || 35
pibenmastusurātakrakāñjikoṣṇodakena vā
pītaṃ jayatyāmavātaṃ saśothaṃ vātaśoṇitam
trikajānūrusandhisthaṃ jvarārocakanāśanam || 36
10pathyākṣadhātrītriphalābhāgaṃ vṛddhavayaḥkramaḥ
pathyāviśvayamānībhistulyābhiścūrṇitaṃ pibet || 37
takreṇoṣṇodakenātha athavā kāñjikena ca
āmavātaṃ nihantyāśu śothaṃ mandāgnitāmapi || 38
ajamodāmaricapippalī-
viḍaṅgasuradārucitrakaśatāhvāḥ
saindhavapippalimūlaṃ
bhāgā navakasya palikāḥ syuḥ || 39
śuṇṭhīdaśapalikā syāt
palāni tāvanti vṛddhadārasya
pathyāpañcapalāni
sarvāṇyekatra kārayeccūrṇam || 40
samaguḍavaṭakāṃ khādata-
ścūrṇaṃ vāpyuṣṇavāriṇā pibataḥ
naśyantyāmānilajāḥ
sarvā rogāḥ sukaṣṭāstu || 41
viśvacikāpratitūnī
tūnīhṛdrogāśca gṛdhrasī cogrā
kaṭibastigudasphuṭanaṃ
caivāsthijaṅghayostīvram || 42
śvayathustathāṅgasandhiṣu
ye cānye'pyāmavātasambhūtāḥ
sarve prayānti nāśaṃ
tama iva sūryāṃśuvidhvastam || 43
11nāgarakvāthakalkābhyāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
caturguṇena tenātha kevalenodakena vā || 44
vātaśleṣmapraśamanamagnisandīpanaṃ param
nāgaraṃ ghṛtamityuktaṃ kaṭyāmaśūlanāśanam || 45
12amṛtāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa kalkena ca mahauṣadhāt
mṛdvagninā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vātaraktaharaṃ param || 46
āmavātāḍhyavātādīn krimiduṣṭabraṇānapi
arśāṃsi gulmaśūlaṃ ca nāśayatyāśu yojitam || 47
13hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ cavyaṃ māṇimanthaṃ tathaiva ca
kalkānkṛtvā ca palikānghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 48
āraṇālāḍhakaṃ datvā tatsarpirjaṭharāpaham
śūlaṃ vibandhamānāhamāmavātaṃ kaṭīgraham
nāśayedgrahaṇīdoṣaṃ mandāgnerdīpanaṃ param || 49
14puṣṭyarthaṃ payasā sādhyaṃ dagdhaviṇmūtrasaṃgrahe
dīpanārthaṃ matimatā mastunā ca prakīrtitam || 50
sarpirnāgarakalkena sauvīrakacaturguṇam
siddhamagnikaraṃ śreṣṭhamāmavātaharaṃ param || 51
15rasonasya palaśataṃ tilasya kuḍavaṃ tathā
hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ kṣārau pañcaiva lavaṇāni ca || 52
śatapuṣpā tathā kuṣṭaṃ pippalīmūlacitrakau
ajamodā yamānī ca dhānyakaṃ cāpi buddhimān || 53
pratyekaṃ tu palaṃ caiṣāṃ sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet
ghṛtabhāṇḍe dṛḍhe caitatsthāpayeddinaṣoḍaśa || 54
prakṣipya tailamānīṃ ca prasthārdhaṃ kāñjikasya ca
khādetkarṣapramāṇaṃ tu toyaṃ madyaṃ pibedanu || 55
āmavāte tathā vāte sarvāṅgaikāṅgasaṃśrite
apasmāre'nale mande kāse śvāse gareṣu ca
sonmādavātabhaṅge ca śūle jantuṣu śasyate || 56
16prasāraṇyāḍhakakvāthe prastho guḍarasonataḥ
pakvaḥ pañcoṣaṇarajaḥ pādaḥ syādāmavātahā || 57
17bahulāyāḥ surāyāstu supakvāyāḥ śataṃ ghaṭe
tato'rdhena rasonaṃ tu saṃśuddhaṃ kuṭṭitaṃ kṣipet || 58
pippalīpippalīmūlamajājīkuṣṭhacitrakam
nāgaraṃ maricaṃ cavyaṃ cūrṇitaṃ cākṣasammitam || 59
saptāhātparataḥ peyā vātarogāmanāśinī
krimikuṣṭhakṣayānāhagulmārśaḥplīhamehanut
agnisandīpanī caiva pāṇḍurogabināśinī || 60
18siddhārthakakhalīprasthaṃ sudhautaṃ nistuṣaṃ jale
maṇḍaprasthaṃ vinikṣipya sthāpayeddivasatrayam || 61
dhānyarāśau tato dadyātsañcūrṇya palikāni ca
alambuṣā gokṣurakaṃ śatapuṣpīpunarnave || 62
prasāraṇī varuṇatvak śuṇṭhī madanameva ca
samyakpākaṃ tu vijñāya siddhā taṇḍulamiśritā || 63
mṛṣṭā sarṣapatailena hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyutā
bhakṣitā lavaṇopetā jayedāmaṃ mahājvaram || 64
ekajaṃ dvandvajaṃ sādhyaṃ sānnipātikameva ca
kaṭyūruvātamānāhajānujaṃ trikamāgatam
udāvartaharī peyā balavarṇāgnikāriṇī || 65
19tvagādihīnāḥ saṃśuṣkāḥ
pratyagrāḥ sakulādayaḥ
ślakṣṇacūrṇīkṛtaṃ teṣāṃ
śīte palaśatatrayam || 66
śatena kaṭutaile'sya vyoṣarāmaṭhadhānyakaiḥ
krimighnadīpyakaniśācavikāgranthikārdrakaiḥ
jīrakadvayavṛścīrasurasārjakaśigrukaiḥ || 67
daśamūlātmaguptābhyāṃ mārkavairlavaṇaistribhiḥ
cūrṇitaiḥ palikaiḥ sārdhamāraṇālapariplutaiḥ || 68
vinyasetsnehapātre ca dhānyarāśau punarnyaset
saptarātrātsamuddhṛtya pānabhakṣaṇabhojanaiḥ || 69
sidhmaleyaṃ prayoktavyā sāme vāte viśeṣataḥ
bhagnarugnābhyupahatāḥ?kampinaḥ pīṭhasarpiṇaḥ || 70
gṛdhrasīmagnisādaṃ ca śūlagulmodarāṇi ca
valīpalitakhālityaṃ hatvā syuramalendriyāḥ || 71
20dadhimatsyaguḍakṣīrapotakīmāṣapiṣṭakam
varjayedāmavātārto gurvabhiṣyandakāri ca || 72
Adhikāra 26
1vamanaṃ laṅghanaṃ svedaḥ pācanaṃ phalavartayaḥ
kṣāracūrṇāni guḍikāḥ śasyante śūlaśāntaye || 1
puṃsaḥ śūlābhipannasya sveda eva sukhāvahaḥ
pāyasaiḥ kṛśaraiḥ piṣṭaiḥ snigdhairvāpi sitotkaraiḥ || 2
vātātmakaṃ hantyacireṇa śūlaṃ
snehena yuktastu kulatthayūṣaḥ
sasaindhavo vyoṣayutaḥ salāvaḥ
sahiṅgusauvarcaladāḍimāḍhyaḥ || 3
balāpunarnavairaṇḍabṛhatīdvayagokṣuraiḥ
sahiṅgu lavaṇaṃ pītaṃ sadyo vātarujāpaham || 4
śūlī vibandhakoṣṭho'dbhiruṣṇābhiścūrṇitāḥ pibet
hiṅguprativiṣāvyoṣavacāsauvarcalābhayāḥ || 5
tumburūṇyabhayāhiṅgupauṣkaraṃ lavaṇatrayam
pibedyavāmbunā vātaśūlagulmāpatandrakī || 6
śyāmā biḍaṃ śigruphalāni pathyā-
viḍaṅgakampillakamaśvamūtrī
kalkaṃ samaṃ madyayutaṃ ca pītvā
śūlaṃ nihanyādanilātmakaṃ tu || 7
2yāminīhiṅgusindhūtthakṣārasauvarcalābhayāḥ
surāmaṇḍena pātavyā vātaśūlaniṣūdanāḥ || 8
viśvameraṇḍajaṃ mūlaṃ kvāthayitvā jalaṃ pivet
hiṅgusauvarcalopetaṃ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇam || 9
hiṅgupuṣkaramūlābhyāṃ hiṅgu sauvarcalena vā
viśvairaṇḍayavakvāthaḥ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇaḥ
tadvadrubuyavakvātho hiṅgusauvarcalānvitaḥ || 10
hiṅgvamlakṛṣṇālavaṇaṃ yamānī-
kṣārābhayāsaindhavatulyabhāgam
cūrṇaṃ pibedvāruṇamaṇḍamiśraṃ
śūle pravṛddhe'nilaje śivāya || 11
3sauvarcalāmlikājājīmaricairdviguṇottaraiḥ
mātuluṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭvā guḍikānilaśūlanut || 12
hiṅgvamlavetasavyoṣayamānīlavaṇatrikaiḥ
bījapūrarasopetairguḍikā vātaśūlanut || 13
bījapūrakamūlaṃ ca ghṛtena saha pāyayet
jayedvātabhavaṃ śūlaṃ karṣamekaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 14
bilvamūlatilairaṇḍaṃ piṣṭvā cāmlatuṣāmbhasā
guḍikāṃ bhrāmayeduṣṇāṃ vātaśūlavināśinīm || 15
tilaiśca guḍikāṃ kṛtvā bhrāmayejjaṭharopari
guḍikā śamayatyeṣā śūlaṃ caivātiduḥsaham || 16
nābhilepājjayecchūlaṃ madanaḥ kāñjikānvitaḥ
jīvantīmūlakalko vā satailaḥ pārśvaśūlanut || 17
4guḍaḥ śāliryavāḥ kṣīraṃ sarpiḥpānaṃ virecanam
jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni bhaiṣajaṃ pittaśūlinām || 18
paittaṃ tu śūle vamanaṃ payobhī
rasaistathekṣoḥ sapaṭolanimbaiḥ
śītāvagāhāḥ pulināḥ savātāḥ
kāṃsyādipātrāṇi jalaplutāni || 19
virecanaṃ pittaharaṃ ca śastaṃ
rasāśca śastāḥ śaśalāvakānām
santarpaṇaṃ lājamadhūpapannaṃ
yogāḥ suśītā madhusaṃprayuktāḥ || 20
chardyāṃ jvare pittabhave'pi śūle
ghore vidāhe tvatitarṣite ca
yavasya peyāṃ madhunā vimiśrāṃ
pibetsuśītāṃ manujaḥ sukhārthī || 21
dhātryā rasaṃ vidāryā vā trāyantī gostanāmbu vā
pivetsaśarkaraṃ sadyaḥ pittaśūlaniṣūdanam || 22
śatāvarīrasaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ prātaḥ pibennaraḥ
dāhaśūlopaśāntyarthaṃ sarvapittāmayāpaham || 23
bṛhatyau gokṣurairaṇḍakuśakāśekṣurālikāḥ
pītāḥ pittabhavaṃ śūlaṃ sadyo hanyuḥ sudāruṇam || 24
5śatāvarīsayaṣṭyāhvavāṭyālakuśagokṣuraiḥ
śṛtaśītaṃ pibettoyaṃ saguḍakṣaudraśarkaram || 25
pittāsṛgdāhaśūlaghnaṃ sadyo dāhajvarāpaham
triphalānimbayaṣṭyāhvakaṭukāragvadhaiḥ śṛtam || 26
pāyayenmadhusaṃmiśraṃ dāhaśūlopaśāntaye
tailameraṇḍajaṃ vāpi madhukakvāthasaṃyutam || 27
śūlaṃ pittodbhavaṃ hanyādgulmaṃ paittikameva ca
triphalāragvadhakvāthaṃ sakṣaudraṃ śarkarānvitam || 28
pāyayedraktapittaghnaṃ dāhaśūlanivāraṇam
pralihyātpittaśūlaghnaṃ dhātrīcūrṇaṃ samākṣikam || 29
6śleṣmādhike chardanalaṅghanāni
śirovirekaṃ madhuśīdhupānam
madhūni godhūmayavānariṣṭān
seveta rukṣānkaṭukāṃśca sarvān || 30
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
yavāgūrdīpanīyā syācchūlaghnī toyasādhitā || 31
lavaṇatrayasaṃyuktaṃ pañcakolaṃ sarāmaṭham
sukhoṣṇenāmbunā pītaṃ kaphaśūlavināśanam || 32
vilvamūlamathairaṇḍaṃ citrakaṃ viśvabheṣajam
hiṅgu saindhavasaṃyuktaṃ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇam || 33
7mustaṃ vacāṃ tiktakarohiṇīṃ ca
tathābhayāṃ nirdahanīṃ ca tulyām
pibettu gomūtrayutāṃ kaphottha-
śūle tathāmasya ca pācanārtham || 34
vacābdāgnyabhayātiktācūrṇaṃ gomūtrasaṃyutam
sakṣāraṃ vā pibetkvāthaṃ bilvādeḥ kaphaśūlanut || 35
mātuluṅgaraso vāpi śigrukvāthastathāparaḥ
sakṣāro madhunā pītaḥ pārśvahṛdbastiśūlanut || 36
āmaśūle kriyā kāryā kaphaśūlavināśinī
sevyamāmaharaṃ sarvaṃ yadagnibalavardhanam || 37
sahiṅgutumburuvyoṣayamānīcitrakābhayāḥ
sakṣāralavaṇāścūrṇaṃ pibetprātaḥ sukhāmbunā || 38
viṇmūtrānilaśūlaghnaṃ pācanaṃ vahnidīpanam
citrakaṃ granthikairaṇḍaśuṇṭhīdhānyaṃ jalaiḥ śṛtam || 39
śūlānāhavivandheṣu sahiṅgu biḍadāḍimam
dīpyakaṃ saindhavaṃ pathyā nāgaraṃ ca catuḥsamam
bhṛśaṃ śūlaṃ jayatyāśu mandasyāgneśca dīpanam || 40
samākṣikaṃ bṛhatyādi pibotpittānilātmake
vyāmiśraṃ vā vidhiṃ kuryācchūle pittānilātmake || 41
pittaje kaphaje vāpi yā kriyā kathitā pṛthak
ekīkṛtya prayuñjīta tāṃ kriyāṃ kaphapittaje || 42
paṭolatriphalāriṣṭakvāthaṃ madhuyutaṃ pibet
pittaśleṣmajvaracchardidāhaśūlopaśāntaye || 43
rasonaṃ madhusaṃmiśraṃ pibetprātaḥ prakāṅkṣitaḥ
vātaśleṣmabhavaṃ śūlaṃ vihantuṃ vahnidīpanam || 44
8viśvorubūkadaśamūlayavāmbhasā tu
dvikṣārahiṅgulavaṇatrayapuṣkarāṇām
cūrṇaṃ pibeddhṛdayapārśvakaṭīgrahāma-
pakvāśayāṃsabhṛśarugjvaragulmaśūlī || 45
kvāthena cūrṇapānaṃ yattatra kvāthapradhānatā
pravartate na tenātra cūrṇāpekṣī caturdravaḥ || 46
9cūrṇaṃ samaṃ rucakahiṅgumahauṣadhānāṃ
śuṇṭhyāmbunā kaphasamīraṇasambhavāsu
hṛtpārśvapṛṣṭhajaṭharārtiviṣūcikāsu
peyaṃ tathā yavarasena tu viḍvibandhe || 47
samaṃ śuṇṭhyambunetyevaṃ yojanā kriyate budhaiḥ
tenālpamānamevātra hiṅgu saṃparidīyate || 48
10hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ pathyābiḍasaindhavatumburu
pauṣkaraṃ ca pibeccūrṇaṃ daśamūlayavāmbhasā || 49
pārśvahṛtkaṭipṛṣṭhāṃsaśūle tantrāpatānake
śothe śleṣāmaseke ca karṇaroge ca śasyate || 50
eraṇḍabilvabṛhatīdvayamātuluṅga-
pāṣāṇabhittrikaṭumūlakṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ
sakṣārahiṅgulavaṇo rubutailamiśraḥ
śroṇyaṃsameḍhrahṛdayastanarukṣu peyaḥ || 51
11hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ kuṣṭaṃ yavakṣāro'tha saindhavam
mātuluṅgarasopetaṃ plīhaśūlāpahaṃ rajaḥ || 52
dagdhamanirgatadhūmaṃ
mṛgaśṛṅgaṃ goghṛtena saha pītam
hṛdayanitambajaśūlaṃ
harati śikhī dārunivahamiva || 53
krimiripucūrṇaṃ līḍhaṃ svarasena vaṅgasenasya
kṣapayatyacirānniyataṃ leho'jīrṇodbhavaṃ śūlam || 54
vidārīdāḍimarasaḥ savyoṣalavaṇānvitaḥ
kṣaudrayukto jayatyāśu śūlaṃ doṣatrayodbhavam || 55
12eraṇḍaphalamūlāni bṛhatīdvayagokṣuram
parṇinyaḥ sahadevī ca siṃhapucchī kṣurālikā || 56
tulyairetaiḥ śṛtaṃ toyaṃ yavakṣārayutaṃ pibet
pṛthagdoṣabhavaṃ śūlaṃ hanyātsarvabhavaṃ tathā || 57
13gomūtrasiddhaṃ maṇḍūraṃ triphalācūrṇasaṃyutam
vilihanmadhusarpirbhyāṃ śūlaṃ hanti tridoṣajam || 58
śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ salavaṇaṃ sahiṅguvyoṣasaṃyutam
uṣṇodakena tatpītaṃ śūlaṃ hanti tridoṣajam || 59
tīkṣṇāyaścūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ triphalācūrṇamuttamam
prayojyaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ sarvaṃ śūlanivāraṇam || 60
mūtrāntaḥpācitāṃ śuddhāṃ lauhacūrṇasamanvitām
saguḍāmabhayāmadyātsarvaśūlapraśāntaye || 61
14pippalī nāgaraṃ bilvaṃ kāravīcavyacitrakam
hiṅgudāḍimavṛkṣāmlavacākṣārāmlavetasam || 62
varṣābhūkṛṣṇalavaṇamajājībījapūrakam
dadhi triguṇitaṃ sarpistatsiddhaṃ dādhikaṃ smṛtam || 63
gulmārśaḥplīhahṛtpārśvaśūlayonirujāpaham
doṣasaṃśamanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dādhikaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam || 64
15kambalāvṛtagātrasya prāṇāyāmaṃ prakurvataḥ
kaṭutailāktaśaktūnāṃ dhūpaḥ śūlaharaḥ paraḥ || 65
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ madyaṃ lavaṇaṃ kaṭuvaidalam
vegarodhaṃ śucaṃ krodhaṃ varjayecchūlavānnaraḥ || 66
Adhikāra 27
1vamanaṃ tiktamadhurairvirekaścāpi śasyate
bastayaśca hitāḥ śūle pariṇāmasamudbhave || 1
viḍaṅgataṇḍulavyoṣaṃ trivṛddantī sacitrakam
sarvāṇyetāni saṃskṛtya sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || 2
guḍena modakaṃ kṛtvā bhakṣayetprātarutthitaḥ
uṣṇodakānupānaṃ tu dadyādagnivivardhanam || 3
jayettridoṣajaṃ śūlaṃ pariṇāmasamudbhavam
2nāgaratilaguḍakalkaṃ
payasā saṃsādhya yaḥ pumānadyāt || 4
ugraṃ pariṇatiśūlaṃ
tasyāpaiti trisaptarātreṇa
śambūkajaṃ bhasma pītaṃ jalenoṣṇena tatkṣaṇāt || 5
paktijaṃ vinihantyetacchūlaṃ viṣṇurivāsurān
akṣadhātryabhayākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ madhuyutaṃ lihet || 6
dadhnā lūnasāreṇādyātsatīlayavaśaktukān
acirānmucyate śūlānnaro'nuparivartanāt || 7
tilanāgarapathyānāṃ bhāgaṃ śambūkabhasmanām
dvibhāgaṃ guḍasaṃyuktaṃ guḍīṃ kṛtvākṣabhāgikām || 8
śītāmbupānāṃ pūrvāhṇe bhakṣayetkṣīrabhojanaḥ
sāyāhne rasakaṃ pītvā naro mucyeta durjayāt || 9
pariṇāmasamutthācca śūlāccirabhavādapi
śambūkaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ caiva pañcaiva lavaṇāni ca || 10
samāṃśāṃ guḍikāṃ kṛtvā kalambūrasakena vā
prātarbhojanakāle vā bhakṣayettu yathābalam || 11
śūlādvimucyate jantuḥ sahasā pariṇāmajāt
yaḥ pibati saptarātraṃ
śaktūnekānkalāyayūṣeṇa || 12
sa jayati pariṇāmarujaṃ
cirajāmapi kimuta nūtanajām
3lohacūrṇaṃ varāyuktaṃ vilīḍhaṃ madhusarpiṣā || 13
pariṇāmaśūlaṃ śamayettanmalaṃ vā prayojitam
kṛṣṇābhayālauhacūrṇaṃ guḍena saha bhakṣayet || 14
paktiśūlaṃ nihantyetajjaṭharāṇyagnimandatām
āmavātavikārāṃśca sthaulyaṃ caivāpakarṣati || 15
pathyāloharajaḥ śuṇṭhīcūrṇaṃ mākṣikasarpiṣā
pariṇāmarujaṃ hanti vātapittakaphātmikām || 16
4sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ kṣāro rucakaṃ raumakaṃ biḍam
dantīlauharajaḥ kiṭṭaṃ trivṛcchūraṇakaṃ samam || 17
dadhigomūtrapayasā mandapāvakapācitam
tadyathāgnibalaṃ cūrṇaṃ pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā || 18
jīrṇe jīrṇe tu bhuñjīta māṃsādighṛtasādhitam
nābhiśūlaṃ yakṛcchūlaṃ gulmaplīhakṛtaṃ ca yat || 19
vidradhyaṣṭhīlikāṃ hanti kaphavātodbhavāṃ tathā
śūlānāmapi sarvepāmauṣadhaṃ nāsti tatparam || 20
pariṇāmasamutthasya viśeṣeṇāntakṛnmatam
5nārikelaṃ satoyaṃ ca lavaṇena prapūritam || 21
vipakvamagninā samyakpariṇāmajaśūlanut
vātikaṃ paittikaṃ caiva ślaiṣmikaṃ sānnipātikam || 22
madhukaṃ triphalācūrṇamayorajaḥsamaṃ lihan
madhusarpiryutaṃ samyaggavyaṃ kṣīraṃ pibedanu || 23
chardiṃ satimirāṃ śūlamamlapittaṃ jvaraṃ klamam
ānāhaṃ mūtrasaṅgaṃ ca śothaṃ caiva nihanti saḥ || 24
6sapippalīguḍaṃ sarpiḥ pacetkṣīracaturguṇe
vinihantyamlapittaṃ ca śūlaṃ ca pariṇāmajam || 25
7kvāthena kalkena ca pippalīnāṃ
siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktam
kṣīrānnapasyaiva nihantyavaśyaṃ
śūlaṃ pravṛddhaṃ pariṇāmasaṃjñam || 26
8kolāgranthikaśṛṅgaveracapalā-
kṣāraiḥ samaṃ cūrṇitaṃ
maṇḍūraṃ surabhījale'ṣṭaguṇite
paktvātha sāndrīkṛtam
taṃ khādedaśanādimadhyaviratau
prāyeṇa dugdhānnabhuk
jetuṃ vātakaphāmayānpariṇatau
śūlaṃ ca śūlāni ca || 27
9kolāgranthikasahitai-
rviśvauṣadhamāgadhīyavakṣāraiḥ
prasthamayorajasāmapi
palikāṃśaiścūrṇitairmiśraiḥ || 28
aṣṭaguṇamūtrayuktaṃ
kramapākātpiṇḍitāṃ nayetsarvam
kolapramāṇā guḍikā-
stisro bhojyādimadhyaviratau ca || 29
rasasarpiryūṣapayo-
māṃsairaśnannaro nivārayati
annavivartanamante
gulmaṃ plīhāgnisādāṃśca || 30
10lohakiṭṭapalānyaṣṭau gomūtrārdhāḍhake pacet
kṣīraprasthena tatsiddhaṃ paktiśūlaharaṃ nṛṇām || 31
11lohakiṭṭapalānyaṣṭau gomūtre'ṣṭaguṇe pacet
cavikānāgarakṣārapippalīmūlapippalīḥ || 32
saṃcūrṇya nikṣipettasminpalāṃśāḥ sāndratāṃ gate
guḍikāḥ kalpayettena paktiśūlanivāriṇīḥ || 33
12maṇḍūraṃ śodhitaṃ patrīṃ lohajāṃ vā guḍena tu
bhakṣayenmucyate śūlātpariṇāmasamudbhavāt || 34
saṃśodhya cūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍūrasya palāṣṭakam
śatāvarīrasasyāṣṭau dadhnastu payasastathā || 35
palānyādāya catvāri tathā gavyasya sarpiṣaḥ
vipacetsarvamaikadhyaṃ yāvatpiṇḍatvamāgatam || 36
siddhaṃ tu bhakṣayenmadhye bhojanasyāgrato'pi vā
vātātmakaṃ pittabhavaṃ śūlaṃ ca pariṇāmajam || 37
nihantyeva hi yogo'yaṃ maṇḍūrasya na saṃśayaḥ
13viḍaṅgaṃ citrakaṃ cavyaṃ triphalā tryūṣaṇāni ca || 38
navabhāgāni caitāni lohakiṭṭasamāni ca
gomūtraṃ dviguṇaṃ datvā mūtrārdhikaguḍānvitam || 39
śanairmṛdvagninā paktvā susiddhaṃ piṇḍatāṃ gatam
snigdhe bhāṇḍe vinikṣipya bhakṣayetkolamātrayā || 40
prāṅmadhyādikrameṇaiva bhojanasya prayojitam
yogo'yaṃ śamayatyāśu paktiśūlaṃ sudāruṇam || 41
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca śothaṃ mandāgnitāmapi
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣaṃ krimigulmodarāṇi ca || 42
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca sthaulyaṃ caivāpakarṣati
varjayecchuṣkaśākāni vidāhyamlakaṭūni ca || 43
paktiśūlāntako hyeṣa guḍīmaṇḍūrasaṃjñakaḥ
śūlārtānāṃ kṛpāhetostārayā parikīrtitaḥ || 44
14vaśiraṃ śvetavāṭyālaṃ madhuparṇī mayūrakam
taṇḍulīyaṃ ca karṣārdhaṃ dattvādhaścordhvameva ca || 45
pākyaṃ sujīrṇaṃ maṇḍūraṃ gomūtreṇa dinadvayam
antarbāṣpamadagdhaṃ ca tathā sthāpyaṃ dinatrayam || 46
vicūrṇya dviguṇenaiva guḍena suvimarditam
bhojanasyādimadhyānte bhakṣyaṃ karṣaṃ tribhāgataḥ || 47
takrānupānaṃ varjyaṃ ca vārkṣamamlakamatra tu
āmlapitte ca śūle ca hitametadyathāmṛtam || 48
15pathyācūrṇaṃ dvipalaṃ
gandhakasāraṃ ca lohakiṭṭaṃ ca
śuddharasasyārdhapalaṃ
bhṛṅgasya rasaṃ ca keśarājasya || 49
prasthonmitaṃ ca datvā
lauhe pātre'tha daṇḍasaṃghṛṣṭam
śuṣkaṃ ghṛtamadhuyuktaṃ
mṛditaṃ sthāpyaṃ ca bhāṇḍake snigdhe || 50
upayuktametadacirā-
nnihanti kaphapittajānrogān
śūlaṃ tathāmlapittaṃ
grahaṇīmapi kāmalāmugrām || 51
16akṣāmalakaśivānāṃ
svarasaiḥ pakvaṃ sulohajaṃ cūrṇam
saguḍaṃ yadyupabhuṅkte
muñcati sadyastridoṣajaṃ śūlam || 52
17lohasya rajaso bhāgastriphalāyāstathā trayaḥ
guḍasyāṣṭau tathā bhāgā guḍānmūtraṃ caturguṇam || 53
etatsarvaṃ ca vipacedguḍapākavidhānavit
lihecca tadyathāśakti kṣaye śūle ca pākaje || 54
18dhātrīcūrṇasyāṣṭau
palāni catvāri lohacūrṇasya
yaṣṭīmadhukarajaśca
dvipalaṃ dadyātsadopale ghṛṣṭam || 55
amṛtākvāthenaita-
ccūrṇaṃ bhāvyaṃ ca saptāham
caṇḍātapeṣu śuṣkaṃ
bhūyaḥ piṣṭvā nave ghaṭe sthāpyam || 56
ghṛtamadhunā saha yuktaṃ
bhuktvādau madhyatastathānte ca
trīnapi vārānkhāde-
tpathyaṃ doṣānubandhena || 57
bhaktasyādau nāśayati
vyādhīnpittānilodbhavānsadyaḥ
madhye'nnaviṣṭambhaṃ
jayati nṛṇāṃ saṃvidahyate nānnam || 58
pānānnakṛtānrogā-
nbhuktyante śīlitaṃ jayati
evaṃ jīryati cānne
nihanti śūlaṃ nṛṇāṃ sukaṣṭamapi || 59
harati sahasā yukto
yogaścāyaṃ jaratpittam
cakṣuṣyaḥ palitaghnaḥ
kaphapittasamudbhavāñjayedrogān || 60
prasādayatyapi raktaṃ
pāṇḍutvaṃ kāmalāṃ jayati
19tanūni lohapatrāṇi tilotsedhasamāni ca || 61
kaśikāmūlakalkena saṃlipya sarṣapeṇa vā
viśoṣya sūryakiraṇaiḥ punarevāvalepayet || 62
triphalāyā jale dhmātaṃ vāpayecca punaḥ punaḥ
tataḥ saṃcūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā karpaṭena tu chānayet || 63
bhakṣayenmadhusarpirbhyāṃ yathāgnyetatprayogataḥ
māṣakaṃ triguṇaṃ vātha caturguṇamathāpi vā || 64
chāgasya payasaḥ kuryādanupānamabhāvataḥ
gavāṃ ghṛtena dugdhena catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇena ca || 65
paktiśūlaṃ nihantyetanmāsenaikena niścitam
lohāmṛtamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā || 66
kakārapūrvakaṃ yacca yaccāmlaṃ parikīrtitam
sevyaṃ tanna bhavedatra māṃsaṃ cānūpasambhavam || 67
20svinnapīḍitakūṣmāṇḍāttulārdhaṃ mṛṣṭamājyataḥ
prasthārdhe khaṇḍatulyaṃ tu pacedāmalakīrasāt || 68
prasthe susvinnakūṣmāṇḍarasaprasthe vighaṭṭayan
darvyā pākaṃ gate tasmiṃścūrṇīkṛtya vinikṣipet || 69
dve dve pale kaṇājājīśuṇṭhīnāṃ maricasya ca
palaṃ tālīsadhanyākacāturjātakamustakam || 70
karṣapramāṇaṃ pratyekaṃ prasthārdhaṃ mākṣikasya ca
paktiśūlaṃ nihantyetaddoṣatrayabhavaṃ ca yat || 71
chardyamlapittamūrcchāśca śvāsakāsāvarocakam
hṛcchalaṃ raktapittaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet
rasāyanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ khaṇḍāmalakasaṃjñitam || 72
21kuḍavamitamiha syānnārikelaṃ supiṣṭaṃ
palaparimitasarpiḥpācitaṃ khaṇḍatulyam
nijapayasi tadetatprasthamātre vipakvaṃ
guḍavadatha suśīte śāṇabhāgānkṣipecca || 73
dhanyākapippalipayodatugādvijīrā-
ñśāṇaṃ trijātamibhakeśaravadvicūrṇya
hantyamlapittamaruciṃ kṣayamasrapittaṃ
śūlaṃ vamiṃ sakalapauruṣakāri hāri || 74
22kalāyacūrṇabhāgau dvau lohacūrṇasya cāparaḥ
kāravellapalāśānāṃ rasenaiva vimarditaḥ || 75
karṣamātrāṃ tataścaikāṃ bhakṣayedguṭikāṃ naraḥ
maṇḍānupānātsā hanti jaratpittaṃ sudāruṇam || 76
lihyādvā traiphalaṃ cūrṇamayaścūrṇasamanvitam
yaṣṭīcūrṇena vā yuktaṃ lihyātkṣaudreṇa tadgade || 77
pittāntaṃ vamanaṃ kṛtvā kaphāntaṃ ca virecanam
annadrave ca tatkāryaṃ jaratpitte yadīritam || 78
āmapakvāśaye śuddhe gacchedannadravaḥ śamam
māṣeṇḍarī? satuṣikā svinnā sarpiryutā hitā || 79
godhūmamaṇḍakaṃ tatra sarpiṣā guḍasaṃyutam
sasitaṃ śītadugdhena mṛditaṃ vā hitaṃ matam || 80
śālitaṇḍulamaṇḍaṃ vā kavoṣṇaṃ sikthavarjitam
vāṭyaṃ kṣīreṇa saṃsiddhaṃ ghṛtapūraṃ saśarkaram || 81
śarkarāṃ bhakṣayitvā vā kṣīramutkvathitaṃ pibet
paṭolapatrayūṣeṇa khādeccaṇakaśaktukān || 82
annadrave jaratpitte vahnirmando bhavedyataḥ
tasmādatrānnapānāni mātrāhīnāni kalpayet || 83
Adhikāra 28
1trivṛtsudhāpatratilādiśāka-
grāmyaudakānūparasairyavānnam
anyaiśca mṛṣṭānilamūtraviḍbhi-
radyātprasannāguḍasīdhupāyī || 1
āsthāpanaṃ mārutaje snigdhasvinnasya śasyate
purīṣaje tu kartavyo vidhirānāhikaśca yaḥ || 2
kṣāravaitaraṇau bastī yuñjyāttatra cikitsakaḥ
śyāmādantīdravantītvaṅmahāśyāmāsnuhītrivṛt || 3
saptalā śaṅkhinī śvetā rājavṛkṣaḥ satilvakaḥ
kampillakaṃ karañjaśca hemakṣīrītyayaṃ gaṇaḥ || 4
sarpistailarajaḥkvāthakalkeṣvanyatameṣu ca
udāvartodarānāhaviṣagulmavināśanaḥ || 5
trivṛtkṛṣṇāharītakyo dvicatuḥpañcabhāgikāḥ
guḍikā guḍatulyāstā viḍvibandhagadāpahāḥ || 6
harītakīyavakṣārapīlūni trivṛtā tathā
ghṛtaiścūrṇamidaṃ peyamudāvartavināśanam || 7
hiṅgukuṣṭhabacāsarjibiḍaṃ ceti dviruttaram
pītaṃ madyena taccūrṇamudāvartaharaṃ param || 8
khaṇḍapalaṃ trivṛtā sama-
mupakulyākarṣacūrṇitaṃ ślakṣṇam
prāgbhojane ca samadhu
biḍālapadakaṃ lihetprājñaḥ || 9
etadgāḍhapurīṣe
pitte sakaphe ca viniyojyam
svādurnṛpayogyo'yaṃ
cūrṇo nārācako nāmnā || 10
2rasonaṃ madyasaṃmiśraṃ pibetprātaḥ prakāṅkṣitaḥ
gulmodāvartaśūlaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ balavardhanam || 11
hiṅgumākṣikasindhūtthaiḥ paktvā vartiṃ sunirmitām
ghṛtābhyaktāṃ gude dadyādudāvartavināśinīm || 12
madanaṃ pippalī kuṣṭhaṃ vacā gaurāśca sarṣapāḥ
guḍakṣārasamāyuktāḥ phalavartiḥ praśasyate || 13
agāradhūmasindhūtthatailayuktāmlamūlakam
kṣuṇṇaṃ nirguṇḍipatraṃ vā svinne pāyau kṣipedbudhaḥ || 14
sauvarcalāḍhyāṃ madirāṃ mūtre tvabhihate pibet
elāṃ vāpyatha madyena kṣīraṃ vāri pibecca saḥ || 15
duḥsparśāsvarasaṃ vāpi kaṣāyaṃ kakubhasya ca
ervārubījaṃ toyena pibedvā lavaṇīkṛtam || 16
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ drākṣārasamathāpi vā
sarvathaivopayuñjīta mūtrakṛcchrāśmarīvidhim || 17
snehasvedairudāvartaṃ jṛmbhajaṃ samupācaret
aśrumokṣo'śruje kāryaḥ svapno madyaṃ priyāḥ kathāḥ || 18
kṣavaje kṣarapatreṇa ghrāṇasthenānayetkṣavam
tathordhvajatruko'bhyaṅgaḥ svedo dhūmaḥ sanāvanaḥ || 19
hitaṃ vātaghnamadyaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ cottarabhaktikam
udgāraje kramopetaṃ snaihikaṃ dhūmamācaret || 20
chardyāghātaṃ yathādoṣaṃ nasyasnehādibhirjayet
bhuktvā pracchardanaṃ dhūmo laṅghanaṃ raktamokṣaṇam || 21
rukṣānnapāne vyāyāmo virekaścātra śasyate
vastiśuddhikarāvāpaṃ caturguṇajalaṃ payaḥ || 22
āvārināśātkvathitaṃ pītavantaṃ prakāmataḥ
ramayeyuḥ priyā nāryaḥ śukrodāvartinaṃ naram || 23
atrābhyaṅgāvagāhāśca madirāścaraṇāyudhāḥ
śāliḥ payonirūhāśca śastaṃ maithunameva ca || 24
kṣudvighāte hitaṃ snigdhamuṣṇamalpaṃ ca bhojanam
tṛṣṇāghāte pibenmanthaṃ yavāgūṃ vāpi śītalām || 25
rasenādyātsuviśrāntaḥ śramaśvāsāturo naraḥ
nidrāghāte pibetkṣīraṃ svapnaḥ saṃvāhanāni ca
udāvartakriyānāhe sāme laṅghanapācanam || 26
3dviruttarā hiṅgu vacā sakuṣṭhā
suvarcikā ceti viḍaṅgacūrṇam
sukhāmbunānāhaviṣūcikārti-
hṛdrogagulmordhvasamīraṇaghnam || 27
vacābhayācitrakayāvaśūkān
sapippalīkātiviṣānsakuṣṭhān
uṣṇāmbunānāhavimūḍhavātān
pītvā jayedāśu hitaudanāśī || 28
trivṛddharītakīśyāmāḥ snuhīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet
vaṭikā mūtrapītāstāḥ śreṣṭhāścānāhabhedikāḥ || 29
phalaṃ ca mūlaṃ ca virecanoktaṃ
hiṅgvarkamūlaṃ daśamūlamagryam
snukcitrakau caiva punarnavā ca
tulyāni sarvairlavaṇāni pañca || 30
snehaiḥ samūtraiḥ saha jarjarāṇi
śarāvasandhau vipacetsulipte
pakvaṃ supiṣṭaṃ lavaṇaṃ tadannaiḥ
pānaistathānāharujāghnamagryam || 31
vāṭhadhūmabiḍavyoṣaguḍamūtrairvipācitā
gude'ṅguṣṭhasamā vartirvidheyānāhaśūlanut || 32
vartistrikaṭukasaindhava-
sarpapagṛhadhūmakuṣṭhamadanaphalaiḥ
madhuni guḍe vā paktvā
pāyāvaṅguṣṭhamānato veśya || 33
vartiriyaṃ dṛṣṭaphalā
gude śanaiḥ praṇihitā ghṛtābhyaktā
ānāhodāvarta-
praśamanī jaṭharagulmanivāriṇī || 34
mūlakaṃ śuṣkamārdraṃ ca varṣābhū pañcamūlakam
ārevataphalaṃ cāpi piṣṭvā tena paceddhṛtam
tatpīyamānaṃ śamayedudāvartamasaṃśayam || 35
5sthirādivargasya punarnabāyāḥ
sampākapūtīkakarañjayośca
siddhaḥ kaṣāye dvipalāṃśikānāṃ
prastho ghṛtātsyātpratiruddhavāte || 36
Adhikāra 29
1laghvannaṃ dīpanaṃ snigdhamuṣṇaṃ vātānulomanam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ yadbhavetsarvaṃ taddhitaṃ sarvagulminām || 1
snigdhasya bhiṣajā svedaḥ kartavyo gulmaśāntaye
srotasāṃ mārdavaṃ kṛtvā jitvā mārutamulbaṇam || 2
bhitvā vibaddhaṃ snigdhasya svedo gulmamapohati
kumbhīpiṇḍeṣṭakāsvedānkārayetkuśalo bhiṣak || 3
upānāhāśca kartavyāḥ sukhoṣṇāḥ śālvaṇādayaḥ
sthānāvaseko raktasya bāhumadhye śirāvyadhaḥ || 4
svedānulomanaṃ caiva praśastaṃ sarvagulminām
peyā vātaharaiḥ siddhāḥ kaulatthā dhanvajā rasāḥ || 5
khaṃḍāḥ sapañcamūlāśca gulmināṃ bhojane hitāḥ
2mātuluṅgaraso hiṅgu dāḍimaṃ biḍasaindhavam || 6
surāmaṇḍena pātavyaṃ vātagulmarujāpaham
nāgarārdhapalaṃ piṣṭaṃ dve pale luñcitasya ca || 7
tilasyaikaṃ guḍapalaṃ kṣīreṇoṣṇena pāyayet
vātagulmamudāvartaṃ yoniśūlaṃ ca nāśayet || 8
pivederaṇḍatailaṃ vā vāruṇīmaṇḍamiśritam
tadeva tailaṃ payasā vātagulmī pibennaraḥ || 9
sādhayecchuddhaśuṣkasya laśunasya catuḥpalam
kṣīrodake'ṣṭaguṇite kṣīraśeṣaṃ ca pāyayet || 10
vātagulmamudāvartaṃ gṛdhrasīṃ viṣamajvaram
hṛdrogaṃ vidradhiṃ śoṣaṃ śamayatyāśu tatpayaḥ || 11
evaṃ tu sādhite kṣīre stokamapyatra dīyate
sarjikākuṣṭhasahitaḥ kṣāraḥ ketakijo'pi vā || 12
tailena pītaḥ śamayedgulmaṃ pavanasambhavam
3vātagulme kaphe vṛddhe vāntiścūrṇādiriṣyate || 13
paitte tu recanaṃ snigdhaṃ rakte raktasya mokṣaṇam
snigdhoṣṇenodite gulme paittike sraṃsanaṃ hitam || 14
rūkṣoṣṇena tu sambhūte sarpiḥ praśamanaṃ param
kākolyādimahātiktavāsādyaiḥ pittagulminam || 15
snehitaṃ sraṃsayetpaścādyojayedbastikarmaṇā
snigdhoṣṇaje pittagulme kampillaṃ madhunā lihet || 16
recanārthī rasaṃ vāpi drākṣāyāḥ saguḍaṃ pibet
dāhaśūlā'nilakṣobhasvapnanāśārucijvaraiḥ || 17
vidahyamānaṃ jānīyādgulmaṃ tamupanāhayet
pakve tu vraṇavatkāryaṃ vyadhaśodhanaropaṇam || 18
svayamūrdhvamadho vāpi sa ceddoṣaḥ prapadyate
dvādaśāhamupekṣeta rakṣannanyānupadravān || 19
paraṃ tu śocanaṃ sarpiḥ śuddhaṃ samadhutiktakam
rohiṇī kaṭukā nimbaṃ madhukaṃ triphalātvacaḥ || 20
karṣāṃśāstrāyamāṇā ca paṭolatrivṛtāpale
dvipalaṃ ca masūrāṇāṃ sādhyamaṣṭaguṇe'mbhasi || 21
ghṛtāccheṣaṃ ghṛtasamaṃ sarpiṣaśca catuḥpalam
pivetsaṃmūrcchitaṃ tena gulmaḥ śāmyati paittikaḥ || 22
jvarastṛṣṇā ca śūlaṃ ca bhramamūrcchāratistathā
dīptāgnayo mahākāyāḥ snehasātmyāśca ye narāḥ || 23
gulminaḥ sarpadaṣṭāśca visarpopahatāśca ye
jyeṣṭhāṃ mātrāṃ pibeyuste palānyaṣṭau viśeṣataḥ || 24
4laṅghanollekhane svede kṛte'gnau saṃpradhukṣite
ghṛtaṃ sakṣārakaṭukaṃ pātavyaṃ kaphagulminā || 25
mando'gnirvedanā mandā gurustimitakoṣṭhatā
sotkleśā cāruciryasya sa gulmo vamanopamaḥ || 26
mande'gnāvanile mūḍhe jñātvā sasnehamāśayam
guḍikāścūrṇaniryūhāḥ prayojyāḥ kaphagulminām || 27
kṣārā'riṣṭagaṇaścāpi dāhaśoṣe vidhīyate
pañcamūlīkṛtaṃ toyaṃ purāṇaṃ vāruṇīrasam || 28
kaphagulmī pibetkāle jīrṇaṃ mādhvīkameva vā
tilairaṇḍātasībījasarṣapaiḥ parilipya vā || 29
śleṣmagulmamayaḥpātraiḥ sukhoṣṇaiḥ svedayedbhiṣak
yamānīcūrṇitaṃ takraṃ biḍena lavaṇīkṛtam || 30
pibetsandīpanaṃ vātamūtravarco'nulomanam
vyāmiśradoṣe vyāmiśraḥ sarva eva kriyākramaḥ || 31
5sannipātodbhave gulme tridoṣaghno vidhirhitaḥ
vacābiḍābhayāśuṇṭhīhiṅgukuṣṭhāgnidīpyakāḥ || 32
dvitriṣaṭcaturevāṣṭasaptapañcāśikāḥ kramāt
cūrṇamadyādibhiḥ pītaṃ gulmānāhodarāpaham || 33
śūlārśaḥ śvāsakāsaghnaṃ grahaṇīdīpanaṃ param
yamānīhiṅgusindhūtthakṣārasauvarcalābhayāḥ || 34
surāmaṇḍena pātavyā gulmaśūlanivāraṇāḥ
6hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ pāṭhāṃ hapuṣāmabhayāṃ śaṭhīm || 35
ajamodājagandhe ca tintiḍīkāmlavetasau
dāḍimaṃ pauṣkaraṃ dhānyamajājīṃ citrakaṃ vacām
dvau kṣārau lavaṇe dve ca cavyaṃ caikatra cūrṇayet || 36
cūrṇametatprayoktavyamannapāneṣvanatyayam
prāgbhaktamathavā peyaṃ madyenoṣṇodakena vā || 37
pārśvahṛdbastiśūleṣu gulme vātakaphātmake
ānāhe mūtrakṛcchre ca gudayonirujāsu ca || 38
grahaṇyarśovikāreṣu plīhni pāṇḍvāmaye'rucau
urovibandhe hikkāyāṃ śvāse kāse galagrahe || 39
bhāvitaṃ mātuluṅgasya cūrṇametadrasena vā
bahuśoguḍikāḥ kāryāḥ kārṣikāḥ syustato'dhikam || 40
7pūtīkapatragajacirbhaṭicavyavahni-
vyoṣaṃ ca saṃstaracitaṃ lavaṇopadhānam
dagdhvā vicūrṇya dadhimaṇḍayutaṃ prayojyaṃ
gulmodaraśvayathupāṇḍugadodbhaveṣu || 41
hiṅgupuṣkaramūlāni tumburūṇi harītakīm
śyāmā biḍaṃ saindhavaṃ ca yavakṣāraṃ mahauṣadham || 42
yavakvāthodakenaitaddhṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ tu pāyayet
tenāsya bhidyate gulmaḥ saśūlaḥ saparigrahaḥ || 43
vacā harītakī hiṅgu saindhavaṃ sāmlavetasam
yavakṣāraṃ yamānīṃ ca pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā || 44
etaddhi gulmanicayaṃ saśūlaṃ saparigraham
bhinatti saptarātreṇa bahnerdīptiṃ karoti ca || 45
pippalīpippalīmūlacitrakājājisaindhavaiḥ
yuktā pītā surā hanti gulmamāśu sudustaram || 46
nādeyīkuṭajārkaśigru bṛhatī-
snugbilvabhallātaka-
vyāghrī kiṃśukapāribhadrakajaṭā-
'pāmārganīpāgnikam
vāsāmuṣkakrapāṭalāḥ salavaṇā
dagdhvā jale pācitaṃ
hiṃgvādiprativāpametaduditaṃ
gulmodarāṣṭhīliṣu || 47
hiṅgūgragandhābiḍaśuṇṭhyajājī-
harītakīpuṣkaramūlakuṣṭham
bhāgottaraṃ cūrṇitametadiṣṭaṃ
gulmodarājīrṇaviṣūcikāsu || 48
8triphalākāñcanakṣīrīsaptalānīlinīvacāḥ
trāyantīhapuṣātiktātrivṛtsaindhavapippalīḥ || 49
pibetsaṃcūrṇya mūtroṣṇavārimāṃsarasādibhiḥ
sarvagulmodaraplīhakuṣṭhārśaḥśothakheditaḥ || 50
śaṭhī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca dantīṃ citrakamāḍhakīm
śṛṅgaveraṃ vacāṃ caiva palikāni samāharet || 51
trivṛtāyāḥ palaṃ caiva kuryāttrīṇi ca hiṅgunaḥ
yavakṣārapale dve ca dve pale cāmlavetasāt || 52
yamānyajājī maricaṃ dhānyakaṃ ceti kārṣikam
upakuñcayajamodābhyāṃ tathā cāṣṭamikāmapi || 53
mātuluṅgarasenaiva guṭikāḥ kārayedbhiṣak
tāsāmekāṃ pibeddve ca tisro vāpi sukhāmbunā || 54
amlaiśca madyairyūṣaiśca ghṛtena payasāthavā
eṣā kāṅkāyaṇenoktā guḍikā gulmanāśinī || 55
arśohṛdrogaśamanī krimīṇāṃ ca vināśinī
gomūtrayuktā śamayetkaphagulmaṃ cirotthitam || 56
kṣīreṇa pittagulmaṃ ca madyairamlaiśca vātikam
triphalārasamūtraiśca niyacchetsānnipātikam
raktagulme ca nārīṇāmuṣṭrīkṣīreṇa pāyayet || 57
9hapuṣāvyoṣapṛthvīkācavyacitrakasaindhavaiḥ
sājājīpippalīmūladīpyakairvipaceddhṛtam || 58
sakolamūlakarasaṃ sakṣīraṃ dadhi dāḍimam
tatparaṃ vātagulmaghnaṃ śūlānāhavibandhanut || 59
yonyarśograhaṇīdoṣaśvāsakāsā'rucijvaram
pārśvahṛdvastiśūlaṃ ca ghṛtametadvyapohati || 60
10pippalyāḥ picurardhārdho dāḍimāddvipalaṃ palaṃ
dhānyātpañca ghṛtācchuṇṭhyāḥ karṣaḥ kṣīraṃ caturguṇam || 61
siddhametairghṛtaṃ sadyo vātagulmaṃ cikitsati
yoniśūlaṃ śiraḥśūlamarśāṃsi viṣamajvarān || 62
11tryūṣaṇatriphalādhānyaviḍaṅgacavyacitrakaiḥ
kalkīkṛtairghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ sakṣīraṃ vātagulmanut || 63
12jale daśaguṇe sādhyaṃ trāyamāṇācatuḥpalam
pañcabhāgasthitaṃ pūtaṃ kalkaiḥ saṃyojya kārṣikaiḥ || 64
rohiṇīkaṭukāmustatrāyamāṇādurālabhāḥ
kalkaistāmalakīvīrājīvantīcandanotpalaiḥ || 65
rasasyāmalakīnāṃ ca kṣīrasya ca ghṛtasya ca
palāni pṛthagaṣṭāṣṭau dattvā samyagvipācayet || 66
pittagulmaṃ raktagulmaṃ visarpaṃ paittikaṃ jvaram
hṛdrogaṃ kāmalāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ hanyādetaddhṛtottamam || 67
palollekhāgate māne na dvaiguṇyamiheṣyate
catvāriṃśatpalaṃ tena toyaṃ daśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 68
13drākṣāmadhukakharjūraṃ vidārīṃ saśatāvarīm
parūṣakāṇi triphalāṃ sādhayetpalasaṃmitām || 69
jalāḍhake pādaśeṣe rasamāmalakasya ca
ghṛtamikṣurasaṃ kṣīramabhayākalkapādikam || 70
sādhayettu ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ śarkarākṣaudrapādikam
yojayetpittagulmaghnaṃ sarvapittavikāranut || 71
sāhacaryādiha pṛthagghṛtādeḥ kvāthatulyatā || 72
14dhātrīphalānāṃ svarase ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pācayeddhṛtam
śarkarāsaindhavopetaṃ taddhitaṃ sarvagulminām || 73
15ṣaḍbhiḥ palairmagadhajāphalamūlacavya-
viśvauṣadhajvalanayāvajakalkapakvam
prasthaṃ ghṛtasya daśamūlyurubūkabhārgī-
kvāthe'pyatho payasi dadhni ca ṣaṭpalākhyam || 74
gulmodarārucibhagandaravahnisāda-
kāsajvarakṣayaśirograhaṇīvikārān
sadyaḥ śamaṃ nayati ye ca kaphānilotthā
bhārgyākhyaṣaṭpalamidaṃ pravadanti tajjñāḥ || 75
16pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
palikaiḥ sayavakṣāraiḥ sarpiḥprasthaṃ vipācayet || 76
kṣīraprasthena tatsarpirhanti gulmaṃ kaphātmakam
grahaṇīpāṇḍurogaghnaṃ plīhakāsajvarāpaham || 77
17bhallātakānāṃ dvipalaṃ pañcamūlaṃ palonmitam
sādhyaṃ vidārīgandhāḍhyamāpotthasalilāḍhake || 78
pādāvaśeṣe pūte ca pippalīṃ nāgaraṃ vacām
viḍaṅgaṃ saindhavaṃ hiṅgu yāvaśūkaṃ biḍaṃ śaṭhīm || 79
citrakaṃ madhukaṃ rāsnāṃ piṣṭvā karṣasamānbhiṣak
prasthaṃ ca payaso dattvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 80
etadbhallātakaṃ nāma kaphagulmaharaṃ param
plīhapāṇḍvāmayaśvāsagrahaṇīkāsagulmanut || 81
18rasonasvarase sarpiḥ pañcamūlarasānvitam
surāranāladadhyamlamūlakasvarasaiḥ saha || 82
vyoṣadāḍimavṛkṣāmlayamānīcavyasaindhavaiḥ
hiṅgvamlavetasājājīdīpyakaiśca palānvitaiḥ || 83
siddhaṃ gulmagrahaṇyarśaḥśvāsonmādakṣayajvarān
kāsā'pasmāramandāgniplīhaśūlānilāñjayet || 84
19jaladroṇe vipaktavyā viṃśatiḥ pañca cābhayāḥ
dantyāḥ palāni tāvanti citrakasya tathaiva ca || 85
tenāṣṭabhāgaśeṣeṇa paceddantīsamaṃ guḍam
tāścābhayātrivṛccūrṇāttailāccāpi catuḥpalam || 86
palamekaṃ kaṇāśuṇṭhyoḥ siddhe lehe ca śītale
kṣaudraṃ tailasamaṃ dadyāccāturjātapalaṃ tathā || 87
tato lehapalaṃ līḍhvā jagdhvā caikāṃ harītakīm
sukhaṃ viricyate snigdho doṣaprasthamanāmayaḥ || 88
plīhaśvayathugulmārśohṛtpāṇḍugrahaṇīgadāḥ
śāmyantyutkleśaviṣamajvarakuṣṭhānyarocakāḥ || 89
20vṛścīramurubūkaṃ ca varṣāhvaṃ bṛhatīdvayam
citrakaṃ ca jaladroṇe pacetpādāvaśeṣitam || 90
māgadhīcitrakakṣaudraliptakumbhe nidhāpayet
madhunaḥ prasthamāvāpya pathyācūrṇārdhasaṃyutam || 91
vyuṣoṣitaṃ daśāhaṃ ca jīrṇabhaktaḥ pibennaraḥ
ariṣṭo'yaṃ jayedgulmamavipākaṃ sudustaram || 92
21raudhirasya tu gulmasya garbhakālavyatikrame
snigdhasvinnaśarīrāyai dadyātsnigdhaṃ virecanam || 93
śatāhvācirabilvatvagdārubhārgīkaṇodbhavaḥ
kalkaḥ pīto haredgulmaṃ tilakvāthena raktajam || 94
tilakvātho guḍavyoṣahiṅgubhārgīyuto bhavet
pānaṃ raktabhave gulme naṣṭapuṣpe ca yoṣitām || 95
sakṣāraṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ madyaṃ prapibedasragulminī
palāśakṣāratoyena siddhaṃ sarpiḥ pibecca sā || 96
uṣṇairvā bhedayedbhinne vidhirāsṛgdaro hitaḥ
na prabhidyeta yadyevaṃ dadyādyoniviśodhanam || 97
kṣāreṇa yuktaṃ palalaṃ sudhākṣīreṇa vā punaḥ
rudhire'tipravṛtte tu raktapittaharī kriyā || 98
bhallātakātkalkakaṣāyapakvaṃ
sarpiḥ pibeccharkarayā vimiśram
tadraktapittaṃ vinihanti pītaṃ
balāsagulmaṃ madhunā sametam || 99
vallūraṃ mūlakaṃ matsyāñśuṣkaśākāni vedanam
na khādeccālukaṃ gulmī madhurāṇi phalāni ca || 100
Adhikāra 30
1vātopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye vāmayetsnigdhamāturam
dvipañcamūlīkvāthena sasnehalavaṇena ca || 1
pippalyelāvacāhiṅguyavakṣāro'tha saindhavam
sauvarcalamatho śuṇṭhīmajamodāvacūrṇitam || 2
phaladhānyāmlakaulatthadadhimadyāsavādibhiḥ
pāyayecchuddhadehaṃ ca snehenānyatamena vā || 3
jāgaraṃ vā pibeduṣṇaṃ kaṣāyaṃ cāgnivardhanam
kāsaśvāsānilaharaṃ śūlahṛdroganāśanam || 4
śrīparṇīmadhukakṣaudrasitāguḍajalairvamet
pittopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye seveta madhuraiḥ śṛtam || 5
ghṛtaṃ kaṣāyāṃścoddiṣṭānpittajvaravināśanān || 6
2śītāḥ pradehāḥ pariṣecanāni
tathā vireko hṛdi pittaduṣṭe
drākṣāsitākṣaudraparūṣakaiḥ syā-
cchuddhe ca pittāpahamannapānam || 7
piṣṭvā pibedvāpi sitājalena
yaṣṭyāhvayaṃ tiktakarohiṇīṃ ca || 8
arjunasya tvacā siddhaṃ kṣīraṃ yojyaṃ hṛdāmaye
sitayā pañcamūlyā vā balayā madhukena vā || 9
ghṛtena dugdhena guḍāmbhasā vā
pibanti cūrṇaṃ kakubhatvaco ye
hṛdrogajīrṇajvararaktapittaṃ
hatvā bhaveyuścirajīvinaste || 10
3vacānimbakaṣāyābhyāṃ vāntaṃ hṛdi kaphotthite
vātahṛdrogahṛccūrṇaṃ pippalyādi ca yojayet || 11
tridoṣaje laṅghanamāditaḥ syā-
dannaṃ ca sarveṣu hitaṃ vidheyam
hīnādhimadhyatvamavekṣya caiva
kāryaṃ trayāṇāmapi karma śastam || 12
cūrṇaṃ puṣkarajaṃ lihyānmākṣikeṇa samāyutam
hṛcchūlakāsaśvāsaghnaṃ kṣayahikkānivāraṇam || 13
tailājyaguḍavipakvaṃ
gaudhūmaṃ vāpi pārthajaṃ cūrṇaṃ
pibati pabo'nucayaṃ sa
bhavati jitasakalahṛdrujaḥ puruṣaḥ || 14
godhūmakakubhacūrṇaṃ
chāgapayogavyasarpiṣi vipakvam
madhuśarkarāsametaṃ
śamayati hṛdrogamuddhataṃ puṃsām || 15
4mūlaṃ nāgavalāyāstu cūrṇaṃ dugdhena pāyayet
hṛdrogaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ kakubhasya ca valkalam || 16
rasāyanaṃ paraṃ balyaṃ vātajinmāṃsayojitam
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam || 17
hiṅgūgragandhābiḍaviśvakṛṣṇā-
kuṣṭhābhayācitrakayāvaśūkam
pibecca sauvarcalapuṣkarāḍhyaṃ
yavāmbhasā śūlahṛdāmayeṣu || 18
daśamūlīkaṣāyaṃ tu lavaṇakṣārayojitam
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ ca hṛdrogaṃ gulmaṃ śūlaṃ ca nāśayet || 19
pāṭhāṃ vacāṃ yavakṣāramabhayāmamlavetasam
durālabhāṃ citrakaṃ ca tryūṣaṇaṃ ca palatrikam || 20
śaṭhīṃ puṣkaramūlaṃ ca tintiḍīkaṃ sadāḍimam
mātuluṅgasya mūlāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 21
sukhodakena madyairvā cūrṇānyetāni pāyayet
arśaḥśūlāni hṛdrogaṃ gulmaṃ cāśu vyaṣohati || 22
puṭadagdhamaśmapiṣṭaṃ
hariṇaviṣāṇaṃ tu sarpiṣā pibataḥ
hṛtpṛṣṭhaśūlamupaśama-
mupayātyacireṇa kaṣṭamapi || 23
5krimihṛdrogiṇaṃ snigdhaṃ bhojayetpiśitaudanam
dadhnā ca palalopetaṃ tryahaṃ paścādvirecayet || 24
sugandhibhiḥ salavaṇairyogaiḥ sājājiśarkaraiḥ
viḍaṅgagāḍhaṃ dhānyāmlaṃ pāyayeddhitamuttamam || 25
krimije ca pibenmūtraṃ viḍaṅgāmayasaṃyutam
hṛdi sthitā patantyevamadhastātkrimayo nṛṇām
yavānnaṃ vitareccāsmai saviḍaṅgamataḥ param || 26
mukhyaṃ śatārdhaṃ ca harītakīnāṃ
saubarcalasyāpi paladvayaṃ ca
pakvaṃ ghṛtaṃ vallabhaketi nāmnā
hṛcchvāsaśūlodaramārutaghnam || 27
6śvadaṃṣṭrośīramañjiṣṭhābalākāśmaryakattṛṇam
darbhamūlaṃ pṛthakparṇī palāśarṣabhakau sthirā || 28
palikānsādhayetteṣāṃ rase kṣīre caturguṇe
kalkaiḥ svaguptarṣabhakamedājīvantijīrakaiḥ || 29
śatāvaryṛddhimṛdvīkāśarkarāśrāvaṇīviṣaiḥ
prasthaḥ siddho ghṛtādvātapittahṛdrogaśūlanut || 30
mūtrakṛcchrapramehārśaḥśvāsakāsakṣayāpahaḥ
dhanuḥstrīmadyabhārādhvakṣīṇānāṃ balamāṃsadaḥ || 31
7ghṛtaṃ balānāgabalārjunāmbu
siddhaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhukalkapādam
hṛdogaśūlakṣataraktapitta-
kāsānilāsṛk śamayatyudīrṇam || 32
pārthasya kalkasvarasena siddhaṃ
śastaṃ ghṛtaṃ sarvahṛdāmayeṣu || 33
Adhikāra 31
1abhyañjanasnehanirūhabasti-
svedopanāhottarabastisekān
sthirādibhirvātaharaiśca siddhā-
ndadyādrasāṃścānilamūtrakṛcchre || 1
amṛtā nāgaraṃ dhātrīvājigandhātrikaṇṭakān
prapibedvātarogārtaḥ saśūlī mūtrakṛcchravān || 2
sekāvagāhāḥ śiśirāḥ pradehā
graiṣmo vidhirbastipayovikārāḥ
drākṣāvidārīkṣurasairghṛtaiśca
kṛcchreṣu pittaprabhaveṣu kāryāḥ || 3
kuśaḥ kāśaḥ śaro darbha ikṣuśceti tṛṇodbhavam
pittakṛcchraharaṃ pañcamūlaṃ bastiviśodhanam
etatsnigdhaṃ payaḥ pītaṃ meḍhragaṃ hanti śoṇitam || 4
2śatāvarīkāśakuśaśvadaṃṣṭrā
vidāriśālīkṣukaśerukāṇām
kvāthaṃ suśītaṃ madhuśarkarāktaṃ
pibañjayetpaittikamūtrakṛcchram || 5
harītakīgokṣurarājavṛkṣa-
pāṣāṇabhiddhanvayavāsakānām
kvāthaṃ pibenmākṣikasaṃprayuktaṃ
kṛcchre sadāhe saruje vibandhe || 6
guḍenāmalakaṃ vṛṣyaṃ śramaghnaṃ tarpaṇaṃ param
pittāsṛgdāhaśūlaghnaṃ mūtrakṛcchranivāraṇam || 7
ervārubījaṃ madhukaṃ sadārvīṃ
paitte pibettaṇḍuladhāvanena
dārvī tathaivāmalakīrasena
samākṣikāṃ paittikamūtrakṛcchre || 8
kṣāroṣṇatīkṣṇoṣaṇamannapānaṃ
svedo yavānnaṃ vamanaṃ nirūhāḥ
takraṃ satiktauṣadhasiddhatailā-
nyabhyaṅgapānaṃ kaphamūtrakṛcchre || 9
mūtreṇa surayā vāpi kadalīsvarasena vā
kaphakṛcchravināśāya ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā truṭiṃ pibet || 10
takreṇa yuktaṃ śitimārakasya
bījaṃ pibetkṛcchravināśahetoḥ
pibettathā taṇḍuladhāvanena
pravālacūrṇaṃ kaphamūtrakṛcchre || 11
śvadaṃṣṭrā viśvatoyaṃ vā kaphakṛcchravināśanam || 12
3sarvaṃ tridoṣaprabhave tu vāyoḥ
sthānānupūrvyā prasamīkṣya kāryam
tribhyo'dhike prāgvamanaṃ kaphe syāt
pitte virekaḥ pavane tu bastiḥ || 13
bṛhatīdhāvanīpāṭhāyaṣṭīmadhukaliṅgakāḥ
pācanīyo bṛhatyādiḥ kṛcchradoṣatrayāpahaḥ || 14
tathābhighātaje kuryātsadyo braṇacikitsitam
mūtrakṛcchre sadā cāsya kāryā vātaharī kriyā || 15
svedacūrṇakriyābhyaṅgabastayaḥ syuḥ purīṣaje
kvāthaṃ gokṣurabījasya yavakṣārayutaṃ pibet || 16
mūtrakṛcchraṃ śakṛjjaṃ ca pītaṃ śīghraṃ nivārayet || 17
hitā kriyā tvaśmariśarkarāyāṃ
yā mūtrakṛcchre kaphamārutotthe || 18
4lehyaṃ śukravibandhotthe śilājatu samākṣikam
vṛṣyairbṛṃhitadhātośca vidheyāḥ pramadottamāḥ || 19
elāhiṅguyutaṃ kṣīraṃ sarpirmiśraṃ pibennaraḥ
mūtradoṣaviśuddhyarthaṃ śukradoṣaharaṃ ca tat || 20
yanmūtrakṛcchre vihitaṃ tu paitte
tatkārayecchoṇitamūtrakṛcchre || 21
trikaṇṭakāragvadhadarbhakāśa-
durālabhāparvatabhedapathyāḥ
nighnanti pītā madhunāśmarīṃ ca
samprāptamṛtyorapi mūtrakṛcchram || 22
kaṣāyo'tibalāmūlasādhitaḥ sarvakṛcchrajit || 23
elāśmabhedakaśilājatupippalīnāṃ
cūrṇāni taṇḍulajalairlulitāni pītvā
yadvā guḍena sahitānyavalihya tāni
cāsannamṛtyurapi jīvati mūtrakṛcchrī || 24
ayorajaḥ ślakṣṇapiṣṭaṃ madhunā saha yojitam
mūtrakṛcchraṃ nihantyāśu tribhirlehairna saṃśayaḥ || 25
sitātulyo yavakṣāraḥ sarvakṛcchranivāraṇaḥ
nidigdhikāraso vāpi sakṣaudraḥ kṛcchranāśanaḥ || 26
5śatāvarīkāśakuśaśvadaṃṣṭrā-
vidārikekṣvāmalakeṣu siddham
sarpiḥ payo vā sitayā vimiśraṃ
kṛcchreṣu pittaprabhaveṣu yojyam || 27
6trikaṇṭakairaṇḍakuśādyabhīru-
karkārukekṣusvarasena siddham
sarpirguḍārdhāṃśayutaṃ prapeyaṃ
kṛcchrāśmarīmūtravighātahetoḥ || 28
7punarnavāmūlatulā daśamūlaṃ śatāvarī
balā turagagandhā ca tṛṇamūlaṃ trikaṇṭakam || 29
vidārīgandhā nāgāhvaguḍūcyatibalā tathā
pṛthagdaśapalānbhāgāñjaladroṇe vipācayet || 30
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa ghṛtasyārdhāḍhakaṃ pacet
madhukaṃ śṛṅgaveraṃ ca drākṣāsaindhavapippalīḥ || 31
dvipalikāḥ pṛthagdadyādyavānyāḥ kuḍavaṃ tathā
triṃśadguḍapalānyatra tailasyairaṇḍajasya ca || 32
prasthaṃ datvā samāloḍya samyaṅmṛdvagninā pacet
etadīśvaraputrāṇāṃ prāgbhojanamaninditam || 33
rājñāṃ rājasamānāṃ ca bahustrīpatayaśca ye
mūtrakṛcchre kaṭistambhe tathā gāḍhapurīṣiṇām || 34
meḍhravaṅkṣaṇaśūle ca yoniśūle ca śasyate
yathoktānāṃ ca gulmānāṃ vātaśoṇitakāśca ye || 35
balyaṃ rasāyanaṃ śītaṃ sukumārakumārakam
punarnavāśate droṇo deyo'nyeṣu tathāparaḥ || 36
Adhikāra 32
1mūtrāghātānyathādoṣaṃ mūtrakṛcchraharairjayet
bastimuttarabastiṃ ca dadyātsnigdhaṃ virecanam || 1
kalkamervārubījānāmakṣamātraṃ sasaindhavam
dhānyāmlayuktaṃ pītvaiva mūtrāghātādvimucyate || 2
pāṭalyā yāvaśūkācca pāribhadrāttilādapi
kṣārodakena madirāṃ tvageloṣaṇasaṃyutām || 3
pibedguḍopadaṃśānvā lihyādetānpṛthakpṛthak
triphalākalkasaṃyuktaṃ lavaṇaṃ vāpi pāyayet || 4
nidigdhikāyāḥ svarasaṃ pibedvātāntarasrutam
jale kuṅkumakalkaṃ vā sakṣaudramuṣitaṃ niśi || 5
satailaṃ pāṭalābhasma kṣāravadvā parisrutam
surāṃ sauvarcalavatīṃ mūtrāghātī pibennaraḥ || 6
dāḍimāmbuyutaṃ mukhyamelābījaṃ sanāgaram
pītvā surāṃ salavaṇāṃ mūtraghātādvimucyate || 7
pibecchilājatukvāthe gaṇe vīratarādike
rasaṃ durālabhāyā vā kaṣāye vāsakasya vā || 8
2trikaṇṭakairaṇḍaśatāvarībhiḥ
siddhaṃ payo vā tṛṇapañcamūlaiḥ
guḍapragāḍhaṃ saghṛtaṃ payo vā
rogeṣu kṛcchrādiṣu śastametam || 9
nalakuśakāśekṣuśiphāṃ
kvathitāṃ prātaḥ suśītalāṃ śītām
pibataḥ prayāti niyataṃ
mūtragraha ityuvāca kacaḥ || 10
godhāvatyā mūlaṃ
kvathitaṃ ghṛtatailagorasairmiśram
pītaṃ niruddhamacirā-
dbhinatti mūtrasya saṃghātam || 11
jalena khadirībījaṃ mūtrāghātāśmarīharam
mūlaṃ tu trijaṭāyāśca takrapītaṃ tadarthakṛt || 12
mūtre vibaddhe karpūracūrṇaṃ liṅge praveśayet
śṛtaśītapayonnāśī candanaṃ taṇḍulāmbunā || 13
pibetsaśarkaraṃ śreṣṭhamuṣṇavāte saśoṇite
śīto'vagāha ābastimuṣṇavātanivāraṇaḥ || 14
kūṣmāṇḍakarasaścāpi pītaḥ sakṣāraśarkaraḥ
strīṇāmatiprasaṅgena śoṇitaṃ yasya sicyate || 15
maithunoparamaścāsya bṛṃhaṇīyo hito vidhiḥ
svaguptāphalamṛdvīkākṛṣṇekṣurasitārajaḥ || 16
samāṃśamardhabhāgāni kṣīrakṣaudraghṛtāni ca
sarvaṃ samyagvimathyākṣamānaṃ līḍhvā payaḥ pibet || 17
hanti śukrāśayotthāṃśca doṣānvandhyāsutapradam
3citrakaṃ śārivā caiva balākālānuśārivā || 18
drākṣā viśālā pippalyastathā citraphalā bhavet
tathaiva madhukaṃ dadyāddadyādāmalakāni ca || 19
ghṛtāḍhakaṃ pacedebhiḥ kalkairakṣasamanvitaiḥ
kṣīradroṇe jaladroṇe tatsiddhamavatārayet || 20
śītaṃ parisrutaṃ caiva śarkarāprasthasaṃyutam
tugākṣīryāśca tatsarvaṃ matimānpratimiśrayet || 21
tato mitaṃ pibetkāle yathādoṣaṃ yathāvalam
vātaretāḥ pittaretāḥ śleṣmaretāśca yo bhavet || 22
raktaretā granthiretāḥ pibedicchannarogatām
jīvanīyaṃ ca vṛṣyaṃ ca sarpiretanmahāguṇam || 23
prajāhitaṃ ca dhanyaṃ ca sarvarogāpahaṃ śivam
sarpiretatprayuñjānā strī garbhaṃ labhate'cirāt || 24
asṛgdoṣāñjayeccaiva yonidoṣāṃśca saṃhatān
mūtradoṣeṣu sarveṣu kuryādetaccikitsitam || 25
Adhikāra 33
1varuṇasya tvacaṃ śreṣṭhāṃ śuṇṭhīgokṣurasaṃyutām
yavakṣāraguḍaṃ datvā kvāthayitvā pibeddhitām || 1
aśmarīṃ vātajāṃ hanti cirakālānubandhinīm
vīrataraḥ sahacarī darbho vṛkṣādanī nalaḥ || 2
gundrākāśakuśāvaśmabhedamoraṭaṭuṇṭukāḥ
kuruṇṭikā ca vaśiro vasukaḥ sāgnimanthakaḥ || 3
indīvarī śvadaṃṣṭrā ca tathā kāpotavaktrakaḥ
vīratarādirityeṣa gaṇo vātavikāranut || 4
aśmarīśarkarāmūtrakṛcchrāghātarujāpahaḥ
śuṇṭhyagnimanthapāṣāṇaśigruvaruṇagokṣuraiḥ || 5
abhayāragvadhaphalaiḥ kvāthaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ
rāmaṭhakṣāralavaṇacūrṇaṃ datvā pibennaraḥ || 6
aśmarīmūtrakṛcchraghnaṃ pācanaṃ dīpanaṃ param
hanyātkoṣṭhāśritaṃ vātaṃ kaṭyūrugudameḍhragam || 7
2pāṣāṇabhedo vasuko vaśiro'śmantakaṃ tathā
śatāvarī śvadaṃṣṭrā ca bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā || 8
kapotavaktrārtagalakāñcanośīragulmakāḥ
vṛkṣādanībhallukaśca varuṇaḥ śākajaṃ phalam || 9
yavāḥ kulatthāḥ kolāni katakasya phalāni ca
uṣakādipratīvāpameṣāṃ kvāthe śṛtaṃ ghṛtam || 10
bhinatti vātasambhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva tu
kṣārānyavāgūḥ peyāśca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni ca kurvīta varge'sminvātanāśane || 11
3uṣakaṃ saindhavaṃ hiṅgukāśīsadvayagugguluḥ
śilājatu tutthakaṃ ca uṣakādirudāhṛtaḥ || 12
uṣakādiḥ kaphaṃ hanti gaṇo medoviśodhanaḥ
aśmarīśarkarāmūtraśūlaghnaḥ kaphagulmanut || 13
4kuśaḥ kāśaḥ śaro gulma itkaro moraṭo'śmabhit
darbho vidārī vārāhī śālimūlaṃ trikaṇṭakaḥ || 14
bhallūkaḥ pāṭalī pāṭhā pattūro'tha kuruṇṭikāḥ
punarnave śirīṣaśca kvathitāsteṣu sādhitam || 15
ghṛtaṃ śilāhvamadhukaṃ bījairindīvarasya ca
trapuṣairvārukāṇāṃ vā bījaiścāvāpitaṃ śṛtam || 16
bhinatti pittasambhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva tu
kṣārānyavāgūḥ peyāśca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni ca kurvīta varge'sminpittanāśane || 17
5gaṇe varuṇakādau ca guggulvelāhareṇubhiḥ
kuṣṭhamustāhvamaricacitrakaiḥ sasurāhvayaiḥ || 18
etaiḥ siddhamajāsarpiruṣakādigaṇena ca
bhinatti kaphasambhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva tu || 19
kṣārānyavāgūḥ peyāśca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni prakurvīta varge'sminkaphanāśane || 20
taruṇārtagalaḥ śigrutarkārīmadhuśigrukāḥ
meṣaśṛṅgīkarañjau ca bimbyagnimanthamoraṭāḥ || 21
śairiyau śirīṣo darbho varī vasukacitrakau
bilvaṃ caivājaśṛṅgī ca bṛhatīdvayameva ca || 22
varuṇādigaṇo hyeṣa kaphamedonivāraṇaḥ
vinihanti śiraḥśūlaṃ gulmādyantaravidradhīn || 23
6varuṇatvakkaṣāyaṃ tu pītaṃ ca guḍasaṃyutam
aśmarīṃ pātayatyāśu bastiśūlanivāraṇam || 24
yavakṣāraṃ guḍonmiśraṃ pibetpuṣpaphalodbhavam
rasaṃ mūtravibandhaghnaṃ śarkāśmarivināśanam || 25
pibedvaruṇamūlatvakkvāthaṃ tatkalkasaṃyutam
kvāthaśca śigrumūlotthaḥ kaduṣṇo'śmarighātanaḥ || 26
nāgaravāruṇagokṣura-
pāṣāṇabhedakapotavalkajaḥ kvāthaḥ
guḍayāvaśūkamiśraḥ
pīto hantyaśmarīmugrām || 27
varuṇatvakśilābhedaśuṇṭhīgokṣurakaiḥ kṛtaḥ
kaṣāyaḥ kṣārasaṃyuktaḥ śarkarāṃ ca bhinattyapi || 28
śvadaṃṣṭrairaṇḍapatrāṇi nāgaraṃ varuṇatvacam
etatkvāthavaraṃ prātaḥ pibedaśmaribhedanam || 29
mūlaṃ śvadaṃṣṭrākṣurakorubūkāt
kṣīreṇa piṣṭaṃ bṛhatīdvayācca
āloḍya daghnā madhureṇa peyaṃ
dināni saptāśmaribhedanārtham || 30
pakvekṣvākurasaḥ kṣāraḥ sitāyukto'śmarīharaḥ || 31
pāṣāṇarogapīḍāṃ sauvarcalayuktā surā jayati
tadvanmadhudugdhayuktā trirātraṃ tilanālabhūtiśca || 32
7elopakulyāmadhukāśmabheda-
kauntīśvadaṃṣṭrāvṛṣakorubūkaiḥ
kvāthaṃ pibedaśmajatupragāḍhaṃ
saśarkare sāśmarimūtrakṛcchre || 33
8trikaṇṭakasya bījānāṃ cūrṇaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam
avikṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ pibedaśmarināśanam
śukrāśmaryāṃ tu sāmānyo vidhiraśmarināśanaḥ || 34
9pāṣāṇabhedaṃ vṛṣakaṃ śvadaṃṣṭrā-
pāṭhābhayāvyoṣaśaṭhīnikumbhāḥ
hiṃsrākharāśvāsitimārakāṇā-
mervārukācca trapuṣācca bījam || 35
upakuñcikāhiṅgusavetasāmlaṃ
syāddve bṛhatyau hapuṣā vacā ca
cūrṇaṃ pibedaśmaribhedi pakvaṃ
sarpiśca gomūtracaturguṇaṃ taiḥ || 36
10kulatthasindhūtthaviḍaṅgasāraṃ
saśarkaraṃ śītaliyāvaśūkam
bījāni kūṣmāṇḍakagokṣurābhyāṃ
ghṛtaṃ pacennā varuṇasya toye || 37
duḥsādhyasarvāśmarimūtrakṛcchraṃ
mūtrābhighātaṃ ca samūtrabandham
etāni sarvāṇi nihanti śīghraṃ
prarūḍhavṛkṣāniva vajrapātaḥ || 38
11śarādipañcamūlyā vā kaṣāyeṇa paceddhṛtam
prasthaṃ gokṣurakalkena siddhamadyātsaśarkaram
aśmarīmūtrakṛcchraghnaṃ retomārgarujāpaham || 39
12varuṇasya tulāṃ kṣuṇṇāṃ jaladroṇe vipācayet
pādaśeṣaṃ parisrāvya ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 40
varuṇaṃ kadalī bilvaṃ tṛṇajaṃ pañcamūlakam
amṛtāṃ cāśmajaṃ deyaṃ bījaṃ ca trapuṣodbhavam || 41
śataparvatilakṣāraṃ palāśakṣārameva ca
yūthikāyāśca mūlāni kārṣikāṇi samāvapet || 42
asya mātrāṃ pibejjanturdeśakālādyapekṣayā
jīrṇe tasminpibetpūrvaṃ guḍaṃ jīrṇaṃ tu mastunā
aśmarīṃ śarkarāṃ caiva mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca nāśayet || 43
13bradhnādhikāre yattailaṃ saindhavādyaṃ prakīrtitam
tattailaṃ dviguṇakṣīraṃ pacedvīratarādinā || 44
kvāthena pūrvakalkena sādhitaṃ tu bhiṣagvaraiḥ
etattailavaraṃ śreṣṭhamaśmarīṇāṃ vināśanam || 45
mūtrāghāte mūtrakṛcchre picchite mathite tathā
bhagne śramābhipanne ca sarvathaiva praśasyate || 46
14tvakpatramūlapuṣpasya varuṇātsatrikaṇṭakāt
kaṣāyeṇa pacettailaṃ bastinā sthāpanena ca
śarkarāśmariśūlaghnaṃ mūtrakṛcchranivāraṇam || 47
15śalyavittāmaśāmyantīṃ pratyākhyāya samuddharet
pāyukṣiptāṅgulībhyāṃ tu gudameḍhrāntare gatām || 48
sevanyāḥ savyapārśve ca yavamātraṃ vimucya tu
vraṇaṃ kṛtvāśmarīmātraṃ karṣettāṃ śastrakarmavit || 49
bhinne tu bastau durjñānānmṛtyuḥ syādaśmarīṃ vinā
niḥśeṣāmaśmarīṃ kuryādbastau raktaṃ ca nirharet || 50
hṛtāśmarīkaduṣṇāmbho gāhayedbhojayecca tam
guḍaṃ mūtraviśuddhyarthaṃ madhvājyāktavraṇaṃ tataḥ || 51
dadyātsājyāṃ tryahaṃ peyāṃ sādhitāṃ mūtraśodhibhiḥ
ādaśāhaṃ tato dadyātpayasā mṛdunodanam || 52
svedayedyavamadhvāḍhyaṃ kaṣāyaiḥ kṣālayedvraṇam
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāyaṣṭilodhraiśca lepayet || 53
etaiśca salilaiḥ siddhaṃ ghṛtamabhyañjane hitam
apraśānte tu saptāhādvraṇe dāho'pi ceṣyate
daivānnābhyāṃ tu yā lagnā tāṃ vipāṭyāpakarṣayet || 54
Adhikāra 34
1śyāmākakodravoddālagodhūmacaṇakāḍhakī
kulatthāśca hitā bhojye purāṇamehināṃ sadā || 1
jāṅgalaṃ tiktaśākāni yavānnaṃ ca tathā madhu
pārijātajayānimbavahnigāyatriṇāṃ pṛthak || 2
pāṭhāyāḥ sāguroḥ pītā dvayasya śāradasya ca
jalekṣumadyasikatāṃ śanairlavaṇapiṣṭakān
sāndramehānkramādghnanti aṣṭau kvāthāḥ samākṣikāḥ || 3
dūrvākaśerupūtīkapuṃbhīkalvava?śaivalam
jalena kvathitaṃ pītaṃ śukramehaharaṃ param || 4
triphalāragvadhadrākṣākaṣāyo madhusaṃyutaḥ
pīto nihanti phenākhyaṃ pramehaṃ niyataṃ nṛṇām || 5
2lodhrābhayākaṭphalamustakānāṃ
viḍaṅgapāṭhārjunadhanvanānām
kadambaśālārjunadīpyakānāṃ
biḍaṅgadārvīdhavaśallakīnām || 6
catvāra ete madhunā kaṣāyāḥ
kaphaprameheṣu niṣevaṇīyāḥ || 7
aśvatthāccaturaṅgulyā nyagrodhādeḥ phalatrikāt
sajiṅgiraktasārācca kvāthāḥ pañca samākṣikāḥ || 8
nīladaridraphenākhyakṣāramañjiṣṭhakāhvayān
mehānhanyuḥ kramādete sakṣaudro raktamehanut
kvāthaḥ kharjūrakāśmaryatindukāsthyamṛtākṛtaḥ || 9
lodhrārjunośīrakucandanānā-
mariṣṭasevyāmalakābhayānām
dhātryarjunāriṣṭakavatsakānāṃ
nīlotpalailātiniśārjunānām || 10
catvāra ete vihitāḥ kaṣāyāḥ
pitte pramehe madhusaṃprayuktāḥ || 11
3chinnāvahnikaṣāyeṇa pāṭhākuṭajarāmaṭham
tiktāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca saṃcūrṇya sarpirmehe pibennaraḥ || 12
kadarakhadirapūgakvāthaṃ kṣaudrāhvaye pibet
agnimanthakaṣāyaṃ tu vasāmehe prayojayet || 13
pāṭhāśirīṣadusparśamūrvākiṃśukatindukam
kapitthānāṃ bhiṣakkvāthaṃ hastimehe prayojayet || 14
kampillasaptacchadaśālajāni
vibhītarauhītakakauṭajāni
kapitthapuṣpāṇi ca cūrṇitāni
kṣaudreṇa lihyātkaphapittamehī || 15
sarvamehaharā dhātryā rasaḥ kṣaudraniśāyutaḥ
kapāyastriphalādārumustakairathavā kṛtaḥ || 16
phalatrikaṃ dāruniśāṃ viśālāṃ
mustaṃ ca niḥkvāthya niśāṃśakalkam
pibetkaṣāyaṃ madhusaṃprayuktaṃ
sarveṣu meheṣu samutthiteṣu || 17
4kaṭaṃkaṭerīmadhukatriphalācitrakaiḥ samaiḥ
siddhaḥ kaṣāyaḥ pātavyaḥ pramehāṇāṃ vināśanaḥ || 18
triphalādārudārvyabdakvāthaḥ kṣaudreṇa mehahā
kuṭajāśanadārvyabdaphalatrayabhavo'thavā || 19
triphalālohaśilājatupathyācūrṇaṃ ca līḍhamekaikam
madhunāmarāsvarasa iva sarvānmehānnirasyati || 20
śālamuṣkakakampillakalkamakṣasamaṃ pibet
dhātrīrasena sakṣaudraṃ sarvamehaharaṃ param || 21
5nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthasyonākāragvadhāśanam
āmrajambūkapitthaṃ ca priyālaṃ kakubhaṃ dhavam || 22
madhuko madhukaṃ lodhraṃ varuṇaṃ pāribhadrakam
paṭolaṃ meṣaśṛṅgī ca dantī citrakamāḍhakī || 23
karañjatriphalāśakrabhallātakaphalāni ca
etāni samabhāgāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 24
nyagrodhādyamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ madhunā saha lehayet
phalatrayarasaṃ cānu pibenmūtraṃ viśudhyati || 25
etena viṃśatirmehā mūtrakṛcchrāṇi yāni ca
praśamaṃ yānti yogena pīḍakā na ca jāyate
nyagrodhādyamidaṃ tvatra cāmrajambvasthi gṛhyate || 26
6trikaṇṭakāśmantakasomavalkai-
rbhallātakaiḥ sātiviṣaiḥ salodhraiḥ
vacāpaṭolārjunanimbamustai-
rharidrayā dīpyakapadmakaiśca || 27
mañjiṣṭhapāṭhāgurucandanaiśca
sarvaiḥ samastaiḥ kaphavātajeṣu
meheṣu tailaṃ vipaceddhṛtaṃ tu
pitteṣu miśraṃ triṣu lakṣaṇeṣu || 28
7kaphamehaharakvāthasiddhaṃ sarpiḥ kaphe hitam
pittamehaghnaniryūhasiddhaṃ pitte hitaṃ ghṛtam || 29
daśamūlaṃ karañjau dvau devadāru harītakī
varṣābhūrvaruṇo dantī citrakaṃ sapunarnavam || 30
sudhānīpakadambāśca bilvabhallātakāni ca
śaṭhī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca pippalīmūlameva ca || 31
pṛthagdaśapalānbhāgāṃstatastoyārmaṇe pacet
yavakolakulatthānāṃ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ ca dāpayet
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 32
niculaṃ triphalā bhārgī rohiṣaṃ gajapippalī
śṛṅgaveraṃ viḍaṅgāni vacā kampillakaṃ tathā || 33
garbheṇānena tatsiddhaṃ pāyayettu yathābalam
etaddhānvantaraṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ sarpiruttamam || 34
kuṣṭhaṃ gulmaṃ pramehāṃśca śvayathuṃ vātaśoṇitam
plīhodaraṃ tathārśāṃsi vidradhiṃ piḍakāśca yāḥ
apasmāraṃ tathonmādaṃ sarpiretanniyacchati || 35
pṛthaktoyārmaṇe tatra paceddravyācchataṃ śatam
śatatrayādhike toyamutsargakramato bhavet || 36
8trikaṭu triphalācūrṇatulyayuktaṃ tu guggulum
gokṣurakvāthasaṃyuktaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayedbhipak || 37
doṣakālabalāpekṣī bhakṣayeccānulomikīm
na cātra parihāro'sti karma kuryādyathepsitam
pramehānmūtraghātāṃśca bālarogodaraṃ jayet || 38
9śālasārāditoyena bhāvitaṃ yacchilājatu
pibettenaiva saṃśuddhadehaḥ piṣṭaṃ yathābalam || 39
jāṅgalānāṃ rasaiḥ sārdhaṃ tasmiñjīrṇe ca bhojanam
kuryādevaṃ tulāṃ yāvadupayuñjīta mānavaḥ || 40
madhumehaṃ vihāyāsau śarkarāmaśmarīṃ tathā
vapurvarṇabalopetaḥ śataṃ jīvatyanāmayaḥ || 41
10viḍaṅgatriphalāmustaiḥ kaṇayā nāgareṇa ca
jīrakābhyāṃ yuto hanti pramehānatidustarān
lauho mūtravikārāṃśca sarvameva na saṃśayaḥ || 42
11mākṣikaṃ dhātumapyevaṃ yuñjyāttasyāpyayaṃ guṇaḥ
śālasārādivargasya kvāthe tu ghanatāṃ gate || 43
dantīlodhraśivākāntalauhatāmrarajaḥ kṣipet
ghanībhūtamadagdhaṃ ca prāśya mehānvyapohati || 44
vyāyāmajātamakhilaṃ bhajanmehānvyapohati
pādatracchatrarahito bhaikṣāśī munivadyataḥ || 45
yojanānāṃ śataṃ gacchedadhikaṃ vā nirantaram
mehāñjetuṃ balenāpi nīvārāmalakāśanaḥ || 46
śarāvikādyāḥ pīḍakāḥ sādhayecchothavadbhiṣak
pakvāścikitsedvraṇavattāsāṃ pāne praśasyate || 47
kvāthaṃ vanaspaterbāstaṃ mūtraṃ ca vraṇaśodhanam
elādikena kurvīta tailaṃ ca vraṇaropaṇam || 48
āragvadhādinā kuryātkvāthamudvartanāni ca
śālasārādisekaṃ ca bhojyādiṃ ca kaṇādinā || 49
sauvīrakaṃ surāṃ śuktaṃ tailaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ guḍam
amlekṣurasapiṣṭānnānūpamāṃsāni varjayet || 50
Adhikāra 35
1śramacintāvyavāyādhvakṣaudrajāgaraṇapriyaḥ
hantyavaśyamatisthaulyaṃ yavaśyāmākabhojanaḥ || 1
asvāpaṃ ca vyavāyaṃ ca vyāyāmaṃ cintanāni ca
sthaulyamicchanparityaktuṃ krameṇātipravardhayet || 2
prātarmadhuyutaṃ vāri sevitaṃ sthaulyanāśanam
uṣṇamannasya maṇḍaṃ vā pibankṛśatanurbhavet || 3
sacavyajīrakavyoṣahiṅgusauvarcalānalāḥ
mastunā śaktavaḥ pītā medoghnā vahnidīpanāḥ || 4
viḍaṅganāgarakṣārakālaloharajo madhu
yavāmalakacūrṇaṃ tu prayogaḥ sthaulyanāśanaḥ || 5
2vyoṣaṃ viḍaṅgaśigrūṇi triphalāṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
bṛhatyau dve haridre dve pāṭhāmativiṣāṃ sthirām || 6
hiṅgukebūkamūlāni yamānīdhānyacitrakam
sauvarcalamajājīṃ ca hapuṣāṃ ceti cūrṇayet || 7
cūrṇatailaghṛtakṣaudrabhāgāḥ syurmānataḥ samāḥ
śaktūnāṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇo bhāgaḥ saṃtarpaṇaṃ pibet || 8
prayogāttasya śāmyanti rogāḥ santarpaṇotthitāḥ
pramehā mūḍhavātāśca kuṣṭhānyarśāṃsi kāmalāḥ || 9
plīhapāṇḍvāmayaḥ śotho mūtrakṛcchramarocakaḥ
hṛdrogo rājayakṣmā ca kāsaśvāsau galagrahāḥ || 10
krimayo grahaṇīdoṣāḥ śvaitryaṃ sthaulyamatīva ca
narāṇāṃ dīpyate cāgniḥ smṛtirbuddhiśca jāyate || 11
3vadarīpatrakalkena peyā kāñjikasādhitā
sthaulyanutsyātsāgnimantharasaṃ vāpi śilājatu || 12
amṛtātruṭivellavatsakaṃ
kaliṅgapathyāmalakāni gugguluḥ
kramavṛddhamidaṃ madhuplutaṃ
piḍakāsthaulyabhagandaraṃ jayet || 13
4vyoṣāgnitriphalāmustaviḍaṅgairgugguluṃ samam
khādansarvāñjayedvyādhīnmedaḥśleṣmāmavātajān || 14
5guggulustālamūlī catriphalā khadiraṃ vṛṣam
trivṛtālambuṣā snukca nirguṇḍī citrakaṃ tathā || 15
eṣāṃ daśapalānbhāgāṃstoye pañcāḍhake pacet
pādaśeṣaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kaṣāyamavatārayet || 16
paladvādaśakaṃ deyaṃ tīkṣṇalauhaṃ sucūrṇitam
purāṇasarpiṣaḥ prasthaṃ śarkarāṣṭapalonmitam || 17
pacettāmramaye pātre suśīte cāvatārite
prasthārdhaṃ mākṣikaṃ deyaṃ śilājatu paladvayam || 18
elā tvakca palārdhaṃ ca viḍaṅgāni paladvayam
maricaṃ cāñjanaṃ kṛṣṇādvipalaṃ triphalānvitam || 19
paladvayaṃ tu kāśīsaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇīkṛtaṃ budhaiḥ
cūrṇaṃ dattvā sumathitaṃ snigdhe bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet || 20
tataḥ saṃśuddhadehastu bhakṣayedakṣamātrakam
anupānaṃ pibetkṣīraṃ jāṅgalānāṃ rasaṃ tathā || 21
vātaśleṣmaharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kuṣṭhamehodarāpaham
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca śvayathuṃ sabhagandaram || 22
mūrcchāmohaviṣonmādagarāṇi vividhāni ca
sthūlānāṃ karṣaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ medure paramauṣadham || 23
karṣayeccātimātreṇa kukṣiṃ pātālasannibham
balyaṃ rasāyanaṃ medhyaṃ vājīkaraṇamuttamam || 24
śrīkaraṃ putrajananaṃ valīpalitanāśanam
nāśnīyātkadalīkandaṃ kāñjikaṃ karamardakam
karīraṃ kāravellaṃ ca ṣaṭ kakārāṇi varjayet || 25
6triphalātiviṣāmūrvātrivṛccitrakavāsakaiḥ
nimbāragvadhaṣaṭgranthāsaptaparṇaniśādvayaiḥ || 26
guḍūcīndrasurākṛṣṇākuṣṭasarṣapanāgaraiḥ
tailamebhiḥ samaṃ pakvaṃ surasādirasāplutam || 27
pānābhyañjanagaṇḍūṣanasyabastiṣu yojitam
sthūlatālasya kaṇḍvādīñjayetkaphakṛtāngadān || 28
7śirīṣalāmajjakahemalodhrai-
stvagdoṣasaṃsvedaharaḥ praharṣaḥ
patrāmbulauhābhayacandanāni
śarīradaurgandhyaharaḥ pradehaḥ || 29
vāsādalaraso lepācchaṅkhacūrṇena saṃyutaḥ
bilvapatrarasairvāpi gātradaurgandhyanāśanaḥ || 30
harītakīlodhramariṣṭapatraṃ
cūtatvaco dāḍimavalkalaṃ ca
eṣo'ṅgarāgaḥ kathito'ṅganānāṃ
jaṅghākaṣāyaśca narādhipānām || 31
dalajalalaghumalayabhava-
vilepanaṃ harati dehadaurgandhyam
vimalāraṇālasahitaṃ
pītamivālambuṣācūrṇam || 32
gomūtrapiṣṭaṃ vinihanti kuṣṭhaṃ
varṇojjvalaṃ gopayasā ca yuktam
kakṣādidaurgandhyaharaṃ payobhiḥ
śastaṃ vaśīkṛdrajanīdvayena || 33
ciñcāpatrasvarasamraditaṃ kakṣādiyojitaṃ jayati
dagdhaharidrodvartanamacirāddehasya daurgandhyam || 34
hastapādaśrutau yojyaṃ gugguluṃ pañcatiktakam
athavā pañcatiktākhyaṃ ghṛtaṃ khādedatandritaḥ || 35
Adhikāra 36
1udare doṣasampūrṇe kukṣau mando yato'nalaḥ
tasmādbhojyāni yojyāni dīpanāni laghūni ca || 1
raktaśālīnyavānmudgāñjāṅgalāṃśca mṛgadvijān
payomūtrāsavāriṣṭamadhuśīdhu tathā pibet || 2
vātodaraṃ balavataḥ pūrvaṃ snehairupācaret
snigdhāya sveditāṅgāya dadyātsnehavirecanam || 3
hṛte doṣe parimlānaṃ veṣṭayedvāsasodaram
tathāsyānavakāśatvādvāyunā dhmāpayetpunaḥ || 4
doṣātimātropacārātsrotomārganirodhanāt
sambhavatyudaraṃ tasmānnityamenaṃ virecayet || 5
virikte ca yathādoṣaharaiḥ peyā śṛtā hitā
vātodarī pibettakraṃ pippalīlavaṇānvitam || 6
śarkarāmaricopetaṃ svādu pittodarī pibet
yamānīsaindhavājājīvyopayuktaṃ kaphodarī || 7
2pibenmadhuyutaṃ takraṃ vyaktāmlaṃ nātipelavam
madhutailavacāśuṇṭhīśatāhvākuṣṭhasaindhavaiḥ || 8
yuktaṃ plīhodarī jātaṃ savyoṣaṃ tu dakodarī
baddhodarī tu hapuṣādīpyakājājisaindhavaiḥ || 9
pibecchidrodarī takraṃ pippalīkṣaudrasaṃyutam
tryūṣaṇakṣāralavaṇairyuktaṃ tu nicayodarī || 10
gauravārocakārtānāṃ samandāgnyatisāriṇām
takraṃ vātakaphārtānāmamṛtatvāya kalpyate || 11
vātodare payo'bhyāso nirūho daśamūlakaḥ
sodāvarte vātaghnāmlaśṛtairaṇḍānuvāsanaḥ || 12
3sāmudrasauvarcalasaindhavāni
kṣāraṃ yavānāmajamodakaṃ ca
sapippalīcitrakaśṛṅgaveraṃ
hiṅgu biḍaṃ ceti samāni kuryāt || 13
etāni cūrṇāni ghṛtaplutāni
bhuñjīta pūrvaṃ kavalaṃ praśastam
vātodaraṃ gulmamajīrṇabhuktaṃ
vāyuprakopaṃ grahaṇīṃ ca duṣṭām || 14
arśāṃsi duṣṭāni ca pāṇḍurogaṃ
bhagandaraṃ ceti nihanti sadyaḥ || 15
4pittodare tu balinaṃ pūrvameva virecayet
anuvāsyābalaṃ kṣīrabastiśuddhaṃ virecayet || 16
payasā satrivṛtkalkenorubūkaśṛtena vā
śātalātrāyamāṇābhyāṃ śṛtenāragvadhena vā || 17
kaphādudariṇaṃ śuddhaṃ kaṭukṣārānnabhojitam
mūtrāriṣṭāyaskṛtibhiryojayecca kaphāpahaiḥ || 18
sannipātodare sarvāṃ yathoktāṃ kārayetkriyām
plīhodare plīhaharaṃ karmodaraharaṃ tathā || 19
svinnāya baddhodariṇe mūtraṃ tīkṣṇauṣadhānvitam
satailaṃ lavaṇaṃ dadyānnirūhaṃ sānuvāsanam || 20
parisraṃsīni cānnāni tīkṣṇaṃ caiva virecanam
chidrodaramṛte svedācchleṣmodaravadācaret || 21
jātaṃ jātaṃ jalaṃ srāvyaṃ śāstroktaṃ śastrakarma ca
jalodare viśeṣeṇa dravasevāṃ vivarjayet || 22
devadārupalāśārkahastipippaliśigrukaiḥ
sāśvagandhaiḥ sagomūtraiḥ pradihyādudaraṃ śanaiḥ || 23
mūtrāṇyaṣṭāvudariṇāṃ seke pāne ca yojayet
snuhīpayobhāvitānāṃ pippalīnāṃ payo'śanaḥ || 24
sahasraṃ ca prayuñjīta śaktito jaṭharāmayī
śilājatūnāṃ mūtrāṇāṃ guggulostraiphalasya ca || 25
snuhīkṣīraprayogaśca śamayatyudarāmayam
snukpayasā paribhāvitataṇḍulacūrṇairnirmitaḥ pūtaḥ || 26
udaramudāraṃ hiṃsyādyogo'yaṃ saptarātreṇa
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā kalpadṛṣṭaṃ prayojayet || 27
jaṭharāṇāṃ vināśāya nāsti tena samaṃ bhuvi
5paṭolamūlaṃ rajanī viḍaṅgaṃ triphalātvacam || 28
kampillakaṃ nīlinīṃ ca trivṛtāṃ ceti cūrṇayet
ṣaḍādyānkārṣikānantyāṃstrīṃśca dvitricaturguṇān || 29
kṛtvā cūrṇaṃ tato muṣṭiṃ gavāṃ mūtreṇa nā pivet
virikto jāṅgalarasairbhuñjīta mṛdumodanam || 30
maṇḍaṃ peyāṃ ca pītvā ca savyoṣaṃ ṣaḍahaḥ payaḥ
śṛtaṃ pibettu taccūrṇaṃ pibedevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 31
hanti sarvodarāṇyetaccūrṇaṃ jātodakānyapi
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca śvayathuṃ cāpakarṣati || 32
6yamānī hapuṣā dhānyaṃ triphalā sopakuñcikā
kāravī pippalīmūlamajagandhā śaṭhī vacā || 33
śatāhvā jīrakaṃ vyoṣaṃ svarṇakṣīrī sacitrakam
dvau kṣārau pauṣkaraṃ mūlaṃ kuṣṭaṃ lavaṇapañcakam || 34
viḍaṅgaṃ ca samāṃśāni dantyā bhāgatrayaṃ tathā
trivṛdviśāle dviguṇe śātalā syāccaturguṇāḥ || 35
eṣa nārāyaṇo nāma cūrṇo rogagaṇāpahaḥ
nainaṃ prāpyābhivardhante rogā viṣṇumivāsurāḥ || 36
takreṇodaribhiḥ peyo gulmibhirvadarāmbunā
ānaddhavāte surayā vātaroge prasannayā || 37
dadhimaṇḍena viṭsaṅge dāḍimāmbubhirarśasi
parikarte ca vṛkṣāmlairuṣṇāmbubhirajīrṇake || 38
bhagandare pāṇḍuroge kāse śvāse galagrahe
hṛdroge grahaṇīdoṣe koṣṭhe mandānale jvare || 39
daṃṣṭrāviṣe mūlaviṣe sagare kṛtrime viṣe
yathārhaṃ snigdhakoṣṭhena peyametadvirecanam || 40
7dantīvacā gavākṣī ca śaṅkhinī tillakaṃ trivṛt
gomūtreṇa pibetkalkaṃ jaṭharāmayanāśanam || 41
sakṣīraṃ māhipaṃ mūtraṃ nirāhāraḥ pibennaraḥ
śāmyatyanena jaṭharaṃ saptāhāditi niścayaḥ || 42
gavākṣīśaṅkhinīdantīnīlinīkalkasaṃyutam
sarvodaravināśaya gomūtraṃ pātumācaret || 43
arkapatraṃ salavaṇamantardhūmaṃ dahettataḥ
mastunā tatpibetkṣīraṃ gulmaplīhodarāpaham || 44
pītaḥ plīhodaraṃ hanyātpippalīmaricānvitaḥ
amlavetasasaṃyuktaḥ śigrukvāthaḥ sasaindhavaḥ || 45
gṛhītvā yasya saṃjñā?pāṭhayitvendravāruṇīmūlam
prakṣipyate sudūre śāmyate plīhodaraṃ tasya || 46
rohitakābhayākṣodabhāvitaṃ mūtramambu vā
pītaṃ sarvodaraplīhamehārśaḥ krimigulmanut || 47
8devadrumaṃ śigru mayūrakaṃ ca
gomūtrapiṣṭānathavā'śvagandhān
pītvāśu hanyādudaraṃ pravṛddhaṃ
kṛmīnsaśothānudaraṃ ca dūṣyam || 48
daśamūladārunāgara-
cchinnaruhāpunarnavābhayākvāthaḥ
jayati jalodaraśotha-
ślīpadagalagaṇḍavātarogāṃśca || 49
harītakīnāgaradevadāru
punarnavāchinnaruhākaṣāyaḥ
saguggulurmūtrayutaśca peyaḥ
śothodarāṇāṃ pravaraḥ prayogaḥ || 50
eraṇḍatailaṃ daśamūlamiśraṃ
gomūtrayuktastriphalāraso vā
nihanti vātodaraśothaśūlaṃ
kvāthaḥ samūtro daśamūlajaśca || 51
9punarnavānimbapaṭolaśuṇṭhī-
tiktābhayādārvamṛtākaṣāyaḥ
sarvāṅgaśothodarakāsaśūla-
śvāsānvitaṃ pāṇḍugadaṃ nihanti || 52
punarnavāṃ dārvabhayāṃ guḍūcīṃ
pibetsamūtrāṃ mahiṣākṣayuktām
tvagdoṣaśothodarapāṇḍuroga-
sthaulyaprasekordhvakaphāmayeṣu || 53
gomūtrayuktaṃ mahiṣīpayo vā
kṣīraṃ gavāṃ vā triphalāvimiśram
kṣīrānnabhukkevalameva gavyaṃ
mūtraṃ pibedvā śvayathūdareṣu || 54
punarnavā dārvamṛtā pāṭhā bilvaṃ śvadaṃṣṭrikā
bṛhatyau dve rajanyau dve pippalyaścitrakaṃ vṛṣam
samabhāgāni saṃcūrṇya gavāṃ mūtreṇa nā pibet || 55
bahuprakāraṃ śvayathuṃ sarvagātravisāriṇam
hanti śūlodarāṇyaṣṭau vraṇāṃścaivoddhatānapi || 56
purāṇaṃ māṇakaṃ piṣṭvā dviguṇīkṛtataṇḍulam
sādhitaṃ kṣīratoyābhyāmabhyasetpāyasaṃ tataḥ || 57
hanti vātodaraṃ śothaṃ grahaṇīṃ pāṇḍutāmapi
siddho bhiṣagbhirākhyātaḥ prayogo'yaṃ niratyayaḥ || 58
daśamūlatulārdharase
sakṣāraiḥ pañcakolaiḥ palikaiḥ
siddhaṃ ghṛtārdhapātraṃ
dvimastukamudaragulmaghnam || 59
10caturguṇe jale mūtre dviguṇe citrakātpale
kalke siddhaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ sakṣāraṃ jaṭharī pibet || 60
11arkakṣīrapale dve ca snuhikṣīrapalāni ṣaṭ
pathyākampillakaṃ śyāmāsampākaṃ girikarṇikā || 61
nīlinī trivṛtā dantī śaṅkhinī citrakaṃ tathā
eteṣāṃ palikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 62
athāsya maline koṣṭhe bindumātraṃ pradāpayet
yāvato'sya pibedbindūṃstāvadvārānviricyate || 63
kuṣṭhaṃ gulmamudāvartaṃ śvayathuṃ sabhagandaram
śamayatyudarāṇyaṣṭau vṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā
etadbindughṛtaṃ nāma yenābhyakto viricyate || 64
12dadhimaṃḍāḍhake siddhātsnukkṣīrapalakalkitāt
ghṛtaprasthātpibenmātrāṃ tadvajjaṭharaśāntaye || 65
tathā siddhaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ payasyaṣṭaguṇe pibet
snukkṣīrapalakalkena trivṛtā ṣaṭpalena ca || 66
snukkṣīradantītriphalāviḍaṅga-
siṃhītrivṛccitrakakalkayuktam
ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ kuḍavapramāṇaṃ
toyena tasyākṣamathārdhakarṣam || 67
pītvoṣṇamambho'nu pibedvirikte
peyāṃ sukhoṣṇāṃ vitaredvidhijñaḥ
nārācametajjaṭharāmayānāṃ
yuktopayuktaṃ śamanaṃ pradiṣṭam || 68
Adhikāra 37
1yamānikācitrakayāvaśūka-
ṣaḍgranthidantīmagadhodbhavānām
plīhānametadvinihanti cūrṇa-
muṣṇāmbunā mastusurāsavairvā || 1
pippalīṃ kiṃśukakṣārabhāvitāṃ saṃprayojayet
gulmaplīhāpahāṃ vahnidīpanīṃ ca rasāyanīm || 2
viḍaṅgājyāgnisindhūtthaśaktūndagdhvā vacānvitān
pibetkṣīreṇa saṃcūrṇya gulmaplīhodarāpaham || 3
tālapuṣpabhavaḥ kṣāraḥ saguḍaḥ plīhanāśanaḥ
kṣāraṃ vā biḍakṛṣṇābhyāṃ pūtīkasyāmlaniḥ srutam || 4
plīhayakṛtpraśāntyarthaṃ pibetprātaryathābalam
pātavyo yuktitaḥ kṣāraḥ kṣīreṇodadhiśuktijaḥ || 5
payasā vā prayoktavyāḥ pippalyaḥ plīhaśāntaye
bhallātakābhayājājī guḍena saha modakaḥ || 6
saptarātrānnihantyāśu plīhānamatidāruṇam
śobhāñjanakaniryūhaṃ saindhavāgnikaṇānvitam || 7
palāśakṣārayuktaṃ vā yavākṣāraṃ prayojayet
2tilānsalavaṇāṃścaiva ghṛtaṃ ṣaṭpalakaṃ tathā || 8
plīhoddiṣṭāṃ kriyāṃ sarvāṃ yakṛtaḥ saṃprayojayet
laśunaṃ pippalīmūlamabhayāṃ caiva bhakṣayet || 9
pibedgomūtragaṇḍūṣaṃ plīharogavimuktaye
plīhajiccharapuṅkhāyāḥ kalkastakreṇa sevitaḥ
śarapuṅkhaiva saṃcarvya jagdhvā peyābhayāthavā || 10
śardiṣṭhāniryūhaḥ sasaindhavastintiḍīkasaṃmiśraḥ
plīhavyuparama yogaḥ pakvāmraraso'thavā samadhuḥ || 11
dadhnā bhuktavato vāmabāhumadhye śirāṃ bhiṣak
vidhyetplīhavināśāya yakṛnnāśāya dakṣiṇe || 12
plīhānaṃ mardayedgāḍhaṃ duṣṭaraktapravṛttaye
3māṇamārgāmṛtāvāsāsthirācitrakasaindhavam || 13
nāgaraṃ tālagaṇḍaṃ ca pratyagraṃ tu trikārṣikam
biḍasauvarcalakṣārapippalyaścāpi kārṣikāḥ || 14
etaccūrṇīkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ gomūtrasyāḍhake pacet
sāndrībhūte guḍīṃ kuryāddattvā tripalamākṣikam || 15
yakṛtplīhodaraharo gulmārśograhaṇīharaḥ
yogaḥ parikaro nāmnā cāgnisandīpanaḥ paraḥ || 16
4rohitakābhayākṣodabhāvitaṃ mūtramambu vā
pītaṃ sarvodaraplīhamehārśaḥkrimigulmanut || 17
pippalī nāgaraṃ dantī samāṃśaṃ dviguṇābhayam
cūrṇaṃ pītaṃ biḍārdhāṃśaṃ plīhaghnaṃ hyuṣṇavāriṇā || 18
kramavṛddhyā daśāhāni daśapippalikaṃ dinam
vardhayetpayasā sārdhaṃ tathaivāpanayetpunaḥ || 19
jīrṇe jīrṇe ca bhuñjīta yaṣṭikaṃ kṣīrasarpiṣā
5pippalīnāṃ prayogo'yaṃ sahasrasya rasāyanaḥ || 20
daśapippalikaḥ śreṣṭho madhyamaḥ ṣaṭ prakīrtitaḥ
yastripippaliparyantaḥ prayogaḥ so'varaḥ smṛtaḥ || 21
bṛhaṇaṃ vṛṣyamāyuṣyaṃ plīhodaravināśanam
vayasaḥ sthāpanaṃ medhyaṃ pippalīnāṃ rasāyanam || 22
pañcapippalikaścāpi dṛśyate vardhamānakaḥ
piṣṭāstā balibhiḥ peyāḥ śṛtā madhyabalairnaraiḥ
śītīkṛtā hrasvabalairdehadoṣāmayānprati || 23
