6pippalīcitrakānmūlaṃ piṣṭvā samyagvipācayet
ghṛtaṃ caturguṇakṣīraṃ yakṛtplīhodarāpaham || 24
7pippalīkalkasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīracaturguṇam
pibetplīhāgnisādādiyakṛdrogaharaṃ param || 25
8citrakasya tulākvāthe ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
āraṇālaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dadhimaṇḍaṃ caturguṇam || 26
pañcakolakatālīsakṣārairlavaṇasaṃyutaiḥ
dvijīrakaniśāyugmairmaricaṃ tatra dāpayet || 27
plīhagulmodarādhmānapāṇḍurogārucijvarān
bastihṛtpārśvakaṭyūruśūlodāvartapīnasān || 28
hanyātpītaṃ tadarśoghnaṃ śothaghnaṃ vahridīpanam
balavarṇakaraṃ cāpi bhasmakaṃ ca niyacchati || 29
9rohitakatvacaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ palānāṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
koladviprasthasaṃyuktaṃ kaṣāyamupakalpayet || 30
palikaiḥ pañcakolaiśca tatsarvaiścāpi tulyayā
rohitakatvacā piṣṭairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 31
plīhābhivṛddhiṃ śamayedetadāśu prayojitam
tathā gulmajvaraśvāsakrimipāṇḍutvakāmalāḥ || 32
10rohitakātpalaśataṃ kṣodayedbadarāḍhakam
sādhayitvā jaladroṇe caturbhāgāvaśeṣite || 33
ghṛtaprasthaṃ samāvāpya chāgakṣīracaturguṇam
tasmindadyādimānkalkānsarvāṃstānakṣasammitām || 34
vyopaṃ phalatrikaṃ hiṅgu yamānīṃ tumburuṃ biḍam
ajājīṃ kṛṣṇalavaṇaṃ dāḍimaṃ devadāru ca || 35
punarnavāṃ viśālāṃ ca yavakṣāraṃ sapauṣkaram
viḍaṅgaṃ citrakaṃ caivahapuṣāṃ cavikāṃ vacām || 36
etairghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ tu sthāpayedbhājane dṛḍhe
pāyayettripalāṃ mātrāṃ vyādhiṃ balamapekṣya ca || 37
rasakenātha yūṣeṇa payasā vāpi bhojayet
upayukte ghṛte tasminvyādhīnhanyādimānbahūn || 38
yakṛtplīhodaraṃ caiva plīhaśūlaṃ yakṛttathā
kukṣiśūlaṃ ca hṛcchūlaṃ pārśvaśūlamarocakam || 39
vibandhaśūlaṃ śamayetpāṇḍurogaṃ sakāmalam
chardyatīsāraśamanaṃ tandrājvaravināśanam
mahārohitakaṃ nāma plīhaghnaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ || 40
Adhikāra 38
1śuṇṭhīpunarnavairaṇḍapañcamūlaśṛtaṃ jalam
vātike śvayathau śastaṃ pānāhāraparigrahe
daśamūlaṃ sarvathā ca śastaṃ vāte viśeṣataḥ || 1
kṣīrāśanaḥ pittakṛte'tha śothe
trivṛdguḍūcītriphalākaṣāyam
pibedgavāṃ mūtravimiśritaṃ vā
phalatrikācūrṇamathākṣamātram || 2
abhayādārumadhukatiktā dantī sapippalī
paṭolaṃ candanaṃ dārvī trāyamāṇendravāruṇī || 3
eṣāṃ kvāthaḥ sasarpiṣkaḥ śvayathujvaradāhadā
visarpatṛṣṇāsantāpasannipātaviṣāpahā
śītavīryairhimajalairabhyaṅgādīṃśca kārayet || 4
2punarnavāviśvatrivṛdguḍūcī-
sampākapathyāmaradārukalkam
śothe kaphotthe mahiṣākṣayuktaṃ
mūtraṃ pibedvā salilaṃ tathaiṣām || 5
kaphe tu kṛṣṇāsikatāpurāṇa-
piṇyākaśigrutvagumāpralepaḥ
kulatthaśuṇṭhījalamūtraseka-
ścaṇḍāgurubhyāmanulepanaṃ ca || 6
ajājipāṭhāghanapañcakola-
vyāghrīrajanyaḥ sukhatoyapītāḥ
śothaṃ tridoṣaṃ cirajaṃ pravṛddhaṃ
nighnanti bhūnimbamahauṣadhe ca || 7
punarnavānimbapaṭolaśuṇṭhī-
tiktāmṛtādārvabhayākaṣāyaḥ
sarvāṅgaśothodarakāsaśūla-
śvāsānvitaṃ pāṇḍugadaṃ nihanti || 8
3ārdrakasya rasaḥ pītaḥ purāṇaguḍamiśritaḥ
ajākṣīrāśināṃ śīghraṃ sarvaśothaharo bhavet || 9
punarnavādāruśuṇṭhīkvāthe mūtre ca kevale
daśamūlarase vāpi gugguluḥ śothanāśanaḥ || 10
bilvapatrarasaṃ pūtaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ śvayathau trije
viṭsaṅge caiva durnāmni vidadhyātkāmalāsvapi || 11
guḍapippaliśuṇṭhīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ śvayathunāśanam
āmājīrṇapraśamanaṃ śūlaghnaṃ bastiśodhanam || 12
puro mūtreṇa sevyeta pippalī vā payo'nvitā
guḍena vābhayā tulyā viśvaṃ vā śotharogiṇām || 13
guḍārdrakaṃ vā guḍanāgaraṃ vā
guḍābhayaṃ vā guḍapippalīṃ vā
karṣābhivṛddhyā tripalapramāṇaṃ
khādennaraḥ pakṣamathāpi māsam || 14
śothapratiśyāyagalāsyarogān
saśvāsakāsārucipīnasāṃśca
jīrṇajvarārśograhaṇīvikārān
hanyāttathānyānkaphavātarogān || 15
4sthalapadmamayaṃ kalkaṃ payasāloḍya pāyayet
plīhāmayaharaṃ caiva sarvāṅgaikāṅgaśothajit || 16
dārugugguluśuṇṭhīnāṃ kalko mūtreṇa śothajit
varṣābhūśṛṅgaverābhyāṃ kalko vā sarvaśothajit || 17
siṃhāsyāmṛtabhaṇḍākīkvāthaṃ kṛtvā samākṣikam
pītvā śothaṃ jayejjantuḥ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ vamiṃ jvaram || 18
bhūnimbaviśvakalkaṃ jagdhvā peyaḥ punarnavākvāthaḥ
apaharati niyatamāśu śothaṃ sarvāṅgagaṃ nṝṇām || 19
śothanutkokilākṣasya bhasma mūtreṇa vāmbhasā
kṣīraṃ śothaharaṃ dāruvarṣābhūnāgaraiḥ śṛtam || 20
peyaṃ vā citrakavyoṣatrivṛddāruprasādhitam
5punarnavāmūlakapitthadāru-
chinnodbhavācitrakamūlasiddhāḥ
rasā yavāgvaṃ ca payāṃsi yūṣāḥ
śothe pradeyā daśamūlagarbhāḥ || 21
kṣāradvayaṃ syāllavaṇāni catvā-
ryayorajo vyoṣaphalatrike ca
sapippalīmūlaviḍaṅgasāraṃ
mustājamodāmaradārubilvam || 22
kaliṅgakaścitrakamūlapāṭhe
yaṣṭyāhvayaṃ sātiviṣaṃ palāśam
sahiṅgu karṣaṃ tvatha śuṣkacūrṇaṃ
droṇaṃ tathā mūlakaśuṇṭhakānām || 23
syādbhasmanastatsalilena sādhya-
māloḍya yāvaddhanamapyadagdham
styānaṃ tataḥ kolasamāṃ ca mātrāṃ
kṛtvā suśuṣkāṃ vidhinā prayuñjyāt || 24
plīhodaraśvitrahalīmakārśaḥ-
pāṇḍvāmayārocakaśothaśoṣān
viṣūcikāgulmagarāśmarīśca
saśvāsakāsānpraṇudetsakuṣṭhān || 25
sauvarcalaṃ saindhavaṃ ca biḍamaudbhidameva ca
caturlavaṇamatra syājjalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet || 26
6punarnavācitrakadevadāru-
pañcoṣaṇakṣāraharītakīnām
kalkena pakvaṃ daśamūlatoye
ghṛtottamaṃ śothaniṣūdanaṃ ca || 27
7punarnavākvāthakalkasiddhaṃ śothaharaṃ ghṛtam
viśvaupadhasya kalkena daśamūlajale śṛtam
ghṛtaṃ nihanyācchvayathuṃ grahaṇīṃ pāṇḍutāmayam || 28
8sacitrakādhānyayamānipāṭhāḥ
sadīpyakatryūṣaṇavetasāmlāḥ
bilvātphalaṃ dāḍimayāvaśūkaṃ
sapippalīmūlamathāpi cavyam || 29
piṣṭvākṣamātrāṇi jalāḍhakena
paktvā ghṛtaprasthamathopayuñjyāt
arśāṃsi gulmāñchvayathuṃ ca kṛcchraṃ
nihanti vahniṃ ca karoti dīptam || 30
9rase vipācayetsarpiḥ pañcakolakulatthayoḥ
punarnavāyāḥ kalkena ghṛtaṃ śothavināśanam || 31
10kṣīraṃ ghaṭe citrakakalkalipte
dadhyāgataṃ sādhu vimathya tena
tajjaṃ ghṛtaṃ citrakamūlakalkaṃ
takreṇa siddhaṃ śvayathughnamagryam || 32
arśo'tisārānilagulmamehāṃ-
staddhanti saṃvardhayate balaṃ ca || 33
11māṇakakvāthakalkābhyāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
ekajaṃ dvandvajaṃ śothaṃ tridoṣaṃ ca vyapohati || 34
12sthalapadmapalānyaṣṭau tryūṣaṇasya catuḥpalam
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedebhiḥ kṣīraṃ datvā caturguṇam
pañca kāsānharecchīghraṃ śothaṃ caiva sudustaram || 35
13śaileyakuṣṭāgurudārukauntī-
tvakpadmakailābupalāśamustaiḥ
priyaṅgusthauṇeyakahemamāṃsī-
tālīsapatraplavapatradhānyaiḥ || 36
śrīveṣṭakadhyāmakapippalībhiḥ
pṛkkānakhairvāpi yathopalābham
vātānvite'dhyaṅgamuśanti tailaṃ
siddhaṃ supiṣṭairapi ca pradeham || 37
14śuṣkamūlakavarṣābhūdārurāsnāmahauṣadhaiḥ
pakvamabhyañjanāttailaṃ saśūlaṃ śvayathuṃ jayet || 38
15punarnavāmṛtādarudaśamūlarasāḍhake
ārdrakasvarase prasthe guḍasya tu tulāṃ pacet || 39
tatsiddhaṃ vyoṣacavyailātvakpalaiḥ kārṣikaiḥ pṛthak
cūrṇīkṛtaiḥ kṣipecchīte madhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ lihet || 40
lehaḥ paunarnavo nāma śothaśūlaniṣūdanaḥ
śvāsakāsā'ruciharo balavarṇāgnivardhanaḥ || 41
16daśamūlakaṣāyasya kaṃse pathyāśataṃ pacet
tulāṃ guḍāddhane dadyādvyoṣakṣāraṃ catuḥpalam || 42
trisugandhaṃ suvarṇāṃśaṃ prasthārdhaṃ madhuno hime
daśamūlīharītakyaḥ śothānhanyuḥ sudāruṇān || 43
jvarārocakagulmārśomehapāṇḍūdarāmayān
pratyekamekakarṣāṃśaṃ trisugandhamito bhavet || 44
kaṃsaharītakī caiṣā carake paṭhyate'nyathā
etanmānena tulyatvaṃ tena tatrāpi varṇyate || 45
17dvipañcamūlasya pacetkaṣāye
kaṃse'bhayānāṃ ca śataṃ guḍācca
lehe susiddhe ca vinīya cūrṇaṃ
vyoṣatrisaugandhyamuṣāsthite ca || 46
prasthārdhamātraṃ madhunaḥ suśīte
kiṃcicca cūrṇādapi yāvaśūkāt
ekābhayāṃ prāśya tataśca lehā-
cchuktiṃ nihanti śvayathuṃ pravṛddham || 47
kāsajvarārocakamehagulmā-
nplīhatridoṣodbhavapāṇḍurogān
kārśyāmavātāvasṛgamlapittaṃ
vaivarṇyamūtrānilaśukradoṣān || 48
atra vyākhyāntaraṃ noktaṃ
vyākhyā pūrvaiva yacchubhā || 49
18lepo'ruṣkaraśothaṃ
nihanti tiladugdhamadhunavanītaiḥ
tattarutalamṛdbhirvā
śāladalairvā tu na cireṇa || 50
śothe viṣanimitte tu viṣoktā sammatā kriyā || 51
grāmyajānūpaṃ piśitalavaṇaṃ śuṣkaśākaṃ navānnaṃ
gauḍaṃ piṣṭānnaṃ dadhi sakṛśaraṃ vijjalaṃ madyamamlam
dhānāvallūraṃ samaśanamatho gurvasātmyaṃ vidāhi
svapnaṃ cārātrau śvayathugadavānvarjayenmaithunaṃ ca || 52
Adhikāra 39
1gugguluṃ rubutailaṃ vā gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
vātavṛddhiṃ nihantyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm || 1
sakṣīraṃ vā pibettailaṃ māsameraṇḍasambhavam
punarnavāyāstailaṃ vā tailaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ tathā || 2
pāne bastau rurostailaṃ peyaṃ vā daśakāmbhasā
candanaṃ madhukaṃ padmamuśīraṃ nīlamutpalam || 3
kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pradehaḥ syāddāhaśotharujāpahaḥ
pañcavalkalakalkena saghṛtena pralepanam || 4
sarvaṃ pittaharaṃ kāryaṃ raktaje raktamokṣaṇam
śleṣmavṛddhiṃ tūṣṇavīryairmūtrapiṣṭaiḥ pralepayet || 5
pītadārukaṣāyaṃ ca pibenmūtreṇa saṃyutam
svinnaṃ medaḥsamutthaṃ tu lepayetsurasādinā || 6
śirovirekadravyairvā sukhoṣṇairmūtrasaṃyutaiḥ
saṃsvedya mūtraprabhavāṃ vastrapaṭṭena veṣṭayet || 7
sīvanyāḥ pārśvato'dhastādvidhyedvrīhimukhena vai
śaṅkhopari ca karṇānte tyaktvā sīvanimādahet || 8
vyatyāsādvā śirāṃ vidhyedantravṛddhinivṛttaye
aṅguṣṭhamadhye tvak chittvā dahedaṅgaviparyaye || 9
2rāsnāyaṣṭyāmṛtairaṇḍabalāgokṣurasādhitaḥ
kvātho'ntravṛddhiṃ hantyāśu rubutailena miśritaḥ || 10
tailameraṇḍajaṃ pītvā balāsiddhapayo'nvitam
ādhmānaśūlopacitāmantravṛddhiṃ jayennaraḥ || 11
harītakīṃ mūtrasiddhāṃ satailāṃ lavaṇānvitām
prātaḥ prātaśca seveta kaphavātāmayāpahām || 12
gomūtrasiddhāṃ rubutailabhṛṣṭāṃ
harītakīṃ saindhavasaṃprayuktām
khādennaraḥ koṣṇajalānupānāṃ
nihati vṛddhiṃ cirajāṃ pravṛddhām || 13
triphalākvāthagomūtraṃ pibetprātaratandritaḥ
kaphavātodbhavaṃ hanti śvayathuṃ vṛṣaṇotthitam || 14
saralāgurukuṣṭhāni devadārumahauṣadham
mūtrāraṇālasaṃyuktaṃ śothaghnaṃ kaphavātanut || 15
bhṛṣṭo rubukatailena kalkaḥ pathyāsamudbhavaḥ
kṛṣṇāsaindhavasaṃyukto vṛddhirogaharaḥ paraḥ || 16
3gavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ saindhavasaṃprayuktaṃ
śambūkabhāṇḍe nihitaṃ prayatnāt
saptāhamādityakarairvipakvaṃ
nihanti kūraṇḍamatipravṛddham || 17
aindrīmūlabhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ rubutailena marditam
tryahādgopayasā pītaṃ sarvavṛddhinivāraṇam || 18
rudrajaṭāmūlaliptā karaṭavyaṅkacarmaṇā
baddhā vṛddhiḥ śamaṃ yāti cirajāpi na saṃśayaḥ || 19
niṣpiṣṭamāraṇālena rūpikāmūlavalkalam
lepo vṛddhyāmayaṃ hanti baddhamūlamapi dṛḍham || 20
vacāsarṣapakalkena pralepo vṛddhināśanaḥ
lajjāgṛdhramalābhyāṃ ca lepo vṛddhiharaḥ paraḥ || 21
4mūlaṃ bilvakapitthayoraralukasyāgnerbṛhatyordvayoḥ
śyāmāpūtikarañjaśigrukatarorviśvauṣadhāruṣkaram
kṛṣṇāgranthikacavyapañcalavaṇakṣārājamodānvitaṃ
pītaṃ kāñjikakoṣṇatoyamathitaṃ cūrṇīkṛtaṃ bradhnanut || 22
avikṣīreṇa godhūmakalkaṃ kundurukasya vā
pralepanaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ syādbradhnaśūlaharaḥ paraḥ || 23
mṛtamātre tu vai kāke viśaste saṃpraveśayet
bradhnaṃ muhūrtaṃ medhāvī tatkṣaṇādarujaṃ bhavet || 24
ajājī hapuṣā kuṣṭaṃ godhūmaṃ badarāṇi ca
kāñjikena samaṃ piṣṭvā kuryādbradhnapralepanam || 25
5saindhavaṃ madanaṃ kuṣṭaṃ śatāhvāṃ niculaṃ vacām
hrīveraṃ madhukaṃ bhārgī devadāru sanāgaram || 26
kaṭphalaṃ pauṣkaraṃ medāṃ cavikaṃ citrakaṃ śaṭhīm
viḍaṅgātiviṣe śyāmāṃ reṇukāṃ nalinīṃ sthirām || 27
bilvājamode kṛṣṇāṃ ca dantīrāsne prapiṣya ca
sādhyameraṇḍajaṃ tailaṃ tailaṃ vātavikāranut || 28
bradhnodāvartagulmārśaḥplīhamehāḍhyamārutān
ānāhamaśmarīṃ caiva hanyāttadanuvāsanāt
ghṛtaṃ saureśvaraṃ yojyaṃ bradhnavṛddhinivṛttaye || 29
Adhikāra 40
1yavamudgapaṭolāni kaṭu rukṣaṃ ca bhojanam
chardiṃ saraktamuktiṃ ca galagaṇḍe prayojayet || 1
taṇḍulodakapiṣṭena mūlena parilepitaḥ
hastikarṇapalāśasya galagaṇḍaḥ praśāmyati || 2
sarṣapāñśagrubījāni śaṇabījātasīyavān
mūlakasya ca bījāni takreṇāmlena peṣayet || 3
gaṇḍāni granthayaścaiva galagaṇḍāḥ sudāruṇāḥ
pralepāttena śāmyanti vilayaṃ yānti cācirāt || 4
jīrṇakarkārukaraso biḍasaindhavasaṃyutaḥ
nasyena hanti taruṇaṃ galagaṇḍaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 5
jalakumbhīkajaṃ bhasma pakvaṃ gomūtragālitam
pibetkodravabhaktāśī galagaṇḍapraśāntaye || 6
sūryāvartarasonābhyāṃ galagaṇḍopanāhane
sphoṭāsrāvaiḥ śamaṃ yāti galagaṇḍo na saṃśayaḥ || 7
2tiktālābuphale pakve saptāhamuṣitaṃ jalam
nadyaṃ vā galagaṇḍaghnaṃ pānātpathyānusevinaḥ || 8
kaṭphalacūrṇāntargala-
varṣo galagaṇḍamapaharati
ghṛtamiśraṃ pītamiva
śvetagirikarṇikāmūlam || 9
mahiṣīmūtravimiśraṃ
lohamalasaṃsthitaṃ ghaṭe māsam
antardhūmavidagdhaṃ
lihyānmadhunātha galagaṇḍe || 10
jihvāyāḥ pārśvato'dhastācchirā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ
tāsāṃ sthūlaśire dve'dhaśchindyātte ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 11
baḍiśenaiva saṃgṛhya kuśapatreṇa buddhimān
srute rakte vraṇe tasmindadyātsaguḍamārdrakam || 12
bhojanaṃ cānabhiṣyandi yūṣaḥ kaulattha iṣyate
karṇayugmabahiḥsandhimadhyābhyāse sthitaṃ ca yat || 13
uparyupari tacchindyādgalagaṇḍe śirātrayam
viḍaṅgakṣārasindhūgrarāsnāgnivyoṣadārubhiḥ || 14
kaṭutumbīphalarasaiḥ kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
cirotthamapi nasyena galagaṇḍaṃ nivārayet || 15
3tailaṃ pibeccāmṛtavallinimba-
hiṃsrāhvayāvṛkṣakapippalībhiḥ
siddhaṃ balābhyāṃ ca sadevadāru
hitāya nityaṃ galagaṇḍarogī || 16
mākṣikāḍhyaḥ sakṛtpītaḥ kvātho varuṇamūlajaḥ
gaṇḍamālāṃ nihantyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm || 17
piṣṭā jyeṣṭhāmbunā peyāḥ kāñcanāratvacaḥ śubhāḥ
viśvabheṣajasaṃyuktā gaṇḍamālāpahāḥ parāḥ || 18
āragvadhaśiphākṣipraṃ piṣṭvā taṇḍulavāriṇā
samyaṅnasyapralepābhyāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ samuddharet || 19
gaṇḍamālāmayārtānāṃ nasyakarmaṇi yojayet
nirguṇḍyāśca śiphāṃ samyagvāriṇā paripeṣitām || 20
4koṣātakīnāṃ svarasena nasyaṃ
tumbyāstu vā pippalisaṃyutena
tailena vāriṣṭabhavena kuryā-
dvacopakulye saha mākṣikeṇa || 21
aindryā vā girikarṇyā vā mūlaṃ gomūtrayogataḥ
gaṇḍamālāṃ haretpītaṃ cirakālotthitāmapi || 22
alambuṣādalodbhūtātsvarasāddve pale pibet
apacyā gaṇḍamālāyāḥ kāmalāyāśca nāśanaḥ || 23
galagaṇḍagaṇḍamālākūraṇḍāṃśca vināśayet
piṣṭaṃ jyeṣṭhāmbunā mūlaṃ lepādbrāhmaṇayaṣṭikam || 24
5abhyaṅgānnāśayenṝṇāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ sudāruṇām
chucchundaryā vipakvaṃ tu kṣaṇāttailavaraṃ dhruvam || 25
6galagaṇḍāpahaṃ tailaṃ siddhaṃ śākhoṭakatvacā
bimbāśvamāranirguṇḍīsādhitaṃ cāpi nāvanam || 26
7nirguṇḍīsvarase cātha lāṅgalīmūlakalkitam
tailaṃ nasyānnihantyāśu gaṇḍamālāṃ sudāruṇam || 27
8vanakārpāsikāmūlaṃ taṇḍulaiḥ saha yojitam
paktvā tu pūpikāṃ khādedapacīnāśanāya tu || 28
śobhāñjanaṃ devadāru kāñjikena tu peṣitam
koṣṇaṃ pralepato hanyādapacīmatidustarām || 29
sarṣapāriṣṭapatrāṇi dagdhvā bhallātakaiḥ saha
chāgamūtreṇa saṃpiṣṭamapacīghnaṃ pralepanam || 30
aśvatthakāṣṭhaṃ niculaṃ gavāṃ dantaṃ ca dāhayet
varāhamajjasaṃyuktaṃ bhasma hantyapacīvraṇān || 31
pārṣṇiṃ prati dvādaśa cāṅgulāni
mitvendrabastiṃ parivarjya samyak
vidārya matsyāṇḍanibhāni vaidyo
nikṛṣya jālānyanalaṃ vidadhyāt || 32
maṇibandhopariṣṭādvā kuryādrekhātrayaṃ bhiṣak
aṅgulyāntaritaṃ samyagapacīnāṃ praśāntaye || 33
daṇḍotpalābhavaṃ mūlaṃ baddhaṃ puṣye'pacīṃ jayet
apāmārgasya vā chindyājjihvātalagate śire || 34
9vyoṣaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ madhukaṃ saindhavaṃ devadāru ca
tailametaiḥ śṛtaṃ nasyātkṛcchrāmapyapacīṃ jayet || 35
10candanaṃ sābhayā lākṣā vacā kaṭukarohiṇī
etaistailaṃ śṛtaṃ pītaṃ samūlāmapacīṃ jayet || 36
11guñjāhayāriśyāmākasarṣapairmūtrasādhitam
tailaṃ tu daśadhā paścātkaṇālavaṇapañcakam || 37
maricaiścūrṇitairyuktaṃ sarvāvasthāgatāṃ jayet
abhyaṅgādapacīmugrāṃ valmīkārśo'rbudavraṇān || 38
12granthiṣvāmeṣu kurvīta bhiṣak śothapratikriyāṃ
pakvānāpāṭya saṃśodhya ropayedvraṇabheṣajaiḥ || 39
hiṃsrā sarohiṇyamṛtā ca bhārgī
śyāmākabilvyagurukṛṣṇagandhāḥ
gopittapiṣṭāḥ saha tālaparṇyā
granthau vidheyo'nilaje pralepaḥ || 40
jalātmakāḥ pittakṛte hitāstu
kṣīrodakābhyāṃ parisecanaṃ ca
kākolibargasya tu śītalāni
pibetkaṣāyāṇi saśarkarāṇi || 41
drākṣārasenekṣurasena vāpi
cūrṇaṃ pibedvāpi harītakīnām
madhūkajambvarjunavetasānāṃ
tvagbhiḥ pradehānavatārayecca || 42
kṛteṣu doṣeṣu yathānupūrvā
granthau bhiṣak śleṣmasamutthite tu
svinne ca vimlāpanameva kuryā-
daṅguṣṭhareṇudṛṣadīsutaiśca || 43
13vikaṅkatāragvadhakākaṇantī-
kākādanītāpasavṛkṣamūlaiḥ
ālepayedenamalābubhārgī-
karañjakālāmadanaiśca vidvān || 44
dantī citrakamūlatvak sudhārkapayasī guḍaḥ
bhallātakāsthikāsīsaṃ lepo bhindyācchilāmapi || 45
granthyarbudādijillepo mātṛvāhakakīṭajaḥ
sarjikāmūlakakṣāraḥ śaṅkhacūrṇasamanvitaḥ
pralepo vihitastīkṣṇo hanti granthyarbudādikān || 46
14granthīnamarmaprabhavānapakvā-
nuddhṛtya vāgniṃ vidadhīta vaidyaḥ
kṣāreṇa vai tānpratisārayettu
saṃlikhya saṃlikhya yathopadeśam || 47
granthyarbudānāṃ na yato viśeṣaḥ
pradeśahetvākṛtidoṣadūṣyaiḥ
tataścikitsedbhiṣagarbudāni
vidhānavidgranthicikitsitena || 48
vātārbude cāpyupanāhanāni
snigdhaiśca māṃsairatha vesavāraiḥ
svedaṃ vidadhyātkuśalastu nāḍyā
śṛṅgeṇa raktaṃ vahuśo harecca || 49
svedopanāhā mṛdavastu pathyāḥ
pittārbude kāyavirecanāni
vighṛṣya codumbaraśākagojī-
patrairbhṛśaṃ kṣaudrayutaiḥ pralimpet || 50
ślakṣṇīkṛtaiḥ sarjarasapriyaṅgu-
pataṅgalodhrārjunayaṣṭikāhvaiḥ || 51
lepanaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇena saha mūlakabhasmanā
kaphārbudāpahaṃ kuryādgranthyādiṣu viśeṣataḥ || 52
15niṣpāvapiṇyākakulatthakalkai-
rmāṃsapragāḍhairdadhimarditaiśca
lepaṃ vidadhyātkrimayo yathātra
muñcantyapatyānyatha makṣikā vā || 53
alpāvaśiṣṭaṃ krimibhiḥ prajagdhaṃ
likhettato'gniṃ vidadhīta paścāt
yadalpamūlaṃ traputāmrasīsaiḥ
saṃveṣṭya patrairatha vāyasairvā || 54
kṣārāgniśastrāṇyavatārayecca
muhurmuhuḥ prāṇamavekṣyamāṇaḥ
yadṛcchayā copagatāni pākaṃ
pākakrameṇopacaredyathoktam || 55
upodikā rasābhyaktāstatpatrapariveṣṭitāḥ
praṇaśyantyacirānnṛṇāṃ pīḍakārbudajātayaḥ || 56
upodikā kāñjikatakrapiṣṭā
tayopanāho lavaṇena miśraḥ
dṛṣṭo'rbudānāṃ praśamāya kaiści-
ddine dine vā triṣu marmajānām || 57
lepo'rbudajidrambhāmocakabhasmatuṣaśaṅkhacūrṇakṛtaḥ
saraṭarudhirārdrakagandhakayavaviḍaṅganāgarairvātha || 58
snuhīgaṇḍīrikāsvedo nāśayedarbudāni ca
sīsakenātha lavaṇaiḥ piṇḍārakaphalena vā || 59
haridrālodhrapattuṅgagṛhadhūmamanaḥśilāḥ
madhupragāḍho lepo'yaṃ medo'rbudaharaḥ paraḥ || 60
etāmeva kriyāṃ kuryādaśeṣāṃ śarkarārbude || 61
Adhikāra 41
1laṅghanālepanasvedarecanai raktamokṣaṇaiḥ
prāyaḥ śleṣmaharairuṣṇaiḥ ślīpadaṃ samupācaret || 1
dhūstūrairaṇḍanirguṇḍīvarṣābhūśigrusarṣapaiḥ
pralepaḥ ślīpadaṃ hanti cirotthamatidāruṇam || 2
niṣpiṣṭamāranālena rūpikāmūlavalkalam
pralepācchlīpadaṃ hanti baddhamūlamatho dvaḍham || 3
piṇḍārakatarusambhaba-
bandākaśiphā jayati sarpiṣā pītā
ślīpadamugraṃ niyataṃ
baddhvā sūtreṇa jaṅghāyām || 4
hitaścālepane nityaṃ citrako devadāru vā
siddhārthaśigrukalko vā sukhoṣṇo mūtrapeṣitaḥ || 5
snehasvedopanāhāṃśca ślīpade'nilaje bhiṣak
kṛtvā gulphopari śirāṃ vidhyettu caturaṅgule || 6
gulphasyādhaḥ śirāṃ vidhyecchlīpade pittasambhave
pittaghnīṃ ca kriyāṃ kuryātpittārbudavisarpavat || 7
mañjiṣṭhāṃ madhukaṃ rāsnāṃ sahiṃsrāṃ sapunarnavām
piṣṭvāraṇālairlepo'yaṃ pittaślīpadaśāntaye || 8
śirāṃ suviditāṃ vidhyedaṅguṣṭhe śleṣmaślīpade
madhuyuktāni cābhīkṣṇaṃ kaṣāyāṇi pibennaraḥ || 9
pibetsarṣapatailena ślīpadānāṃ nivṛttaye
pūtīkarañjacchadajaṃ rasaṃ vāpi yathābalam || 10
2anenaiva vidhānena putrajīvakajaṃ rasam
kāñjikena pibeccūrṇaṃ mūtrairvā vṛddhadārajam || 11
rajanīṃ guḍasaṃyuktāṃ gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
varṣotthaṃ ślīpadaṃ hanti dadrukuṣṭhaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 12
gandharvatailabhṛṣṭāṃ harītakīṃ gojalena yaḥ pibati
ślīpadabandhanamukto bhavatyasau saptarātreṇa || 13
dhānyāmlaṃ tailasaṃyuktaṃ kaphavātavināśanam
dīpanaṃ cāmadoṣaghnametacchlīpadanāśanam || 14
godhāvatīmūlayuktāṃ khādenmāṣeṇḍarīṃ naraḥ
jayecchlīpadakopotthaṃ jvaraṃ sadyo na saṃśayaḥ || 15
ślīpadaghno raso'bhyāsādguḍūcyāstailasaṃyutaḥ
trikaṭu triphalā cavyaṃ dārvīvaruṇagokṣuram || 16
alambuṣāṃ guḍūcīṃ ca samabhāgāni cūrṇayet
sarveṣāṃ cūrṇamāhṛtya vṛddhadārasya tatsamam || 17
kāñjikena ca tatpeyamakṣamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
jīrṇe cāparihāraṃ syādbhojanaṃ sarvakāmikam || 18
nāśayecchlīpadaṃ sthaulyamāmavātaṃ sudāruṇam
gulmakuṣṭhānilaharaṃ vātaśleṣmajvarāpaham || 19
3pippalītriphalādārunāgaraṃ sapunarnavam
bhāgairdvipalikaireṣāṃ tatsamaṃ vṛddhadārakam || 20
kāñjikena pibeccūrṇaṃ karṣamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
jīrṇe cāparihāraṃ syādbhojanaṃ sarvakāmikam || 21
ślīpadaṃ vātarogāṃśca hanyātplīhānameva ca
agniṃ ca kurute ghoraṃ bhasmakaṃ ca niyacchati || 22
4kṛṣṇācitrakadantīnāṃ karṣamardhapalaṃ param
viṃśatiśca harītakyo guḍasya tu palaṃ dvayam
madhunā modakaṃ khādecchlīpadaṃ hanti dustaram || 23
5surasāṃ devakāṣṭhaṃ ca trikaṭu triphale tathā
lavaṇānyatha sarvāṇi viḍaṅgānyatha citrakam || 24
cavikā pippalīmūlaṃ guggulurhapuṣā vacā
yavāgrajaṃ ca pāṭhā ca śaṭyelā vṛddhadārukam || 25
kalkaiśca kārṣikairebhirghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa dhānyayūṣadraveṇa ca || 26
dadhimaṇḍasamāyuktaṃ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ pṛthak pṛthak
pakvaṃ svādughṛtaṃ kalkātpibetkarṣatrayaṃ haviḥ || 27
ślīpadaṃ kaphavātoktaṃ māṃsaraktāśritaṃ ca yat
medaśritaṃ ca pittotthaṃ hanyādeva na saṃśayaḥ || 28
apacīṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ ca antravṛddhiṃ tathārbudam
nāśayedgrahaṇīdoṣaṃ śvayathuṃ gudajāni ca || 29
paramagnikaraṃ hṛdyaṃ koṣṭhakrimivināśanam
ghṛtaṃ saureśvaraṃ nāma ślīpadaṃ hanti sevitam
jīvakena kṛtaṃ hyetadrogānīkavināśanam || 30
6viḍaṅgamaricārkeṣu nāgare citrake tathā
bhadradārvyelakākhyeṣu sarveṣu lavaṇeṣu ca
tailaṃ pakvaṃ pibedvāpi ślīpadānāṃ nivṛttaye || 31
Adhikāra 42
1jalaukāpātanaṃ śastaṃ sarvasminneva vidradhau
mṛdurvireko laghvannaṃ svedaḥ pittottaraṃ vinā || 1
vātaghnamūlakalkaistu vasātailaghṛtaplutaiḥ
sukhoṣṇo bahalo lepaḥ prayojyo vātavidradhau || 2
svedopanāhāḥ kartavyāḥ śigrumūlasamanvitāḥ
yavagodhūmamudgaiśca siddhāpiṣṭaiḥ pralepayet || 3
vilīyate kṣaṇenaivamapakvaścaiva vidradhiḥ
punarnavādāruviśvadaśamūlābhayāmbhasā || 4
gugguluṃ rubutailaṃ vā pibenmārutavidradhau
paittikaṃ śarkarālājā madhukaiḥ śārivāyutaiḥ || 5
pradihyātkṣīrapiṣṭairvā payasyośīracandanaiḥ
pibedvā triphalākvāthaṃ trivṛtkalkākṣasaṃyutam || 6
pañcavalkalakalkena ghṛtamiśreṇa lepanam
yaṣṭyāhvaśārivādūrvānalamūlaiḥ sacandanaiḥ || 7
kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pralepastu pittavidradhiśāntaye
iṣṭakā sikatālohagośakṛttuṣapāṃśubhiḥ || 8
mūtrapiṣṭaiśca satataṃ svedayet cchleṣmavidradhim
daśamūlakaṣāyeṇa sasnehena rasena vā || 9
śothaṃ vraṇaṃ vā koṣṇena saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet
triphalāśigruvaruṇadaśamūlāmbhasā pibet || 10
gugguluṃ mūtrayuktaṃ vā vidradhau kaphasambhave
pittavidradhivatsarvāṃ kriyāṃ niravaśeṣataḥ || 11
vidradhyoḥ kuśalaḥ kuryādraktāgantunimittayoḥ
śobhāñjanakaniryūho hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyutaḥ || 12
aciraṃ vidradhiṃ hanti prātaḥprātarniṣevitaḥ
śigrumūlaṃ jale dhautaṃ darapiṣṭaṃ pragālayet || 13
tadrasaṃ madhunā pītvā hantyantarvidradhiṃ naraḥ
śvetavarṣābhuvo mūlaṃ mūlaṃ varuṇakasya ca || 14
jalena kvathitaṃ pītamapakvaṃ vidradhiṃ jayet
varuṇādigaṇakvāthamapakve'bhyantarotthite
uṣakādipratīvāpaṃ pibetsaṃśamanāya vai || 15
śamayati pāṭhāmūlaṃ
kṣaudrayutaṃ taṇḍulāmbhasā pītam
antarbhūtaṃ vidradhi-
muddhatamāśveva manujasya || 16
apakve tvetaduddiṣṭaṃ pakve tu vraṇavatkriyā || 17
srute'pyūrdhvamadhaścaiva maireyāmlasurāsavaiḥ
peyo varuṇakādistu madhuśigruraso'thavā || 18
priyaṅgudhātakīlodhraṃ kaṭphalaṃ tiliśatvacam
etaistailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ vidradhau ropaṇaṃ param || 19
Adhikāra 43
1ādau vimlāpanaṃ kuryāddvitīyamavasecanam
tṛtīyamupanāhaṃ ca caturthīṃ pāṭanakriyām || 1
pañcamaṃ śodhanaṃ caiva ṣaṣṭhaṃ ropaṇamiṣyate
ete kramā vraṇasyoktāḥ saptamo vaikṛtāpahaḥ || 2
mātuluṅgāgnimanthau ca bhadradārumahauṣadham
ahiṃsrā caiva rāsnā ca pralepo vātaśothahā || 3
kalkaḥ kāñjikasampiṣṭaḥ snigdhaḥ śākhoṭakatvacaḥ
suparṇa iva nāgānāṃ raktaśothavināśanaḥ || 4
dūrvā ca nalamūlaṃ ca madhukaṃ candanaṃ tathā
śītalāśca gaṇāḥ sarve pralepaḥ pittaśothahā || 5
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaplakṣavetasavalkalaiḥ
sasarpiṣkaiḥ pralepaḥ syācchothanirvāpaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 6
āgantau śoṇitotthe ca eṣa eva kriyākramaḥ
ajagandhāśvagandhā ca kālā saralayā saha || 7
ekeśikājaśṛṅgī ca pralepaḥ śleṣmaśothahā
punarnavāśigrudārudaśamūlamahauṣadhaiḥ || 8
kaphavātakṛte śothe lepaḥ koṣṇo vidhīyate
na rātrau lepanaṃ dadyāddattaṃ ca patitaṃ tathā || 9
na ca paryuṣitaṃ śuṣyamāṇaṃ naivāvadhārayet
śuṣyamāṇamupekṣeta pradehaṃ pīḍanaṃ prati || 10
na cāpi mukhamālimpettena doṣaḥ prasicyate
sthirānmandarujaḥ śothānsnehairvātakaphāpahaiḥ || 11
abhyajya svedayitvā ca veṇunāḍyā tataḥ śanaiḥ
vimlāpanārthaṃ mṛdgīyāttaletāṅguṣṭhakena vā || 12
raktāvasecanaṃ kuryādādāveva vicakṣaṇaḥ
śothe mahati saṃbaddhe vedanāvati ca vraṇe || 13
2yo na yāti śamaṃ lepasvedasekāpatarpaṇaiḥ
so'pi nāśaṃ vrajatyāśu śothaḥ śoṇitamokṣaṇāt || 14
ekataśca kriyāḥ sarvā raktamokṣaṇamekataḥ
raktaṃ hi vyamlatāṃ yāti taccennāsti na cāsti ruk || 15
sa cedevamupākrāntaḥ śotho na praśamaṃ vrajet
tasyopanāhaiḥ pakvasya pāṭanaṃ hitamucyate || 16
tailena sarpiṣā vāpi tābhyāṃ vā śaktupiṇḍikā
sukhoṣṇaḥ śothapākārthamupanāhaḥ praśasyate || 17
satilā sātasībījā dadhyamlāśaktupiṇḍikā
sakiṇvakuṣṭhalavaṇā śastā syādupanāhane || 18
bālavṛddhāsahakṣīṇabhīrūṇāṃ yoṣitāmapi
marmopari ca jāte ca pakve śothe ca dāruṇe
gavāṃ dantaṃ jale ghṛṣṭaṃ nvidumātraṃ pralepayet || 19
atyantakaṭhine cāpi śothe pācanabhedanam
kaṭutailānvitairlepātsarpanirmokabhasmabhiḥ || 20
cayaḥ śāmyati gaṇḍasya prakopaḥ sphuṭati drutam
cirabilvāgnikau dantī citrako hayamārakaḥ || 21
kapotakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ purīṣāṇi ca dāraṇam
kṣāradravyāṇi vā yāni kṣāro vā dāraṇaḥ paraḥ || 22
dravyāṇāṃ picchilānāṃ tu tvaṅmūlāni prapīḍanam
yavagodhūmamāṣāṇāṃ cūrṇāni ca samāsataḥ || 23
tataḥ prakṣālanaṃ kvāthaḥ paṭolīnimbapatrajaḥ
aviśuddhe viśuddhe ca nyagrodhāditvagudbhavaḥ || 24
pañcamūladvayaṃ vāte nyagrodhādiśca paittike
āragvadhādiko yojyaḥ kaphaje sarvakarmasu || 25
3tilakalkaḥ salavaṇo dve haridre trivṛddhṛtam
madhukaṃ nimbapatrāṇi lepaḥ syādvraṇaśodhanaḥ || 26
4nimbapatraṃ tilā dantī trivṛtsaindhavamākṣikam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamano lepaḥ śodhanakesarī || 27
ekaṃ vā śārivāmūlaṃ sarvavraṇaviśodhanam
paṭolaṃ tilayaṣṭyāhvatrivṛddantīniśāhvayam || 28
nimbapatrāṇi cālepaḥ sapaṭuvraṇaśodhanaḥ
triphalākhadiro dārvīnyagrodhādibalā kuśāḥ || 29
nimbakolakapatrāṇi kaṣāyaḥ śodhane hitaḥ
anena pūtimāṃsānāṃ māṃsasthānamarohatām || 30
kalkaḥ saṃropaṇaḥ kāryastilānāṃ madhukānvitaḥ
nimbapatramadhubhyāṃ tu yuktaḥ saṃśodhanaḥ smṛtaḥ || 31
pūrvābhyāṃ sarpiṣā vāpi yuktaścāpyuparohaṇaḥ
nimbapatratilaiḥ kalko madhunā kṣataśodhanaḥ
ropaṇaḥ sarpiṣā yukto yavakalke'pyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 32
nimbapatraghṛtakṣaudradārvīmadhukasaṃyutā
vartistilānāṃ kalko vā śothayedropayedvraṇam || 33
5saptadaladugdhakalkaḥ
śamayati duṣṭavraṇaṃ pralepena
madhuyuktā śarapuṅkhā
sarvabraṇaropaṇī kathitā || 34
mānuṣaśiraḥ kapālaṃ
tadasthi vā lepayeta mūtreṇa
ropaṇamidaṃ kṣatānāṃ
yogaśatairapyasādhyānām || 35
vraṇānviśodhayedvartyā sūkṣmāsyānmarmasandhigān
abhayātrivṛtādantīlāṅgalīmadhusaindhavaiḥ || 36
suṣavīpatrapattūrakarṇamoṭakuṭherakāḥ
pṛthagete pralepena gambhīravraṇaropaṇāḥ || 37
pañcavalkalacūrṇairvā śukticūrṇasamanvitaiḥ
dhātakīcūrṇalodhrairvā tathā rohanti te vraṇāḥ || 38
sadāhā vedanāvanto vraṇā ye mārutottarāḥ
teṣāṃ tilānumāṃścaiva bhṛṣṭānpayasi nirvṛtān || 39
tenaiva payasā piṣṭvā dadyādālepanaṃ bhiṣak
vātādibhūtānsāsrāvāndhūpayedugravedanān || 40
yavājyabhūrjamadanaśrīveṣṭakasurāhvayaiḥ
śrīvāsaguggulvaguruśālaniryāsadhūpitāḥ || 41
kaṭhinatvaṃ vraṇā yānti naśyantyugrāśca vedanāḥ || 42
tilāḥ payaḥ sitā kṣaudraṃ tailaṃ madhukacandanam
lepena śotharugdāharaktaṃ nirvāpayedvraṇān || 43
pittavidradhivīsarpaśamanaṃ lepanādikam
agnidagdhe vraṇe samyakprayuñjīta cikitsakaḥ || 44
mahārāṣṭrijaṭālepo dagdhapiṣṭāvacūrṇitam
jīrṇagehatṛṇāccūrṇaṃ dagdhavraṇahitaṃ matam || 45
6jīvakakalkaṃ paścā-
tsikthakasarjarasamiśritaṃ harati
ghṛtamabhyaṅgātpāvaka-
dagdho duḥkhaṃ kṣaṇārdhena || 46
antardagdhakuṭhārako dahanajaṃ lepānnihanti vraṇa-
maśvatthasyaviśuṣkavalkalakṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ tathā guṇḍanāt
abhyaṅgādvinihantitailamakhilaṃ gaṇḍūpadaiḥ sādhitaṃ
piṣṭvā śālmalitūlakairjalagatā lepāttathā vālukāḥ || 47
sadyaḥ kṣataṃ vraṇaṃ vaidyaḥ saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet
yaṣṭīmadhukakalkena kiñciduṣṇena sarpiṣā || 48
buddhvāgantuvraṇaṃ vaidyo ghṛtaṃ kṣaudrasamanvitam
śītāṃ kriyāṃ prayuñjīta pittaraktoṣmanāśinīm || 49
kāntakramukamekaṃ suślakṣṇaṃ gavyasarpiṣā piṣṭam
śamayati lepānniyataṃ vraṇamāgantūdbhavaṃ na sandehaḥ || 50
apāmārgasya saṃsiktaṃ patrotthena rasena vā
sadyo vraṇeṣu raktaṃ tu pravṛttaṃ paritiṣṭhati || 51
karpūrapūritaṃ baddhaṃ saghṛtaṃ saṃprarohati
sadyaḥ śastrakṣataṃ puṃsāṃ vyathāpākavivarjitam || 52
śarapuṅkhā kākajaṅghā prasūta mahiṣīmalam
lajjā ca sadyaskavraṇaghnaṃ pṛthageva tu || 53
śuno jihvākṛtaścūrṇaḥ sadyaḥ kṣatavirohaṇaḥ
cakratailaṃ kṣate viddhe ropaṇaṃ paramaṃ matam || 54
yavakṣāraṃ bhakṣayitvā piṇḍaṃ dadyādvraṇopari
śṛṃgālakolimūlena naṣṭaśailaṃ viniḥsaret || 55
lāṅgalīmūlalepādvā gavākṣīmūlatastathā
kṣatoṣmaṇo nigrahārthaṃ tatkālaṃ visṛtasya ca || 56
7kaṣāyaśītamadhurāḥ snigdhā lepādayo hitāḥ
āmāśayasthe rudhire vamanaṃ pathyamucyate || 57
pakvāśayasthe deyaṃ ca virecanamasaṃśayaḥ
kvātho vaṃśatvageraṇḍaśvadaṃṣṭrāśmabhidā kṛtaḥ || 58
sahiṅgusaindhavaḥ pītaḥ koṣṭhasthaṃ srāvayedasṛk
yavakolakulatthānāṃ niḥsnehena rasena ca || 59
bhuñjītānnaṃ yavāgūṃ vā pibetsaindhavasaṃyutām
atyarthamasraṃ sravati prāyaśo yatra vīkṣate || 60
tato raktakṣayādvāyau kupite'tirujākare
snehapānaṃ parīṣekaṃ snehalepopanāhanam || 61
snehabastiṃ ca kurvīta vātaghnauṣadhasādhitām
iti sāptāhikaḥ proktaḥ sadyo vraṇahito vidhiḥ || 62
saptāhātparataḥ kuryācchārīravraṇavatkriyām
karañjāriṣṭanirguṇḍīraso hanyādvraṇakrimīn || 63
kalāyavidalīpatraṃ koṣāmrāsthi ca pūraṇāt
surasādirasaiḥ seko lepanaḥ svarasena vā || 64
nimbasampākajātyarkasaptaparṇāśvamārakāḥ
krimighnā mūtrasaṃyuktāḥ sekā lepanadhāvanaiḥ || 65
prakṣālya māṃsapeṣyā vā krimīnapaharedvraṇān
laśunenāthavā dadyāllepanaṃ krimināśanam || 66
8ye kledapākasrutigandhavanto
vraṇā mahāntaḥ sarujaḥ saśothāḥ
prayānti te guggulumiśritena
pītena śāntiṃ triphalārasena || 67
9triphalācūrṇasaṃyukto guggulurvaṭakīkṛtaḥ
niryantraṇo vibandhaghno vraṇaśodhanaropaṇaḥ || 68
amṛtāgugguluḥ śasto hitaṃ tailaṃ ca vajrakam
viḍaṅgatriphalāvyoṣacūrṇaṃ guggulunā samam || 69
sarpiṣā vaṭakīkṛtvā khādedvā hitabhojanaḥ
duṣṭavraṇāpacīmehakuṣṭhanāḍīvraṇāpahaḥ || 70
10amṛtāpaṭolamūla-
triphalātrikaṭukrimighnānām
samabhāgānāṃ cūrṇaṃ
sarvasamo guggulorbhāgaḥ || 71
prativāsaramekaikāṃ
khādedakṣaṇapramāṇām
jetuṃ vraṇānvātāsṛ-
ggulmodaraśvayathurogādīn || 72
11jātīnimbapaṭolapatrakaṭukādārvīniśāśā-
rivāmañjiṣṭhābhayatutthasikthamadhukairnaktāhvabījaiḥ
samaiḥ sarpiḥ siddhamanena sūkṣmavadanā marmāśritāḥ
srāviṇo gambhīrāḥ sarujo vraṇāḥ sagatikāḥ
śuṣyanti rohanti ca || 73
12gaurā haridrā mañjiṣṭhā māṃsī madhukameva ca
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ hrīberaṃ bhadramustaṃ sacandanam || 74
jātīnimbapaṭolaṃ ca karañjaṃ kaṭurohiṇī
madhūcchiṣṭaṃ samadhukaṃ mahāmedā tathaiva ca || 75
pañcavalkalatoyena ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
eṣa gauro mahāvīryaḥ sarvabraṇaviśodhanaḥ || 76
āgantuḥ sahajāścaiva sucirotthāśca ye vraṇāḥ
viṣamāmapi nāḍīṃ ca śodhayecchīghrameva ca || 77
gaurādyaṃ jātikādyaṃ ca tailamevaṃ prasādhyate
tailaṃ sūkṣmānane duṣṭe vraṇe gambhīra eva ca || 78
13naktamālasya patrāṇi varuṇāni phalāni ca
sumanāyāśca patrāṇi paṭolāriṣṭayostathā || 79
dve haridre madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madhukaṃ tiktarohiṇī
mañjiṣṭhā candanośīramutpalaṃ śārive trivṛt
eteṣāṃ kārṣikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 80
duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ tathā nāḍīviśodhanam
sadyaśchinnavraṇānāṃ ca karañjādyamiheṣyate || 81
14prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāmadhukośīrapadmakaiḥ
saharidraiḥ śṛtaṃ sarpiḥ sakṣīraṃ vraṇaropaṇam || 82
15tiktāsikthaniśāyaṣṭīnaktāhvaphalapallavaiḥ
paṭolamālatīnimbapatrairvraṇyaṃ ghṛtaṃ pacet || 83
16sindūrakuṣṭhaviṣahiṅgurasonacitra
bāṇāṅghrilāṅgalikakalkavipakvatailam
prāsādamantrayutaphūtkṛtanunnapheno
duṣṭavraṇapraśamano viparītamallaḥ || 84
khaṅgābhighātagurugaṇḍamahopadaṃśa-
nāḍīvraṇavraṇavicarcikakuṣṭhapāmāḥ
etānnihanti viparītakamallanāma
tailaṃ yatheṣṭaśayanāsanabhojanasya || 85
17kuṭhārakātpalaśataṃ sādhayellalvane'mbhasi
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 86
kalkaiḥ kuṭhārāpāmārgaproṣṭhikāmakṣikāyutaiḥ || 87
18prapauṇḍarīkaṃ madhukaṃ kākolyau dve sacandane
siddhamebhiḥ samaṃ tailaṃ tatparaṃ vraṇaropaṇam || 88
19dūrvāsvarasasiddhaṃ vā tailaṃ kampillakena ca
dārvītvacaśca kalkena pradhānaṃ ropaṇaṃ vraṇe || 89
yenaiva vidhinā tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ tenaiva sādhayet
raktapittottaraṃ jñātvā sarpirevāvapācayet || 90
20mañjiṣṭhāṃ candanaṃ mūrvāṃ piṣṭvā sarpirvipācayet
sarveṣāmagnidagdhānāmetadropaṇamiṣyate || 91
21siddhaṃ kaṣāyakalkābhyāṃ pāṭalyāḥ kaṭutailakam
dagdhavraṇarujāsrāvadāhavisphoṭanāśanam || 92
22candanaṃ ghaṭaśuṅgaṃ ca mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ tathā
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ mūrvā ca pataṅgaṃ dhātakī tathā || 93
ebhistailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīrasamanvitam
agnidagdhavraṇeṣviṣṭaṃ mrakṣaṇādropaṇaṃ param || 94
23manaḥśilāle mañjiṣṭhā salākṣārajanīdvayam
pralepaḥ saghṛtakṣaudrastvagviśuddhikaraḥ paraḥ || 95
ayorajaḥ sakāsīsaṃ triphalākusumāni ca
pralepaḥ kurute kārṣṇyaṃ sarva eva navatvaci || 96
kālīyakalatāmrāsthihemakālārasottamaiḥ
lepaḥ sagomayarasaḥ savarṇakaraṇaḥ paraḥ || 97
catuṣpadāṃ hi tvagromakhuraśṛṅgāsthibhasmanā
tailāktā cūrṇitā bhūmirbhavedromavatī punaḥ
vraṇagranthiṃ granthivacca jayetkṣāreṇa vā bhiṣak || 98
Adhikāra 44
1nāḍīnāṃ gatimanviṣya śastreṇāpāṭya śāstravit
sarvavraṇakramaṃ kuryācchodhanaṃ ropaṇādikam || 1
nāḍīṃ vātakṛtāṃ sādhupāṭitāṃ lepayedbhiṣak
pratyakpuṣpīphalayutaistilaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ pralepayet
paittikīṃ tilamañjiṣṭhānāgadantīniśāyugaiḥ || 2
śleṣmikīṃ tilayaṣṭyāhvanikumbhāriṣṭasaindhavaiḥ
śalyajāṃ tilamadhvājyairlepayecchinnaśodhitām || 3
āragvadhaniśākālācūrṇājyakṣaudrasaṃyutā
mūtravartirvraṇe yojyā śodhanī gatināśinī || 4
ghoṇṭāphalatvaṅmadanātphalāni
pūgasya ca tvak lavaṇaṃ ca mukhyam
snuhyarkadugdhena sahaiṣa kalko
vartīkṛto hantyacireṇa nāḍīm || 5
vartīkṛtaṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktaṃ
nāḍīghnamuktaṃ lavaṇottamaṃ vā
duṣṭavraṇe yadvihitaṃ ca tailaṃ
tatsevyamānaṃ gatimāśu hanti || 6
jātyarkasampākakaraṃ ca dantī-
sindhūtthasauvarcalayāvaśūkaiḥ
vartiḥ kṛtā hantyacireṇa nāḍī
snukkṣīrapiṣṭā saha mākṣikeṇa || 7
māhiṣadadhikodravānna-
miśritaṃ harati ciravirūḍhāṃ ca
bhuktaṃ kaṅgunikāmū-
lacūrṇamatidāruṇāṃ nāḍīm || 8
2kṛśadurbalabhīrūṇāṃ gatirmarmāśritā ca yā
kṣārasūtreṇa tāṃ chindyānna śastreṇa kadācana || 9
eṣaṇyā gatimanviṣya kṣārasūtrānusāriṇīm
sūcīṃ vidadhyādabhyante connāmya cāśu nirharet || 10
sūtrasyāntaṃ samānīya gāḍhaṃ bandhaṃ samācaret
tataḥ kṣīṇabalaṃ vīkṣya sūtramanyatpraveśayet || 11
kṣārāktaṃ matimānvaidyo yāvanna chidyate gatiḥ
bhagandare'pyeṣa vidhiḥ kāryo vaidyena jānatā || 12
arbudādiṣu cotkṣipya mūle sūtraṃ nidhāpayet
sūcībhiryavavakrābhirācitaṃ śvāsamantataḥ || 13
mūle sūtreṇa badhnīyācchinne copacaredvraṇam
3guggulutriphalāvyoṣaiḥ samāṃsairājyayojitaḥ
nāḍīduṣṭavraṇaśūlabhagandaravināśanaḥ || 14
4sarjikāsindhudantyagnirūpikānalanīlikā
svaramañjarībījeṣu tailaṃ gomūtrapācitam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ kaphanāḍīvraṇāpaham || 15
5kumbhīkakharjūrakaritthabilva-
vanaspatīnāṃ tu śalāṭuvarge
kṛtvā kaṣāyaṃ vipacettu taila-
māvāpya mustaṃ saralaṃ priyaṅgum || 16
saugandhikāmocarasāhipuṣpa-
lodhrāṇi datvā khalu dhātakīṃ ca
etena śalyaprabhavā hi nāḍī
rohedvraṇo vai mukhamāśu caiva || 17
6bhallātakārkamaricairlavaṇottamena
siddhaṃ viḍaṅgarajanīdvayacitrakaiśca
syānmārkavasya ca rasena nihanti tailaṃ
nāḍīṃ kaphānilakṛtāmapacīṃ vraṇāṃśca || 18
7samūlapatrāṃ nirguṇḍīṃ pīḍayitvā rasena tu
tena siddhaṃ samaṃ tailaṃ nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāpaham || 19
hitaṃ pāmāpacīnāṃ tu pānābhyañjananāvanaiḥ
vividheṣu ca sphoṭeṣu tathā sarvavraṇeṣu ca || 20
8haṃsapādāriṣṭapatraṃ jātīpatraṃ tato rasaiḥ
tatkalkairvipacettailaṃ nāḍīvraṇavirohaṇam || 21
Adhikāra 45
1gudasya śvayathuṃ jñātvā viśoṣya śodhayettataḥ
raktāvasecanaṃ kāryaṃ yathā pākaṃ na gacchati || 1
vaṭapatreṣṭakāśuṇṭhīguḍūcyaḥ sapunarnavāḥ
supiṣṭāḥ pīḍakārambhe lepaḥ śasto bhagandare || 2
pīḍakānāmapakvānāmapatarpaṇapūrvakam
karma kuryādvirekāntaṃ bhinnānāṃ vakṣyate kriyām || 3
eṣaṇīpāṭanaṃ kṣāravahnidāhādikaṃ kramam
vidhāya vraṇavatkāryaṃ yathādoṣaṃ yathākramam || 4
trivṛttilānāgadantīmañjiṣṭhā saha sarpiṣā
utsādanaṃ bhavedetatsaindhavakṣaudrasaṃyutam || 5
rasāñjanaṃ haridre dve mañjiṣṭhānimbapallavāḥ
trivṛttejovatīdantīkalko nāḍīvraṇāpahaḥ || 6
kuṣṭhaṃ trivṛttilādantīmāgadhyaḥ saindhavaṃ madhu
rajanītriphalātutthaṃ hitaṃ vraṇaviśodhanam || 7
snuhyarkadugdhadārvībhirvartiṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ
bhagandaragatiṃ jñātvā pūrayettāṃ prayatnataḥ || 8
eṣā sarvaśarīrasthāṃ nāḍīṃ hanyānna saṃśayaḥ || 9
tilābhayālodhramariṣṭapatraṃ
niśāvacālodhramagāradhūmaḥ
bhagandare nāḍyupadaṃśayośca
duṣṭavraṇe śodhanaropaṇo'yam || 10
2kharāsrapakvabhūrogacūrṇalepo bhagandaram
hanti dantyagnyativiṣālepastadvacchuno'sthi vā || 11
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ biḍālāsthipralepanam
bhagandaraṃ nihantyāśu duṣṭavraṇaharaṃ param || 12
triphalāpurakṛṣṇānāṃ tripañcaikāṃśayojitā
guḍikā śothagulmārśobhagandaravatāṃ hitā || 13
3trikaṭutriphalāmustaviḍaṅgāmṛtacitrakam
śaṭhyelāpippalīmūlaṃ hapuṣā suradāru ca || 14
tumburu puṣkaraṃ cavyaṃ viśālā rajanīdvayam
biḍaṃ sauvarcalaṃ kṣārau saindhavaṃ gajapippalī || 15
yāvantyetāni cūrṇāni tāvaddviguṇagugguluḥ
kolapramāṇāṃ guḍikāṃ bhakṣayenmadhunā saha || 16
kāsaśvāsaṃ tathā śothamarśāṃsi sabhagandaram
hṛcchūlaṃ pārśvaśūlaṃ ca kukṣibastigude rujam || 17
aśmarīṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca atravṛddhiṃ tathā krimīn
cirajvaropasṛṣṭānāṃ kṣayopahatacetasām || 18
ānāhaṃ ca tathonmādaṃ kuṣṭhāni codarāṇi ca
nāḍīduṣṭavraṇānsarvānpramehaṃ ślīpadaṃ tathā
saptaviṃśatiko hyeṣa sarvaroganiṣūdanaḥ || 19
4jambūkasya ca māṃsāni bhakṣayedvyañjanādibhiḥ
ajīrṇavarjī māsena mucyate nā bhagandarāt || 20
pañcatiktaṃ ghṛtaṃ śastaṃ pañcatiktaśca gugguluḥ
nyagrodhādigaṇo yastu hitaḥ śodhanaropaṇaḥ || 21
tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā tatpakvaṃ bhagandaravināśanam
citrakārkau trivṛtpāṭhe malapūhayamārakau || 22
sudhāṃ vacāṃ lāṅgalikīṃ haritālaṃ suvarcikām
jyotiṣmatīṃ ca saṃyojya tailaṃ dhīro vipācayet || 23
etadviṣyandanaṃ nāma tailaṃ dadyādbhagandare
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ tathā || 24
5karavīraniśādantīlāṅgalīlavaṇāgnibhiḥ
mātuluṅgārkavatsāhvaiḥ pacettailaṃ bhagandare || 25
6niśārkakṣīrasindhvagnipurāśvahanavatsakaḥ
siddhamabhyañjane tailaṃ bhagandaravināśanam || 26
vyāmāmaṃ maithunaṃ yuddhaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ gurūṇi ca
saṃvatsaraṃ parihareduparūḍhavraṇo naraḥ || 27
Adhikāra 46
1snigdhasvinnaśarīrasya dhvajamadhye śirāvyadhaḥ
jalaukaḥpātanaṃ vā syādūrdhvādhaḥ śodhanaṃ tathā
pāko rakṣyaḥ prayatnena śiśnakṣayakaro hi saḥ || 1
paṭolanimbatriphalāḍugūcī-
kvāthaṃ pibedvā khadirāśanābhyām
sagugguluṃ vā triphalāyutaṃ vā
sarvopadaṃśāpaharaḥ prayogaḥ || 2
2prapauṇḍarīkaṃ madhukaṃ rāsnā kuṣṭhaṃ punarnavā
śaralāgurubhadrāhvairvātike lepasecane || 3
gairikāñjanamañjiṣṭhāmadhukośīrapadmakaiḥ
sacandanotpalaiḥ snigdhaiḥ paittikaṃ saṃpralepayet || 4
nimbārjunāśvatthakadambaśāla-
jambūvaṭodumbaravetaseṣu
prakṣālanālepaghṛtāni kuryā-
ccūrṇāni pittāsrabhavopadaṃśe || 5
triphalāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa bhṛṅgarājarasena vā
vraṇaprakṣālanaṃ kuryādupadaṃśapraśāntaye || 6
dahetkaṭāhe triphalāṃ samāṃśāṃ madhusaṃyutām
upadaṃśe pralepo'yaṃ sadyo ropayati vraṇam || 7
rasāñjanaṃ śirīṣeṇa pathyayā vā samanvitam
sakṣaudraṃ vā pralepena sarvaliṅgagadāpaham || 8
babboladalacūrṇena dāḍimatvagbhavena vā
guṇḍanaṃ nrasthicūrṇena upadaṃśaharaṃ param || 9
lepaḥ pūgaphalenāśvamāramūlena vā tathā
sevennityaṃ yavānnaṃ ca pānīyaṃ kaupameva ca || 10
jayājātyaśvamārārkasampākānāṃ dalaiḥ pṛthak
kṛtaṃ prakṣālane kvāthaṃ meḍhrapāke prayojayet || 11
3bhūnimbanimbatriphalāpaṭola-
karañjajātīkhadirāśanānām
satoyakalkairghṛtamāśu pakvaṃ
sarvopadaṃśāpaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 12
4karañjanimbārjunaśālajambū-
vaṭādibhiḥ kalkakaṣāyasiddham
sarpirnihanyādupadaṃśadoṣaṃ
sadāhapākaṃ srutirāgayuktam || 13
5agāradhūmarajanīsurākiṭṭaṃ ca taistribhiḥ
bhāgottaraiḥ pacettailaṃ kaṇḍūśotharujāpaham
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ tathā || 14
arśasāṃ chinnadagdhānāṃ kriyā kāryopadaṃśavat || 15
Adhikāra 47
1hitaṃ ca sarpiṣaḥ pānaṃ pathyaṃ cāpi virecanam
hitaḥ śoṇitamokṣaśca yaccāpi laghubhojanam || 1
sarṣapīṃ likhitāṃ sūkṣmaiḥ kaṣāyairavacūrṇayet
tairevābhyañjanaṃ tailaṃ sādhayedvraṇaropaṇam || 2
kriyeyamadhimanthe'pi raktaṃ srāvyaṃ tathobhayoḥ
aṣṭhīlāyāṃ hṛte rakte śleṣmagranthivadācaret || 3
kumbhikāyāṃ haredraktaṃ pakvāyāṃ śodhite vraṇe
tindukatriphalālodhrairlaipastailaṃ ca ropaṇam || 4
alajyāṃ hṛtaraktāyāmayameva kriyākramaḥ
svedayedgrathitaṃ snigdhaṃ nāḍīsvedena buddhimān || 5
sukhoṣṇairupanāhaiśca sasnigdhairupanāhayet
uttamākhyāṃ tu piḍakāṃ saṃcchidya baḍiśoddhṛtām || 6
kalkaiścūrṇaiḥ kaṣāyāṇāṃ kṣaudrayuktairupācaret
kramaḥ pittavisarpoktaḥ puṣkarīmūḍhayorhitaḥ || 7
tvakpāke sparśahānyāṃ ca secayenmṛditaṃ punaḥ
balātailena koṣṇena madhuraiścopanāhayet || 8
rasakriyā vidhātavyā likhite śatapolake
pṛthakparṇyādisiddhaṃ ca tailaṃ deyamanantaram || 9
pittavidradhivaccāpi kriyā śoṇitaje'rbude
kaṣāyakalkasarpīṃṣi tailaṃ cūrṇaṃ rasakriyām || 10
śodhane ropaṇe caiva vīkṣya vīkṣyāvatārayet
arbudaṃ māṃsapākaṃ ca vidradhiṃ tilakālakam
pratyākhyāya prakurvīta bhiṣakteṣāṃ pratikriyām || 11
Adhikāra 48
1ādau bhagnaṃ viditvā tu secayecchītalāmbunā
paṅkenālepanaṃ kāryaṃ bandhanaṃ ca kuśānvitam
suśrutoktaṃ ca bhagneṣu vīkṣya bandhādimācaret || 1
avanāmitamunnahyedunnataṃ cāvanāmayet
ācchedatikṣiptamadhogataṃ copari vartayet || 2
ālepanārthaṃ mañjiṣṭhāmadhukaṃ cāmlapeṣitam
śatadhautaghṛtonmiśraṃ śālipiṣṭaṃ ca lepanam || 3
saptarātrātsaptarātrātsaumyeṣvṛtuṣu mokṣaṇam
kartavyaṃ syāttrirātrācca tathāgneyeṣu jānatā || 4
kāle ca sāmaśītoṣṇe pañcarātrādvimokṣayet
nyagrodhādikaṣāyaṃ ca saśītaṃ pariṣecane || 5
pañcamūlīvipakvaṃ tu kṣīraṃ dadyātsavedane
sukhoṣṇamavatāryaṃ vā cakratailaṃ vijānatā || 6
māṃsaṃ māṃsarasaḥ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ yūṣaḥ satīlajaḥ
bṛṃhaṇaṃ cānnapānaṃ ca deyaṃ bhagnaṃ vijānatā || 7
gṛṣṭikṣīraṃ sasarpiṣkaṃ madhurauṣadhasādhitam
śītalaṃ drākṣayā yuktaṃ prātarbhagnaḥ pibennaraḥ || 8
saghṛtenāsthisaṃhāraṃ lākṣāgodhūmamarjunam
sandhiyukte'sthibhagne ca pibetkṣīreṇa mānavaḥ || 9
rasonamadhulākṣājyasitākalkaṃ samaśnatām
chinnabhinnacyutāsthīnāṃ saṃdhānamacirādbhavet || 10
pītavarāṭikācūrṇaṃ dviguñjaṃ vā triguñjakam
apakvakṣīrapītaṃ syādasthibhagnaprarohaṇam || 11
2kṣīraṃ salākṣāmadhukaṃ sasarpiḥ
syājjīvanīyaṃ ca sukhāvahaṃ ca
bhagnaḥ pibettvakpayasārjunasya
godhūmacūrṇaṃ saghṛtena vātha || 12
lākṣāsthisaṃhṛtkakubhāśvagandhā-
ścūrṇīkṛtā nāgavalāpuraśca
saṃbhagnayuktāsthirujaṃ nihanyā-
daṅgāni kuryātkuliśopamāni || 13
atrānyato'pi dṛṣṭatvāttulyaścūrṇena gugguluḥ || 14
3ābhāphalatrikairvyoṣaiḥ sarvairebhiḥ samīkṛtaiḥ
tulyo guggulurāyojyo bhagnasandhiprasādhakaḥ || 15
4savraṇasya tu bhagnasya vraṇaṃ sarpirmadhūttamaiḥ
pratisārya kaṣāyaiśca śeṣaṃ bhagnavadācaret || 16
bhagnaṃ naiti yathā pākaṃ prayateta tathā bhiṣak
vātavyādhivinirdiṣṭānsnehānatra prayojayet || 17
rātrau rātrau tilānkṛṣṇānvāsayedasthire jale
divādivaivaṃ saṃśoṣya kṣīreṇa paribhāvayet || 18
tṛtīyaṃ saptarātraṃ ca bhāvayenmadhukāmbunā
tataḥ kṣīraṃ punaḥ pītānsuśuṣkāṃścūrṇayedbhiṣak || 19
kākolyādiśvadaṃṣṭrāṃ ca mañjiṣṭhāṃ śārivāṃ tathā
kuṣṭhaṃ sarjarasaṃ māṃsīṃ suradāru sacandanam || 20
śatapuṣpāṃ ca saṃcūrṇya tilacūrṇena yojayet
pīḍanārthaṃ ca kartavyaṃ sarvagandhaiḥ śṛtaṃ payaḥ || 21
caturguṇena payasā tattailaṃ vipacetpunaḥ
elāmaṃśumatīṃ patraṃ jīrakaṃ tagaraṃ tathā || 22
lodhraṃ prapauṇḍarīkaṃ ca tathā kālānuśārivām
śaileyakaṃ kṣīraśuklāmanantāṃ samadhūlikām || 23
piṣṭvā śṛṅgāṭakaṃ caiva prāguktānyauṣadhāni ca
ebhistadvipacettailaṃ śāstravinmṛdunāgninā || 24
etatailaṃ sadā pathyaṃ bhagnānāṃ sarvakarmasu
ākṣepake pakṣāghāte cāṅgaśoṣe tathārdite || 25
manyāstambhe śiroroge karṇaśūle hanugrahe
bādhirye timire caiva ye ca strīṣu kṣayaṃ gatāḥ || 26
pathyaṃ pāne tathābhyaṅge nasye vastiṣu yojayet
grīvāskandhorasāṃ vṛddhiranenaivopajāyate || 27
mukhaṃ ca padmapratimaṃ svasvagandhisamīraṇam
gandhatailamidaṃ nāmnā sarvavātavikāranut || 28
rājārhametatkartavyaṃ rājñāmeva vicakṣaṇaiḥ
tilacūrṇacaturthāṃśaṃ militaṃ cūrṇamiṣyate || 29
lavaṇaṃ kaṭukaṃ kṣāramamlaṃ maithunamātapam
vyāyāmaṃ ca na seveta bhagno rūkṣānnameva ca || 30
Adhikāra 49
1vātottareṣu sarpirvamanaṃ śleṣmottareṣu kuṣṭheṣu
pittottareṣu mokṣo raktasya virecanaṃ cāgryam || 1
proñchanamalpe kuṣṭhe mahati ca śastaṃ śirāvyadhanam
bahudoṣaḥ saṃśodhyaḥ kuṣṭhī bahuśo'nurakṣatā prāṇān || 2
vacāvāsāpaṭolānāṃ nimbasya phalinītvacaḥ
kaṣāyo madhunā pīto vāntikṛnmadanānvitaḥ || 3
2virecanaṃ tu kartavyaṃ trivṛddantīphalatrikaiḥ || 4
ye lepāḥ kuṣṭhānāṃ
prayujyante nirgatāsradoṣāṇām
saṃśodhitāśayānāṃ
sadyaḥ siddhirbhavati teṣām || 5
manaḥśilāle maricāni taila-
mārkaṃ payaḥ kuṣṭhaharaḥ pradehaḥ
karañjabījaiḍagajaḥ sakuṣṭho
gomūtrapiṣṭaśca varaḥ pradehaḥ || 6
patrāṇi piṣṭvā caturaṅgulasya
takreṇa parṇānyatha kākamācyāḥ
tailāktagātrasya narasya kuṣṭhā-
nyudvartayedaśvahanacchadaiśca || 7
āragvadhaḥ saiḍagajaḥ karañjo
vāsā guḍūcī madanaṃ haridre
śryāhvaḥ surāhvaḥ khadiro dhavaśca
nimbo viḍaṅgaṃ karavīrakaśca || 8
granthiśca bhaurjo laśunaḥ śirīṣaḥ
salomaśo guggulukṛṣṇagandhe
phaṇijjako vatsakasaptaparṇī-
pīlūni kuṣṭaṃ sumanaḥpravālaḥ || 9
vacāhareṇutrivṛtāni kumbhī
bhallātakaṃ gairikamañjanaṃ ca
manaḥśilāle gṛhadhūma elā-
kāsīsalodhrārjunamustasarjāḥ || 10
ityardharūpairvihitāḥ ṣaḍete
gopittapītāḥ punareva piṣṭāḥ
siddhāḥ paraṃ sarṣapatailayuktā-
ścūrṇapradehā bhiṣajā prayojyāḥ || 11
kuṣṭhāni kṛcchrāṇi navaṃ kilāsaṃ
surendraluptaṃ kiṭibhaṃ sadadrum
bhagandarārśāṃsyapacīṃ sapāmāṃ
hanyuḥ prayuktā acirānnarāṇām || 12
3manaḥśilātvakkuṭajātsakuṣṭā-
tsalomaśaḥ saiḍagajaḥ karañjaḥ
granthiśca bhaurjaḥ karavīramūlaṃ
cūrṇāni sādhyāni tuṣodakena || 13
palāśanirdāharasena vāpi
karṣoddhṛtānyāḍhakasaṃmitena
dārvīpralepaṃ pravadanti lepa-
metatparaṃ kuṣṭhavināśanāya || 14
kuṣṭaṃ haridre sarasaṃ paṭolaṃ
nimbāśvagandhe suradāruśigru
sasarṣapaṃ tumburudhānyavanya-
caṇḍāśca dūrvāśca samāni kuryāt || 15
taistakrayuktaiḥ prathamaṃ śarīraṃ
tailāktamudvartayituṃ yateta
tathāsya kaṇḍūḥ piḍakāḥ sakoṭhāḥ
kuṣṭhāni śothāśca śamaṃ prayānti || 16
4dhātryakṣapathyākrimiśatruvahni-
bhallātakāvalgujalauhabhṛṅgaiḥ
bhāgābhivṛddhaistilatailamiśraiḥ
sarvāṇi kuṣṭhāni nihanti lepaḥ || 17
viḍaṅgasaindhavaśivāśaśirekhāsarṣapakarañjarajanībhiḥ
gojalapiṣṭo lepaḥ kuṣṭhadaro divasanāthasamaḥ || 18
viḍaṅgaiḍagajākuṣṭhaniśāsindhūtthasarṣapaiḥ
dhānyāmlapiṣṭairlepo'yaṃ dadrūkuṣṭharujāpahaḥ || 19
dūrvābhayāsaindhavacakramarda-
kuṭherakāḥ kāñjikatakrapiṣṭāḥ
tribhiḥ pralepairatibaddhamūlaṃ
dadruṃ ca kuṣṭhaṃ ca nivārayanti || 20
tulyo rasaḥ sālatarostuṣeṇa
sacakramardo'pyabhayāvimiśraḥ
pānīyabhaktena tadāmbupiṣṭo
lepaḥ kṛto dadrugajendrasiṃhaḥ || 21
5prapunnāḍasya bījāni dhātrīsarjarasaḥ snuhī
sauvīrapiṣṭaṃ dadrūṇāmetadudvartanaṃ param || 22
cakramardakabījāni karañjaṃ ca samāṃśakam
stokaṃ sudarśanāmūlaṃ dadrukuṣṭhavināśanam || 23
lepanādbhakṣaṇāccaiva tṛṇakaṃ dadrunāśanam
yūthīpunnāgamūlaṃ ca lepātkāñjikapeṣitam || 24
kāsamardakamūlaṃ ca sauvīreṇa ca peṣitam
dadrukiṭibhakuṣṭhāni jayedetatpralepanāt || 25
śikharirasena supiṣṭaṃ mūlakabījaṃ pralepataḥ sidhmaḥ
kṣāreṇa vā kadalyā rajanīmiśreṇa nāśayati || 26
gandhapāṣāṇacūrṇena yavakṣāreṇa peṣitam
sidhmanāśaṃ vrajatyāśu kaṭutailayutena vā || 27
kāsamardakabījāni mūlakānāṃ tathaiva ca
gandhapāṣāṇamiśrāṇi sidhmānāṃ paramauṣadham || 28
dhātrīrasaḥ sarjarasaḥ sapākyaḥ
sauvīrapiṣṭaśca tathā yutaśca
bhavanti sidhmāni yathā na bhūya-
stathaivamudvartanakaṃ karoti || 29
6kuṣṭaṃ mūlakabījaṃ priyaṅgavaḥ sarṣapāstathā rajanī
etatkeśarakuṣṭhaṃ nihanti bahuvārṣikaṃ sidhma || 30
nīlakuruṇṭakapatraṃ svarasenālipya gātramatibahuśaḥ
limpenmūlakabījaistakreṇaitaddhi sidhmanāśāya || 31
cakrāhvayaṃ snuhīkṣīrabhāvitaṃ mūtrasaṃyutam
ravitaptaṃ hi kiñcittu lepanātkiṭibhāpaham || 32
āragvadhasya patrāṇi āranālena peṣayet
dadrūkiṭibhakuṣṭhāni hanti sidhmānameva ca || 33
bījāni vā mūlakasarṣapāṇāṃ
lākṣārajanyau prapuṇāḍavījam
śrīveṣṭakavyoṣaviḍaṅgakuṣṭhaṃ
piṣṭvā ca mūtreṇa tu lepanaṃ syāt || 34
dadrūṇi sidhmaṃ kiṭibhāni pāmāṃ
kāpālakuṣṭhaṃ viṣamaṃ ca hanyāt || 35
7eḍagajakuṣṭhasaindhavasauvīrasarṣapaiḥ krimighnaiśca
krimisidhmadadrumaṇḍalakuṣṭhānāṃ nāśano lepaḥ || 36
snukkāṇḍe sarṣapātkalkaḥ kukūlānalapācitaḥ
lepādvicarcikāṃ hanti rāgavega iva trapām || 37
snukkāṇḍe śuṣire dagdhvā gṛhadhūmaṃ sasaindhavam
antardhūmaṃ tailayuktaṃ lepāddhanti vicarcikām || 38
eḍagajātilasarṣapakuṣṭhamāgadhikālavaṇatrayamastu
pūtikṛtaṃ divasatrayametaddhanti vicarcikādadrusakuṣṭham || 39
unmattakasya bījena māṇakakṣāravāriṇā
kaṭutailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ śīghraṃ hanyādvipādikām || 40
nārikelodake nyastastaṇḍulaḥ pūtitāṃ gataḥ
lepādvipādikāṃ hanti cirakālānubandhinīm || 41
sarjarasaḥ sindhusambhava-
guḍamadhumahiṣākṣagairikaṃ saghṛtam
sikthakametatpakvaṃ
pādasphuṭanāpahaṃ siddham || 42
avalgujaṃ kāsamardaṃ cakramardaṃ niśāyutam
māṇimanthena tulyāṃśaṃ mastukāñjikapeṣitam || 43
kacchūṃ kaṇḍūṃ jayatyugrāṃ siddha eṣa prayogarāṭ
komalaṃ siṃhāsyadalaṃ
saniśaṃ surabhijalena saṃpiṣṭam || 44
divasatrayeṇa niyataṃ
kṣapayati kacchūṃ vilepanataḥ
8haridrākalkasaṃyuktaṃ gomūtrasya paladvayam || 45
pibennaraḥ kāmacārī kacchupāmāvināśanam
śothapāṇḍvāmayaharī gulmamehakaphāpahā
kacchupāmāharī caiva pathyāgomūtrasādhitā || 46
pibati sakaṭutailaṃ gaṇḍapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ
ravikiraṇasutaptaṃ pāmalo yaḥ palārdham
tridinatadanusiktaḥ kṣīrabhojī ca śīghraṃ
bhavati kanakadīptyā kāmayukto manuṣyaḥ || 47
9niśāsudhāragvadhakākamācī-
patraiḥ sadārvīprapuṇāḍabījaiḥ
takreṇa piṣṭaiḥ kaṭutailamiśraiḥ
pāmādiṣūdvartanametadiṣṭam || 48
sindūramaricacūrṇaṃ mahiṣīnavanītasaṃyutaṃ bahuśaḥ
lepādvinihanti pāmāṃ tailaṃ karavīrasiddhaṃ vā || 49
māṃsīcandanasampākakarañjāriṣṭasarṣapam
śaṭīkuṭajadārvyabdaṃ hanti kuṣṭhamayaṃ gaṇaḥ || 50
bhallātakadvīpisudhārkamūlaṃ
guñjāphalaṃ tryūṣaṇaśaṅkhacūrṇam
tutthaṃ sakuṣṭhaṃ lavaṇāni pañca
kṣāradvayaṃ lāṅgalikāṃ ca paktvā || 51
snuhārkadugdhe ghanamāyasasthaṃ
śalākayā taṃ vidadhīta lepam
kuṣṭhe kilāse tilakālake ca
aśeṣadurnāmasu carmakīle || 52
viṣavaruṇaharidrācitrakāgāradhūma-
manalamaricadūrvāḥ kṣīramarkasnuhābhyām
dahati patitamātrātkuṣṭhajātīraśeṣāḥ
kuliśamiva saroṣācchakrahastādvimuktam || 53
10śaśāṅkalekhā saviḍaṅgasārā
sapippalīkā sahutāśamūlā
sāyomalā sāmalakā satailā
sarvāṇi kuṣṭhānyupahanti līḍhā || 54
tīvreṇa kuṣṭhena parītadeho
yaḥ somarājīṃ niyamena khādet
saṃvatsaraṃ kṛṣṇatiladvitīyāṃ
sa somarājīṃ vapuṣātiśete || 55
gharmasevī kaduṣṇena vāriṇā vāgujīṃ pibet
kṣīrabhojī trisaptāhātkuṣṭharogādvimucyate || 56
ekastilasya bhāgau dvau somarājyāstathaiva ca
bhakṣyamāṇamidaṃ prātarguhyadadruvināśanam || 57
avalgujādbījakarṣaṃ pītvā koṣṇena vāriṇā
bhojanaṃ sarpiṣā kāryaṃ sarvakuṣṭhapraṇāśanam || 58
triphalāpaṭolarajanīmañjiṣṭhārohiṇīvacānimbaiḥ
eṣa kaṣāyo'bhyasto nihanti kaphapittajaṃ kuṣṭham || 59
11chinnāyāḥ svaraso vāpi sevyamāno yathāvalam
jīrṇe ghṛtena bhuñjīta svalpaṃ yūṣodakena vā
atipūtiśarīro'pi divyarūpo bhavennaraḥ || 60
paṭolakhadirāriṣṭatriphalākṛṣṇavetrajam
tiktāśanaḥ pibetkvāthaṃ kuṣṭhī kuṣṭhaṃ vyapohati || 61
tilājyatriphalākṣaudravyoṣabhallātaśarkarāḥ
vṛṣyāḥ sapta samo medhyaḥ kuṣṭhahā kāmacāriṇaḥ || 62
viḍaṅgatriphalākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ līḍhaṃ samākṣikam
hanti kuṣṭhaṃ krimīnmehānnāḍīvraṇabhagandarān || 63
indrāśanaṃ samādāya praśaste'hani coddhṛtam
taccūrṇaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ lihetkṣīraghṛtāśanaḥ || 64
hatvā ca sarvakuṣṭhāni jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam || 65
yaḥ khādedabhayāriṣṭaṃ maricāmalakāni vā
sa jayetsarvakuṣṭhāni māsādūrdhvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 66
dahyamānācyutaḥ kumbhe mūlage khadirādrasaḥ
sājyadhātrīrasakṣaudro hanyātkuṣṭhaṃ rasāyanam || 67
12vayasyeḍagajākuṣṭhakṛṣṇābhirguḍikā kṛtā
bāstamūtreṇa saṃpiṣṭā lepācchvitravināśinī || 68
pūtīkārkasnuṅnarendradrumāṇāṃ
mūtre piṣṭāḥ pallavāḥ saumanāśca
lepācchvitraṃ ghnanti dadruvraṇāṃśca
kuṣṭhānyarśāṃsi bhagnanāḍīvraṇāṃśca || 69
gajacitravyāghracarmamasītailavilepanāt
śvitraṃ nāśaṃ vrajetkiṃ vā pūtikīṭavilepanāt || 70
kuḍavo'valgujabījāddharitālacaturthabhāgasaṃmiśraḥ
mūtreṇa gavāṃ piṣṭaḥ savarṇakaraṇaḥ paraḥ śvitre || 71
13dhātrīkhadirayoḥ kvāthaṃ pītvā valgujasaṃyutam
śaṅkhendudhavalaṃ śvitraṃ tūrṇaṃ hanti na saṃśayaḥ || 72
kṣareṣu dagdhe gajalaṇḍaje ca
gajasya mūtreṇa bahu srute ca
droṇapramāṇaṃ daśabhāgayukte
dattvā pacedbījamavalgujasya || 73
etadyadā cikkaṇatāmupaiti
tadā susiddhāṃ guḍikāṃ prayuñjayāt
viśvaṃ vilimpedatha tena ghṛṣṭaṃ
tadā vrajatyāśu savarṇabhāvam || 74
śvetajayantīmūlaṃ piṣṭaṃ pītaṃ ca gavyapayasaiva
śvitraṃ nihanti niyataṃ ravivāre vaidyanāthasyājñā || 75
14puṣpakāle tu puṣpāṇi phalakāle phalāni ca
saṃcūrṇya picumardasya tvaṅmūlāni dalāni ca || 76
dviraṃśāni samāhṛtya bhāgikāni prakalpayet
triphalātryūṣaṇaṃ brāhmī śvadaṃṣṭrāruṣkarāgnikāḥ || 77
viḍaṅgasāravārāhīlohacūrṇāmṛtāḥ samāḥ
haridrādvayāvalgujavyādhighātāḥ saśarkarāḥ || 78
kuṣṭendrayavapāṭhāśca kṛtvā cūrṇaṃ susaṃyutam
khadirāsananimbānāṃ ghanakvāthena bhāvayet || 79
saptadhā pañcanimbaṃ tu mārkavasvarasena tu
snigdhaśuddhatanurdhīmānyojayecca śubhe dine || 80
madhunā tiktahaviṣā khadirāśanavāriṇā
lehyamuṣṇāmbunā vāpi kolavṛddhyā palaṃ pibet || 81
jīrṇe ca bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ snigdhaṃ laghu hitaṃ ca yat
vicarcikodumbarapuṇḍarīka-
kapāladadrūkiṭibhālasādīn || 82
śatāruvisphoṭavisarpapāmāṃ
kaphaprakopaṃ trividhaṃ kilāsam || 83
bhagandaraślīpadavātaraktaṃ
jātāndhyanāḍīvraṇaśīrṣarogān
sarvānpramehānpradarāṃśca sarvān
daṃṣṭrāviṣaṃ mūlaviṣaṃ nihanti || 84
sthūlodaraḥ siṃhakṛśodaraśca
suśliṣṭasandhirmadhunopayogāt
samopayogādapi ye daśanti
sarpādayo yānti vināśamāśu || 85
jīvecciraṃ vyādhijarāvimuktaḥ
śubherataścandrasamānakāntiḥ || 86
15citrakaṃ triphalā vyoṣamajājīṃ kāravīṃ vacām
saindhavātiviṣe kuṣṭhaṃ cavyailāyāvaśūkajam || 87
viḍaṅgānyajamodāṃ ca mustānyamaradāru ca
yāvantyetāni sarvāṇi tāvanmātraṃ tu guggulum || 88
saṃkṣudya sarpiṣā sārdhaṃ guḍikāṃ kārayedbhiṣak
prātarbhojanakāle ca bhakṣayettu yathābalam || 89
hantyaṣṭādaśakuṣṭhāni krimīnduṣṭavraṇāni ca
grahaṇyarśovikārāṃśca mukhāmayagalagrahān || 90
gṛdhrasīmatha bhagnaṃ ca gulmaṃ cāśu niyacchati
vyādhīnkoṣṭhagatāṃścānyāñjayedviṣṇurivāsurān || 91
16pañca bhallātakāṃśchitvā sādhayedvidhivajjale
kaṣāyaṃ taṃ pibecchītaṃ ghṛtenāktauṣṭhatālukaḥ || 92
pañcavṛddhyā pibedyāvatsaptatiṃ hrāsayettataḥ
jīrṇe'dyādodanaṃ śītaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīropasaṃhitam || 93
etadrasāyanaṃ medhyaṃ valīpalitanāśanam
kuṣṭhārśaḥkrimidoṣaghnaṃ duṣṭaśukravināśanam || 94
tailaṃ bhallātakānāṃ ca pibenmāsaṃ yathābalam
sarvopatāpanirmukto jīvedvarṣaśataṃ dṛḍham || 95
pralepodvartanasnānapānabhojanakarmaṇi
śīlitaṃ khādiraṃ vāri sarvatvagdoṣanāśanam || 96
nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ dārvīṃ
durālabhāṃ tiktakarohiṇīṃ triphalām
kuryādardhapalāṃśānparpaṭakaṃ trāyamāṇāṃ ca || 97
salilāḍhakasiddhānāṃ rase'ṣṭabhāgasthite kṣipetpūte
candanakirātatiktakamāgadhikātrāyamāṇāṃ ca || 98
mustāvatsakabījaṃ kalkīkṛtamardhakarṣānbhāgān
navasarpiṣaśca ṣaṭpalametatsiddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ peyam || 99
kuṣṭhajvaragulmārśograhaṇīpāṇḍvāmayaśvayathūn
hanti pāmāvisarpapīḍakānkaṇḍumadagandhasiddham || 100
17nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ vyāghrīṃ ca guḍūcīṃ vāsakaṃ tathā
kuryāddaśapālānbhāgānekaikasya sukuṭṭitān || 101
jaladroṇe vipaktavyaṃ yāvatpādāvaśeṣitam
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacettena triphalāgarbhasaṃyutam || 102
pañcatiktamidaṃ khyātaṃ sarpiḥ kuṣṭhavināśanam
aśītiṃ vātajānrogāṃścatvāriṃśacca paittikān || 103
viṃśatiṃ ślaiṣmikāṃścaiva pānādevāpakarṣati
duṣṭavraṇakrimīnarśaḥ pañcakāsāṃśca nāśayet || 104
18triphalādviniśāvāsāyāsapārpaṭakūlakān
trāyantīkaṭukānimbānpratyekaṃ dvipalonmitān || 105
kvāthayitvā jaladroṇe pādaśeṣeṇa tena tu
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacetkalkaiḥ pippalīghanacandanaiḥ || 106
trāyantīśakrabhūnimbaistatpītaṃ tiktakaṃ ghṛtam
hanti kuṣṭhajvarārśāṃsi śvayathuṃ grahaṇīgadam
pāṇḍurogaṃ visarpaṃ ca klībānāmapi śasyate || 107
19saptacchadaṃ prativiṣāṃ sampākaṃ tiktarohiṇīṃ pāṭhām
mustamuśīraṃ triphalāṃ paṭolapicumardaparpaṭakam || 108
dhanvāyāsaṃ sacandanamupakulye padmakaṃ rajanyau ca
ṣaḍgranthāṃ saviśālāṃ śatāvarīśārive cobhe || 109
vatsakabījaṃ vāsāṃ mūrvāmamṛtāṃ kirātatiktaṃ ca
kalkānkuryānmatimānyaṣṭyāhvaṃ trāyamāṇāṃ ca || 110
kalkastu caturbhāgo
jalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ raso'mṛtaphalānām
dviguṇo ghṛtācca deya-
statsarpiḥ pāyayetsiddham || 111
kuṣṭhāni raktapittaṃ
prabalānyarśāṃsi raktavāhīni
visarpamamlapittaṃ
vātāsṛkpāṇḍurogaṃ ca || 112
visphoṭakānsapāmā-
nunmādakānkāmalāṃ jvaraṃ pāṇḍum
hṛdrogagulmapiḍakā-
masṛgdaraṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ ca || 113
hanyādetatsadyaḥ
pītaṃ kāle yathābalaṃ sarpiḥ
yogaśatairaṣyajitā-
nmahāvikārānmahātiktakam || 114
20khadirasya tulāḥ pañca śiṃśapāśanayostule
tulārdhāḥ sarva evaite karañjāriṣṭavetasāḥ || 115
parpaṭaḥ kuṭajaścaiva vṛṣyaḥ krimiharastathā
haridre kṛtamālaśca guḍūcī triphalā trivṛt || 116
saptaparṇastu saṃkṣuṇṇo daśadroṇe ca vāriṇaḥ
aṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu kaṣāyamavatārayet || 117
dhātrīrasaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ sarpiṣaścāḍhakaṃ pacet
mahātiktakakalkaiśca yathoktaiḥ palasaṃmitaiḥ || 118
nihanti sarvakuṣṭhāni pānābhyaṅgānniṣevaṇāt
mahākhadiramityetatparaṃ kuṣṭhavināśanam || 119
21nimbāmṛtāvṛṣapaṭolanidigdhikānāṃ
bhāgānpṛthagdaśapalānvipaceddhaṭe'pām
aṣṭāṃśaśeṣitajalena suniḥsṛtena
prasthaṃ ghṛtasya vipacetpicubhāgakalkaiḥ || 120
pāṭhāviḍaṅgasuradārugajopakulyā-
dvikṣāranāgaraniśāmiśicavyakuṣṭhaiḥ
tejovatīmaricavatsakadīpyakāgni
rohiṇyuruṣkaravacākaṇamūlayuktaiḥ || 121
mañjiṣṭhayātiviṣayāvarayā yamānyā
saṃśuddhaguggulupalairapi pañcasaṃkhyaiḥ
tatsevitaṃ viṣamatipravalaṃ samīraṃ
sandhyasthimajjagatamapyatha kuṣṭhamīdṛk || 122
nāḍīvraṇārbudabhagandaragaṇḍamālāṃ
jatrūrdhvasarvagatagulmagudotthamehān
yakṣmāruciśvasanapīnasakāsaśoṣa-
hṛtpāṇḍurogagalavidradhivātaraktam || 123
22vāsāguḍūcītriphalāpaṭola-
karañjanimbāśanakṛṣṇavetram
tatkvāthakalkena ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ
tadvajrakaṃ kuṣṭhaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 124
viśīrṇakarṇāṅgulihastapādaḥ
krimyardito bhinnagalo'pi martyaḥ
paurāṇikīṃ kāntimavāpya jīve-
davyāhato varṣaśataṃ ca kuṣṭhī || 125
23āragvadhaṃ dhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ haritālaṃ manaḥśilām
rajanīdvayasaṃyuktaṃ pacettailaṃ vidhānavit
etenābhyañjayecchvitrī kṣipraṃ śvitraṃ vinaśyati || 126
24mañjiṣṭhāruṅniśācakramardāragvadhapallavaiḥ
tṛṇakasvarase siddhaṃ tailaṃ kuṣṭhaharaṃ kaṭu || 127
25haridrātriphalādāruhayamārakacitrakam || 128
saptacchadaśca nimbatvakkarañjo bālakaṃ nakhī
kuṣṭhameḍagajāvījaṃ lāṅgalīgaṇikārikā || 129
jātīpatraṃ ca dārvī ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilāḥ
kaliṅgātilapatraṃ ca arkakṣīraṃ ca gugguluḥ || 130
guḍatvaṅmaricaṃ caiva kuṅkumaṃ granthiparṇakam
sarjaparṇāśakhadiraviḍaṅgaṃ pippalīṃ vacām || 131
ghanareṇvamṛtāyaṣṭikeśaraṃ dhyāmakaṃ viṣam
viśvakaṭphalamañjiṣṭhāvolastumbīphalaṃ tathā || 132
snuhīsampākayoḥ patraṃ vāgujīvījamāṃsike
elājyotiṣmatīmūlaṃ śirīṣo gomayādrasaḥ || 133
candane kuṣṭhanirguṇḍī viśālā mallikādvayam
vāsāśvagandhā brāhmī ca śryāhvaṃ campakakaṭphalam || 134
etaiḥ kalkaiḥ pacettailaṃ tṛṇakasvarasadravam
sarvatvagdoṣaharaṇaṃ mahātṛṇakasaṃjñitam || 135
26saptaparṇakarañjārkamālatīkaravīrajam
mūlaṃ snuhāśirīṣābhyāṃ citrakāsphotayorapi || 136
karañjabījaṃ triphalāṃ trikaṭuṃ rajanīdvayam
siddhārthakaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca prapunnāḍatilaiḥ saha || 137
mūtrapiṣṭaiḥ pacettailamebhiḥ kuṣṭhavināśanam
abhyaṅgādvajrakaṃ nāma nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāpaham || 138
27maricālaśilāhvārkapayośvārijaṭātrivṛt
śakṛdrasaviśālāruṅniśāyugdārucandanaiḥ || 139
kaṭutailātpacetprasthaṃ dvyakṣairviṣapalānvitaiḥ
sagomūtraṃ tadabhyaṅgāddadruśvitravināśanam
sarveṣvapi ca kuṣṭheṣu tailametatpraśasyate || 140
28maricaṃ trivṛtādantīkṣīramārkaṃ śakṛdrasaḥ
devadāru haridre dve māṃsī kuṣṭhaṃ sacandanam || 141
viśālā karavīraṃ ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilā
citrako lāṅgalākhyā ca viḍaṅgaṃ cakramardakam || 142
śirīṣaṃ kuṭajo nimbaṃ saptaparṇasnuhāmṛtāḥ
sampāko naktamālo'bdaḥ khadiraṃ pippalī vacā || 143
jyotiṣmatī ca palikā viṣasya dvipalaṃ bhavet
āḍhakaṃ kaṭutailasya gomūtraṃ tu caturguṇam || 144
mṛtpātre lauhapātre vā śanairmṛdvagninā pacet
paktvā tailavaraṃ hyetanmrakṣayetkauṣṭhikānvraṇān || 145
pāmāvicarcikādadrukaṇḍūvisphoṭakāni ca
valayaḥ palitaṃ chāyānīlīvyaṅgastathaiva ca
abhyaṅgena praṇaśyanti saukumāryaṃ ca jāyate || 146
prathame vayasi strīṇāṃ yāsāṃ nasyaṃ tu dīyate
parāmapi jarāṃ prāpya na stanā yānti namratām || 147
balīvardasturaṅgo vā gajo vā vāyupīḍitaḥ
ebhirabhyañjanairgāḍhaṃ bhavenmārutavikramaḥ || 148
29naktamālaṃ haridre dve arkastagarameva ca
karavīraṃ vacākuṣṭhamāsphotā raktacandanam || 149
mālatī saptaparṇaṃ ca mañjiṣṭhā sindhuvārikā
eṣāmardhapalānbhāgānviṣasyāpi palaṃ tathā || 150
caturguṇe gavāṃ mūtre tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
śvitravisphoṭakiṭibhakīṭalūtāvicarcikāḥ || 151
kaṇḍūkacchuvikārāśca ye vraṇā viṣadūṣitāḥ
viṣatailamidaṃ nāmnā sarvavraṇaviśodhanam || 152
30śvetakaravīraraso gomūtraṃ citrakaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca
kuṣṭheṣu tailayogaḥ siddho'yaṃ saṃmato bhiṣajām || 153
31śvetakaravīramūlaṃ viṣāṃśasādhitaṃ gavāṃ mūtre
carmadalapāmāsidhmavisphoṭakrimikiṭibhajittailam || 154
32sindūrārdhapalaṃ piṣṭvā jīrakasya palaṃ tathā
kaṭutailaṃ pacenmānīṃ sadyaḥ pāmāharaṃparam || 155
33sindūraṃ candanaṃ māṃsī viḍaṅgaṃ rajanīdvayam
priyaṅgu padmakaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ mañjiṣṭhāṃ khadiraṃ vacām || 156
jātyarkatrivṛtānimbakarañjaviṣameva ca
kṛṣṇavetrakalodhraṃ ca prapunnāḍaṃ ca saṃharet || 157
ślakṣṇapiṣṭāni sarvāṇi yojayettailamātrayā
abhyaṅgena prayuñjīta sarvakuṣṭhavināśanam || 158
pāmāvicarcikākaṇḍūvisarpādivināśanam
raktapittotthitānhanti rogānevaṃvidhānbahūn || 159
34mañjiṣṭhātriphalālākṣāniśāśilālagandhakaiḥ
cūrṇitaistailamādityapākaṃ pāmāharaṃ param || 160
35svarasena ca dūrvāyāḥ pacettailaṃ caturguṇam
kacchuvicarcikāpāmā abhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 161
36arkapatrarase pakvaṃ kaṭutailaṃ niśāyutam
manaḥśilāyutaṃ vāpi pāmākacchvādināśanam || 162
37gaṇḍīrikācitrakamārkavārka-
kuṣṭhadrumatvaglavaṇaiḥ samūtraiḥ
tailaṃ pacenmaṇḍaladadrukuṣṭha-
duṣṭavraṇārukkiṭibhāpahāri || 163
38citrakasyātha nirguṇḍyā hayamārasya mūlataḥ
nāḍī ca bījādviṣataḥ kāñjipiṣṭaṃ palaṃpalam || 164
karañjatailāṣṭapalaṃ kāñjikasya palaṃ punaḥ
miśritaṃ sūryasantaptaṃ tailaṃ kuṣṭhavraṇāsrajit || 165
39somarājī haridre dve sarṣapāragvadhaṃ gadam
karañjaiḍagajābījaṃ garbhaṃ datvā vipācayet || 166
tailaṃ sarṣapasambhūtaṃ nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāpaham
anenāśu praśāmyanti kuṣṭhānyaṣṭādaśaiva tu || 167
nīlikāpiḍakāvyaṅgaṃ gambhīraṃ vātaśoṇitam
kaṇḍūkṛcchrapraśamanaṃ kacchupāmāvināśanam || 168
40pakṣātpakṣācchardanānyabhyupeyā-
nmāsānmāsātsraṃsanaṃ cāpyadhastāt
tryahāttryahānnastataścāvapīḍā-
nmāseṣvasṛṅmokṣayetṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu || 169
yoṣinmāṃsasurātyāgaḥ śālimudgayavādayaḥ
purāṇāstiktaśākaṃ ca jāṅgalaṃ kuṣṭhināṃ hitam || 170
Adhikāra 50
1abhyaṅgaḥ kaṭutailena sekaścoṣṇāmbubhistataḥ
udardhe vamanaṃ kāryaṃ paṭolāriṣṭavāriṇā || 1
triphalāpurakṛṣṇābhirvirekaścātra śasyate
triphalāṃ kṣaudrasahitāṃ pibedvā navakārṣikām
visarpoktamamṛtādi bhiṣagatrāpi yojayet || 2
sitāṃ madhukasaṃyuktāṃ guḍamāmalakaiḥ saha
saguḍaṃ dīṣyakaṃ yastu khādetpathyānnabhuṅnaraḥ || 3
tasya naśyati saptāhādudardhaḥ sarvadehajaḥ
siddhārtharajanīkalkaiḥ prapunnāḍatilaiḥ saha || 4
kaṭutailena saṃmiśrametadudvartanaṃ hitam
dūrvāniśāyuto lepaḥ kacchupāmāvināśanaḥ || 5
krimidadruharaścaiva śītapittaharaḥ paraḥ
agnimanthabhavaṃ mūlaṃ piṣṭaṃ pītaṃ ca sarpiṣā || 6
śītapittodardhakoṭhānsaptāhādeva nāśayet
kuṣṭhoktaṃ ca kramaṃ kuryādamlapittaghnameva ca || 7
udardhoktāṃ kriyāṃ cāpi koṭharoge samāsataḥ
sarpiḥ pītvā mahātiktaṃ kāryaṃ śoṇitamokṣaṇam || 8
2nimbasya patrāṇi sadā ghṛtena
dhātrīvimiśrāṇyathavopayuñjyāt
visphoṭakoṭhakṣataśītapittaṃ
kaṇḍvasrapittaṃ sahasā ca jahyāt || 9
kṣārasindhūtthatailaiśca gātrābhyaṅgaṃ prayojayet
gambhārikāphalaṃ pakvaṃ śuṣkamutsveditaṃ punaḥ || 10
kṣīreṇa śītapittaghnaṃ khāditaṃ pathyasevinā
tailodvartanayogena yojya elādiko gaṇaḥ || 11
śuṣkamūlakayūpeṇa kaulatthena rasena vā
bhojanaṃ sarvadā kāryaṃ lāvatittirijena vā || 12
śītalānyannapānāni buddhvā doṣagatiṃ bhiṣak
uṣṇāni vā yathākālaṃ śītapitte prayojayet || 13
Adhikāra 51
1vāntiṃ kṛtvāmlāpitte tu virekaṃ mṛdu kārayet
samyagvāntaviriktasya susnigdhasyānuvāsanam || 1
āsthāpanaṃ cirodbhūte deyaṃ doṣādyapekṣayā
kriyā śuddhasya śamanī hyanubandhavyapekṣayā || 2
doṣasaṃsargaje kāryā bheṣajāhārakalpanā
ūrdhvagaṃ vamanairdhīmānadhogaṃ recanairharet
tiktabhūyiṣṭhamāhāraṃ pānaṃ vāpi prakalpayet || 3
yavagodhūmavikṛtīṃstīkṣṇasaṃskāravarjitāḥ
yathāsvaṃ lājaśaktūnvā sitāmadhuyutānpibet || 4
nistuṣayavavṛṣadhātrī
kvāthastrisugandhimadhuyutaḥ pītaḥ
apanayati cāmlapittaṃ
yadi bhuṅkte mudgayūṣeṇa || 5
kaphapittavamīkaṇḍūjvaravisphoṭadāhahā
pācano dīpanaḥ kvāthaḥ śṛṅkaverapaṭolayoḥ || 6
paṭolaṃ nāgaraṃ dhānyaṃ kvāthayitvā jalaṃ pibet
kaṇḍupāmārtiśūlaghnaṃ kaphapittāgnimāndyajit || 7
paṭolaviśvāmṛtarohiṇīkṛtaṃ
jalaṃ pibetpittakaphocchraye tu
śūlabhramārocakavahnimāndya-
dāhajvaracchardinivāraṇaṃ tat || 8
yavakṛṣṇāpaṭolānāṃ kvāthaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ pibet
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca aruciṃ ca vamiṃ tathā || 9
2vāsāmṛtāparpaṭakanimbabhūnimbamārkavaiḥ
triphalākulakaiḥ kvāthaḥ sakṣaudraścāmlanāśanaḥ || 10
phalatrikaṃ paṭolaṃ ca tiktākvāthaḥ sitāyutaḥ
pītaḥ klītakamadhvākto jvaracchardyamlapittajit || 11
pathyābhṛṅgarajaścūrṇaṃ yuktaṃ jīrṇaguḍena tu
jayedamlapittajanyāṃ chardimannavidāhajām || 12
3vāsānimbapaṭola-
triphalāśanayāsayojito jayati
adhikakaphāmlapittaṃ
prayojito gugguluḥ krameṇa || 13
chinnākhadirayaṣṭyāhvadārvyambho vā madhudravam
sadrākṣāmabhayāṃ khādetsakṣaudrāṃ saguḍāṃ ca tām || 14
kaṭukā sitāvalehyā paṭolaviśvaṃ ca kṣaudrasaṃyuktam
raktasrutau ca yuktyā vā khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍakaṃ śreṣṭham || 15
paṭoladhanyākamahauṣadhābdaiḥ
kṛtaḥ kaṣāyo vinihanti śīghram
mandānalaṃ pittabalāsadāha-
cchardijvarāmānilaśūlarogān || 16
chinnodbhavānimbapaṭolapatraṃ
palatrikaṃ sukvathitaṃ suśītam
kṣaudrānvitaṃ pittamanekarūpaṃ
sudāruṇaṃ hanti hi cāmlapittam || 17
4paṭolatriphalānimbaśṛtaṃ madhuyutaṃ pibet
pittaśleṣmajvaracchardidāhaśūlopaśāntaye || 18
siṃhāsyāmṛtabhaṇḍākī kvāthaṃ pītvā samākṣikam
amlapittaṃ jayejjantuḥ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ vamim || 19
vāsāghṛtaṃ tiktaghṛtaṃ pippalīghṛtameva ca
amlapitte prayoktavyaṃ guḍakūṣmāṇḍakaṃ tathā || 20
paktiśūlāpahā yogāstathā khaṇḍāmalakyapi
pippalīmadhusaṃyuktā cāmlapittavināśinī || 21
jambīrasvarasaḥ pītaḥ sāyaṃ hantyamlapittakam || 22
guḍapippalipathyābhistulyābhirmodakaḥ kṛtaḥ
pittaśleṣmāpahaḥ prokto mandamagniṃ ca dīpayet || 23
hiṅgu ca katakaphalāni
ciñcātvaco ghṛtaṃ ca puṭadagdham
śamayati tadamlapitta-
mamlabhujo yadi yathottaraṃ dviguṇam || 24
kāntāpātre varākalko vyuṣito'bhyāsayogataḥ
sitākṣaudrasamāyuktaḥ kaphapittaharaḥ smṛtaḥ || 25
5ekoṃ'śaḥ pañcanimbānāṃ dviguṇo vṛddhadārakaḥ
śakturdaśaguṇo deyaḥ śarkarāmadhurīkṛtaḥ || 26
śītena vāriṇā pītaḥ śūlaṃ pittakaphotthitam
nihanti cūrṇaṃ sakṣaudramamlapittaṃ sudāruṇam || 27
6āśubhaktodakaiḥ piṣṭamabhrakaṃ tatra saṃsthitam || 28
kandamāṇāsthisaṃhārakhaṇḍakarṇarasairatha
taṇḍulīyaṃ ca śāliṃ ca kālamāriṣajena ca || 29
vṛścīrabṛhatībhṛṅgalakṣaṇākeśarājajaiḥ
peṣaṇaṃ bhāvanaṃ kuryātpuṭaṃ cānekaśo bhiṣak || 30
yāvanniścandrakaṃ tatsyācchuddhirevaṃ vihāyasaḥ
svarṇamākṣikaśāliṃ ca dhmātaṃ nirvāpitaṃ jale || 31
traiphale'tha vicūrṇyaivaṃ lauhaṃ kāntādikaṃ punaḥ
bṛhatpatrakarīkarṇatriphalāvṛddhadārajaiḥ || 32
māṇakandāsthisaṃhāraśṛṅgaverabhavai rasaiḥ
daśamūlīmuṇḍitikātālamūlīsamudbhavaiḥ || 33
puṭitaṃ sādhuyatnena śuddhimevamayo vrajet
vaśiraṃ śvetavāṭyālaṃ madhuparṇī mayūrakam || 34
taṇḍulīyaṃ ca varṣāhvaṃ datvādhaścordhvameva ca
pākyaṃ sajīrṇamaṇḍūraṃ gomūtreṇa dinatrayam || 35
antarbāṣpamadagdhaṃ ca tathā sthāpyaṃ dinatrayam
vicūrṇitaṃ śuddhiriyaṃ lohakiṭṭasya darśitā || 36
jayantyā vardhamānasya ārdrakasya rasena tu
vāyasyāścānupūrvyaivaṃ mardanaṃ rasaśodhanam || 37
gandhakaṃ navanītākhyaṃ kṣudritaṃ lauhabhājane
tridhā caṇḍātape śuṣkaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasāplutam || 38
tato vahnau dravībhūtaṃ tvaritaṃ vastragālitam
yatnādbhṛṅgarase kṣiptaṃ punaḥ śuṣkaṃ viśudhyati || 39
7gaganāddvipalaṃ cūrṇaṃ lauhasya palamātrakam
lauhakiṭṭapalārdhaṃ ca sarvamekatra saṃsthitam || 40
maṇḍūkaparṇīvaśiratālamūlīrasaiḥ punaḥ
varībhṛṅgakeśarājakālamāriṣajairatha || 41
triphalābhadramustābhiḥ sthālīpākādvicūrṇitam
rasagandhakayoḥ karṣau pratyekaṃ grāhyamekataḥ || 42
tanmardanācchilākhalve yatnataḥ kajjalīkṛtam
vacā cavyaṃ yamānī ca jīvakeśarapuṣpikā || 43
vyoṣaṃ mustaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca granthikaṃ kharamañjarī
trivṛtā citrako dantī sūryāvarto'sitastathā || 44
bhṛṅgamāṇakakandaśca khaṇḍakarṇaka eva ca
daṇḍotpalākeśarājakālāvakaḍako'pi ca || 45
eṣāmardhapalaṃ grāhyaṃ paṭaghṛṣṭaṃ sucūrṇitam
pratyekaṃ triphalāyāśca palārdhaṃ palameva ca || 46
etatsarvaṃ samāloḍya lohapātre tu bhāvayet
ātape daṇḍasaṃghṛṣṭamārdrakasya rasaistridhā || 47
tadrasena śilāpiṣṭaṃ guḍikāṃ kārayedbhiṣak
badarāsthinibhāṃ śuṣkāṃ suniguptāṃ nidhāpayet || 48
tatprātarbhojanādau tu sevitaṃ guḍikātrayam
amlodakānupānaṃ ca hitaṃ madhuravarjitam || 49
dugdhaṃ ca nārikelaṃ ca varjanīyaṃ viśeṣataḥ
bhojyaṃ yatheṣṭamiṣṭaṃ ca vāri bhaktāmlakāñjikam || 50
hantyamlapittaṃ vividhaṃ śūlaṃ ca pariṇāmajam
pāṇḍurogaṃ ca gulmaṃ ca śothodaragudāmayān || 51
yakṣmāṇaṃ pañcakāsāṃśca mandāgnitvamarocakam
plīhānaṃ śvāsamānāhamāmavātaṃ sudāruṇam
guḍī kṣudhāvatī seyaṃ vikhyātā roganāśinī || 52
8piṣṭvājājīṃ sadhanyākaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
kaphapittāruciharaṃ mandānalavamiṃ jayet || 53
9paṭolaśuṇṭhyoḥ kalkābhyāṃ kevalaṃ kulakena vā
ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipaktavyaṃ kaphapittaharaṃ param || 54
10pippalīkvāthakalkena ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ madhuplutam
pibettatprātarutthāya amlapittanivṛttaye || 55
11drākṣāmṛtāśakrapaṭolapatraiḥ
sośīradhātrīghanacandanaiśca
trāyantikāpadmakirātadhānyaiḥ
kalkaiḥ pacetsarpirupetamebhiḥ || 56
yuñjīta mātrāṃ saha bhojanena
sarvartupāne'pi bhiṣagvidadhyāt
balāsapittaṃ grahaṇīṃ pravṛddhāṃ
kāsāgnisādaṃ jvaramamlapittam
sarvaṃ nihanyāddhṛtametadāśu
samyakprayuktaṃ hyamṛtopamaṃ ca || 57
12śatāvarīmūlakalkaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ payaḥsamam
pacenmṛdvagninā samyak kṣīraṃ datvā caturguṇam || 58
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca vātapittodbhavāngadān
raktapittaṃ tṛṣāṃ mūrcchaṃ śvāsaṃ santāpameva ca || 59
Adhikāra 52
1virekavamanālepasecanāsṛgvimokṣaṇaiḥ
upācaredyathādoṣaṃ visarpānavidāhibhiḥ || 1
paṭolapicumardābhyāṃ pippalyā madanena ca
visarpe vamanaṃ śastaṃ tathaivendrayavaiḥ saha || 2
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ sarpistrivṛtayā saha
prayoktavyaṃ virekārthaṃ visarpajvaraśāntaye || 3
rasamāmalakānāṃ vā ghṛtamiśraṃ pradāpayet
tṛṇavarjaṃ prayoktavyaṃ pañcamūlacatuṣṭayam
pradehasekasarpirbhirvisarpe vātasambhave || 4
2kuṣṭaṃ śatāhvāsuradārumustā-
vārāhikustumburukṛṣṇagandhāḥ
vāterkavaṃśārtagalāśca yojyāḥ
sekeṣu lepeṣu tathā ghṛteṣu || 5
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāpadmakośīracandanaiḥ
sayaṣṭīndīvaraiḥ pitte kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pralepayet || 6
kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakapadmagundrāḥ
saśaivalāḥ sotpalakardamāśca
vastrāntarāḥ pittakṛte visarpe
lepā vidheyāḥ saghṛtāḥ suśītāḥ || 7
pradehāḥ pariṣekāśca śasyante pañcavalkalaiḥ
padmakośīramadhukacandanairvā praśasyate || 8
pitte tu padminīpaṅkaṃ piṣṭaṃ vā śaṅkhaśaivalam
gundrāmūlaṃ tu śuktirvā gairikaṃ vā ghṛtānvitam || 9
nyagrodhapādā gundrā ca kadalīgarbha eva ca
visagranthikalepaḥ syācchatadhautaghṛtāplutaḥ || 10
hareṇavo masūrāśca mudgāścaiva saśālayaḥ
pṛthakpṛthakpradehāḥ syuḥ sarvairvā sarpiṣā saha || 11
drākṣāragvadhakāśmaryatriphalairaṇḍapīlubhiḥ
trivṛddharītakībhiśca visarpe śodhanaṃ hitam || 12
3gāyatrīsaptaparṇābdavāsāragvadhadārubhiḥ
kuṭannaṭairbhavellepo visarpe śleṣmasambhave || 13
ajāśvagandhā śaralātha kālā
saikeśikā vāpyathavājaśṛṅgī
gomūtrapiṣṭo vihitaḥ pralepo
hanyādvisarpaṃ kaphajaṃ suśīghram || 14
madanaṃ madhukaṃ nimbaṃ vatsakasya phalāni ca
vamanaṃ ca vidhātavyaṃ visarpe kaphasambhave || 15
triphalā padmakośīrasamaṅgākaravīrakam
phalamūlamanantā ca lepaḥ śleṣmavisarpahā || 16
āragvadhasya patrāṇi tvacaḥ śleṣmātakodbhavāḥ
śirīṣapuṣpakāmācī hitā lepāvacūrṇanaiḥ || 17
4mustāriṣṭapaṭolānāṃ kvāthaḥ sarvavisarpanut
dhātrīpaṭolamudgānāmathavā ghṛtasaṃplutaḥ || 18
amṛtaviṣapaṭolaṃ nimbakalkairupetaṃ
triphalakhadirasāraṃ vyādhighātaṃ ca tulyam
kvathitamidamaśeṣaṃ guggulorbhāgayuktaṃ
jayati viṣavisarpānkuṣṭhamaṣṭādaśākhyam || 19
5amṛtavṛṣapaṭolaṃ mustakaṃ saptaparṇaṃ
khadiramasitavetraṃ nimbapatraṃ haridre
vividhaviṣavisarpānkuṣṭhavisphoṭakaṇḍū-
rapanayati masūrīṃ śītapittaṃ jvaraṃ ca || 20
paṭolāmṛtabhūnimbavāsakāriṣṭaparpaṭaiḥ
khadirābdayutaiḥ kvātho visphoṭārtijvarāpahaḥ || 21
6paṭolatriphalāriṣṭaguḍūcīmustacandanaiḥ
samūrvā rohiṇī pāṭhā rajanī sadurālabhā || 22
kaṣāyaṃ pāyayedetacchleṣmapittajvarāpaham
kaṇḍūtvagdoṣavisphoṭaviṣavīsarpanāśanam || 23
bhūnimbavāsākaṭukāpaṭola-
phalatrikācandananimbasiddhaḥ
visarpadāhajvaravastraśoṣa-
visphoṭatṛṣṇāvaminutkaṣāyaḥ || 24
sakaphe pittayukte tu triphalāṃ yojayetpuraiḥ || 25
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ paṭolaṃ kaṭukāṃ tathā
soṣṇaṃ guggulusaṃmiśraṃ pibedvā khadirāṣṭakam || 26
kuṇḍalīpicumardāmbu khadirendrayavāmbu vā
visphoṭaṃ nāśayatyāśu vāyurjaladharāniva || 27
candanaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca taṇḍulīyakaśārive
śirīṣavalkalaṃ jātīlepaḥ syāddāhanāśanaḥ || 28
śukatarunatamāṃsī rajanī padmā ca tulyāni
piṣṭāni śītatoyena lepaḥ syātsarvavisphoṭe || 29
śirīṣamūlamañjiṣṭhācavyāmalakayaṣṭikāḥ
sajātīpallavakṣaudrā visphoṭe kavalagrahāḥ || 30
śirīṣodumbarau jambu sekālepanayorhitāḥ
śleṣmātakatvaco vāpi pralepāścyotane hitāḥ || 31
śirīṣayaṣṭīnatacandanaila-
māṃsīharidrādvayakuṣṭabālaiḥ
lepo daśāṅgaḥ saghṛtaḥ pradiṣṭo
visarpakaṇḍūjvaraśothahārī || 32
śirīṣośīranāgāhvahiṃsrābhirlepanāddrutam
visarpaviṣavisphoṭāḥ praśāmyanti na saṃśayaḥ || 33
7vṛṣakhadirapaṭolapatranimba-
tvagamṛtāmalakīkaṣāyakalkaiḥ
ghṛtamabhinavametadāśu pakvaṃ
jayati visarpagadānsakuṣṭhagulmān || 34
8paṭolasaptacchadanimbavāsā
phalatrikaṃ chinnaruhāvipakvam
tatpañcatiktaṃ ghṛtamāśu hanti
tridoṣavisphoṭavisarpakaṇḍūḥ || 35
9padmakaṃ madhukaṃ lodhraṃ nāgapuṣpasya keśaram
dve haridre viḍaṅgāni sūkṣmailā tagaraṃ tathā || 36
kuṣṭhaṃ lākṣāpatrakaṃ ca sikthakaṃ tutthameva ca
bahuvāraḥ śirīṣaśca kapitthaphalameva ca || 37
toyenāloḍya tatsarvaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
yāṃśca rogānnihanyādvai tānnibodha mahāmune || 38
sarpakīṭākhudaṣṭeṣu lūtāmūtrakṛteṣu ca
vividheṣu sphoṭakeṣu tathā kuṣṭhavisarpiṣu || 39
nāḍīṣu gaṇḍamālāsu prabhinnāsu viśeṣataḥ
agastyavihitaṃ dhanyaṃ padmakaṃ tu mahāghṛtam || 40
10rogastu snāyukākhyo yaḥ kriyā tatra visarpavat
gavyaṃ sarpistryahaṃ pītvā nirguṇḍīsvarasaṃ tryaham
pibetsnāyukamatyugraṃ hantyavaśyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 41
śobhāñjanamūladalaiḥ
kāñjikapiṣṭaiḥ salavaṇairlepaḥ
hanti snāyukarogaṃ
yadvā mocakatvaco lepaḥ || 42
Adhikāra 53
1sarvāsāṃ vamanaṃ pathyaṃ paṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
kaṣāyaiśca vacāvatsayaṣṭyāhvaphalakalkitaiḥ || 1
sakṣaudraṃ pāyayedbrāhmyā rasaṃ vā hailamocikam
vāntasya recanaṃ deyaṃ śamanaṃ cābale nare || 2
suṣavīpatraniryāsaṃ haridrācūrṇasaṃyutam
romāntījvaravisphoṭamasūrīśāntaye pibet || 3
ubhābhyāṃ hṛtadoṣasya viśuṣyanti masūrikāḥ
nirvikārāścālpapūyāḥ pacyante cālpavedanāḥ || 4
kaṇṭākumbhāḍumūlaṃ kvathanavidhikṛtaṃ
hiṅgumāṣaikayuktaṃ
pītaṃ bījaṃ jayāyāḥ saghṛtamuṣitavāḥ
pītamaṅghriḥ sikaṭyā
māghāmūlaṃ śiphā vā damanakusumajā
soṣaṇā vātha pūtiḥ
yogā vāsyambunaite prathamamathagade
dṛśyamāne prayojyāḥ || 5
uddhṛtya muṣṭimācchādya bheṣajaṃ yatprayujyate
tanmuṣṭiyogamityāhurmuṣṭiyogaparāyaṇāḥ || 6
2uṣṭrakaṇṭakamūlaṃ vāpyanantāmūlameva vā
vidhigṛhītaṃ jyeṣṭhāmbu pītaṃ hanti masūrikām || 7
tadvacchṛgālakaṇṭakamūlaṃ ca vyuṣitāmbhasā
masūrīṃ mūrcchito hanti gandhakārdhastu pāradaḥ || 8
niśāciñcācchade śītavāripīte tathaiva tu
yāvatsaṃkhyāmasūryaṅge tāvadbhiḥ śelujairdalaiḥ || 9
chinnairāturanāmnā tu guṇī vyeti na vardhate
vyuṣitaṃ vāri sakṣaudraṃ pītaṃ dāhaguṇīharam || 10
śelutvakkṛtaśītāmbhaḥseko vā kāyaśoṣaṇe
ugrājyavaṃśanīlīyavavṛṣakārpāsakīkasabrahmī || 11
svarasamayūrakalākṣādhūpo romāntikādiharaḥ
tarpaṇaṃ vātajāyāṃ prāglājacūrṇaiḥ saśarkaraiḥ || 12
bhojanaṃ tiktayūṣaiśca pratudānāṃ rasena vā
dvipañcamūlaṃ rāsnā ca dārvyuśīraṃ durālabhā || 13
sāmṛtaṃ dhānyakaṃ mustaṃ jayedvātasamutthitām
3guḍūcīṃ madhukaṃ rāsnāṃ pañcamūlaṃ kaniṣṭhakam || 14
candanaṃ kāśmaryaphalaṃ balāmūlaṃ bikaṅkatam
pākakāle masūryāṃ tu vātajāyāṃ prayojayet || 15
drākṣākāśmaryakharjūrapaṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
lājāmalakadusparśaiḥ sitāyuktaiśca paittike || 16
śirīṣodumbarāśvatthaśelunyagrodhavalkalaiḥ
pralepaḥ saghṛtaḥ śīghraṃ braṇavisphoṭadāhahā || 17
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ bhūnimbaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
ślaiṣmikyāṃ pittajāyāṃ vā pāne niḥkvāthya dāpayet || 18
4nimbaṃ parpaṭakaṃ pāṭhāṃ paṭolaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
vāsāṃ durālabhāṃ dhātrīmuśīraṃ candanadvayam || 19
eṣa nimbādikaḥ khyātaḥ pītaḥ śarkarayā yutaḥ
hanti tridoṣamasūrīṃ jvaravīsarpasambhavām || 20
utthitā praviśedyā tu punastāṃ bāhyato nayet || 21
5paṭolakuṇḍalīmustavṛṣadhanvayavāsakaiḥ
bhūnimbanimbakaṭukāparpaṭaiśca śṛtaṃ jalam || 22
masurīṃ śamayedāmāṃ pakvāṃ caiva viśoṣayet
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiñcidvisphoṭajvaraśāntaye || 23
paṭolamūlāruṇataṇḍulīyakaṃ
pibeddharidrāmalakalkasaṃyutam
masūrikāsphoṭavidāhaśāntaye
tadeva romāntivamijvarāpaham || 24
paṭolamūlāruṇataṇḍulīyakaṃ
tathaiva dhātrīkhadireṇa saṃyutam
pibejjalaṃ sukvathitaṃ suśītalaṃ
masūrikārogavināśanaṃ param || 25
6khadiratriphalāriṣṭapaṭolāmṛtavāsakaiḥ
kvātho'ṣṭakāṅgo jayati romāntikamasūrikāḥ
kuṣṭhavīsarpavisphoṭakaṇḍvādīnapi tānataḥ || 26
7amṛtādikaṣāyastu jayetpittakaphātmikām
sauvīreṇa tu saṃpiṣṭaṃ mātuluṅgasya keśaram || 27
pralepātpātayatyāśu dāhaṃ cāśu niyacchati
pādadāhaṃ prakurute piḍakā pādasaṃbhavā || 28
tatra sekaṃ praśaṃsanti vahuśastaṇḍulāmbunā
pākakāle tu sarvāstā viśoṣayati mārutaḥ || 29
tasmātsaṃbṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na tu pathyaṃ viśoṣaṇam
guḍūcī madhukaṃ drākṣā moraṭaṃ dāḍimaiḥ saha || 30
pākakāle tu dātavyaṃ bheṣajaṃ guḍasaṃyutam
tena pākaṃ vrajatyāśu na ca vāyuḥ prakupyati || 31
lihedvā bādaraṃ cūrṇaṃ pācanārthaṃ guḍena tu
anenāśu vipacyante vātapittakaphātmikāḥ || 32
8śūlādhmānaparītasya kampamānasya vāyunā
dhanvamāṃsarasāḥ śastā īṣatsaindhavasaṃyutāḥ || 33
dāḍimāmlarasairyuktā yūṣāḥ syurarucau hitāḥ
pibedambhastaptaśītaṃ bhāvitaṃ khadirāśanaiḥ || 34
śauce vāri prayuñjīta gāyatrībahuvārajam
jātīpatraṃ samañjiṣṭhaṃ dārvīpūgaphalaṃ śamīm || 35
dhātrīphalaṃ samadhukaṃ kvathitaṃ madhusaṃyutam
mukharoge kaṇṭharoge gaṇḍūṣārthaṃ praśasyate || 36
akṣṇoḥ sekaṃ praśaṃsanti gavedhumadhukāmbunā
madhukaṃ triphalāmūrvādārvītvaṅnīlamutpalam || 37
uśīralodhramañjiṣṭhāḥ pralepāścyotane hitāḥ
naśyantyanena dṛgjātā masūryo na dravanti ca || 38
pañcavalkalacūrṇena kledinīmavacūrṇayet
bhasmanā kecidicchanti kecidgomayareṇunā || 39
krimipātabhayāccāpi dhūpayeccharalādinā
vedanādāhaśāntyarthaṃ srutānāṃ ca viśuddhaye || 40
saguggulaṃ varākvāthaṃ yuñjyādvā kharidāṣṭakam
kṛṣṇābhayārajo lihyānmadhunā kaṇṭhaśuddhaye || 41
athāṣṭāṅgāvaleho vā kavaḍaścārdrakādibhiḥ
pañcatiktaṃ prayuñjīta pānābhyañjanabhojanaiḥ || 42
kuryādvraṇavidhānaṃ ca tailādīnvarjayecciram
viṣaghnaiḥ siddhamantraiśca prasṛjyāttu punaḥ punaḥ
tathā śoṇitasaṃsṛṣṭāḥ kāścicchoṇitamokṣaṇaiḥ || 43
9niśādvayośīraśirīṣamustakaiḥ
salodhrabhadraśriyanāgakeśaraiḥ
sasvedavisphoṭavisarpakuṣṭha-
daurgandhyaromāntiharaḥ pradehaḥ || 44
bimbyatimuktakā'śokaplakṣavetasapallavaiḥ
niśi paryuṣitaḥ kvātho masūrībhayanāśanaḥ || 45
caitrāsitabhūtadine raktapatākānvitā snuhībhavane
dhavalitakalaśanyastā pāparujo dūrato dhatte || 46
Adhikāra 54
1tatrājagallikāmāmāṃ jalaukābhirupācaret
śuktisaurāṣṭrikākṣārakalkaiścālepayenmuhuḥ || 1
kaṭhināṃ kṣārayogaiśca drāvayedajagallikām
śleṣmavidradhikalkena jayedanuśayīṃ bhiṣak || 2
vivṛtāmindravṛddhāṃ ca gardabhīṃ jālagardabham
irivellikāṃ gandhanāmāṃ jayetpittavisarpavat || 3
madhurauṣadhasiddhena sarpiṣā śamayedvraṇān
raktāvasekairbahubhiḥ svedanairapatarpaṇaiḥ || 4
jayedvidārikāṃ lepaiḥ śigrudevadrumodbhavaiḥ
panasikāṃ kacchapikāmanena vidhinā bhiṣak || 5
sādhayetkaṭhinānanyāñśothāndoṣasamudbhavān
antrālajīṃ kacchapikāṃ tathā pāṣāṇagardabham || 6
suradāruśilākuṣṭhaiḥ svedayitvā pralepayet
kaphamārutaśothaghno lepaḥ pāṣāṇagardabhe || 7
2śastreṇoddhṛtya valmīkaṃ kṣārāgnibhyāṃ prasādhayet
manaḥśilālabhallātasūkṣmailāgurucandanaiḥ || 8
jātīpallavakalkaiśca nimbatailaṃ vipācayet
valmīkaṃ nāśayettaddhi bahucchidraṃ bahusvanam || 9
pādadārīṣu ca śirāṃ vyadhayettalaśodhanīm
snehasvedopapannau tu pādau cālepayenmuhuḥ || 10
madhūcchiṣṭavasāmajjāghṛtakṣārairvimiśritaiḥ
sarjākhyasindhūdbhavayoścūrṇaṃ madhughṛtāplutam
nirmathya kaṭutailāktaṃ hitaṃ pādapramārjanam || 11
3upodikāsarṣapanimbamoca-
karkārukairvārukabhasmatoye
tailaṃ vipakvaṃ lavaṇāṃśayuktaṃ
tatpādadārīṃ vinihanti lepāt || 12
alase'mlaiściraṃ siktau caraṇau parilepayet
paṭolāriṣṭakāśīsatriphalābhirmuhurmuhuḥ || 13
karañjabījaṃ rajanī kāśīsaṃ madhukaṃ madhu
rocanā haritālaṃ ca lepo'yamalase hitaḥ || 14
lākṣābhayārasālepaḥ kāryaṃ vā raktamokṣaṇam
jātīpatraṃ ca saṃmardya dadyādalasake bhiṣak || 15
4bṛhatīrasasiddhena tailenābhyajya buddhimān
śilārocanakāśīsacūrṇairvā pratisārayet || 16
dahetkadaramuddhṛtya tailena dahanena vā
cippamuṣṇāmbunā svinnamutkṛtyābhyajya taṃ braṇam || 17
dattvā sarjarasaṃ cūrṇaṃ baddhvā vraṇavadācaret
svarasena haridrāyāḥ pātre kṛṣṇāyase'bhayām || 18
ghṛṣṭvā tajjena kalkena limpeccippaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
cippe saṭaṅkaṇāsphotāmūlalepo nakhapradaḥ || 19
nimbodakena vamanaṃ padminīkaṇṭake hitam
nimbodakakṛtaṃ sarpiḥ sakṣaudraṃ pānamiṣyate || 20
padmanālakṛtaḥ kṣāraḥ padminīṃ hanti lepataḥ
nimbāragvadhakalkairvā muhurudvartanaṃ hitam || 21
nilīpaṭolamūlābhyāṃ sājyābhyāṃ lepanaṃ hitam
jālagardabharoge tu sadyo hanti cavedanām || 22
5ahipūtanake dhātryāḥ pūrvaṃ stanyaṃ viśodhayet
triphalākhadirakvāthairbraṇānāṃ dhāvanaṃ sadā || 23
karañjatriphalātiktaiḥ sarpiḥ siddhaṃ śiśorhitam
rasāñjanaṃ viśeṣeṇa pānālepanayorhitam || 24
gudabhraṃśe gudaṃ snehairabhyajyāśu praveśayet
praviṣṭe svedayeccāpi baddhaṃ goṣphaṇayā bhṛśam || 25
komalaṃ padminīpatraṃ yaḥ khādeccharkarānvitam
etanniścitya nirdiṣṭaṃ na tasya gudanirgamaḥ || 26
vṛkṣāmlānalacāṅgerībilvapāṭhāyavāgrajam
takreṇa śīlayetpāyubhraṃśārto'naladīpanam || 27
gudaṃ ca gavyapasayā mrakṣayedaviśaṅkitaḥ
duṣpraveśo gudabhraṃśo viśatyāśu na saṃśayaḥ || 28
mūṣikāṇāṃ vasābhirvā gude samyakpralepanam
svinnamūṣikamāṃsena cāthavā svedayedgudam || 29
6cāṅgerīkoladadhyamlanāgarakṣārasaṃyutam
ghṛtamutkvathitaṃ peyaṃ gudabhraṃśarujāpaham
śuṇṭhīkṣārāvatra kalkau śiṣṭaṃ tu dravamiṣyate || 30
7kṣīre mahatpañcamūlaṃ mūṣikāmantavarjitām
paktvā tasminpacettailaṃ vātaghnauṣadhasādhitam || 31
gudabhraṃśamidaṃ tailaṃ pānābhyaṅgātprasādhayet || 32
8svedopanāhaṃ parikartikāyāṃ
kṛtvā samabhyajya ghṛtena paścāt
praveśayeccarma śanaiḥ praśiṣṭai-
rmāṃsaiḥ sukhoṣṇairupanāhayecca || 33
snehasvedaistathaivaināṃ cikitsedavapāṭikām
niruddhaprakaśe nāḍīṃ dvimukhīṃ kanakādijām || 34
kṣiptvā tyaktvā cullakādisnehena pariṣecayet
tailena vā vacādārugandhaiḥ siddhena ca vyahāt || 35
punaḥ sthūlatarā nāḍī deyā srotovivṛddhaye
śastreṇa sevanīṃ tyaktvā bhitvā vraṇavadācaret || 36
snigdhaṃ ca bhojanaṃ baddhe gude'pyeṣa kriyākramaḥ
carmakīlaṃ jatumaṇiṃ masakāṃstilakālakān || 37
uddhṛtya śastreṇa dahetkṣārāgnibhyāmaśeṣataḥ
rubunālasya cūrṇena dharṣī masakanāśanaḥ || 38
8nirmokabhasmagharṣādvā masaḥ śāntiṃ vrajetsadā
yuvānapiḍakānyacchanīlikāvyaṅgaśarkarāḥ || 39
śirāvyadhaiḥ pralepaiśca jayedabhyañjanaistathā
lodhradhānyavacālepastāruṇyapiḍakāpahaḥ || 40
tadvadgorocanāyuktaṃ maricaṃ sukhalepataḥ
siddhārthakavacālodhrasaindhavaiśca pralepanam || 41
vamanaṃ ca nihantyāśu piḍakāṃ yauvanodbhavām
vyaṅgeṣu cārjunatvagvā mañjiṣṭhā vā samākṣikā || 42
