lepaḥ sanavanītā vā śvetāśvakhurajā masī
raktacandanamañjiṣṭhālodhrakuṣṭhapriyaṅgavaḥ || 43
vaṭāṅkuramasūrāśca vyaṅgaghnā mukhakāntidāḥ
vyaṅgānāṃ lepanaṃ śastaṃ rudhireṇa śaśasya ca || 44
masūraiḥ sarpiṣā piṣṭairliptamāsyaṃ payo'nvitaiḥ
saptāhācca bhavetsatyaṃ puṇḍarīkadalaprabham || 45
mātuluṅgajaṭāsarpiḥśilāgośakṛto rasaḥ
mukhakāntikaro lepaḥ piḍakātilakālajit || 46
navanītaguḍakṣaudrakolamajjapralepanam
vyaṅgajidvaruṇatvagvā chāgakṣīraprapeṣitā || 47
jātīphalakalkalepo nīlivyaṅgādināśanaḥ
sāyaṃ ca kaṭutailenābhyaṅgo vaktraprasādanaḥ || 48
9kālīyakotpalāmaya-
dadhisarabadarāsthimadhyaphalinībhiḥ
liptaṃ bhavati ca vadanaṃ
śaśiprabhaṃ saptarātreṇa || 49
tuṣarahitamasṛṇayava-
cūrṇasayaṣṭīmadhukalodhralepena
bhavati mukhaṃ parinirjita-
cāmīkaracārusaubhāgyam || 50
rakṣoghnaśarvarīdvayaṃ
mañjiṣṭhāgairikāhvayaistu payaḥ
siddhena liptamānana-
mudyadvidhubimbavadvibhāti || 51
pariṇatadadhiśarapuṃkhaiḥ
kuvalayadalakuṣṭhacandanośīraiḥ
mukhakamalakāntikārī
bhrukuṭītilakālakāñjayati || 52
10haridrādvayayaṣṭyāhvakālīyakakucandanaiḥ
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāpadmapadmakakuṅkumaiḥ || 53
kapitthatindukaplakṣavaṭapatraiḥ payo'nvitaiḥ
lepayetkalkitairebhistailaṃ vābhyañjanaṃ caret || 54
piplavaṃ nīlikāvyaṅgāṃstilakānmukhadūṣikān
nityasevī jayetkṣipraṃ mukhaṃ kuryānmanoramam || 55
11madhukasya kaṣāyeṇa tailasya kuḍavaṃ pacet
kalkaiḥ priyaṅgumañjiṣṭhācandanotpalakeśaraiḥ || 56
kanakaṃ nāma tattailaṃ mukhakāntikaraṃ param
abhīrunīlikāvyaṅgaśodhanaṃ paramarcitam || 57
12mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ lākṣā mātuluṅgaṃ sayaṣṭikam
karṣapramāṇairetaistu tailasya kuḍavaṃ tathā || 58
ājaṃ payastaddviguṇaṃ śanairmṛdvagninā pacet
nīlikāpiḍakāvyaṅgānabhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 59
mukhaṃ prasannopacitaṃ valīpalitavarjitam
saptarātraprayogeṇa bhavetkanakasannibham || 60
13kuṅkumaṃ candanaṃ lākṣā mañjiṣṭhā madhuyaṣṭikā
kālīyakamuśīraṃ ca padmakaṃ nīlamutpalam || 61
nyagrodhapādāḥ plakṣasya śuṅgāḥ padmasya keśaram
dvipañcamūlasahitaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ palikaiḥ pṛthak || 62
jalāḍhakaṃ vipaktavyaṃ pādaśeṣamathoddharet
mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ lākṣā pattaṅgaṃ madhuyaṣṭikā || 63
karṣapramāṇairetaistu tailasya kuḍavaṃ tathā
ajākṣīraṃ taddviguṇaṃ śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 64
samyakpakvaṃ paraṃ hyetanmukhavarṇaprasādanam
nīlikāpiḍakāvyaṅgānabhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 65
saptarātraprayogeṇa bhavetkāñcanasannibham
kuṅkumādyamidaṃ tailamaśvibhyāṃ nirmitaṃ purā || 66
14kuṅkuma kiṃśukaṃ lākṣā mañjiṣṭhā raktacandanam
kālīyakaṃ padmakaṃ ca mātuluṅgasya keśaram || 67
kusumbhaṃ madhuyaṣṭīkaṃ phalinī madayantikā
niśe dve rocanā padmamutpalaṃ ca manaḥśilā || 68
kākolyādisamāyuktairetairakṣasamairbhiṣak
lākṣārasapayobhyāṃ ca tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 69
kuṅkumādyamidaṃ tailaṃ cābhyaṅgātkāñcanopamam
karoti vadanaṃ sadyaḥ puṣṭilāvaṇyakāntidam
saubhāgyalakṣmījananaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 70
15madhukaṃ candanaṃ kaṅgu sarṣapaṃ padmakaṃ tathā
kālīyakaṃ haridrāca lodhramebhiśca kalkitaiḥ || 71
vipaceddhi ghṛtaṃ vaidyastatpakvaṃ vastragālitam
pādāṃśaṃ kuṅkumaṃ sikthaṃ kṣiptvā mandānale pacet || 72
tatsiddhaṃ śiśire nīre prakṣipyākarṣayettataḥ
tadetadvarṇakaṃ nāma ghṛtaṃ varṇaprasādanam || 73
anenābhyāsaliptaṃ hi valībhūtamapi kramāt
niṣkalaṅkendubimbābhaṃ syādvilāsavatīmukham || 74
arūṃṣikāyāṃ rudhire'vasikte
śirāvyadhenātha jalaukasā vā
nimbāmbusiktaiḥ śirasi pralepo
deyo'śvavarcorasasaindhavābhyām || 75
purāṇamatha piṇyākaṃ purīṣaṃ kukkuṭasya vā
mūtrapiṣṭaṃ pralepo'yaṃ śīghraṃ hanyādarūṃṣikām || 76
arūṃṣighnaṃ bhṛṣṭakuṣṭacūrṇaṃ tailena saṃyutam
16haridrādvayabhūnimbatriphalāriṣṭacandanaiḥ
etattailamarūṃṣīṇāṃ siddhamabhyañjane hitam || 77
dāraṇe tu śirāṃ vidhyetsnigdhāṃ svinnāṃ lalāṭajām
avapīḍāśirobastīnabhyaṅgāṃścāvacārayet || 78
kodravāṇāṃ tṛṇakṣārapānīyaṃ paridhāvane
kāryo dāraṇake mūrdhni pralepo madhusaṃyutaḥ || 79
priyālabījamadhukakuṣṭamiśraiḥ sasaindhavaiḥ
kāñjikasthā trisaptāhaṃ māṣā dāraṇakāpahā || 80
saha nīlotpalakeśarayaṣṭī
madhukatilaiḥ sadṛśamāmalakam
cirajātamapi ca śīrṣe
dāraṇarogaṃ śamaṃ nayati || 81
17triphalādyā rajomāṃsīmārkavotpalaśārivaiḥ
sasaindhavaiḥ pacettailamabhyaṅgādukthikāṃ jayet || 82
citrakaṃ dantimūlaṃ ca koṣātakīsamanvitam
kalkaṃ piṣṭvā pacettailaṃ keśadadruvināśanam || 83
guñjāphalaiḥ śṛtaṃ tailaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasena tu
kaṇḍūdāraṇahṛtkuṣṭhakapālavyādhināśanam || 84
18bhṛṅgarājatriphalotpalaśāri
lauhapurīṣasamanvitakāri
tailamidaṃ paca dāraṇahāri
kuñcitakeśaghanasthirakāri || 85
prapauṇḍarīkamadhukapippalīcandanotpalaiḥ
kārṣikaistailakuḍavaṃ tairdvirāmalakīrasaḥ || 86
sādhyaḥ sapratimarśaḥ syātsarvaśīrṣagadāpahaḥ
19mālatīkaravīrāgninaktamālavipācitam || 87
tailamabhyañjane śastamindraluptāpahaṃ param
idaṃ hi tvaritaṃ hanti dāraṇaṃ niyataṃ nṛṇām || 88
dhātryāmramajjalepātsyātsthirorusnigdhakeśatā
indralupte śirāṃ viddhvā śilākāsīsatutthakaiḥ || 89
lepayetparitaḥ kalkaistailaṃ cābhyañjane hitam
kuṭannaṭaśikhījātīkarañjakaravīrajaiḥ || 90
avagāḍhapadaṃ caiva pracchayitvā punaḥ punaḥ
guñjāphalaiściraṃ limpetkeśabhūmiṃ samantataḥ || 91
hastidantamasīṃ kṛtvā mukhyaṃ caiva rasāñjanam
lomānyanena jāyante nṛṇāṃ pāṇitaleṣvapi || 92
bhallātakabṛhatīphalaguñjāmūlaphalebhya ekena
madhusahitena viliptaṃ surapatiluptaṃ śamaṃ yāti || 93
bṛhatīphalarasapiṣṭaṃ guñjāphalamūlaṃ caindraluptasya
kanakanighṛṣṭasya sato dātavyaṃ pracchitasya sadā || 94
ghṛṣṭasya karkaśaiḥ patrairindraluptasya guṇḍanam
cūrṇitairmaricaiḥ kāyamindraluptanivāraṇam || 95
chāgakṣīrarasāñjana-
puṭadagdhagajendradantamasīliptāḥ
jāyante saptarātrāt
khalvyāmapi kuñcitāścikurāḥ || 96
madhukendīvaramūrvā
tilājyagokṣīrabhṛṅgalepena
acirādbhavanti ghanakeśā
dṛḍhamūkāyatānṛjavaḥ || 97
20snuhīpayaḥ payo'rkasya mārkavo lāṅgalīviṣam
mūtramājaṃ sagomūtraṃ raktikā sendravāruṇī || 98
siddhārthaṃ tīkṣṇatailaṃ ca garbhaṃ dattvā vipācitam
vahninā mṛdunā pakvaṃ tailaṃ khālityanāśanam || 99
kūrmapṛṣṭhasamānāpi rujyāyā romataskarī
digdhā sānena jāyeta ṛkṣaśārīralomaśā || 100
21vaṭāvarohakeśinyoścūrṇenādityapācitam
guḍūcīsvarase tailaṃ cābhyaṅgātkeśaropaṇam || 101
22candanaṃ madhukaṃ mūrvā triphalā nīlamutpalam
kāntā vaṭāvarohaśca guḍūcī visameva ca || 102
lauhacūrṇaṃ tathā keśī śāvire dve tathaiva ca
mārkavasvarasenaiva tailaṃ mṛdvagninā pacet || 103
śirasyutpatitāḥ keśā jāyante ghanakuñcitāḥ
dṛḍhamūlāśca snigdhāśca tathā bhramarasannibhāḥ
nasyenākālapalitaṃ nihanyāttailamuttamam || 104
23tailaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhukaiḥ kṣīre dhātrīphalaiḥ śṛtam
nasye dattaṃ janayati keśāñśmaśrūṇi cāpyatha || 105
triphalā nīlinīpatraṃ lohaṃ bhṛṅgarajaḥsamam
avimūtreṇa saṃyuktaṃ kṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam || 106
triphalācūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ vinikṣipet
īṣatpakve nārīkele bhṛṅgarājarasānvite || 107
māsamekaṃ tu nikṣipya samyaggarbhātsamuddharet
tataḥ śiro muṇḍayitvā lepaṃ dadyādbhiṣagvaraḥ || 108
saṃveṣṭya kadalīpatrairmocayetsaptame dine
kṣālayettriphalākvāthaiḥ kṣīramāṃsarasāśinaḥ || 109
kapālarañjanaṃ caitatkṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam
utpalaṃ payasā sārdhaṃ māsaṃ bhūmau nidhāpayet || 110
keśānāṃ kṛṣṇakaraṇaṃ snehanaṃ ca vidhīyate
bhṛṅgapuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpaṃ meṣīdugdhaprapeṣitam || 111
tenaivāloḍitaṃ lauhapātrasthaṃ bhūmyadhaḥ kṛtam
saptāhāduddhṛtaṃ paścādbhṛṅgarājarasena tu || 112
āloḍyābhyajya ca śiro veṣṭayitvā vasenniśām
prātastu kṣālanaṃ kāryamevaṃ syānmūrdharañjanam
evaṃ sindūrabālāmbaśaṅkhabhṛṅgarasaiḥ kriyā || 113
24navadagdhaśaṅkhacūrṇaṃ
kāñjikasiktaṃ hi sīsakaṃ ghṛṣṭvā
lepātkacānarkadalava-
baddhān śubhrānkaroti nīlatarān || 114
lohamalāmalakalkaiḥ
sajavākusumairnaraḥ sadā snāyī
palitānīha na paśyati
gaṅgāsnāyīva narakāṇi || 115
nimbasya bījāni hi bhāvitāni
bhṛṅgasya toyena tathāśanasya
tailaṃ tu teṣāṃ vinihanti nasyā-
ddugdhānnabhoktuḥ palitaṃ samūlam || 116
nimbasya tailaṃ prakṛtisthameva
nasye niṣiktaṃ vidhinā yathāvat
māsena gokṣīrabhujo narasya
jarāgrabhūtaṃ palitaṃ nihanti || 117
kṣīrātsamārkavarasāddviprasthe madhukātpale
tailasya kuḍavaṃ pakvaṃ tannasyaṃ palitāpaham || 118
25ādityavallimūlāni kṛṣṇaśairīyakasya ca
surasasya ca patrāṇi phalaṃ kṛṣṇāśanasya ca || 119
mārkavaṃ kākamācī ca madhukaṃ devadāru ca
pṛthagdaśapalāṃśāni pippalyatriphalāñjanam || 120
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ mañjiṣṭhālodhraṃ kṛṣṇāgurūtpalam
āmrāsthikardamaḥ kṛṣṇo mṛṇālī raktacandanam || 121
nīlībhallātakāsthīni kāsīsaṃ madayantikā
somarājyaśanaḥ śastraṃ kṛṣṇo piṇḍītacitrakau || 122
puṣpāṇyarjunakāśmaryoścāmrajambūphalāni ca
pṛthakpañcapalairbhāgaiḥ supiṣṭairāḍhakaṃ pacet || 123
baibhītakasya tailasya dhātrīrasacaturguṇam
kuryādādityapākaṃ vā yāvacchuṣko bhavedrasaḥ || 124
lohapātre tataḥ pūtaṃ saṃśuddhamupayojayet
pāne nasyakriyāyāṃ ca śiro'bhyaṅge tathaiva ca || 125
etaccākṣuṣyamāyuṣyaṃ śirasaḥ sarvaroganut
mahānīlamiti khyātaṃ palitaghnamanuttamam || 126
26bhṛṅgarājarase pakvaṃ śikhipittena kalkitam
ghṛtaṃ nasyena palitaṃ hanyātsaptāhayogataḥ || 127
kāñjikapiṣṭaśelukamajjñi sacchidralauhage
yadarkatāpātpatati tailaṃ tannasyamrakṣaṇāt || 128
keśā nīlālisaṅkāśāḥ sadyaḥ snigdhā bhavanti ca
nayanaśravaṇagrīvādantarogāṃśca hantyadaḥ || 129
kāsīsaṃ rocanātulyaṃ haritālaṃ rasāñjanam
amlapiṣṭaiḥ pralepo'yaṃ vṛṣakacchvahipūtayoḥ || 130
paṭolapatratriphalārasāñjanavipācitam
pītaṃ ghṛtaṃ nihantyāśu kṛcchrāmapyahipūtanām || 131
rajanīmārkavamūlaṃ piṣṭaṃ śītena vāriṇā tulyam
hanti visarpaṃ lepādvarāhadaśanāhvayaṃ ghoram || 132
nāgakeśaracūrṇaṃ vā śatadhautena sarpiṣā
piṣṭvā lepo vidhātavyo dāhe harṣe ca pādayoḥ || 133
Adhikāra 55
1oṣṭhaprakope vātotthe śālvanenopanāhanam
mastiṣke caiva nasye ca tailaṃ vātaharaiḥ śṛtam
svedo'bhyaṅgaḥ snehapānaṃ rasāyanamiheṣyate || 1
śrīveṣṭakaṃ sarjarasaṃ gugguluṃ suradāru ca
yaṣṭimadhukacūrṇaṃ ca vidadhyātpratisāraṇam || 2
vedhaṃ śirāṇāṃ vamanaṃ virekaṃ
tiktasya pānaṃ rasabhojanaṃ ca
śītānpralepānpariṣecanaṃ ca
pittopasṛṣṭeṣvadhareṣu kuryāt || 3
pittaraktābhighātotthāñjalaukābhirupācaret
pittavidradhivaccāpi kriyāṃ kuryādaśeṣataḥ || 4
śirovirecanaṃ dhūmaḥ svedaḥ kavaladhāraṇam
hṛtarakte prayoktavyamoṣṭhakope kaphātmake || 5
trikaṭuḥ sarjikākṣāraḥ kṣāraśca yāvaśūkajaḥ
kṣaudrayuktaṃ vidhātavyametacca pratisāraṇam
medoje svedite bhinne śodhite jvalano hitaḥ || 6
priyaṅgutriphalālodhraṃ sakṣaudraṃ pratisāraṇam
hitaṃ ca triphalācūrṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pralepanam || 7
sarjarasakanakagairika-
dhanyākaghṛtatailasindhusaṃyuktam
siddhaṃ sikthakamadhure
sphuṭitoccaṭite vraṇaṃ harati || 8
2śītāde hṛtarakte tu toye nāgarasarṣapān
niḥkvāthya triphalāṃ cāpi kuryādgaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam || 9
priṅgayavaśca mustā ca triphalā ca pralepanam || 10
kuṣṭaṃ dārvīmabdalodhraṃ samaṅgā
pāṭhā tiktā tejanī pītikā ca
cūrṇaṃ śastaṃ gharṣaṇaṃ taddvijānāṃ
raktasrāvaṃ hanti kaṇḍūṃ rujāṃ ca || 11
caladantasthirakaraṃ kāryaṃ bakulacarvaṇam
ārtagaladalakvāthagaṇḍūṣo dantacālanut || 12
dantacāle hitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tilogrācarvaṇaṃ sadā
dantapuppuṭake kāryaṃ taruṇe raktamokṣaṇam || 13
sapañcalavaṇaḥ kṣāraḥ sakṣaudraḥ pratisāraṇam
dantānāṃ todaharṣe ca vātaghnāḥ kavalā hitāḥ || 14
dantacāle tu gaṇḍūṣo bakulatvakkṛto hitaḥ
mākṣikaṃ pippalīsarpirmiśritaṃ dhārayenmukhe || 15
dantaśūlaharaṃ proktaṃ pradhānamidamauṣadham
visrāvite dantaveṣṭe vraṇaṃ tu pratisārayet || 16
lodhrapattuṅgamadhukalākṣācūrṇairmadhūttaraiḥ
gaṇḍūṣe kṣīriṇo yojyāḥ sakṣaudraghṛtaśarkarāḥ || 17
3śaiśire hṛtarakte ca lodhramustarasāñjanaiḥ
sakṣaudraiḥ śasyate lepo gaṇḍūṣe kṣīriṇo hitāḥ || 18
kriyāṃ paridare kuryācchītādoktāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ
saṃśodhyobhayataḥ kāryaṃ śiraścopakuśe tataḥ || 19
kākodumbarikāgojīpatrairvisrāvayedbhiṣak
kṣaudrayuktaiśca lavaṇaiḥ savyoṣaiḥ pratisārayet || 20
pippalyaḥ sarṣapāḥ śvetā nāgaraṃ naiculaṃ phalam
sukhodakena saṃgṛhya kavalaṃ tasya yojayet || 21
śastreṇa dantavaidarbhe dantamūlāni śodhayet
tataḥ kṣāraṃ prayuñjīta kriyāḥ sarvāśca śītalāḥ || 22
uddhṛtyādhikadantaṃ tu tato'gnimavacārayet
krimidantakavaccātra vidhiḥ kāryo vijānatā
chittvādhimāṃsaṃ sakṣaudrairetaiścūrṇairupācaret || 23
pāṭhāvacātejovatīsarjikāyāvaśūkajaiḥ
kṣaudradvitīyāḥ pippalyaḥ kavalaścātra kīrtitaḥ || 24
paṭolanimbatriphalākaṣāyaścātra dhāvane
śirovirekaśca hito dhūmo vairecanaśca yaḥ || 25
nāḍīvraṇaharaṃ karma dantanāḍīṣu kārayet
yaṃ dantamadhijāyeta nāḍī taddantamuddharet || 26
4chittvādhimāṃsaṃ śastreṇa yadi noparijo bhavet
śodhayitvā daheccāpi kṣāreṇa jvalanena vā || 27
gatirhinasti hanvasthi daśane samupekṣite
tasmātsamūlaṃ daśanamuddharedbhagnamasthi ca || 28
uddhṛte tūttare dante śoṇitaṃ saṃprasicyate
raktābhiyogātpūrvoktā ghorā rogā bhavanti ca || 29
calamapyuttaraṃ dantamato nāpaharedbhiṣak
kaṣāyaṃ jātīmadanakaṭukasvādukaṇṭakaiḥ || 30
lodhrakhadiramañjiṣṭhāyaṣṭyāhvaiścāpi yatkṛtam
tailaṃ saṃśodhanaṃ taddhi hanyāddantagatāṃ gatim || 31
kaṣāyaṃ parataḥ kṛtvā piṣṭvā lodhrādikalkitam
kaṇṭakīmadano yojyaḥ svādukaṇṭo vikaṅkataḥ || 32
sukhoṣṇāḥ snehakavalāḥ sarpiṣastraivṛtasya vā
niryūhāścānilaghnānāṃ dantaharṣapramardanāḥ || 33
snaihikaśca hito dhūmo nasyaṃ snaihikameva ca
ahiṃsandantamūlāni śarkarāmuddharedbhiṣak || 34
lākṣācūrṇairmadhuyutaistatastāṃ pratisārayet
hantaharṣakriyāṃ cāpi kuryānniravaśeṣataḥ || 35
5kapālikāḥ kṛcchrasādhyāstatrāpyeṣā kriyāmatā
jayedvisrāvaṇaiḥ svinnamacalaṃ krimidantakam || 36
tathāvapīḍairvātaghnaiḥ snehagaṇḍūṣadhāraṇaiḥ
bhadradārvādivarṣābhūlepaiḥ sigdhaiśca bhojanaiḥ
hiṅgu soṣṇaṃ tu matimānkrimidanteṣu dāpayet || 37
bṛhatībhūmikadambaka-
pañcāṅgulikaṇṭakārikāthaiḥ
gaṇḍūṣastailayutaḥ
krimidantakavedanāśamanaḥ || 38
nīlīvāyasajaṅghā-
snugdugdhīnāṃ tu mūlamekaikam
saṃcarvya daśanavidhṛtaṃ
daśanakrimipātanaṃ prāhuḥ || 39
calamuddhṛtya vā sthānaṃ dahettu śuṣirasya vā
6tato vidārīyaṣṭyāhvaśṛṅgāṭakakaśerubhiḥ
tailaṃ daśaguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ siddhaṃ nasye tu yojayet || 40
hanumokṣe samuddiṣṭā kāryā cārditavatkriyā
phalānyamlāni śītāmbu rukṣānnaṃ dantadhāvanam || 41
tathātikaṭhinānbhakṣyāndantarogī vivarjayet
saptacchadārkadugdhābhyāṃ pūraṇaṃ krimidantanut || 42
jīvanīyena dugdhena krimirandhraprapūraṇam
arkakṣīreṇaivamekayogaḥ sadbhiḥ praśasyate || 43
droṇapuṣpīdravaḥ phenamadhutailasamāyutaḥ
krimidantavināśāya kāryaṃ karṇasya pūraṇam || 44
paṭolakaṭukāvyoṣapāṭhāsaindhavabhāṃrgikaiḥ
cūrṇairmadhuyuto lepaḥ kavalo madhutailakaiḥ
jihvārogeṣu kartavyaṃ vidhānamidamauṣadham || 45
mustāmadhukanirguṇḍīkhadirośīradārubhiḥ
samañjiṣṭhāviḍaṅgaiśra siddhaṃ tailaṃ haretkrimīn || 46
7oṣṭhaprakope'nilaje yaduktaṃ prāk cikitsitam
kaṇṭakeṣvanilottheṣu tatkāryaṃ bhiṣajā khalu || 47
pittajeṣu nighṛṣṭeṣu nisrute duṣṭaśoṇite
pratisāraṇagaṇḍūṣānnasyaṃ ca madhuraṃ hitam || 48
kaṇṭakeṣu kaphottheṣu likhiteṣvasṛjaḥ kṣaye
pippalyādirmadhuyutaḥ kāryaṃ tu pratisāraṇam || 49
gṛhṇīyātkavalānvāpi gaurasarṣapasaindhavaiḥ
paṭolanimbavārtākukṣārayūṣaiśca bhojayet || 50
jihvājāḍyaṃ cirajaṃ
māṇakabhasmalavaṇagharṣaṇaṃ hanti
īṣatsnukkṣīrāktaṃ
jambīrādyamlacarvaṇaṃ vāpi || 51
karkaṭāṅghrikṣīrapakvaghṛtābhyaṅgena naśyati
dantaśabdaḥ karkaṭāṅghrilepādvā dantayojitāt || 52
upajihvāṃ tu saṃlikhya kṣāreṇa pratisārayet
śirovirekagaṇḍūṣadhūmaiścaināmupācaret || 53
vyoṣakṣārābhayāvahnicūrṇametatpragharṣaṇam
upajihvāpraśāntyarthametaistailaṃ vipācayet || 54
chinnāṃ gharṣedgalaśuṇṭhīṃ vyoṣogrākṣaudrasindhujaiḥ
kuṣṭhoṣaṇavacāsindhukaṇāpāṭhāplavairapi || 55
sakṣaudrairbhiṣajā kāryaṃ galaśuṇṭhyā vigharṣaṇam
upanāsāvyadho hanti galaśuṇṭhīmaśeṣataḥ || 56
galaśuṇṭhīharaṃ tadvacchephālīmūlacarvaṇam
vacāmativiṣāṃ pāṭhāṃ rāsnāṃ kaṭukarohiṇīm
niḥkvāthya picumardaṃ ca kavalaṃ tatra yojayet || 57
8kṣārasiddheṣu mudgeṣu yūṣāścāpyaśane hitāḥ
tuṇḍīkeryadhruṣe kūrme saṃghāte tālupuppuṭe || 58
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryo viśeṣaḥ śastrakarmaṇi
tālupāke tu kartavyaṃ vidhānaṃ pittanāśanam || 59
snehasvedau tāluśoṣe vidhiścānilanāśanaḥ
sādhyānāṃ rohiṇīnāṃ tu hitaṃ śoṇitamokṣaṇam || 60
chardanaṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca gaṇḍūṣo nasyakarma ca
ghātikīṃ tu hṛte rakte lavaṇaiḥ pratisārayet || 61
sukhoṣṇāṃstailakavalāndhārayeccāpyabhīkṣṇaśaḥ
pataṅgaśarkarākṣodraiḥ paittikīṃ pratisārayet || 62
drākṣāparūṣakakvātho hitaśca kavalagrahe
āgāradhūmakaṭukaiḥ kaphajāṃ pratisārayet || 63
śvetāvillaṅgadantīṣu siddhaṃ tailaṃ sasaindhavam
nasyakarmaṇi dātavyaṃ kavalaṃ ca kaphocchraye || 64
pittavatsādhayedvaidyo rohiṇīṃ raktasaṃbhavām
visrāvya kaṇṭhaśālūkaṃ sādhayettuṇḍikerivat || 65
ekakālaṃ yavānnaṃ ca bhuñjīta snigdhamalpaśaḥ
upajihvikavaccāpi sādhayedadhijihvikām || 66
unnāmya jihvāmākṛṣya baḍiśenādhijihvikām
chedayenmaṇḍalāgreṇa tīkṣṇoṣṇairgharṣaṇādibhiḥ || 67
ekavṛndaṃ tu visrāvya vidhiṃ śodhanamācaret
śilāyuścāpi yo vyādhistaṃ ca śastreṇa sādhayet || 68
amarmasthaṃ supakvaṃ ca bhedayedgalavidradhim
kaṇṭharogeṣvasṛṅmokṣastīkṣṇairnasyādikarma ca || 69
kvāthapānaṃ tu dārvītvaṅnimbatārkṣakaliṅgajam
harītakīkaṣāyo vā peyo mākṣikasaṃyutaḥ || 70
kaṭukātiviṣādārupāṭhāmustakaliṅgakāḥ
gomūtrakvathitāḥ peyāḥ kaṇṭharogavināśanāḥ || 71
9gṛhadhūmo yavakṣāraḥ pāṭhāvyoṣarasāñjanam
tejohvātriphalālohaṃ citrakaśceti cūrṇitam || 72
sakṣaudraṃ dhārayedetadgalarogavināśanam
kālakaṃ nāma taccūrṇaṃ dantajihvāsyaroganut || 73
pippalīppipalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
sarjikākṣāratulyāṃśaiścūrṇo'yaṃ galaroganut || 74
10manaḥśilāyavakṣāro haritālaṃ sasaindhavam
dārvītvakceti taccūrṇaṃ mākṣikeṇa samāyutam || 75
mūrchitaṃ ghṛtamaṇḍena kaṇṭharogeṣu dhārayet
mukharogeṣu ca śreṣṭhaṃ pītakaṃ nāma kīrtitam || 76
11yavāgrajaṃ tejavatīṃ sapāṭhāṃ
rasāñjanaṃ dāru niśāṃ sakṛṣṇām
kṣaudreṇa kuryādguḍikāṃ mukhena
tāṃ dhārayetsarvagalāmayeṣu || 77
daśamūlaṃ pibeduṣṇaṃ yūṣaṃ mūlakulatthayoḥ
kṣīrekṣurasagomūtradadhimastvamlakāñjikaiḥ || 78
vidadhyātkavalānvīkṣya doṣaṃ tailaghṛtairapi
pañcakolakatālīsapatrailāmaricatvacaḥ || 79
palāśamuṣkakakṣārayavakṣārāśca cūrṇitāḥ
guḍe purāṇe kvathite dviguṇe guḍikāḥ kṛtāḥ || 80
karkandhumātrāḥ saptāhaṃ sthitā muṣkakabhasmani
kaṇṭharogeṣu sarveṣu dhāryāḥ syuramṛtopamāḥ || 81
12mūtrasvinnāṃ śivāṃ tulyāṃ madhurīkuṣṭhatālakaiḥ
abhyasya mukharogāṃstu jayedvirasatāmapi || 82
vātātsarvasaraṃ cūrṇairlavaṇaiḥ pratisārayet
tailaṃ vātaharaiḥ siddhaṃ hitaṃ kavalanasyayoḥ || 83
pittātmake sarvarase śuddhakāyasya dehinaḥ
sarvapittaharaḥ kāryo vidhirmadhuraśītalaḥ || 84
pratisāraṇagaṇḍūṣāndhūmaṃ saṃśodhanāni ca
kaphātmake sarvasare kramaṃ kuryātkaphāpaham || 85
mukhapāke śirāvedhaḥ śiraḥkāyavirecanam
kāryaṃ ca vahudhā nityaṃ jātīpatrasya carvaṇam || 86
jātīpatrāmṛtādrākṣāyāsadārvīphalatrikaiḥ
kvāthaḥ kṣaudrayutaḥ śīto gaṇḍūṣo mukhapākanut || 87
kṛṣṇajīrakakuṣṭheṃdrayavānāṃ cūrṇatastryahāt
mukhapāke vraṇakledadaurgandhyamupaśāmyati || 88
rasāñjanaṃ lodhramathābhayaṃ ca
manaḥśilānāgaragairikaṃ ca
pāṭhā haridrā gajapippalī ca
syāddhāraṇaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ mukhasya || 89
13paṭolanimbajambvāmramālatīnavapallavāḥ
pañcapallavajaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kaṣāyo mukhadhāvane || 90
pañcavalkakaṣāyo vā triphalākvātha eva vā
mukhapākeṣu sakṣaudraḥ prayojyo mukhadhāvane || 91
svarasaḥ kvathito dārvyā ghanībhūto rasakriyā
sakṣaudrā mukharogāsṛkdoṣanāḍīvraṇāpahā || 92
saptacchadośīrapaṭolamusta-
harītakītiktakarohiṇībhiḥ
yaṣṭyāhvarājadrumacandanaiśca
kkāthaṃ pibetpākaharaṃ mukhasya || 93
paṭolaśuṇṭhītriphalāviśālā-
trāyantitiktādviniśāmṛtānām
pītaḥ kaṣāyo madhunā nihanti
mukhe sthitaścāsyagadānaśeṣān || 94
kvathitāstriphalāpāṭhāmṛdvīkājātipallavāḥ
niṣevyā bhakṣaṇīyā vā triphalā mukhapākahā || 95
tilā nīlotpalaṃ sarpiḥ śarkarā kṣīrameva ca
sakṣaudro dagdhavaktrasya gaṇḍūṣo dāhapākanut
tailena kāñjikenātha gaṇḍūṣaścūrṇadāhahā || 96
ghanakuṣṭhailādhānyakayaṣṭīmadhvelavālukākavalaḥ
vadane'tipūtigandhaṃ harati surālaśunagandhaṃ ca || 97
14tulāṃ tathā nīlakuraṃṭakasya
droṇe'mbhasaḥ saṃśrapayedyathāvat
pūrtvā caturbhāgarase tu tailaṃ
pacecchanairardhapalaprayuktaiḥ || 98
kalkairanantākhadirārimeda-
jambvāmrayaṣṭīmadhukotpalānām
tattailamāśveva dhṛtaṃ mukhena
sthairyaṃ dvijānāṃ vidadhāti sadyaḥ || 99
15irimedatvakpalaśata-
mabhinavamāpotthaṃ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā
toyāḍhakaiścaturbhi-
rniḥkvāthya caturthaśeṣeṇa || 100
tena kvāthena matimā-
ntailasyārdhāḍhakaṃ śanairvipacet
kalkairakṣasamāṃśai-
rmañjiṣṭhālodhramadhukānām || 101
irimedakhadirakaṭphala-
lākṣānyagrodhamustasūkṣmailānām
karpūrāgurupadmaka-
lavaṅgakaṃkolajātīphalānām || 102
pataṅgagairikavarāṅgakusumadhātakīnāṃ ca
siddhaṃ bhiṣagvidadhyādidaṃ mukhottheṣu rogeṣu || 103
pariśīrṇadantavidradhiśaiśiraśītādadantaharṣeṣu
krimidantadāraṇacalitapraduṣṭamāṃsāvaśīrṇeṣu
mukhadaurgandhye kāryaṃ prāgukteṣvāmayeṣu tailamidam || 104
16tailaṃ lākṣārasaṃ kṣīraṃ pṛthakprasthaṃ samaṃ pacet
caturguṇe'rimakvāthe dravaiśca palasammitaiḥ || 105
lodhrakaṭphalamañjiṣṭhāpadmakeśarapadmakaiḥ
candanotpalayaṣṭyāhvaistailaṃ gaṇḍūṣadhīraṇam || 106
dālanaṃ dantacālaṃ ca hanumokṣaṃ kapālikām
śītādaṃ pūtivaktraṃ ca aruciṃ virasāsyatām
hanyādāsyagadānetānkuryāddantānapi sthirān || 107
17bakulasya phalaṃ lodhraṃ vajravallīkuruṇṭakam
caturaṅgulavavvolavājikarṇerimāśanam || 108
eṣāṃ kaṣāyakalkābhyāṃ tailaṃ pakvaṃ mukhe dhṛtam
sthairyaṃ karoti calatāṃ dantānāṃ dhāvanena ca || 109
18elālatālavanikāphalaśītakoṣa-
koladvikāni khadirasya kṛte kaṣāye
tulyāṃśakāni daśabhāgamite nidhāya
prodbhinnakaitakapuṭe puṭavadvipācya || 110
prāgaṃśatulyaśaśinābhitadekasaṃghaṃ
piṣṭvā navena sahakārarasena hastau
liptvā yathābhilaṣitāṃ guḍikāṃ vidadhyāt
strīpuṃsayorvadanasaurabhavandhubhūtām || 111
19khadirasya tulāṃ samyagjaladroṇe vipācayet
śeṣe'ṣṭabhāge tatraiva prativāpaṃ pradāpayet || 112
jātīkarpūrapūgāni kakkolakaphalāni ca
ityeṣā guḍikā kāryā mukhasaubhāgyavardhinī
dantauṣṭhamukharogeṣu jihvātālvāmayeṣu ca || 113
20gāyatrīsāratulayerimavalkalānāṃ
sārdhaṃ tulāyugalamambughaṭaiścaturbhiḥ
niḥkvāthya pādamavaśiṣṭaṃ suvastrapūtaṃ
bhūyaḥ pacedatha śanairmṛdupāvakena || 114
tasminghanatvamupagacchati cūrṇameṣāṃ
ślakṣṇaṃ kṣipecca kavalagrahabhāgikānām
elāmṛṇālasitacandanacandanāmbu-
śyāmātamālavikaṣāghanalohayaṣṭī || 115
lajjāphalatrayarasāñjanadhātakībha-
śrīpuṣpagairikakaṭaṅkaṭakaṭphalānām
padmāhvalodhravaṭarohayavāsakānāṃ
māṃsīniśāsurabhivalkalasaṃyutānām || 116
kakkolajātiphalakoṣalavaṅgakāni
cūrṇīkṛtāni vidadhīta palāṃśakāni
śīte'vatārya ghanasāracatuḥpalaṃ ca
kṣiptvā kalāyasadṛśīrvaṭikāḥ prakuryāt || 117
śuṣkā mukhe vinihitā vinivārayanti
rogāngalauṣṭharasanādvijatālujātān
kuryurmukhe surabhitāṃ paṭutāṃ ruciṃ ca
sthairyaṃ paraṃ daśanagaṃ rasanālaghutvam || 118
Adhikāra 56
1kapitthamātulāṅgāmlaśṛṅgaverarasaiḥ śubhaiḥ
sukhoṣṇaiḥ pūrayetkarṇaṃ karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 1
śṛṅgaveraṃ ca madhu ca saindhavaṃ tailameva ca
kaṭūṣṇaṃ karṇayordeyametadvā vedanāpaham || 2
laśunārdrakaśigrūṇāṃ suraṅgyā mūlakasya ca
kadalyāḥ svarasaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kaduṣṇaḥ karṇapūraṇe
samudraphenacūrṇena yuttayā vāpyavacūrṇayet || 3
ārdrakasūryāvartaka-
śobhāñjanamūlamūlakasvarasāḥ
madhutailasaindhavayutāḥ
pṛthaguṣṇāḥ karṇaśūlaharāḥ || 4
śobhāñjanakaniryāsastilatailena saṃyutaḥ
vyaktoṣṇaḥ pūraṇaḥ karṇe karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 5
aṣṭānāmapi mūtrāṇāṃ mūtreṇānyatamena ca
koṣṇena pūrayetkarṇau karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 6
aśvatthapatrakhalvaṃ vā vidhāya bahupatrakam
tailāktamaṅgārapūrṇaṃ vidadhyācchravaṇopari || 7
yattailaṃ cyavate tasmātkhalvādaṅgāratāpitāt
tatprāptaṃ śravaṇasrotaḥ sadyo gṛhṇāti vedanām || 8
arkapatrapaṭe dagdhasnuhīpatrabhavo rasaḥ
kaduṣṇaṃ pūraṇādeva karṇaśūlanivāraṇaḥ || 9
2mahataḥ pañcamūlasya kāṇḍānyaṣṭāṅgulāni ca
kṣaumeṇāveṣṭya saṃsicya tailenādīpayettataḥ || 10
yattailaṃ cyavate tebhyaḥ sukhoṣṇaṃ tatprayojayet
jñeyaṃ taddīpikātailaṃ sadyo gṛhṇāti vedanām || 11
evaṃ kuryādbhadrakāṣṭhe kuṣṭhe kāṣṭhe ca sārale
matimāndīpikātailaṃ karṇaśūlanivāraṇam || 12
arkasya patraṃ pariṇāmapīta-
mājyena liptaṃ śikhināvataptam
āpīḍya toyaṃ śravaṇe niṣiktaṃ
nihanti śūlaṃ bahuvedanaṃ ca || 13
tīvraśūlāture karṇe saśabde kleśavāhini
bastamūtraṃ kṣipetkoṣṇaṃ saindhavenāvacūrṇitam || 14
vaṃśāvalekhasaṃyukte mūtre vājāvike bhiṣak
tailaṃ pacettena karṇaṃ pūrayetkarṇaśūlinaḥ || 15
hiṅgutumburuśuṇṭhībhiḥ sādhyaṃ tailaṃ tu sārṣapam
karṇaśūle pradhānaṃ tu pūraṇaṃ hitamucyate || 16
3bālamūlakaśuṇṭhīnāṃ kṣāro hiṅgu sanāgaram
śatapuṣpāvacākuṣṭhaṃ dāruśigrurasāñjanam || 17
sauvarcalaṃ yavakṣāraḥ sarjikodbhidasaindhavam
bhūrjagranthiviḍaṃ mustaṃ madhu śuktaṃ caturguṇam || 18
mātuluṅgarasaścaiva kadalyā rasa eva ca
tailamebhirvipaktavyaṃ karṇaśūlaharaṃ param || 19
bādhiryaṃ karṇanādaśca pūyāsrāvaśca dāruṇaḥ
pūraṇādasya tailasya krimayaḥ karṇasaṃśritāḥ || 20
kṣipraṃ vināśaṃ gacchanti kṛṣṇātreyasya śāsanāt
kṣāratailamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mukhadantāmayāpaham || 21
madhupradhānaṃ śuktaṃ tu madhuśuktaṃ tathāparam
jambīrasya phalarasaṃ pippalīmūlasaṃyutam || 22
madhubhāṇḍe vinikṣiṣya dhānyarāśau nidhāpayet
māsena tajjātarasaṃ madhuśuktamudāhṛtam || 23
karṇanāde karṇakṣveḍe kaṭutailena pūraṇam
nādabādhiryayoḥ kuryātkarṇaśūloktamauṣadham || 24
4apāmārgakṣārajale
tatkṛtakalkena sādhitaṃ tilajam
apaharati karṇanādaṃ
bādhiryaṃ cāpi pūraṇataḥ || 25
5sarjikāmūlakaṃ śuṣkaṃ hiṅgukṛṣṇāmahauṣadham
śatapuṣpā ca taistailaṃ pakvaṃ śuktacaturguṇam
praṇādaśūlabādhiryaṃ srāvaṃ cāśu vyapohati || 26
6daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
etat kalkaṃ pradāyaiva bādhirye paramauṣadham || 27
7phalaṃ vilvasya mūtreṇa piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipācayet
sājakṣīraṃ taddhi haredvādhiryaṃ karṇapūraṇe || 28
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ praṇāde nasyapūrvakaḥ
guḍanāgaratoyena nasyaṃ syādubhayorapi || 29
cūrṇaṃ pañcakaṣāyāṇāṃ kapittharasasaṃyutam
karṇasrāve praśaṃsanti pūraṇaṃ madhunā saha || 30
mālatīdalarasamadhunā pūritamathavā gavāṃ mūtraiḥ
dūreṇa parityajyate ca śravaṇayugaṃ pūtirogeṇa || 31
haritālaṃ sagomūtraṃ pūraṇaṃ pūtikarṇajit
sarjatvakcūrṇasaṃyuktaḥ kārpāsīphalajo rasaḥ
madhunā saṃyutaḥ sādhu karṇasrāve praśasyate || 32
8jambavāmrapatraṃ taruṇaṃ samāṃśaṃ
kapitthakārpāsaphalaṃ ca sārdram
kṣuṇvā rasaṃ taṃ madhunā vimiśraṃ
srāvāpahaṃ saṃpravadantitajjñāḥ || 33
etaiḥ śṛtaṃ nimbakarañjatailaṃ
sasārṣapaṃ srāvaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 34
puṭapākavidhisvinnahastiviḍjātagoṇḍakaḥ
rasaḥ satailasindhūtthaḥ karṇasrāvaharaḥ paraḥ || 35
9jambūkasya tu māṃsena kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
tasya pūraṇamātreṇa karṇanāḍī praśāmyati || 36
niśāgandhapale pakvaṃ kaṭutailaṃ palāṣṭakam
dhūstūrapatrajarase karṇanāḍījiduttamam || 37
10atha karṇapratīnāhe snehasvedau prayojayet
tato viriktaśirasaḥ kriyāṃ prāptāṃ samācaret || 38
karṇapākasya bhaiṣajyaṃ kuryātkṣatavisarpavat
nāḍīsvedo'tha vamanaṃ dhūmamūrdhvavirecanam || 39
vidhiśca kaphahā sarvaḥ karṇakaṇḍūṃ vyapohati
kledayitvā tu tailena svedena pravilāpya ca || 40
śodhayetkarṇagūthaṃ tu bhiṣak samyak śalākayā
nirguṇḍīsvarasastailaṃ sindhudhūmarajo guḍaḥ || 41
pūraṇātpūtikarṇasya śamano madhusaṃyutaḥ
jātipatrarase tailaṃ vipakvaṃ pūtikarṇajit || 42
varuṇārkakapitthāmrajambūpallavasādhitam
pūtikarṇāpahaṃ tailaṃ jātīpatrarasena vā || 43
sūryāvartakasvarasaṃ sindhuvārarasastathā
lāṅgalīmūlajarasaṃ tryūṣaṇenāvacūrṇitam || 44
pūrayetkrimikarṇaṃ tu jantūnāṃ nāśanaṃ param
krimikarṇakanāśārthaṃ krimighnaṃ yojayedvidhim || 45
vārtākudhūmaśca hitaḥ sarṣapasneha eva ca
halisūryāvartavyoṣasvarasenātipūrite || 46
karṇe patanti sahasā sarvāstu krimijātayaḥ
nīlavunnārasastailasindhukāñjikasaṃyutaḥ || 47
kaduṣṇaḥ pūraṇātkarṇe niḥśeṣaḥ krimipātanaḥ
dhūpanaḥ karṇadaurgandhye gugguluḥ śreṣṭha ucyate || 48
rājavṛkṣāditoyena surasādijalena vā
karṇaprakṣālanaṃ kāryaṃ cūrṇairetaiḥ prapūraṇam || 49
ghṛtaṃ rasāñjanaṃ nāryāḥ kṣīreṇa kṣaudrasaṃyutam
praśasyate cirotthe'pi sāsrāve pūtikarṇake || 50
11kuṣṭhahiṅguvacādāruśatāhvāviśvasaindhavaiḥ
pūtikarṇāpahaṃ tailaṃ bastamūtreṇa sādhitam || 51
12vidradhau cāpi kurvīta vidradhyuktaṃ hi bheṣajam
śatāvarīvājigandhāpayasyairaṇḍabījakaiḥ || 52
tailaṃ vipakvaṃ sakṣīraṃ pālīnāṃ puṣṭikṛtparam
guñjācūrṇayute jāte māhiṣe kṣīra udgatam || 53
navanītaṃ tadabhyaṅgātkarṇapālivivardhanam
viṣagarbhaṃ tiktatumbītailamaṣṭaguṇe kharāt || 54
mūtre pakvaṃ tadabhyaṅgātkarṇapālivivardhanam
kalkena jīvanīyena tailaṃ payasi sādhitam || 55
ānūpamāṃsakvāthena pālīpoṣaṇavardhanam
māhiṣanavanītayutaṃ saptāhaṃ dhānyarāśiparivasitaṃ || 56
navamusalikandacūrṇamṛddhikaraṃ karṇapālīnām
karṇasya durvyadhe bhūte saṃrambho vedanā bhavet || 57
tatra durvyadharohārthaṃ lepo madhvājyasaṃyutaiḥ
madhūkayavamañjiṣṭhārubumūlaiḥ samantataḥ || 58
anekadhātucchinnasya sandhiḥ karṇasya vai bhiṣak
yo yathābhiniviṣṭaḥ syāttaṃ tathā viniyojayet || 59
dhānyāmloṣṇodakābhyāṃ tu seko vātena dūṣite
raktapittena payasā śleṣmaṇā tūṣṇavāriṇā || 60
tataḥ sīvya sthiraṃ kuryātsandhiṃ bandhena vā punaḥ
madhvājyena tato'bhyajya picunā sandhiveṣṭakam
kapālacūrṇena tataścūrṇayetpathyayāthavā || 61
Adhikāra 57
1pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ syāccitrakaharītakī
sarpirguḍaḥ ṣaḍaṅgaśca yūṣaḥ pīnasaśāntaye || 1
2vyoṣacitrakatālīsatintiḍīcāmlavetasam
sacavyājājītulyāṃśamelātvakpatrapādikam || 2
vyoṣādikaṃ cūrṇamidaṃ purāṇaguḍasaṃyutam
pīnasaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ rucisvarakaraṃ param || 3
3pāṭhādvirajanīmūrvāpippalījātipallavaiḥ
dantyā ca tailaṃ saṃsiddhaṃ nasyaṃ samyaktu pīnase || 4
vyāghrīdantīvacāśigrusurasavyoṣasaindhavaiḥ
pācitaṃ nāvanaṃ tailaṃ pūtināsāgadaṃ jayet || 5
trikaṭuviḍaṅgasaindhavabṛhatīphalaśigrusurasadaṃtībhiḥ
tailaṃ gojalasiddhaṃ nasyaṃ syātpūtinasyasya || 6
kaliṅgahiṅgumaricalākṣāsurasakaṭphalaiḥ
kuṣṭhogrāśigrujantughnairavapīḍaḥ praśasyate || 7
taireva mūtrasaṃyuktaiḥ kaṭu tailaṃ vipācayet
apīnase pūtinasye śamanaṃ kīrtitaṃ param || 8
nāsāpāke pittaharaṃ vidhānaṃ
kāryaṃ sarvaṃ bāhyamabhyantaraṃ ca
haredraktaṃ kṣīrivṛkṣatvacaśca
yojyāḥ seke saghṛtāśca pradehāḥ || 9
pūyāsraraktapittaghnāḥ kaṣāyā nāvanāni ca
śuṇṭhīkuṣṭhakaṇābilvadrākṣākalkakaṣāyavat
sādhitaṃ tailamājyaṃ vā nasyaṃ kṣavathurukpraṇut || 10
dīpte roge paittike saṃvidhānaṃ
sarvaṃ kuryānmādhuraṃ śītalaṃ ca
nāsānāhe snehapānaṃ pradhānaṃ
snigdhā dhūmā mūrdhni bastiśca nityam || 11
4vātike tu pratiśyāye pibetsarpiryathākramam
pañcabhirlavaṇaiḥ siddhaṃ prathamena gaṇena ca || 12
nasyādiṣu vidhiṃ kṛtsnamavekṣetārditeritam
pittaraktotthayoḥ peyaṃ sarpirmadhurakaiḥ śṛtam || 13
pariṣekānpradehāṃśca kuryādapi ca śītalān
kaphaje sarpiṣā snigdhaṃ tilamāṣavipakvayā || 14
yavāgvā vāmayitvā vā kaphaghnaṃ kramamācaret
dārvīṅgudīnikumbhaiśca kiṇihyā surasena ca || 16
vartayo'tra kṛtā yojyā dhūmapāne yathāvidhi
athavā saghṛtānsaktūnkṛtvā mallikasampuṭe
navapratiśyāyavatāṃ dhūmaṃ vaidyaḥ prayojayet || 17
yaḥ pibati śayanakāle śayanārūḍhaḥ suśītalaṃ mahyāṃ
salilaṃ pīnasayuktaḥ sa mucyate tena rogeṇa || 18
puṭapakvaṃ jayāpatraṃ sindhutailasamanvitam
pratiśyāyeṣu sarveṣu śīlitaṃ paramauṣadham || 19
soṣaṇaṃ guḍasaṃyuktaṃ snigdhadadhyamlabhojanam
navapratiśyāyaharaṃ viśeṣātkaphapācanam || 20
pratiśyāye nave śasto yūṣaściñcādalodbhavaḥ
tataḥ pakvaṃ kaphaṃ jñātvā harecchīrṣavirecanaiḥ || 21
śiraso'bhyañjanasvedanasyakaṭvamlabhojanaiḥ
vamanairghṛtapānaiśca tānyathāsvamupācaret || 22
bhakṣayati bhuktamātre
salavaṇamutsvinnamāṣamatyuṣṇam
sa jayati sarvasamutthaṃ
cirajātaṃ ca pratiśyāyam || 23
pippalyaḥ śigrubījāni viḍaṅgaṃ maricāni ca
avapīḍaḥ praśasto'yaṃ pratiśyāyanivāraṇaḥ || 24
samūtrapiṣṭāścoddiṣṭāḥ kriyāḥ krimiṣu yojayet
nāvanārthaṃ krimighnāni bheṣajāni ca buddhimān
śeṣāṇāṃ tu vikārāṇāṃ yathāsvaṃ syāccikitsitam || 25
5raktakaravīrapuṣpaṃ jātyaśanakamallikāyāśca
etaiḥ samaṃ tu tailaṃ nāsārśonāśanaṃ śreṣṭham || 26
6gṛhadhūmakaṇādārukṣāranaktāhvasaindhavaiḥ
siddhaṃ śikharibījaiśca tailaṃ nāsārśasāṃ hitam || 27
7citrakacavikādīpyaka-
nidigdhikākarañjabījalavaṇārkeḥ
gomūtrayuktaṃ siddhaṃ
tailaṃ nāsārśasāṃ vihitam || 28
8citrakasyāmalakyāśca guḍūcyā daśamūlajam
śataṃ śataṃ rasaṃ datvā pathyācūrṇāḍhakaṃ guḍāt || 29
śataṃ paceddhanībhūte palaṃ dvādaśakaṃ kṣipet
vyopatrijātayoḥ kṣārātpalārdhamapare'hani || 30
prasthārdhaṃ madhuno dattvā yathāgnyadyādatantritaḥ
vṛddhaye'gneḥ kṣayaṃ kāsaṃ pīnasaṃ dustaraṃ krimīn
gulmodāvartadurnāmaśvāsānhanti rasāyanam || 31
Adhikāra 58
1laṅghanālepanasvedaśirāvyadhavirecanaiḥ
upācaredabhiṣyandānañjanāścyotanādibhiḥ || 1
śrīvāsātiviṣālodhraiścūrṇitairalpasaindhavaiḥ
avyakte'kṣigade kāryaṃ protasthairguṇḍanaṃ bahiḥ || 2
akṣikukṣibhavā rogāḥ pratiśyāyavraṇajvarāḥ
pañcaite pañcarātreṇa praśamaṃ yānti laṅghanāt || 3
svedaḥ pralepastiktānnaṃ seko dinacatuṣṭayam
laṅghanaṃ cākṣirogāṇāmāmānāṃ pācanāni ṣaṭ
añjanaṃ pūraṇaṃ kvāthapānamāmena śasyate || 4
dhātrīphalaniryāso navadṛkkopaṃ nihanti pūraṇataḥ
sakṣaudrasaindhavo vā śigrūdbhavapatrarasasekaḥ || 5
dārvīrasāñjanaṃ vāpi stanyayuktaṃ prapūraṇam
nihanti śīghraṃ dāhāśruvedanāḥ syandasambhavāḥ || 6
karavīrataruṇakisalaya-
cchedodbhavabahulasalilasaṃpūrṇam
nayanayugaṃ bhavati dṛḍhaṃ
sahasaiva tatkṣaṇātkupitam || 7
śikharimūlaṃ tāmrakabhājane stokasaindhavonmiśram
mastu nighṛṣṭaṃ bharaṇāddharati navaṃ locanotkopam || 8
saindhavadāruharidrāgairikapathyārasāñjanaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ
datto bahiḥ pralepo bhavatyaśeṣākṣirogaharaḥ || 9
tathā śāravakaṃ lodhraṃ ghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ biḍālakaḥ
kāryo harītakītadvaddhṛtabhṛṣṭo biḍālakaḥ || 10
śālākyakṣṇorbahirlepo biḍālaka udāhṛtaḥ || 11
girimṛccandananāgarakhaṭikāṃśayojito bahirlepaḥ
kurute vacayā miśro locanamagadaṃ na sandehaḥ || 12
bhūmyāmalakī ghṛṣṭāsasaindhava gṛhavāriyojitā tāmre
yātā ghanatvamakṣṇorjayati bahirlepataḥ pīḍām || 13
2āścyotanaṃ mārutaje kvātho bilvādibhirhitaḥ
koṣṇaḥ sairaṇḍabṛhatītarkārīmadhuśigrubhiḥ || 14
eraṇḍapallave mūle tvaci cājaṃ payaḥ śṛtam
kaṇṭakāryāśca mūleṣu sukhoṣṇaṃ secane hitam || 15
sampakve'kṣigade kāryaṃ cāñjanādikamiṣyate
praśastavartmatā cākṣṇoḥ saṃrambhāśrupraśāntatā || 16
mandavedanatā kaṇḍūḥ pakvākṣigadalakṣaṇam
añjanādividhiścāgre nikhilenābhidhāsyate || 17
bṛhatyeraṇḍamūlatvakśigrormūlaṃ sasaindhavam
ajākṣīreṇa piṣṭaṃ syādvartirvātākṣiroganut || 18
haridre madhukaṃ pathyādevadāru ca peṣayet
ājena payasā śreṣṭhamabhiṣyande tadañjanam || 19
gairikaṃ saindhavaṃ kṛṣṇāṃ nāgaraṃ ca yathottaram
piṣṭaṃ dviraṃśato'dbhirvā guḍikāñjanamiṣyate || 20
prapauṇḍarīkayaṣṭyāhvaniśāmalakapadmakaiḥ
śītairmadhusitāyuktaiḥ sekaḥ pittākṣiroganut || 21
drākṣāmadhukamañjiṣṭhājīvanīyaiḥ śṛtaṃ payaḥ
prātarāścyotanaṃ pathyaṃ śothaśūlākṣirogiṇām || 22
3nimbasya patraiḥ parilipya lodhraṃ
svedāgninā cūrṇamathāpi kalkam
āścyotanaṃ mānuṣadugdhayuktaṃ
pittāsravātāpahamagryamuktam || 23
kaphaje laṅganaṃ svedo nasyaṃ tiktānnabhojanam
tīkṣṇaiḥ pradhamanaṃ kuryāttīkṣṇaiścaivopanāhanam || 24
phaṇijjakāsphotakapītabilva-
pattūrapīlusurasārjabhaṅgaiḥ
svedaṃ vidadhyādathavā pralepaṃ
barhiṣṭhaśuṇṭhīsuradārukuṣṭhaiḥ || 25
śuṇṭhīnimbadalaiḥ piṇḍaḥ sukhoṣṇaiḥ svalpasaindhavaiḥ
dhāryaścakṣuṣi saṃlepācchothakaṇḍūrujāpahaḥ || 26
valkalaṃ pārijātasya tailakāñjikasaindhavam
kapodbhūtākṣiśūlaghnaṃ tarughnaṃ kuliśaṃ tathā || 27
sasaindhavaṃ lodhramathājyabhṛṣṭaṃ
sauvīrapiṣṭaṃ sitavastrabandham
āścyotanaṃ tannayanasya kuryāt
kaṇḍūṃ ca dāhaṃ ca rujāṃ ca hanyāt || 28
4snigdhairuṣṇaiśca vātotthaḥ pittajo mṛduśītalaiḥ
tīkṣṇarukṣoṣṇaviśadaiḥ praśāmyanti kaphātmakāḥ || 29
tīkṣṇoṣṇamṛduśītānāṃ vyatyāsātsānnipātikaḥ
tirīṭatriphalāyaṣṭīśarkarābhadramustakaḥ
piṣṭaiḥ śītāmbunā seko raktābhiṣyandanāśanaḥ || 30
kaśerumadhukānāṃ ca cūrṇamambarasaṃyutam
nyastamapsvantarīkṣyāsu hitamāścyotanaṃ bhavet || 31
dārvīpaṭolamadhukaṃ sanimbaṃ padmakotpalam
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ caitāni pacettoye caturguṇe || 32
vipācya pādaśeṣaṃ tu tatpunaḥ kuḍavaṃ pacet
śītībhūte tatra madhu dadyātpādāṃśikaṃ tataḥ || 33
rasakriyaiṣā dāhāśrurogaraktarujāpahā
tiktasya sarpiṣaḥ pānaṃ bahuśaśca virecanam || 34
akṣṇorapi samantācca pātanaṃ tu jalaukasaḥ
pittābhiṣyandaśamano vidhiścāpyupapāditaḥ || 35
śigrupallavaniryāsaḥ sughṛṣṭastāmrasampuṭe
ghṛtena dhūpito hanti śothagharṣāśruvedanāḥ || 36
piṣṭairnimbasya patrairativimalatarai-
rjātisindhūtthamiśrā
antargarbhaṃ dadhānā paṭutaraguḍikā-
piṣṭalodhreṇa bhṛṣṭā
tūlaiḥ sauvīrasārdrairatiśayamṛdubhi-
rveṣṭitā sā samantā-
ccakṣuḥkopapraśāntiṃ ciramuparidṛśo-
rbhrāmyamāṇā karoti || 37
5bilvapatrarasaḥ pūtaḥ saindhavājyena cānvitaḥ
śulbe varāṭikāghṛṣṭo dhūpito gomayāgninā || 38
payasāloḍitaścākṣṇoḥ pūraṇācchothaśūlanut
abhiṣyande'dhimanthe ca srāve rakte ca śasyate || 39
salavaṇakaṭutailaṃ kāñjikaṃ kāṃsyapātre
ghanitamupalaghṛṣṭaṃ dhūpitaṃ gomayāgnau
sapavanakaphakopaṃ chāgadugdhāvasiktaṃ
jayati nayanaśūlaṃ srāvaśothaṃ sarāgam || 40
tarusthaviddhāmalakarasaḥ sarvākṣiroganut
purāṇaṃ sarvathā sarpiḥ sarvanetrāmayāpaham || 41
ayameva vidhiḥ sarvo manthādiṣvapi śasyate
aśāntau sarvathā manthe bhruvorupari dāhayet || 42
jalaukaḥpātanaṃ śastaṃ netrapāke virecanam
śirāvyadhaṃ vā kurvīta sekā lepāśca śukravat || 43
6vibhītakaśivādhātrīpaṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
kvātho guggulunā peyaḥ śothaśūlākṣipākahā || 44
pīluṃ ca savraṇaṃ śukraṃ rāgādīṃścāpi nāśayet
etaiścāpi ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ rogāṃstāṃśca vyapohati || 45
7āṭarūṣābhayānimbadhātrīmustākṣakūlakaiḥ
raktasrāvaṃ kaphaṃ hanti cakṣuṣyaṃ vāsakādikam || 46
8vāsā ghanaṃ nimbapaṭolapatraṃ
tiktāmṛtācandanavatsakatvak
kaliṅgadārvī dahanaṃ ca śuṇṭhī-
bhūnimbadhātryāvabhayāvibhītam || 47
śyāmāyavakvāthamathāṣṭabhāgaṃ
pibedimaṃ pūrvadine kaṣāyam
taimiryakaṇḍūpaṭalārbudaṃ ca
śukraṃ nihanyādvraṇamavraṇaṃ ca || 48
pīluṃ ca kācaṃ ca mahārajaśca
naktāndhyarāgaṃ śvayathuṃ saśūlam
nihanti sarvānnayanāmayāṃśca
vāsādirepaḥ prathitaprabhāvaḥ || 49
9pathyāstisrobibhītakyaḥ ṣaṭū dhātryodvādaśaivatu
prasthārdhe salile kvāthamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam || 50
pītvābhiṣyandamāsrāvaṃ rāgaśca timiraṃ jayet || 51
saṃrambharāgaśūlāśrunāśanaṃ dṛkprasādanam
netre tvabhihate kuryācchītamāścyotanādikam || 52
dṛṣṭiprasādajananaṃ vidhimāśu kuryāt
snigdhairhimaiśca madhuraiśca tathā prayogaiḥ
svedāgnidhūmabhayaśokarujābhitāpai-
rabhyāhatāmapi tathaiva bhiṣakcikitset || 53
āgantudoṣaṃ prasamīkṣya kāryaṃ
vaktroṣmaṇā sveditamāditastu
āścyotanaṃ strīpayasā ca sadyo
yaccāpi pittakṣatajāpahaṃ syāt || 54
sūryoparāgānalavidyudādi-
vilokanenopahatekṣaṇasya
santarpaṇaṃ snigdhahimādikāryaṃ
sāyaṃ niṣevyāstriphalāprayogāḥ || 55
niśābdatriphalādārvī sitāmadhukasaṃyutam
abhighātākṣiśūlaghnaṃ nārīkṣīreṇa pūraṇam || 56
itkuṭāṅkurajastadvatsvaraso netrapūraṇam
ājaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīrapātraṃ madhukaṃ cotpalāni ca || 57
jīvakarṣabhakau cāpi piṣṭvā sarpirvipācayet
sarvanetrābhighāteṣu sarpiretatpraśasyate || 58
10saindhavaṃ dāru śuṇṭhī ca mātuluṅgī raso ghṛtam
stanyodakābhyāṃ kartavyaṃ śuṣkapāke tadañjanam || 59
vātābhiṣyandavaccānyadvāte mārutaparyaye
pūrvabhuktaṃ hitaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ cāpyatha bhojane || 60
vṛkṣādanyāṃ kapitthe ca pañcamūle mahatyapi
sakṣīraṃ karkaṭarase siddhaṃ cāpi pibed ghṛtam || 61
abhiṣyandamadhīmanthaṃ raktotthamathavārjunam
śirotpātaṃ śirāharṣamanyāṃścākṣibhavāngadān || 62
snigdhasyājyena kaumbhena śirāvyādhaiḥ śamaṃ nayet
amlādhyuṣitaśāntyarthaṃ kuryāllepānsuśītalān || 63
taindukaṃ traiphalaṃ sarpirjīrṇaṃ vā kevalaṃ hitam
śirāvyadhaṃ vinā kāryaḥ pittasyandaharo vidhiḥ || 64
sarpiḥ kṣaudrāñjanaṃ ca syācchirotpātasya bheṣajam
tadvatsaindhavakāsīsaṃ stanyapiṣṭaṃ ca pūjitam || 65
śirāharṣe'ñjanaṃ kuryātphāṇitaṃ madhusaṃyutam
madhunā tārkṣyaśailaṃ vā kāsīsaṃ vā samākṣikam || 66
11vraṇaśukrapraśāntyarthaṃ paḍaṅgaṃ gugguluṃ pibet
katakasya phalaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tindukaṃ rūpyameva ca || 67
kāṃsye nighṛṣṭaṃ stanyena kṣataśukrārtirāgajit
candanaṃ gairikaṃ lākṣāmālatī kalikāsamā || 68
braṇaśukraharī vartiḥ śoṇitasya prasādanī
śirayā vā haredraktaṃ jalaukābhiśca locanāt || 69
akṣamajjāñjanaṃ sāyaṃ stanyena śukranāśanam
ekaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ ca chāgīkṣīrāvasecitam || 70
rāgāsruvedanāṃ hanyātkṣatapākātyayājakāḥ
tutthakaṃ vāriṇā yuktaṃ śukraṃ hantyakṣipūraṇāt || 71
samudraphenadakṣāṇḍatvaksindhūtthaiḥ samākṣikaiḥ
śigrubījayutairvartiḥ śukraghnī śīgruvāriṇā || 72
dhātrīphalaṃ nimbapaṭolapatraṃ
yaṣṭyāhvalodhraṃ khadiraṃ tilāśca
kvāthaḥ suśīto nayane nipiktaḥ
sarvaprakāraṃ vinihanti śukram || 73
12kṣuṇṇapunnāgapatreṇa paribhāvitavāriṇā
śyāmākvāthe'mbunā vātha secanaṃ kusumāpaham || 74
dakṣāṇḍatvakśilāśaṅkhakācacandanagairikaiḥ
tulyairañjanayogo'yaṃ puṣpārmādivilekhanaḥ || 75
śirīṣabījamaricapippalīsaindhavairapi
śukre pragharṣaṇaṃ kāryamathavā saindhavena ca || 76
bahuśaḥ palāśakusuma-
svarasaiḥ paribhāvitā jayatyacirāt
naktāhvavījavartiḥ
kusumacayaṃ dṛkṣu cirajamapi || 77
saindhavatriphalākṛṣṇākaṭukāśaṅkhanābhayaḥ
satāmrarajaso vartiḥ piṣṭvā śukravināśinī || 78
candanaṃ saindhavaṃ pathyāpalāśataruśoṇitam
kramavṛddhamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ śukrārmādivilekhanam || 79
13dantairhastivarāhoṣṭragavāśvājakharodbhavaiḥ
saśaṅkhamauktikāmbhodhiphenairmaricapādikaiḥ
kṣataśukramapi vyādhiṃ dantavartirnivartayet || 80
śaṅkhasya bhāgāścatvārastato'rdhena manaḥśilā
manaḥśilārdhaṃ maricaṃ maricārdhena saindhavam || 81
etaccūrṇāñjanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ śukrayostimireṣu ca
piccaṭe madhunā yojyamarbude mastunā tathā || 82
tāpyaṃ madhukasāro vā bījaṃ cākṣasya saindhavam
madhunāñjanayogāḥ syuścatvāraḥ śukraśāntaye || 83
vaṭakṣīreṇa saṃyuktaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ karpūrajaṃ rajaḥ
kṣipramañjanato hanti śukraṃ cāpi ghanonnatam || 84
triphalāmajjamaṅgalyāmadhukaṃ raktacandanam
pūraṇaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ kṣataśukrājakāśrujit || 85
tālasya nārīkelasya tathaivāruṣkarasya ca
karīrasya ca vaṃśānāṃ kṛtvā kṣāraṃ parisrutam || 86
karabhāsthikṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣāreṇa paribhāvitam
saptakṛtvo'ṣṭakṛtvo vā ślakṣṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ tu kārayet || 87
etacchuṣkeṣvasādhyeṣu kṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam
yāni śukrāṇi sādhyāni teṣāṃ paramamañjanam || 88
14paṭolaṃ kaṭukāṃ dārvīṃ nimbaṃ vāsāṃ phalatrikam
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ trāyantīṃ ca palonmitām || 89
prasthamāmalakānāṃ ca kvāthayedulvane'mbhasi
pādaśeṣe rase tasminghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 90
kalkairbhūnimbakuṭajamustayaṣṭyāhvacandanaiḥ
sapippalīkaistatsiddhaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ śukrayorhitam || 91
ghrāṇakarṇākṣivartmatvaṅmukharogavraṇāpaham
kāmalājvaravīsarpagaṇḍamālāharaṃ param || 92
15kṛṣṇāviḍaṅgamadhuyaṣṭikasindhujanma-
viśvauṣadhaiḥ payasi siddhamidaṃ chagalyāḥ
tailaṃ nṛṇāṃ timiraśukraśiro'kṣiśūla-
pākātyayāñjayati nasyavidhau prayuktam || 93
ajakāṃ pārśvato viddhvā sūcyā visrāvya codakam
vraṇaṃ gomayacūrṇena pūrayetsarpiṣā saha || 94
saindhavaṃ vājipādaṃ ca gorocanasamanvitam
śelutvagrasasaṃyuktaṃ pūraṇaṃ cājakāpaham || 95
16śaśakasya śiraḥkalke śeṣāṅgakvathite jale
ghṛtasya kuḍavaṃ pakvaṃ pūraṇaṃ cājakāpaham || 96
śaśakasya kaṣāye ca sarpiṣaḥ kuḍavaṃ pacet
ṣṭīprapauṇḍarīkasya kalkena payasā samam || 97
chagalyāḥ pūraṇācchukrakṣatapākātyayājakāḥ
hanti bhrūśaṅkhaśūlaṃ ca dāharāgānaśeṣataḥ || 98
17triphalā ghṛtaṃ madhu yavāḥ
pādābhyaṅgaḥ śatāvarī mudgāḥ
cakṣuṣyaḥ saṃkṣepāt
vargaḥ kathito bhiṣagbhirayam || 99
lihyātsadā vā triphalāṃ sucūrṇitāṃ
madhupragāḍhāṃ timire'tha pittaje
samīraje tailayutāṃ kaphātmake
madhupragāḍhāṃ vidadhīta yuktitaḥ || 100
kalkaḥ kvātho'thavā cūrṇaṃ triphalāyā niṣevitam
madhunā haviṣā vāpi samastatimirāntakṛt || 101
yastraiphalaṃ cūrṇamapathyavarjī
sāyaṃ samaśnāti havirmadhubhyām
sa mucyate netragatairvikārai-
rbhṛtyairyathā kṣīṇadhano manuṣyaḥ || 102
saghṛtaṃ vā varākvāthaṃ śīlayettimirāmayī
jātā rogā vinaśyanti na bhavanti kadācana
triphalāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa prātarnayanadhāvanāt || 103
jalagaṇḍūṣaiḥ prātarbahuśo'mbhobhiḥ prapūrya mukharandhram
nirdayamukṣannakṣi kṣapayati timirāṇi nāsadyaḥ || 104
bhuktvā pāṇitalaṃ ghṛṣṭvā cakṣuṣoryatpradīyate
acireṇaiva tadvāri timirāṇi vyapohati || 105
18katakasya phalaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ sitā
pheno rasāñjanaṃ kṣaudraṃ viḍaṅgāni manaḥśilā
kukkuṭāṇḍakapālāni vartireṣā vyapohati || 106
timiraṃ paṭalaṃ kācamarma śukraṃ tathaiva ca
kaṇḍūkledārbudaṃ hanti malaṃ cāśu sukhāvatī || 107
19harītakī vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī maricāni ca
bibhītakasya majjā ca śaṅkhanābhirmanaḥ śilā || 108
sarvametatsamaṃ kṛtvā chāgakṣīreṇa peṣayet
nāśayettimiraṃ kaṇḍūṃ paṭalānyarbudāni ca || 109
adhikāni ca māṃsāni yaśca rātrau na paśyati
api dvivārṣikaṃ puṣpaṃ māsenaikena sādhayet || 110
vartiścandrodayā nāma nṛṇāṃ dṛṣṭiprasādanī || 111
harītakī haridrā ca pippalyo lavaṇāni ca
kaṇḍūtimirajidvartirna kvacitpratihanyate || 112
20aśītistilapuṣpāṇi ṣaṣṭiḥpippalītaṇḍulāḥ
jātīkusumapañcāśanmaricāni ca ṣoḍaśa
eṣā kumārikā vartirgataṃ cakṣurnivārayet || 113
21triphalākukkuṭāṇḍatvakkāsīsamayaso rajaḥ
nīlotpalaṃ viḍaṅgāni phenaṃ ca saritāṃ pateḥ || 114
ājena payasā piṣṭvā bhāvayettāmrabhājane
saptarātraṃ sthitaṃ bhūyaḥ piṣṭvā kṣīreṇa vartayet || 115
eṣā dṛṣṭipradā vartirandhasyābhinnacakṣuṣaḥ
candanatriphalāpūgapalāśataruśoṇitaiḥ || 116
jalapiṣṭairiyaṃ vartiraśeṣatimirāpahā
niśādvayābhayāmāṃsīkuṣṭhakṛṣṇāvicūrṇitā || 117
sarvanetrāmayānhanyādetatsaugatamañjanam
vyoṣotpalābhayākuṣṭhatārkṣyairvarti kṛtāḥ haret || 118
arbudaṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ timirārmāśrunisrutim
tryūṣaṇaṃ triphalāvakrasaindhavālamanaḥśilāḥ || 119
kledopadehakaṇḍūghnī vartiḥ śastā kaphāpahā
ekaguṇā māgadhikā
dviguṇā ca harītakī salilapiṣṭā || 120
vartiriyaṃ nayanasukhā-
rmatimirapaṭalakācāśruharī || 121
22añjanaṃ śvetamaricaṃ pippalī madhuyaṣṭikā
bibhītakasya madhyaṃ tu śaṅkhanābhirmanaḥśilā || 122
etāni samabhāgāni ajākṣīreṇa peṣayet
chāyāśuṣkāṃ kṛtāṃ vartiṃ netreṣu ca prayojayet || 123
arbudaṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ timiraṃ raktarājikām
adhimāṃsaṃ malaṃ caiva yaśca śatrau na paśyati
vartiścandraprabhā nāma jātāndhyamapi śodhayet || 124
23triphalāvyoṣasindhūtthayaṣṭītuttharasāñjanam
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ jantughnaṃ lodhraṃ tāmraṃ caturdaśa || 125
dravyāṇyetāni saṃcūrṇya vartiḥ kāryā nabho'mbunā
nāgārjunena likhitā stambhe pāṭaliputrake || 126
nāśanī timirāṇāṃ ca paṭalānāṃ tathaiva ca
sadyaḥ prakopaṃ stanyena striyā vijayate dhruvam || 127
kiṃśukasvarasenātha pīlupuṣpakaraktataḥ
añjanāllodhratoyena cāsannatimiraṃ jayet || 128
cirasaṃcchādite netre bastamūtreṇa saṃyutā
unmīlayatyakṛcchreṇa prasādaṃ cādhigacchati || 129
24pippalīṃ satagarotpalapatrāṃ
vartayetsamadhukāṃ saharidrām
etayā satatamañjayitavyaṃ
yaḥ suparṇasamamicchati cakṣuḥ || 130
vyoṣāyaścūrṇasindhūtthatriphalāñjanasaṃyutā
guḍikā jalapiṣṭeyaṃ kokilā timirāpahā || 131
trīṇi kaṭūni karañjaphalāni
dve rajanī sahasaindhavakaṃ ca
bilvatarorvaruṇasya ca mūlaṃ
vāricaraṃ daśamaṃ pravadanti || 132
hanti tamastimiraṃ paṭalaṃ ca
picciṭaśukrakamathārjunaṃ ca
añjanakaṃ janarañjanakaṃ ca
dṛkca na naśyati varṣaśataṃ ca || 133
25nīlotpalaṃ viḍaṅgāni pippalī raktacandanam
añcanaṃ saindhavaṃ caiva sadyastimiranāśanam || 134
patragairikakarpūrayaṣṭīnīlotpalāñjanam
nāgakeśarasaṃyuktamaśeṣatimirāpaham || 135
śaṅkhasya caturo bhāgāstadardhena manaḥśilā
manaḥśilārdhaṃ maricaṃ maricārdhena pippalī || 136
vāriṇā timiraṃ hanti arbudaṃ hanti mastunā
picciṭaṃ madhunā hanti strīkṣīreṇa taduttamam || 137
haridrānimbapatrāṇi pippalyo maricāni ca
bhadramustaṃ viḍaṅgāni saptamaṃ viśvabheṣajam || 138
gomūtreṇa guḍī kāryā chāgamūtreṇa cāñjanam
jvarāṃśca nikhilānhanti bhūtāveśaṃ tathaiva ca || 139
vāriṇā timiraṃ hanti madhunā paṭalaṃ tathā
naktāndhyaṃ bhṛṅgarājena nārīkṣīreṇa puṣpakam
śiśireṇa parisrāvamarbudaṃ picciṭaṃ tathā || 140
26saṃgṛhyoparatānalaktakarase-
nāmṛjya gaṇḍūpadān
lākṣārañjitatūlavartinihitān
yaṣṭīmadhūnmiśritān
prakṣālyottamasarpiṣānalaśikhā-
santāpajaṃ kajjalaṃ
dūrāsannaniśāndhyasarvatimira-
pradhvaṃsakṛccoditam || 141
bhūmau nighṛṣṭayāṅgulyā añjanaṃ śamanaṃ tayoḥ
timirakācārmaharaṃ dhūmikāyāśca nāśanam || 142
triphalābhṛṅgamahauṣadhamadhvājyacchāgapayasi gomūtre
nāgaṃ sapta niṣiktaṃ karoti garuḍopamaṃ cakṣuḥ || 143
triphalāsalilayoge bhṛṅgarājadrave ca
haviṣi ca viṣakalke kṣāra āje madhūgre
pratidinamatha taptaṃ saptadhā sīsamekaṃ
praṇihitamatha paścātkārayettacchalākām || 144
saviturudayakāle sāñjanā vyañjanā vā
karakarikasametā narmapaicciṭyarogān
asitasitasamutthānsandhivartmābhijātān
harati nayanarogānsevyamānā śalākā || 145
27ciñcāpatrarasaṃ nidhāya vimale
caudumbare bhājane
mūlaṃ tatra nighṛṣṭasaindhavayutaṃ
gauñjyaṃ viśoṣyātape
taccūrṇaṃ vimalāñjanena sahitaṃ
netrāñjane śasyate
kācārmārjunapicciṭe satimire
srāvaṃ ca nirvāsayet || 146
citrāṣaṣṭhīyoge saindhavamamalaṃ vicūrṇya tenākṣi
śamamañjanena timiraṃ gacchati varṣādasādhyamapi || 147
dadyāduśīraniryūhe cūrṇitaṃ kaṇasaindhavam
tacchrutaṃ saghṛtaṃ bhūyaḥ pacetkṣaudraṃ kṣipeddhane || 148
śīte tasminhitamidaṃ sarvaje timire'ñjanam || 149
dhātrīrasāñjanakṣaudrasarpirbhistu rasakriyā
pittānilākṣirogaghnī taimiryapaṭalāpahā || 150
28śṛṅgaveraṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ yaṣṭītailena miśritam
nasyametena dātavyaṃ mahāpaṭalanāśanam || 151
liṅganāśe kaphodbhūte yathāvadvidhipūrvakam
viddhvā daivakṛte chidre netraṃ stanyena pūrayet || 152
tato dṛṣṭeṣu rūpeṣu śalākāmāharecchanaiḥ
nayanaṃ sarpiṣābhyajya vastrapaṭṭena veṣṭayet || 153
tato gṛhe nirābādhe śayitottāna eva ca
udgārakāsakṣavathuṣṭhīvanotkampanāni ca || 154
tatkālaṃ nācaredūrdhvaṃ yantraṇāsnehapotavat
tryahāttryahāddhārayettu kaṣāyairanilāpahaiḥ || 155
vāyorbhayāttryahādūrdhvaṃ snehayedakṣi pūrvavat
daśarātraṃ tu saṃyamya hitaṃ dṛṣṭiprasādanam || 156
paścātkarma ca seveta laghvannaṃ cāpi mātrayā
rāgaścoṣorvudaṃ śotho vudbudaṃ kekarākṣitā || 157
adhimanthādayaścānye rogāḥ syurduṣṭavedhajāḥ
ahitācārato vāpi yathāsvaṃ tānupācaret || 158
rujāyāmakṣiroge vā bhūyo yogānnivodha me
29kalkitāḥ saghṛtā dūrvāyavagairikaśārivāḥ || 159
sukhalepāḥ prayoktavyā rujā rogopaśāntaye
payasyāśārivāpatramañjiṣṭhāmadhukairapi || 160
ajākṣīrānvitairlepaḥ sukhoṣṇaḥ pathya ucyate
vātaghnasiddhe payasi siddhaṃ sarpiścaturguṇe || 161
kākolyādipratīvāpaṃ prayuñjyātsarvakarmasu
śāmyatyevaṃ na cecchūlaṃ snigdhasvinnasya mokṣayet || 162
tataḥ śirāṃ daheccāpi matimānkīrtitāṃ yathā
dṛṣṭerataḥ prasādārthamañjane śṛṇu me śubhe || 163
30meṣaśṛṅgasya patrāṇi śirīṣadhavayorapi
mālatyāścāpi tulyāni muktāvaidūryameva ca || 164
ajākṣīreṇa saṃpiṣya tāmre saptāhamāvapet
praṇidhāya tu tadvartiṃ yojayedañjane bhiṣak || 165
srotojaṃ vidrumaṃ phenaṃ sāgarasya manaḥśilā
maricāni ca tadvartiṃ kārayetpūrvavadbhiṣak || 166
rasāñjanaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ tālīsaṃ svarṇagairikam
gośakṛdrasasaṃyuktaṃ pittopahatadṛṣṭaye || 167
31nalinotpalakiñjalkaṃ gośakṛdrasasaṃyutam
guḍikāñjanametatsyāddinarātryandhayorhitam || 168
nadījaśaṅkhatrikaṭūnyathāñjanaṃ
manaḥśilā dve ca niśe gavāṃ śakṛt
sacandaneyaṃ guḍikātha cāñjane
praśasyate rātridineṣvapaśyatām || 169
kaṇācchāgayakṛnmadhye paktvā tadrasapeṣitā
acirāddhanti naktāndhyaṃ tadvatsakṣaudramūṣaṇam || 170
pacettu gaudhaṃ hi yakṛtprayuktaṃ
prakalpitaṃ māgadhikābhirambunā
niṣevitaṃ tatsakṛdañjanena
nihanti naktāndhyamasaṃśayaṃ khalu || 171
dadhnā nighṛṣṭaṃ maricaṃ rātryandhyāñjanamuttamam
tāmbūlayuktaṃ khadyotabhakṣaṇaṃ ca tadarthakṛt || 172
śapharīmatsyakṣāro naktāndhyaṃ cāñjanādvinihanti
tadvadrāmaṭhaṭaṅkaṇakarṇamalaṃ caikaśo'ñjanānmadhunā || 173
keśarājānvitaṃ siddhaṃ matsyaṇḍaṃ hanti bhakṣitam
naktāndhyaṃ niyataṃ nṝṇāṃ saptāhātpathyasevinām || 174
32triphalākvāthakalkābhyāṃ sapayaskaṃ śṛtaṃ ghṛtam
timirāṇyacirāddhanti pītametanniśāmukhe || 175
33triphalāyā rasaprasthaṃ prasthaṃ bhṛṅgarasasya ca
vṛṣasya ca rasaprasthaṃ śatāvaryāśca tatsamam || 176
ajākṣīraṃ guḍūcyāśca āmalakyā rasaṃ tathā
prasthaṃ prasthaṃ samāhṛtya sarvairebhirghṛtaṃ pacet || 177
kalkaḥ kaṇāsitādrākṣātriphalānīlamutpalam
madhukaṃ kṣīrakākolī madhuparṇī nidigdhikā || 178
tatsādhusiddhaṃ vijñāya śubhe bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
ūrdhvapānamadhaḥpānaṃ madhyapānaṃ ca śasyate || 179
yāvanto netrarogāstānpānādevāpakarṣati
sarakte raktaduṣṭe ca rakte cātisrute'pi ca || 180
naktāndhye timire kāce nīlikāpaṭalārbude
abhiṣyande'dhimanthe ca pakṣakope sudāruṇe || 181
netrarogeṣu sarveṣu vātapittakapheṣu ca
adṛṣṭiṃ mandadṛṣṭiṃ ca kaphavātapradūṣitām || 182
sravato vātapittābhyāṃ sakaṇḍvāsannadūradṛk
gṛdhradṛṣṭikaraṃ sadyo balavarṇāgnivardhanam
sarvanetrāmayaṃ hanyāttriphalādyaṃ mahaddhṛtam || 183
34triphalā tryūṣaṇaṃ drākṣā madhukaṃ kaṭurohiṇī
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ sūkṣmailā viḍaṅgaṃ nāgakeśaram || 184
nīlotpalaṃ śārive dve candanaṃ rajanīdvayam
kārṣikaiḥ payasā tulyaṃ triguṇaṃ triphalārasam || 185
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedetatsarvanetrarujāpaham
timiraṃ doṣamāsrāvaṃ kāmalāṃ kācamarbudam || 186
vīsarpaṃ pradaraṃ kaṇḍūṃ raktaṃ śvayathumeva ca
khālityaṃ palitaṃ caiva keśānāṃ patanaṃ tathā || 187
viṣamajvaramarmāṇi śukraṃ cāśu vyapohati
anye ca bahavo rogā netrajā ye ca vartmajāḥ
tānsarvānnāśayatyāśu bhāskarastimiraṃ yathā || 188
na caivāsmātparaṃ kiñcidṛṣibhiḥ kāśyapādibhiḥ
dṛṣṭiprasādanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ yathā syāttraiphalaṃ ghṛtam || 189
phalatrikābhīrukaṣāyasiddhaṃ
kalkena yaṣṭīmadhukasya yuktam
sarpiḥ samaṃ kṣaudracaturthabhāgaṃ
hanyāttridoṣaṃ timiraṃ pravṛddham || 190
35bhṛṅgarājarasaprasthe yaṣṭīmadhupalena ca
tailasya kuḍavaṃ pakvaṃ sadyo dṛṣṭiṃ prasādayet
nasyādvalīpalitaghnaṃ māsenaitanna saṃśayaḥ || 191
gavāṃ śakṛtkvāthavipakvamuttamaṃ
hitaṃ ca tailaṃ timireṣu nastataḥ
ghṛtaṃ hitaṃ kevalameva paittike
tathānutailaṃ pavanāsṛgutthayoḥ || 192
36jīvakarpabhakau medā-
drākṣāṃśumatī nidigdhikā bṛhatī
madhukaṃ balā viḍaṅgaṃ
mañjiṣṭhā śarkarā tathā rāsnā || 193
nīlotpalaṃ śvadaṃṣṭrā prapauṇḍarīkaṃ punarnavā lavaṇam
pippalyaḥ sarveṣāṃ bhāgairakṣāṃśikaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ || 194
tailaṃ yadi vā sarpirdatvā kṣīraṃ caturguṇaṃ pakvam
timiraṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ naktāndhyaṃ cārvudaṃ tathāndhyaṃ ca || 195
śvetaṃ ca liṅganāśaṃ nāśayati paraṃca nīlikāvyaṅgam
mukhanāsādaurgandhyaṃ palitaṃ cākālajaṃ hanustambham || 196
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ śoṣaṃ hikkāṃ stambhaṃ tathātyayaṃ netre
mukhajairkṣya?mardhabhedaṃ rogaṃ vāhugrahaṃ śiraḥstambham
rogānathordhvajatroḥ sarvānacireṇa nāśayati || 197
nasyārthaṃ kuḍavaṃ tailaṃ paktavyaṃ nṛpavallabhe
akṣāṃśaiḥ śāṇikaiḥ kalkairanye bhṛṅgāditailavat || 198
37tailasyapacetkuḍavaṃmadhukasya palena kalkapiṣṭena
āmalakarasaprasthaṃ kṣīraprasthena saṃyutaṃ kṛtvā || 199
abhijinnāmnā tailaṃ timiraṃ hanyānmuniproktam
vimalāṃ kurute dṛṣṭiṃ naṣṭāmapyānayedidaṃ śīghraṃ || 200
38arma tu chedanīyaṃ syātkṛṣṇāprāptaṃ bhavedyadā
baḍiśāviddhamunnamya tribhāgaṃ cātra varjayet || 201
pippalī triphalā lākṣā lauhacūrṇaṃ sasaindhavam
bhṛṅgarājarase piṣṭaṃ guḍikāñjanamiṣyate || 202
arma satimiraṃ kācaṃ kaṇḍūṃ śukraṃ tadarjunam
ajakāṃ netrarogāṃśca hanyānniravaśepataḥ || 203
puṣpākhyatārkṣyajasitodadhiphenaśaṅkha-
sindhūtthagairikaśilāmaricaiḥ samāṃśaiḥ
piṣṭaiśca mākṣikarasena rasakriyeyaṃ
hantyarmakācatimirārjunavartmarogān || 204
kauntasya sarpiṣaḥ pānairvirekālepasecanaiḥ
svāduśītaiḥ praśamayecchuktikāmañjanaistataḥ || 205
pravālamuktāvaidūryaśaṅkhasphaṭikacandanam
suvarṇarajataṃ kṣaudramañjanaṃ śuktikāpaham || 206
śaṅkhaḥ kṣaudreṇa saṃyuktaḥ katakaḥ saindhavena vā
sitayārṇavapheno vā pṛthagañjanamarjune || 207
paittaṃ vidhimaśeṣeṇa kuryādarjunaśāntaye
vaidehī śvetamaricaṃ saindhavaṃ nāgaraṃ samam || 208
mātuluṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭamañjanaṃ piṭakāpaham || 209
39bhittvopanāhaṃ kaphajaṃ pippalīmadhusaindhavaiḥ
vilikhenmaṇḍalāgreṇa pracchayedvā samantataḥ || 210
pathyākṣadhātrīphalamadhyabījai-
stridvyekabhāgairvidadhīta vartim
tayāñjayedaśrumatipragāḍha-
makṣṇorharetkaṣṭamapi prakopam || 211
srāveṣu triphalākvāthaṃ yathādoṣaṃ prayojayet
kṣaudreṇājyena pippalyā miśraṃ vidhyecchirāṃ tathā || 212
triphalāmūtrakāsīsasaindhavaiḥ sarasāñjanaiḥ
rasakriyā krimigranthau bhinne syātpratisāraṇam || 213
40svinnāṃ bhittvā viniṣpīḍya bhinnāmañjananāmikām
śilailānatasindhūtthaiḥ sakṣaudraiḥ pratisārayet || 214
rasāñjanamadhubhyāṃ ca bhinnāṃ vā śastrakarmavit
pratisāryāñjanairyuñjyāduṣṇairdīpaśikhodbhavaiḥ || 215
svedayeddhṛṣṭayāṅgulyā haredraktaṃ jalaukasā
rocanākṣāratutthāni pippalyaḥ kṣaudrameva ca || 216
pratisāraṇamekaikaṃ bhinnena gaṇa iṣyate
nimiṣe nāsayā peyaṃ sarpistena ca pūraṇam || 217
svedayitvā bisagranthi chidrāṇyasya nirāśrayam
pakvaṃ bhittvā tu śastreṇa saindhavenāvacūrṇayet || 218
vartmāvalekhaṃ bahuśastadvacchoṇitamokṣaṇam
punaḥ punarvirekaṃ ca pillarogāturo bhajet || 219
pillī snigdho vametpūrvaṃ śirāvyādhaṃ srute'sṛji
śilārasāñjanavyoṣagopittaiścakṣuraṃjayet || 220
haritālavacādārumurasārasapeṣitam
abhayārasapiṣṭaṃ vā tagaraṃ pillanāśanam || 221
bhāvitaṃ bastamūtreṇa sasnehaṃ devadāru ca
kākamācīphalaikena ghṛtayuktena buddhimān || 222
dhūpayetpillarogārtaṃ patanti krimayo'cirāt
41rasāñjanaṃ sarjaraso jātīpuṣpaṃ manaḥ śilā || 223
samudrapheno lavaṇaṃ gairikaṃ maricāni ca
etatsamāṃśaṃ madhunā piṣṭaṃ praklinnavartmani || 224
añjanaṃ kledakaṇḍūghnaṃ pakṣmaṇāṃ ca prarohaṇam
mastakāsthiculukyāstu tuṣodalavaṇānvitam || 225
tāmrapātre'ñjanaṃ ghṛṣṭaṃ pille praklinnavartmani
tāmrapātre guhāmūlaṃ sindhūtthaṃ maricānvitam || 226
āraṇālena saṃghṛṣṭamañjanaṃ pillanāśanam
haridre triphalāṃ lodhraṃ madhukaṃ raktacandanam || 227
bhṛṅgarājarase piṣṭvā gharṣayellohabhājane
tathā tāmre ca saptāhaṃ kṛtvā vartiṃ rajo'thavā || 228
picciṭī dhūmadarśī ca timiropahatekṣaṇaḥ
prātarniśyañjayennityaṃ sarvanetrāmayāpaham || 229
mañjiṣṭhāmadhukotpalodadhikapha-
tvaksevyagorocanā-
māṃsīcandanaśaṅkhapatragirimṛ-
ttālīsapuṣpāñjanaiḥ
sarvaireva samāṃśamañjanamidaṃ
śastaṃ sadā cakṣuṣoḥ
kaṇḍūkledamalāśruśoṇitarujā-
pillārmaśukrāpaham || 230
42tutthakasya palaṃ śvetamaricāni ca viṃśatiḥ
triṃśatā kāñjikapalaiḥ piṣṭvā tāmre nidhāpayet || 231
pillānapillānkurute bahuvarṣotthitānapi
tatsekenopadehāśrukaṇḍūśothāṃśca nāśayet || 232
yāpyaḥ pakṣmoparodhastu romoddharaṇalekhanaiḥ
vartmanyupacitaṃ lekhyaṃ srāvyamutkliṣṭaśoṇitam || 233
pravṛddhāntarmukhaṃ roma sahiṣṇoruddharecchanaiḥ
sandaṃśenoddharedṝṣṭyāṃ pakṣmaromāṇi buddhimān || 234
rakṣannakṣi dahetpakṣma taptahemaśalākathā
pakṣmaroge punarnaivaṃ kadācidromasaṃbhavaḥ || 235
utsaṅginī bahulakardamavartmanī ca
śyāvaṃ ca yacca paṭitaṃ tviha baddhavartma
kliṣṭaṃ ca pothakiyutaṃ tviha vartma yacca
kumbhīkinī ca saha śarkarayāvalekhyāḥ || 236
śleṣmopanāhanagaṇau ca bisaṃ ca bhedyo
granthiśca yaḥ krimikṛto'ñjananāmikā ca || 237
43ghṛtasaindhavacūrṇena kaphānāhaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
vilikhenmaṇḍalāgreṇa pracchayedvā samantataḥ || 238
paṭolāmalakakvāthairāścyotanavidhirhitaḥ
phaṇijjakarasonasya rasaiḥ pothakināśanaḥ || 239
ānāhapiḍakāṃ svinnāṃ tiryagbhittvāgninā dahet
arśastathā vartma nāmnā śuṣkārśo'rbudameva ca || 240
maṇḍalāgreṇa tīkṣṇena mūle chindyādbhiṣak śanaiḥ
sindhūtthapippalīkuṣṭaparṇinītriphalārasaiḥ || 241
surāmaṇḍena vartiḥ syācchleṣmābhiṣyandanāśinī
pothakīvartmoparodhakrimigranthikatūṇake || 242
Adhikāra 59
1vātike śiraso roge snehasvedānsanāvanān
pānānnamupahārāṃśca kuryādvātāmayāpahān || 1
kuṣṭameraṇḍatailaṃ ca lepātkāñjikapeṣitam
śirortiṃ nāśayatyāśu puṣpaṃ vā mucukundajam || 2
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ nasye dadyācchirogade
āśirovyāyataṃ carma kṛtvāṣṭāṅgulamucchritam || 3
tenāveṣṭya śiro'dhastānmāṣakalkena lepayet
niścalasyopaviṣṭasya tailairuṣṇaiḥ prapūrayet || 4
dhārayedārujaḥśānteryāmaṃ yāmārdhameva vā
śirobastirjayatyeṣa śirorogaṃ marudbhavam || 5
hanumanyākṣikarṇārtimarditaṃ mūrdhakampanam
tailenāpūrya mūrdhānaṃ pañcamātrāśatāni ca || 6
tiṣṭhecchleṣmaṇi pitte'ṣṭau daśavāte śirogadī
eka eva vidhiḥ kāryastathā karṇākṣipūraṇe || 7
2paitte ghṛtaṃ payaḥsekāḥ śītalepāḥ sanāvanāḥ
jīvanīyāni sarpīṃṣi pānānnaṃ cāpi pittanut || 8
pittātmake śiroroge snigdhaṃ samyagvirecayet
mṛdvīkātriphalekṣūṇāṃ rasaiḥ kṣīrairghṛtairapi || 9
śatadhautaghṛtābhyaṅgaḥ śītavātādisevanam
śītasparśāśca saṃsevyāḥ sadā dāhārtiśāntaye || 10
candanośīrayaṣṭyāhvabalāvyāghrīnakhotpalaiḥ
kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pradehaḥ syācchṛtairvā pariṣecanam || 11
mṛṇālabisaśālūkacandanotpalakeśaraiḥ
snigdhaśītaiḥ śiro dihyāttadvadāmalakotpalaiḥ || 12
yaṣṭyāhvacandanānantākṣīrasiddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ hitam
nāvanaṃ śarkarādrākṣāmadhukairvāpi pittaje || 13
tvakpatraśarkarāpiṣṭā nāvanaṃ taṇḍulāmbunā
kṣīrasarpirhitaṃ nasyaṃ rasā vā jāṅgalāḥ śubhāḥ || 14
raktaje pittavatsarvaṃ bhojanālepasecanam
śītoṣṇayośca vyatyāso viśeṣo raktamokṣaṇam || 15
3kaphaje laṅghanaṃ svedo rūkṣoṣṇaiḥ pācanātmakaiḥ
tīkṣṇāvapīḍā dhūmāśca tīkṣṇāśca kavalā hitāḥ || 16
acchaṃ ca pāyayetsarpiḥ purāṇaṃ svedayettataḥ
madhūkasāreṇa śiraḥ svinnaṃ cāsya virecayet || 17
kṛṣṇābdaśuṇṭhīmadhukaśatāhvotpalapākalaiḥ
jalapiṣṭaiḥ śirolepaḥ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇaḥ || 18
devadāru nataṃ kuṣṭaṃ naladaṃ viśvabheṣajam
lepaḥ kāñjikasaṃpiṣṭastailayuktaḥ śiro'rtinut || 19
sannipātabhave kāryā doṣatrayaharī kriyā
sarpiḥpānaṃ viśeṣeṇa purāṇaṃ tvādiśanti hi || 20
trikaṭukapuṣkararajanī
rāsnāsuradāruturagagandhānām
kvāthaḥ śiro'rtijālaṃ
nāsāpīto nivārayati || 21
nāgarakalkamiśraṃ kṣīraṃ nasyena yojitaṃ puṃsām
nānādoṣodbhūtāṃ śirorujaṃ hanti tīvratarām || 22
nātotpalaṃ candanakuṣṭhayuktaṃ
śirorujāyāṃ saghṛtaḥ pradehaḥ
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ suradāru kuṣṭaṃ
yaṣṭyāhvamelākamalotpale ca
śirorujāyāṃ saghṛtaḥ pradeho
lohairakāpadmakacorakaiśca || 23
4śatāhvairaṇḍamūlaṃ ca grāvavyāghrīphalaiḥ śṛtam
tailaṃ nasyaṃ marucchleṣmatimirordhvagadāpaham || 24
5jīvakarṣabhakau drākṣāsitāyaṣṭībalotpalaiḥ
tailaṃ nasyaṃ payaḥpakvaṃ vātapittaśirogade || 25
6jīvakarṣabhakau drākṣā madhūkaṃ madhukaṃ balā
nīlotpalaṃ candanaṃ ca vidārī śarkarā tathā || 26
tailaprasthaṃ pacedebhiḥ śanaiḥ payasi paḍguṇe
jāṅgalasya tu māṃsasya tulārdhasya rasena tu || 27
siddhametadbhavennasyaṃ tailamardhāvabhedakam
bādhiryaṃ karṇaśūlaṃ ca timiraṃ galaśuṇḍikām || 28
vātikaṃ paittikaṃ caiva śīrṣarogaṃ niyacchati
dantacālaṃ śiraḥśūlamarditaṃ cāpakarpati || 29
7eraṇḍamūlaṃ tagaraṃ śatāhvā
jīvanti rāsnā sahasaindhavaṃ ca
bhṛṅgaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ madhuyaṣṭikā ca
viśvauṣadhaṃ kṛṣṇatilasya tailam || 30
ājaṃ payastailavimiśritaṃ ca
caturguṇe bhṛṅgarase vipakvam
ṣaḍbindavo nāsikayā vidheyāḥ
śīghraṃ nihanyuḥ śiraso vikārān || 31
cyutāṃśca keśāṃścalitāṃśca dantān
durvaddhamūlāṃśca dṛḍhīkaroti
suparṇadṛṣṭipratimaṃ ca cakṣu-
rbāhvorbalaṃ cābhyadhikaṃ dadāti || 32
kṣayaje kṣayamāsādyaṃ kartavyo bṛṃhaṇo vidhiḥ
pāne nasye ca sarpiḥ syādvātaghnairmadhuraiḥ śṛtam || 33
krimije vyoṣanaktāhvaśigrubījaiśca nāvanam
ajāmūtrayutaṃ nasyaṃ krimije krimijitparam || 34
8apāmārgaphalavyoṣaniśākṣārakarāmaṭhaiḥ
saviḍaṅgaṃ śṛtaṃ mūtre tailaṃ nasyaṃ krimiṃ jayet || 35
nāgaraṃ saguḍaṃ viśvaṃ pippalī vā sasaindhavā
bhujastambhādirogeṣu sarveṣūrdhvagadeṣu ca || 36
sūryāvarte vidhātavyaṃ nasyakarmādi bheṣajam
pāyayetsaguḍaṃ sarpirghṛtapūrāṃśca bhakṣayet || 37
sūryāvarte śirovedho nāvanaṃ kṣīrasarpiṣā
hitaḥ kṣīraghṛtābhyāsastābhyāṃ caiva virecanam
kṣīrapiṣṭaistilaiḥ svedo jīvanīyaiśca śasyate || 38
saśarkaraṃ kuṅkumamājyabhṛṣṭaṃ
nasyaṃ vidheyaṃ pavanāsṛgutthe
bhrūśaṅkhakarṇākṣiśirordhaśūle
dinābhivṛddhiprabhave ca roge || 39
kṛtamālapallavarase
kharamañjarikalkasiddhanavanītam
nasyena jayati niyataṃ
sūryāvartaṃ sudurvāram || 40
9daśamūlīkaṣāyaṃ tu sarpiḥsaindhavasaṃyutam
nasyamardhāvabhedaghnaṃ sūryāvartaśiro'rtinut || 41
śirīṣamūlakaphalairavapīḍaṃ ca yojayet
avapīḍo hito vā syādvacāpippalībhiḥ śṛtaḥ || 42
jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni kārayedupanāhanam
tenāsya śāmyati vyādhiḥ sūryāvartaḥ sudāruṇaḥ
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ kāryaścārdhāvabhedake || 43
śārivotpalakuṣṭāni madhukaṃ cāmlapeṣitam
sarpistailayuto lepaḥ sūryāvartārdhabhedayoḥ || 44
pivetsaśarkaraṃ kṣīraṃ nīraṃ vā nārikelajam
suśītaṃ vāpi pānīyaṃ sarpirvā nastatastayoḥ || 45
anantavāte kartavyaḥ sūryāvartahito vidhiḥ
śirāvedhaśca kartavyo'nantavātapraśāntaye || 46
āhāraśca vidhātavyo vātapittavināśanaḥ
madhumastukasaṃyāvahaviḥpūraiśca yaḥ kramaḥ || 47
sūryāvarte hitaṃ yattacchaṅkhake svedavarjitam
kṣīrasarpiḥ praśaṃsanti nastapānaṃ ca śaṅkhake || 48
śatāvarīṃ kṛṣṇatilānmadhukaṃ nīlamutpalam
mūrvāṃ punarnavāṃ cāpi lepaṃ sādhvavatārayet || 49
śītatoyāvasekāṃśca kṣīrasekāṃśca śītalān
kalkaiśca kṣīrivṛkṣāṇāṃ śaṅkhakasya pralepanam || 50
krauñcakādambahaṃsānāṃ śarāryāḥ kacchapasya ca
rasaiḥ saṃvihitasyātha tasya śaṅkhakasandhijāḥ || 51
ūrdhvaṃ tisraḥ śirāḥ prājño bhindyādeva na tāḍayet
śiraḥkampe'mṛtārāsnābalāsnehasugandhibhiḥ || 52
snehasvedādi vātaghnaṃ śirobastiśca śasyate
10yaṣṭīmadhubalārāsnādaśamūlāmbusādhitam
madhuraiśca ghṛtaṃ siddhamūrdhvajatrugadāpaham || 53
11daśamūlabalārāsnāmadhukaistripalaiḥ saha
mayūraṃ pakṣapittāntraśakṛtpādāsyavarjitam || 54
jale paktvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ tasminkṣīrasamaṃ pacet
madhuraiḥ kārṣikaiḥ kalkaiḥ śirorogārditāpaham || 55
karṇanāsākṣijihvāsyagalarogavināśanam
mayūrādyamidaṃ khyātamūrdhvajatrugadāpaham || 56
ākhubhiḥ kukkuṭairhaṃsaiḥ śaśaiścāpi hi buddhimān
kalkenānena vipacetsarpirūrdhvagadāpaham || 57
daśamūlādinā tulyo mayūra iha gṛhyate
anye tvākṛtimānena mayūragrahaṇaṃ viduḥ || 58
12prapauṇḍarīkamadhukapippalīcandanotpalaiḥ
siddhaṃ dhātrīrase tailaṃ nasyenābhyañjanena vā
sarvānūrdhvagadānhanti palitāni ca śīlitam || 59
13śataṃ mayūramāṃsasya daśamūlabalātulām
droṇe'mbhasaḥ pacetkṣuṇvā tasminpādasthite tataḥ || 60
niṣivya payaso droṇaṃ pacettatra ghṛtāḍhakam
prapauṇḍarīkavargoktairjīvanīyaiśca bheṣajaiḥ || 61
medhābuddhismṛtikaramūrdhvajatrugadāpaham
māyūrametannirdiṣṭaṃ sarvānilaharaṃ param || 62
manyākarṇaśironetrarujāpasmāranāśanam
viṣavātāmayaśvāsaviṣamajvarakāsanut || 63
Adhikāra 60
1daghnā sauvarcalājājī madhukaṃ nīlamutpalam
pibetkṣaudrayutaṃ nārī vātāsṛgdarapīḍitā || 1
pibedaiṇeyakaṃ raktaṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam
vāsakasvarasaṃ paitte guḍūcyā rasameva vā || 2
rohītakānmūlakalkaṃ pāṇḍure'sṛgdare pibet
jalenāmalakādvījakalkaṃ vā sasitāmadhu || 3
dhātakyāścākṣamātraṃ vā āmalakyā madhudravam
kākajānukamūlaṃ vā mūlaṃ kārpāsameva vā || 4
pāṇḍupradaraśāntyarthaṃ pibettaṇḍulavāriṇā
aśokavalkalakvāthaśṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ suśītalam
yathābalaṃ pibetprātastīvrāsṛgdaranāśanam || 5
dārvīrasāñjanavṛṣābdakirātabilva-
bhallātakairavakṛto madhunā kaṣāyaḥ
pīto jayatyatibalaṃ pradaraṃ saśūlaṃ
pītāsitāruṇavilohitanīlaśuklam || 6
rasāñjanaṃ taṇḍulīyasya mūlaṃ
kṣaudrānvitaṃ taṇḍulatoyapītam
asṛgdaraṃ sarvabhavaṃ nihanti
śvāsaṃ ca bhārgī saha nāgareṇa || 7
2daśamūlaṃ samuddhṛtya peṣayettaṇḍulāmbunā
etatpītvā tryahānnārī pradarātparimucyate || 8
kṣaudrayuktaṃ phalarasaṃ koṣṭhodumbarajaṃ pibet
asṛgdaravināśāya saśarkarapayo'nnabhuk || 9
pradaraṃ hanti balāyā mūlaṃ dugdhena madhuyutaṃ pītam
kuśavāṭyālakamūlaṃ taṇḍulasalilena raktākhyam || 10
śamayati madirāpānaṃ tadubhayamapiraktasaṃjñaśuklākhyau || 11
guḍena badarīcūrṇaṃ mocamāmaṃ tathā payaḥ
pītā lākṣā ca saghṛtā pṛthakpradaranāśanā || 12
raktapittavidhānena pradarāṃścāpyupācaret
asṛgdare viśeṣeṇa kuṭajāṣṭakamācaret || 13
3pāṭhājambvāmrayormedhyaṃ śilābhedarasāñjanam
ambaṣṭhakīmocarasaḥ samaṅgāpadmakeśarān || 14
bāhlīkātiviṣāmustaṃ bilvaṃ lodhraṃ sagairikam
kaṭphalaṃ maricaṃ śuṇṭhīmṛdvīkā raktacandanam || 15
kaṭvaṅgavatsakānantādhātakīmadhukārjunam
puṣyeṇoddhṛtya tulyāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 16
tāni kṣaudreṇa saṃyujya pāyayettaṇḍulāmbunā
asṛgdarātisāreṣu raktaṃ yaccopaveśyate || 17
doṣāgantukṛtā ye ca bālānāṃ tāṃśca nāśayet
yonidoṣaṃ rajodoṣaṃ śvetaṃ nīlaṃ sapītakam || 18
strīṇāṃ śyāvāruṇaṃ yacca tatprasahya nivartayet
cūrṇaṃ puṣyānugaṃ nāma hitamātreyapūjitam || 19
4mudgamāṣasya niryūhe rāsnācitrakanāgaraiḥ
siddhaṃ sapippalīvilvaiḥ sarpiḥ śreṣṭhamasṛgdare || 20
kumudaṃ padmakośīraṃ godhūmo raktaśālayaḥ
mudgaparṇī payasyā ca kāśmarī madhuyaṣṭikā || 21
balātibalayormūlamutpalaṃ tālamastakam
vidārī śatamūlī ca śālaparṇī sajīvakā || 22
phalaṃtrikasyabījāni pratyagraṃ kadalīphalam
eṣāmardhapalānbhāgāngavyaṃ kṣīraṃ caturguṇam || 23
pānīyaṃ dviguṇaṃ hṛttvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
pradare raktapitte ca raktagulme halīmake || 24
bahurūpaṃ cayatpittaṃ kāmalāvātaśoṇite
arocake jvare jīrṇe pāṇḍuroge made bhrame || 25
taruṇī cālpapuṣpā ca yā ca garmaṃ na vindati
ahanyahani ca strīṇāṃ bhavati prītivardhanam
śītakalyāṇakaṃ nāma paramuktaṃ rasāyanam || 26
5śatāvarīrasaprasthaṃ kṣodayitvāvapīḍayet
ghṛtaprasthasamāyuktaṃ kṣīraṃ dviguṇitaṃ bhiṣak || 27
atra kalkānimāndadyātsthūlodumbarasaṃmitān
jīvanīyāni yānyaṣṭau yaṣṭipadmakacandane || 28
śvadaṃṣṭrā cātmaguptā ca balā nāgabalā tathā
śālaparṇī pṛśniparṇī vidārī śārivādvayam || 29
śarkarā ca samā deyā kāśmaryāśca phalāni ca
samyak siddhaṃ tu vijñāya taddhṛtaṃ cāvatārayet || 30
raktapittavikāreṣu vātapittakṛteṣu ca
vātaraktaṃ kṣayaṃ śvāsaṃ hikkāṃ kāsaṃ ca dustaram || 31
aṅgadāhaṃ śirodāhaṃ raktapittasamudbhavam
asṛgdaraṃ sarvabhavaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ sudāruṇam
etānrogāñśamayati bhāskarastimiraṃ yathā || 32
Adhikāra 61
1yonivyāpatsu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ śasyate karma vātajit
bastyabhyaṅgapariṣekapralepāḥ picudhāraṇam || 1
vacopakuñcikājātīkṛṣṇāvṛṣakasaindhavam
ajamodāṃ yavakṣāraṃ citrakaṃ śarkarānvitam || 2
piṣṭvā prasannayāloḍya khādettadghṛtabharjitam
yonipārśvārtihṛdrogagulmārśovinivṛttaye || 3
guḍūcītriphalādantīkvāthaiśca pariṣecanam
natavārtākinīkuṣṭhasaindhavāmaradārubhiḥ || 4
tailātprasādhitāddhāryaḥ picuryonau rujāpahaḥ
pittalānāṃ tu yonīnāṃ sekābhyaṅgapicukriyāḥ || 5
śītāḥ pittaharāḥ kāryāḥ snehanārthaṃ ghṛtāni ca
yonyāṃ balāsaduṣṭāyāṃ sarvarukṣoṣṇamauṣadham || 6
pippalyā maricairmāṣaiḥ śatāhvākuṣṭhasaindhavaiḥ
vartistulyā pradeśinyā dhāryā yoniviśodhanī || 7
hiṃsrākalkaṃ tu vātārtā koṣṇamabhyajya dhārayet
pañcavalkasya pittārtā śyāmādīnāṃ kaphottarā || 8
mūṣikāmāṃsasaṃyuktaṃ tailamātapabhāvitam
abhyaṅgāddhanti yonyarśaḥ svedastanmāṃsasaindhavaiḥ || 9
2gopitte matsyapitte vā kṣaumaṃ triḥsaptabhāvitam
madhunā kiṇvacūrṇaṃ vā dadyādacaraṇāpaham || 10
srotasāṃ śodhanaṃ śothakaṇḍūkledaharaṃ ca tat
kāminyāḥ pūtiyonyāśca kartavyaḥ svedano vidhiḥ || 11
kramaḥ kāryastataḥ snehapicubhistarpaṇaṃ bhavet
śallakījiṅginījambudhavatvakpañcavalkalaiḥ || 12
kaṣāyaiḥ sādhitaḥ snehaḥ picuḥ syādviplutāpahaḥ
karṇinyāṃ vartikā kuṣṭhapippalyarkāgrasaindhavaiḥ || 13
bastamūtrakṛtā dhāryā sarvaṃ ca śleṣmanuddhitam
traivṛtaṃ snehanaṃ sveda udāvartānilārtiṣu
tadeva ca mahāyonyāṃ srastāyāṃ tu vidhīyate || 14
ākhormāṃsaṃ sapadi bahudhā khaṇḍakhaṇḍīkṛtaṃ yat
taile pācyaṃ dravati niyataṃ yāvadetanna samyak
tattailāktaṃ vasanamaniśaṃ yonibhāge dadhānā
hanti vrīḍākarabhagaphalaṃ nātra sandehabuddhiḥ || 15
3śatapuṣpātailalepādbadarīdalajāttathā
peṭikāmūlalepācca yonirbhinnā praśāmyati || 16
suṣavīmūlalepena praviṣṭāntarbahirbhavet
yonirmūṣarasābhyaṅgānniḥsṛtā praviśedapi || 17
lodhratumbīphalālepo yonidārḍhyaṃ karoti ca
vetasamūlaniḥkvāthakṣālanena tathaiva ca || 18
mūṣikāvāgulivasāmrakṣaṇaṃ yonidārḍhyadam
vacānīlotpalaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ maricāni tathaiva ca || 19
aśvagandhā haridrā ca gāḍhīkaraṇamuttamam || 20
madanaphalamadhukarpūrapūritaṃ bhavati kāminījanasya
vigalitayauvanasya ca varāṅgamatigāḍhaṃ sukumāram || 21
4pañcapallavayaṣṭyāhvamālatīkusumairghṛtam
ravipakvamanyathā vā yonigandhavināśanam || 22
ikṣvākubījadantīcapalāguḍamadanakiṇvayaṣṭyāhvaiḥ
sasnukkṣīrairvartiryonigatā kusumasañjananī || 23
sakāñjikaṃ javāpuṣpaṃ bhṛṣṭaṃ jyotiṣmatīdalam
dūrvāpiṣṭaṃ ca samprāśya vanitā tvārtavaṃ labhet || 24
dhātryañjanābhayācūrṇaṃ toyapītaṃ rajo haret
śelucchadamiśrapiṣṭaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ ca tadarthakṛt || 25
puṣpoddhṛtaṃ lakṣaṇāyāścakrāṅgāyāstu kanyayā
piṣṭaṃ mūlaṃ dugdhaghṛtapītamṛtau tu putradam || 26
kvāthena hayagandhāyāḥ sādhitaṃ saghṛtaṃ payaḥ
ṛtusnātā bālā pītvā garbhaṃ dhatte na saṃśayaḥ || 27
pippalyaḥ śṛṅgaveraṃ ca maricaṃ keśaraṃ tathā
ghṛtena saha pātavyaṃ vandhyāpi labhate sutam || 28
5svarṇasya rūpyakasya ca
cūrṇe tāmrasya cājyasaṃmiśre
pīte śuddhe kṣetre
bheṣajayogādbhavedgarbhaḥ || 29
kṛtvā śuddhau snānaṃ
vilaṅghya divasāntare tataḥ prātaḥ
snātvā dvijāya bhattayā
sampūjya tathaiva lokanātheśam || 30
śvetabalāṅghrikayaṣṭīṃ
karṣaṃ karṣaṃ palaṃ tu śarkarāyāḥ
piṣṭvaikavarṇajīvita
vatsāyā gostu dugdhena || 31
samadhikaghṛtena pītaṃ
nātra dine deyamannamanyacca
kṣudhite sadugdhamannaṃ
dadyādāpuruṣasannidhestasyāḥ || 32
samadivase śubhayoge
dakṣiṇapārśvāvalambinī dhīrā
tyaktastryantarasaṅga-
prahṛṣṭamanaso'tivṛddhadhātośca
puruṣasya saṅgamātrā-
llabhate putraṃ tato niyatam || 33
6goṣṭhajātavaṭakasya prāguktaśākhaje śubhe
māṣau dvau ca tathā gaurasarṣapau dadhiyojitau
puṣpāpītau drutāpannagarbhāyāḥ putrakārakau || 34
kānakānrājatānvāpi lauhānpuruṣakānamūn
dhyātāgnivarṇānpayaso dadhno vāpyudakasya vā
kṣiptvāñjalau pibetpuṣye garbhe putratvakārakān || 35
7mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ triphalā śarkarā balā
medā payasyā kākolī mūlaṃ caivāśvagandhajam || 36
ajamodā haridre dve hiṅgukaṃ kaṭurohiṇī
utpalaṃ kumudaṃ drākṣā kākolyau candanadvayam || 37
eteṣāṃ kārṣikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
śatāvarīrasakṣīraṃ ghṛtāddeyaṃ caturguṇam || 38
sarpiretannaraḥ pītvā nityaṃ strīṣu vṛṣāyate
putrāñjanayate nārī medhāḍhyānpriyadarśanān || 39
yā caiva sthiragarbhā syādyā vā janayate mṛtam
alpāyuṣaṃ vā janayedyā ca kanyāṃ prasūyate || 40
yonidoṣe rajodoṣe parisrāve ca śasyate
prajāvardhanamāyuṣyaṃ sarvagrahanivāraṇam || 41
nāmnā phalaghṛtaṃ hyetadaśvibhyāṃ parikīrtitam
anuktaṃ lakṣmaṇāmūlaṃ kṣipantyatra cikitsakāḥ || 42
jīvadvatsaikavarṇāyā ghṛtamatra praśasyate
āraṇyagomayenāpi vahnijvālā pradīyate || 43
8sahacare dve triphalāṃ guḍūcīṃ sapunarnavām
śukanāsāṃ haridre dve rāsnāṃ medāṃ śatāvarīm || 44
kalkīkṛtya ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacetkṣīracaturguṇam
tatsiddhaṃ prapibennārī yoniśūlaprapīḍitā || 45
piṇḍitā calitā yā ca niḥsṛtā vivṛtrā ca yā
piṇḍayonistu visrastā ṣaṇḍayoniśca yā smṛtā || 46
prapadyante tu tāḥ sthānaṃ garbhaṃ gṛhṇanti cāsakṛt
etatphalaghṛtaṃ nāma yonidoṣaharaṃ param || 47
9siddhārthakaṃ vacā brāhmī śaṅkhapuṣpī punarnavā
payasyāmayayaṣṭyāhvakaṭukailāphalatrayam || 48
śārive rajanī pāṭhā bhṛṅgadāru suvarcalā
mañjiṣṭhā triphalā śyāmā vṛṣapuṣpaṃ sagairikam || 49
dhīmānpaktvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ samyaṅmātrābhimantritam
dvimāsagarbhiṇī nārī ṣaṇmāsāt na prayojayet || 50
sarvāṅgaṃ janayetputraṃ śūraṃ paṇḍitamāninam
jaḍagadgadamūkatvaṃ pānādevāpakarṣati || 51
saptarātraprayogeṇa naraḥ śrutidharo bhavet
nāgnirdahati tadveśma na vajraṃ hanti na grahāḥ || 52
na tatra mriyate bālo yatrāste somasaṅgitaḥ
vandhyāpi labhate putraṃ sarvāmayavivarjitam
yoniduṣṭāśca yā nāryo retoduṣṭāśca ye narāḥ || 53
asya prabhāvātkukṣisthaḥ sphuṭavāgvyāharatyapi
drākṣā parūṣakāśmaryau phalatrayamudāhṛtam || 54
“oṃ namo mahāvināyakāyā
mṛtaṃ rakṣa mama phalasiddhiṃ
dehi rudravacanena svāhā”
saptadūrvābhimantritam || 55
10nīlotpalośīramadhūkayaṣṭī
drākṣāvidārīkuśapañcamūlaiḥ
syājjīvanīyaiśca ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ
śatāvarīkārasadugdhamiśram || 56
taccharkarāpādayutaṃ praśasta-
masṛgdare mārutaraktapitte
kṣīṇe bale retasi saṃpranaṣṭe
kṛcchre ca raktaprabhave ca gulme || 57
11śatāvarīmūlatulāścatasraḥ saṃprapīḍayet
rasena kṣīratulyena pacettena ghṛtāḍhakam || 58
jīvanīyaiḥ śatāvaryā mṛdvīkābhiḥ parūṣakaiḥ
piṣṭaiḥ priyālaiścākṣāṃśairdviyaṣṭīmadhukairbhiṣak || 59
siddhaśīte ca madhunaḥ pippalyāścāṣṭakaṃ palam
dattvā daśapalaṃ cātra sitāyāstadvimiśritam || 60
brāhmaṇānprāśayetpūrvaṃ lihyātpāṇitalaṃ tataḥ
yonyasṛkśukradoṣaghnaṃ vṛṣyaṃ puṃsavanaṃ ca tat || 61
kṣatakṣayaṃ raktapittaṃ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ halīmakam
kāmalāṃ vātaraktaṃ ca visarpaṃ hṛcchirograham
unmādādīnapasmārānvātapittātmakāñjayet || 62
dagdhvā śaṅkhaṃ kṣipedrambhāsvarase tattu peṣitam
tulyālaṃ lepato hanti romaguhyādisambhavam || 63
raktāñjanāpucchacūrṇayuktaṃ tailaṃ tu sārṣapam
saptāhaṃ vyuṣitaṃ hanti mūlādromāṇyasaṃśayaḥ
kusumbhatailābhyaṅgo vā romṇāmutpāṭite'ntakṛt || 64
12āragvadhamūlapalaṃ karṣadvitayaṃ ca śaṅkhacūrṇasya
haritālasya ca kharaje mūtraprasthe kaṭutailaṃ pakvam || 65
tailaṃ tadidaṃ śaṅkhaharitālacūrṇitaṃ lepāt
nirmūlayati ca romāṇyanyeṣāṃ sambhavo naiva || 66
karpūrabhallātakaśaṅkhacūrṇaṃ
kṣāro yavānāṃ ca manaḥśilā ca
tailaṃ vipakvaṃ haritālamiśraṃ
romāṇi nirmūlayati kṣaṇena || 67
13śuktiśambūkaśaṅkhānāṃ dīrghavṛntātsamuṣkakāt
dagdhvā kṣāraṃ samādāya kharamūtreṇa gālayet || 68
kṣārārdhamāgaṃ vipacettailaṃ ca sārṣapaṃ vudhaḥ
idamantaḥ pure deyaṃ tailamātreyapūjitam || 69
bindurekaḥ patedyatra tatra romāpunarbhavaḥ
madanādivraṇe deyamaśvibhyāṃ ca vinirmitam || 70
arśasāṃ kuṣṭharogāṇāṃ pāmādadruvicarcikām
kṣāratailamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvakledaharaṃ param || 71
Adhikāra 62
1madhukaṃ śākabījaṃ ca payasā suradāru ca
aśmantakaḥ kṛṣṇatilāstāmravallī śatāvarī || 1
vṛkṣādanī payasyā ca tathaivotpalaśārivā
anantā śārivā rāsnā padmā madhukameva ca || 2
bṛhatīdvayakāśmaryakṣīriśuṅgāstvaco ghṛtam
pṛthakparṇī balā śigru śvadaṃṣṭrā madhuyaṣṭikā || 3
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ bisaṃ drākṣā kaśeru madhukaṃ sitā
māseṣu sapta yogāḥ syurardhaślokāstu saptasu || 4
yathākramaṃ prayoktavyā garbhasrāve payo'nvitāḥ
kapitthabilvabṛhatīpaṭolekṣunidigdhikāḥ || 5
mūlāni kṣīrasiddhāni dāpayedbhiṣagaṣṭame
navame madhukānantāpayasyāśārivāḥ pibet || 6
payastu daśame śuṇṭhyā śṛtaśītaṃ praśasyate
sakṣīrā vā hitā śuṇṭhī madhukaṃ devadāru ca || 7
evamāpyāyate garbhastīvrā ruk copaśāmyati
kuśakāśorubūkānāṃ mūlairgokṣurakasya ca
śṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ sitāyuktaṃ garbhiṇyāḥ śūlanutparam || 8
2kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakajīvanīya-
padmotpalairaṇḍaśatāvarībhiḥ
siddhaṃ payaḥ śarkarayā vimiśraṃ
saṃsthāpayedgarbhamudīrṇaśūlam || 9
kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakapadmakotpalaṃ
samudgaparṇīmadhukaṃ saśarkaram
saśūlagarbhasrutipīḍitāṅganā
payovimiśraṃ payasānnabhuk pibet || 10
garbhe śuṣke tu vātena bālānāṃ cāpi śuṣyatām
sitāmadhukakāśmaryairhitamutthāpane payaḥ || 11
garbhaśoṣe tvāmagarbhāḥ prasahāśca sadā hitāḥ
3pāṭhā lāṅgalisiṃhāsyamayūrakajaṭaiḥ pṛthak || 12
nābhibastibhagālepātsukhaṃ nārī prasūyate
parūṣakasthirāmūlalepastadvatpṛthakpṛthak || 13
vāsāmūle dhruvaṃ tadvatkaṭivaddhe sūte drutam
pāṭhāyāstu śiphāṃ yonau yā nārī saṃpradhārayet || 14
uraḥprasavakāle ca sā sukhena prasūyate
tuṣāmbuparipiṣṭena mūlena parilepayet || 15
lāṅgalyāścaraṇau sūte kṣiprametena garbhiṇī
āṭarūṣakamūlena nābhibastibhagālepaḥ kartavyaḥ || 16
tālatarūdbhavamūle muktakacche dhṛte puṃsām
gṛhāmbunā gehadhūmapānaṃ garbhāpakarṣaṇam || 17
mātuluṅgasya mūlāni madhukaṃ madhusaṃyutam
ghṛtena saha pātavyaṃ sukhaṃ nārī prasūyate || 18
puṭadagdhasarpakañcuka-
masṛṇamasī kusumasārasahitāñjitākṣī
jhaṭiti viśalyā jāyeta
garbhavatī mūḍhagarbhāpi || 19
4gṛhāmbunā hiṅgusindhupānaṃ garbhāpakarṣaṇam
ihāmṛtaṃ ca somaśca citrabhānuśca bhāmini
uccaiḥśravāśca turago mandire nivasantu te || 20
idamamṛtamapāṃ samuddhṛtaṃ vai-
bhavalaghugarbhamimaṃ vimuñcatu strī
tadanalapavanārkavāsavāste
saha lavaṇāmbudharairdiśantu śāntim || 21
muktāḥ pāśā vipāśāśca muktāḥ sūryeṇa raśmayaḥ
muktaḥ sarvabhayādgarbha ehyehi mārica svāhā || 22
jalaṃ cyavanamantreṇa saptavārābhimantritam
pītvā prasūyate nārī--
5dṛṣṭvā cobhayatriṃśakam || 23
tathobhayapañcadaśadarśanaṃ sukhasūtikṛt
nāṃrī ṛtuvasubhiḥ saha pakṣadigaṣṭādaśabhireva ca || 24
arkabhuvanābdhisahitairubhayatriṃśakamidamāścaryam
vasuguṇābdhyekabāṇanavaṣaṭsaptayugaiḥ kramāt || 25
sarvaṃ pañcadaśadvistu triṃśakaṃ navakoṣṭhake
kaṭutumbyahinirmokakṛtavedhanasarṣapaiḥ || 26
kaṭutailānvito dhūmo yoneḥ pātayate'marām
kacaveṣṭitayāṅgulyā ghṛṣṭe kaṇṭhe sukhaṃ patatyamarā || 27
6eraṇḍasya vaneḥ? kāko gaṅgātīramupāgataḥ
itaḥ pibati pānīyaṃ viśalyā garbhiṇī bhavet || 28
anena saptadhāmantrya jalaṃ deyaṃ viśalyakam || 29
mūlena lāṅgalikyā vā saṃlipte pāṇipāde ca
amarāpātanaṃ madyaiḥ pippalyādirajaḥ pibet || 30
garīmadanadahanamūlaṃ cirajamapi
garbhaṃ mṛtamamṛtaṃ vā nipātayati || 31
7śālimūlākṣamātraṃ vā mūtreṇāmlena vānvitam
upakuñcikāṃ pippalīṃ ca madirāṃ lābhataḥ pibet || 32
sauvarcalena saṃyuktāṃ yoniśūlanivāraṇīm
sūtāyā hṛcchirobastiśūlaṃ makkandasaṅgitam || 33
yavakṣāraṃ pibettatra sarpiṣoṣṇodakena vā
pippalyādigaṇakvāthaṃ pibedvā lavaṇānvitam || 34
pārāvataśakṛtpītaṃ śālitaṇḍulavāriṇā
garbhapātāntarotthe tu raktasrāvanivāraṇam || 35
jalapiṣṭavaruṇapatraiḥsaghṛtairudvartanālepau tu
kikkiśarogaṃ harato gomayagharṣādatho vihitau || 36
8hrīberāraṇālaraktacandanabalā
dhanyākavatsādanī
mustośīrayavāsaparpaṭaviṣā-
kvāthaṃ pibedgarbhiṇī
nānādoṣayutātisārakagade
raktasrutau vā jvare
yogo'yaṃ munibhiḥ purā nigaditaḥ
sūtyāmaye śasyate || 37
amṛtānāgarasahacara-
bhadrotkaṭapañcamūlajaladalaśṛtam
madhusaṃyuktaṃ nivāra
yati sajvaraṃ sūtikātaṅkam || 38
sahacarapuṣkaravetasamūlaṃ
vaikaṅkataṃ dāru kulatthasamam
jalamatra saindhavahiṅguyutaṃ
sadyo ghorasūtikāśūlaharam || 39
9daśamūlīkṛtaḥ kvāthaḥ sadyaḥ sūtirujāpahaḥ
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ cavyaṃ śuṇṭhī yamānikā || 40
jīvake dve haridre dve biḍasauvarcalaṃ tathā
etairevauṣadhaiḥ piṣṭairāraṇālaṃ vipācitam || 41
āmavātaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaphaghnaṃ vahnidīpanam
kāñjikaṃ vajrakaṃ nāma strīṇāmagnivivardhanam || 42
makkandaśūlaśamanaṃ paraṃ kṣīrābhimardanam
kṣīrapākavidhānena kāñjikasyāpi sādhanam || 43
10jīrakaṃ hapuṣā dhānyaṃ śatāhvā suradāru ca
yamānī tryaṣṭako hiṅgupatrikā kāsamardakam || 44
pippalī pippalīmūlamajamodātha bāppikā
citrakaṃ ca palāṃśāni tathānyacca catuḥpalam || 45
kaśerukaṃ nāgaraṃ ca kuṣṭhaṃ dīpyakameva ca
guḍasya ca śataṃ dadyād ghṛtaprasthaṃ tathaiva ca || 46
kṣīradviprasthasaṃyuktaṃ śanairmṛdvagninā pacet
pañcajīraka ityeṣa sūtikānāṃ praśasyate || 47
garbhārthinīnāṃ nārīṇāṃ bṛṃhaṇīye samārute
viṃśatiṃ vyāpado yoneḥ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ kṣayam || 48
halīmakaṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ daurgandhyaṃ bahumūtratām
hanti pīnonnatakucāḥ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇāḥ
upayogātstriyo nityamalakṣmīmalavarjitāḥ || 49
11vanakārpāsikekṣūṇāṃ mūlaṃ sauvīrakeṇa vā
vidārīkandaṃ surayā pibedvā stanyavardhanam || 50
dugdhena śālitaṇḍulacūrṇapānaṃ vivardhayet
stanyaṃ saptāhataḥ kṣīrasevinyāstu na saṃśayaḥ || 51
haridrādiṃ vacādiṃ vā pibetstanyaviśuddhaye
tatra vātātmake stanye daśamūlījalaṃ pibet || 52
pittaduṣṭe'mṛtābhīrupaṭolaṃ nimbacandanam
dhātrī kumāraśca pibetkvāthayitvā saśārivam || 53
kaphe vā triphalāmustābhūnimbaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
dhātrī stanyaviśuddhyarthaṃ mudgayūṣarasāśinī || 54
bhārgīvacādārupāṭhāḥ pibetsātiviṣāḥ śṛtāḥ || 55
kukkurāmañjukāmūlaṃ carvitamāsye vidhāritaṃ jayati
saptāhātstanakīlaṃ stanyaṃ caikāntataḥ kurute || 56
12śothaṃ stanotthitamavekṣya bhiṣagvidadhyā-
dyadvidradhāvabhihitaṃ tviha bheṣajaṃ tu
āme vidahyati tathaiva gate ca pākaṃ
tasyāḥ stanau satatameva ca nirduhīta || 57
viśālāmūlalepastu hanti pīḍāṃ stanotthitām
niśākanakaphalābhyāṃ lepaścāpi stanārtihā || 58
mūṣikavasayā śūkaragajamahiṣamāṃsacūrṇasaṃyutayā
abhyaṅgamardanābhyāṃ kaṭhinapīnastanau bhavataḥ || 59
mahiṣībhavanavanītaṃ vyādhibalogrā tathaiva nāgabalā
piṣṭā mardanayogātpīnaṃ kaṭhinaṃ stanaṃ kurute || 60
13śrīparṇīrasakalkābhyāṃ tailaṃ siddhaṃ tilodbhavaṃ
tattailaṃ tūnakenaiva stanasyopari dhārayet
patitāvutthitau strīṇāṃ bhaveyātāṃ payodharau || 61
14kāsīsaturagagandhā
śārivāgajapippalīvipakvena
tailena yānti vṛddhiṃ
stanakarṇavarāṅgaliṅgāni || 62
prathamartau taṇḍulāmbho
nasyaṃ kuryātstanau sthirau
gomahiṣīghṛtasahitaṃ
tailaṃ śyāmākṛtāñjalivacābhiḥ || 63
satrikaṭuniśābhiḥ
siddhaṃ nasyaṃ stanotthāpanaṃ param
tanūkaroti madhyaṃ
pītaṃ mathitena mādhavīmūlam || 64
syācchithilāpi ca gāḍhā
suragopājyābhyaṅgasaṅgato yoniḥ
śaravahalasthirabandhana-
rajjvā santāḍanāddhi dayitena || 65
naśyatyabalādveṣaḥ patyau sahajaḥ kṛto'thavā yogaiḥ
dattvaiva dugdhabhaktaṃ viprāyotpāṭya sitabalāmūlam
puṣye kanyāpiṣṭaṃ dattamanicchāharaṃ bhakṣye || 66
Adhikāra 63
1kuṣṭhavacābhayābrāhmīkamalaṃ kṣaudrasarpiṣā
varṇāyuḥkāntijananaṃ lehaṃ bālasya dāpayet || 1
stanyābhāve payaśchāgaṃ gavyaṃ vā tadguṇaṃ pibet
tarkādho guḍikāṃ taptāṃ nirvāṣya kaṭutailake
tattailaṃ pānato hanti bālānāmulbamuddhatam || 2
vyoṣaśivogrā rajanī kalkaṃ vā pītamatha payasā
ulbaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ kurute paṭutāṃ vālasya cātyantam || 3
mṛtpiṇḍenāgnitaptena kṣīrasiktena soṣmaṇā
