svedayedutthitāṃ nābhiṃ śothastena praśāmyati || 4
nābhipāke niśālodhrapriyaṅgumadhukaiḥ śṛtam
tailamabhyañjane śastamebhirvāpyavacūrṇanam || 5
2somagrahaṇe vidhivat
tkekriśikhāmūlamuddhṛtaṃ baddham
jaghane'tha kandharāyāṃ
kṣapayatyahiṇḍikāṃ niyatam || 6
saptadalapuṣpaṃ maricaṃ piṣṭaṃ gorocanāsahitam
pītaṃ tadvattaṇḍulabhaktakṛto dagdhapiṣṭakaprāśaḥ || 7
jambūkanāsāvāyasajihvā nābhirvarāhasaṃbhūtā
kāṃsyaṃ raso'tha garalaṃ prāvṛḍbhekasya vāmajaṅghāsthi || 8
ityekaśothamilitaṃ vidhṛtaṃ grīvādikaṭideśe
ahiṇḍikāpraśamanamabhyaṅgo nātipathyavidhiḥ || 9
anāmake ghurghurikābukkāmaricarocanā
navanītaṃ ca saṃmiśrya khādettadroganāśanam || 10
tailāktaśirastālunisaptadalārkasnuhībhavaṃ kṣīram
dattvā rajanīcūrṇe datte naśyedanāmakorogaḥ || 11
lehayecca śunā bālaṃ navanītena lepitam
sphuṭakapatrajarasodvartanaṃ ca hi taddhitam || 12
3tailasya bhāgamekaṃ
mūtrasya dvau ca śimbidalarasasya
gavyaṃ payaścaturguṇa
mevaṃ dattvā pacettailam
tenābhyaṅgaḥ satataṃ rogamanāmakākhyamapaharati || 13
arkatūlakamāvikaromāṇyādāya keśarājasya
svarasenākte vastre kṛtvā vartiṃ ca tailāktām || 14
tajjātakajjalāñjitalocanayugalo'pyalaṃkṛtobālaḥ
kaṣṭamanāmakarogaṃ kṣapayati bhūtādikaṃ cāpi || 15
cālanikātalasaṃsthitapotaṃ saṃplāvya gavyamūtreṇa
okodaśālikāyāṃ rajakakṣārodakasnānam || 16
dāsakrayaṇaśrāvaṇavarā-
ṭikārasendrapūritā ghṛtā kaṇṭhe
nalinīdale ca śayanaṃ
sukaṣṭamanāmakākhyarogaghnam || 17
bhaiṣajyaṃ pūrvamuddiṣṭaṃ narāṇāṃ yajjvarādiṣu
deyaṃtadeva bālānāṃ mātrā tasya kanīyasī || 18
prathame māsi jātasya śiśorbheṣajaraktikā
avalehyā tu kartavyā madhukṣīrasitāghṛtaiḥ || 19
ekaikāṃ vardhayet tāvadyāvatsaṃvatsaro bhavet
tadūrdhvaṃ māsavṛddhiḥ syādyāvadāṣoḍaśābdikāḥ || 20
4haridrādvayayaṣṭyāhvasiṃhīśakrayavaiḥ kṛtaḥ
śiśorjvarātisāraghnaḥ kapāyastasya doṣajit || 21
5ghanakṛṣṇāruṇāśṛṅgīcūrṇaṃ kṣaudreṇa saṃyutam
śiśorjvarātisāraghnaṃ kāsaśvāsavamīharam || 22
6dhātakībilvadhanyākalodhrendrayavavālakaiḥ
lehaḥ kṣaudreṇa vālānāṃ jvarātīsāravāntijit || 23
rajanīdāruśaralaśreyasī bṛhatīdvayam
pṛśniparṇī śatāhvā ca līḍhaṃ mākṣikasarpiṣā || 24
grahaṇīdīpanaṃ hanti mārutārtiṃ sakāmalām
jvarātīsārapāṇḍughnaṃ bālānāṃ sarvaśothanut || 25
miśī kṛṣṇāñjanaṃ lājā śṛṅgīmaricamākṣikaiḥ
lehaḥ śiśorvidhātavyaśchardikāsajvarāpahaḥ || 26
7śṛṅgīṃ samustātiviṣāṃ vicūrṇya
lehaṃ vidadhyānmadhunā śiśūnām
kāsajvaracchardibhirarditānāṃ
samākṣikāṃ cātiviṣāṃ tathaikām || 27
pītaṃ pītaṃ vamedyastu stanyaṃ tanmadhusarpiṣā
dvivārtākīphalarasaṃ pañcakolaṃ ca lehayet || 28
āmrāsthilājasindhūtthairlehaḥ kṣaudreṇa chardinut || 29
pippalīmaricānāṃ tu cūrṇaṃ samadhuśarkaram
rasena mātuluṅgasya hikkācchardinivāraṇam || 30
peṭīpāṭhāmūlājjambvaḥ
sahakāravalkalataḥ kalkaḥ
ityekaśaśca piṇḍo
vidhṛto hṛnnābhimadhyatālvādi
chardyatisārajaṃ vegaṃ
prabalaṃ dhatte tadeva niyamena || 31
8bilvaṃ ca puṣpāṇi ca dhātakīnāṃ
jalaṃ salodhraṃ gajapippalī ca
kvāthāvalehau madhunā vimiśrau
bāleṣu yojyāvatisāriteṣu || 32
samaṅgādhātakīlodhraśārivābhiḥ śṛtaṃ jalam
durdhare'pi śiśordeyamatīsāre samākṣikam || 33
nāgarātiviṣāmustavālakendrayavaiḥ śṛtam
kumāraṃ pāyayetprātaḥ sarvātīsāranāśanam || 34
samaṅgā dhātakī padmaṃ vayasyā kacchurā tathā
piṣṭairetairyavāgūḥ syātsarvātīsāranāśinī || 35
bilvamūlakaṣāyeṇa lājāścaiva saśarkarāḥ
āloḍya pāyayedbālaṃ chardyatīsāranāśanam || 36
kalkaḥ priyaṅgukolāsthimadhyamustarasāñjanaiḥ
kṣaudralīḍhaḥ kumāṃrasya charditṛṣṇātisāranut || 37
9mocarasaḥ samaṅgā ca dhātakī padmakeśaram
piṣṭairetairyavāgūḥ syādraktātīsāranāśinī || 38
lehastailasitākṣaudratilayaṣṭyāhvakalkitaḥ
bālasya rundhyānniyataṃ raktasrāvaṃ pravāhikām || 39
lājāsayaṣṭīmadhukaṃ śarkarākṣaudrameva ca
taṇḍulodakasaṃsiktaṃ kṣipraṃ hanti pravāhikām || 40
aṅkoṭamūlamathavā
taṇḍulasalilena vaṭajamūlaṃ vā
pītaṃ hantyatisāraṃ
grahaṇīrogaṃ sudurvāram || 41
sitajīrasarjacūrṇaṃ
bilvadalotthāmbumiśritaṃ pītam
hantyāmaraktaśūlaṃ
guḍasahitaḥ śvetasarjo vā || 42
maricamahaupadhakuṭajaṃ
dviguṇīkṛtamuttarottaraṃ kramaśaḥ
guḍatakrayuktametad
grahaṇīrogaṃ nihantyāśu || 43
bilvaśakrāmbamocābdasiddhamājaṃ payaḥ śiśoḥ
samāṃ saraktāṃ grahaṇīṃ pītaṃ hanyāttrirātrataḥ || 44
tadvadajākṣīrasamo jambūtvagudbhavo rasaḥ
gudapāke tu bālānāṃ pittaghnīṃ kārayetkriyām || 45
rasāñjanaṃ viśeṣeṇa pānālepanayorhitam || 46
10kaṇopaṇasitākṣodrasūkṣmailāsaindhavaiḥ kṛtaḥ
mūtragrahe prayoktavyaḥ śiśūnāṃ leha uttamaḥ || 47
ghṛtena sindhuviśvailāddhiṅgubhārgīrajo lihan
ānāhaṃ vātikaṃ śūlaṃ jayettoyena vā śiśuḥ || 48
harītakī vacā kuṣṭaṃ kalkaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam
pītvā kumāraḥ stanyena mucyate tālupātanāt || 49
mukhapāke tu vālānāṃ sāmrasāramayorajaḥ
gairikaṃ kṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ bheṣajaṃ sarasāñjanam || 50
aśvatthatvagdalakṣaudrairmukhapāke pralepanam
dārvīyaṣṭyābhayājātīpatrakṣaudraistathāparam || 51
saha jambīrarasena snugdalarasagharṣaṇaṃ sadyaḥ
kṛtamupahanti hi pākaṃ mukhajaṃ bālasya cāśveva || 52
lāvatittirapallūrarajaḥ puṣparasānvitam
drutaṃ karoti bālānāṃ padmakeśaravanmukham || 53
11dantodbhavottharogeṣu na bālamatiyantrayet
svayamapyupaśāmyanti jātadantasya te gadāḥ || 54
sadanto yastu vā ye tu dantāḥ prothasya cottarāḥ
kuryustasya kutaḥ śāntiṃ bālasyāpi dvijātayaḥ
dadyātsadakṣiṇaṃ bālaṃ nainamenaṃ prapūjayet || 55
pañcamulīkaṣāyeṇa saghṛtena payaḥ śṛtam
saśṛṅgaveraṃ saguḍaṃ śṛtaṃ hikkārditaḥ pibet || 56
suvarṇagairikasyāpi cūrṇāni madhunā saha
līḍhvā sukhamavāpnoti kṣipraṃ hikkārditaḥ śiśuḥ || 57
citrakaṃ śṛṅgaveraṃ ca tathā dantī gavākṣyapi
cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tu sarveṣāṃ sukhoṣṇenāmbunā pibet
śvāsaṃ kāsamatho hikkāṃ kumārāṇāṃ praṇāśayet || 58
drākṣāyāsābhayākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ sakṣaudrasarpiṣā
līḍhaṃ śvāsaṃ nihantyāśu kāsaṃ ca tamakaṃ tathā || 59
puṣkarātiviṣāśṛṅgīmāgadhīdhanvayāsakaiḥ
taccūrṇaṃ madhunā līḍhaṃ śiśūnāṃ pañcakāsanut || 60
dāḍimasya ca bījāni jīrakaṃ nāgakeśaram
cūrṇitaṃ śarkarākṣaudralīḍhaṃ tṛṣṇāvināśanam || 61
12māyūrapakṣabhasma
vyuṣitajalaṃ tena bhāvitaṃ peyam
tṛṣṇāghnaṃ vaṭakāṣṭaka-
bhasmajalaṃ vaktraśoṣajiddhṛtaṃ vaktre || 62
piṣṭaiśchāgena payasā dārvīmustakagairikaiḥ
bahirālepanaṃ śastaṃ śiśornetrāmayāpaham || 63
manaḥśilā śaṅkhanābhiḥ pippalyo'tha rasāñjanam
vartiḥ kṣaudreṇa saṃyuktā bāle sarvākṣiroganut || 64
mātuḥ stanyakaṭusnehakāñjikairbhāvito jayet
svedāddīpaśikhottapto netrāmayamalaktakaḥ || 65
śuṇṭhībhṛṅganiśākalkaḥ puṭapākaḥ sasaindhavaḥ
kukūlake'kṣirogeṣu bhadramāścyotanaṃ hitam || 66
krimighnālaśilādārvīlākṣākāñcanagairikaiḥ
cūrṇāñjanaṃ kukūle syācchiśūnāṃ pothakīṣu ca || 67
sudarśanāmūlacūrṇādañjanaṃ syātkukūlake || 68
gṛhadhūmaniśākuṣṭavājikendrayavaiḥ śiśoḥ
lepastakreṇa hantyāśu sidhmapāmāvicarcikāḥ || 69
13pādakalke'śvagandhāyāḥ kṣīre daśaguṇe pacet
ghṛtaṃ peyaṃ kumārāṇāṃ puṣṭikṛdvalavardhanam || 70
14cāṅgerīsvarase sarpiśchāgakṣīrasame pacet
kapitthavyoṣasindhūtthasamaṅgotpalabālakaiḥ || 71
sabilvadhātakīmocaiḥ siddhaṃ sarvātisāranut
grahaṇīṃ dustarāṃ hanti bālānāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ || 72
15śaṅkhapuṣpī vacā brāhmī kuṣṭhaṃ triphalayā saha
drākṣā saśarkarā śuṇṭhī jīvantī jīrakaṃ balā || 73
śaṭhī durālabhā bilvaṃ dāḍimaṃ surasaḥ sthirā
mustaṃ puṣkaramūlaṃ ca sūkṣmailā gajapippalī || 74
eṣāṃ karṣasamairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
kaṣāye kaṇṭakāryāśca kṣīre tasmiṃścaturguṇe || 75
etatkumārakalyāṇaghṛtaratnaṃ sukhapradam
balavarṇakaraṃ dhanyaṃ puṣṭyagnibalavardhanam || 76
chāyāsarvagrahālakṣmīkrimidantagadāpaham
sarvabālāmayaharaṃ dantodbhedaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 77
16vacā kuṣṭhaṃ tathā brāhmī siddhārthakamathāpi ca
śārivā saindhavaṃ caiva pippalīghṛtamaṣṭamam || 78
medhyaṃ ghṛtamidaṃ siddhaṃ pātavyaṃ ca dine dine
dṛḍhasmṛtiḥ kṣipramedhāḥ kumāro buddhimānbhavet || 79
na piśācā na rakṣāṃsi na bhūtā na ca mātaraḥ
prabhavanti kumārāṇāṃ pivatāmaṣṭamaṅgalam || 80
17lākṣārasasamaṃ siddhaṃ tailaṃ mastu caturguṇam
rāsnācandanakuṣṭābdavājigandhāniśāyugaiḥ || 81
śatāhvādāruyaṣṭyāhvamūrvātiktāhareṇubhiḥ
bālānāṃ jvararakṣoghnamabhyaṅgādbalavarṇakṛt || 82
18sahāmuṇḍitikodīcyakvāthasnānaṃ grahāpaham
saptacchadaniśākuṣṭacandanaiścānulepanam || 83
sarpatvaglaśunaṃ mūrvāsarṣapāriṣṭapallavāḥ
baiḍālaviḍajālomameṣaśṛṅgīvacāmadhu || 84
dhūpaḥ śiśorjvaraghno'yamaśeṣagrahanāśanaḥ
baliśāntīṣṭakarmāṇi kāryāṇi grahaśāntaye || 85
mantraścāyaṃ prayoktavyastatrādau sarvakāmikaḥ || 86
19“oṃṃ namo bhagavate garuḍāya tryambakāya sa-
dyastavastutaḥ svāhā | oṃṃ kaṃ paṃ ṭaṃ śaṃ vainate-
yāya namaḥ | oṃṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ kṣaḥ” | bāladehapramāṇena
puṣpamālāṃ tu sarvataḥ | pragṛhya macchikābhaktabali-
rdeyastu śāntikaḥ | “oṃkārī svarṇapakṣī vālakaṃ
rakṣa rakṣa svāhā” | garuḍabaliḥ | oṃṃ nārāyaṇāya
namaḥ || 87
20prathame divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti na-
ndanā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ
bhavati jvaraḥ | aśubhaṃ śabdaṃ muñcati ātkāraṃ
ca karoti stanyaṃ na gṛhṇāti | baliṃ tasya prava-
kṣyāmi yena sampadyate śubham | nadyubhayataṭamṛ-
ttikāṃ gṛhītvā puttalikāṃ kṛtvā śuklaudanaṃ śu-
klapuṣpaṃ śuklasaptadhvajāḥ saptapradīpāḥ saptasva-
stikāḥ saptavaṭakāḥ saptaśaṣkulikāḥ jambulikāḥ
saptamuṣṭikāḥ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tāmbūlaṃ matsyaṃ māṃsaṃ
surāmagrabhaktaṃ ca pūrvasyāṃ diśi catuṣpathe madhyāhne
balirdeyaḥ | tato'śvatthapatraṃ kumbhe prakṣiṣya śā-
ntyudakena snāpayet | rasonasiddhārthakameṣaśṛ-
ṅganimbapatraśivanirmālyairbālakaṃ dhūpayet | “oṃṃ
namo nārāyaṇāya amukasya vyādhiṃ hana hana
muñca muñca hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā” evaṃ dinatrayaṃ
baliṃ dattvā caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ
sampadyate śubham || 88
21dvitīye divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti
sunandā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa pra-
thamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | cakṣurunmīlayati gātramudve-
jayati na śete krandati stanyaṃ na gṛhṇāti ā-
tkāraśca bhavati | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena
sampadyate śubham | taṇḍulaṃ hastapṛṣṭhaikaṃ dadhiguḍa-
ghṛtaṃ ca miśritaṃ śarāvaikaṃ gandhatāmbūlaṃ pītapuṣpaṃ
pītasaptadhvajāḥ saptapradīpāḥ daśasvastikāḥ ma-
tsyamāṃsasurātilacūrṇāni | paścimasyāṃ diśi
catuṣpathe balirdeyaḥ | dināni trīṇi sandhyāyāṃ
tataḥ śāntyudakena snāpayet | śivanirmālyasi-
ddhārthamārjāraloma uśīrabālaghṛtairdhūpaṃ dadyāt
“oṃṃ namo nārāyaṇāya amukasya vyādhiṃ hana
dana muñca muñca hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā” | caturtha-
divase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet tataḥ sampadyate
śubham || 89
22tṛtīye divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti pū-
tanā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ
bhavati jvaraḥ | gātramudvejayati stanyaṃ na gṛhṇāti
muṣṭiṃ badhnāti krandati ūrdhvaṃ nirīkṣate | baliṃ tasya
pravakṣyāmi yena sampadyate śubham | nadyubhayataṭamṛ-
ttikāṃ gṛhītvā puttalikāṃ kṛtvā gandhapuṣpatāmbū-
laraktacandanaṃ raktapuṣpaṃ raktasaptadhvajāḥ saptapradīpāḥ
saptasvastikāḥ pakṣimāṃsasurā agrabhaktaṃ ca da-
kṣiṇasyāṃ diśi aparāhṇe catuṣpathe balirdātavyaḥ
śivanirmālyaguggulusarṣapanimbapatrameṣaśṛṅgairdina-
trayaṃ dhūpayet | “oṃṃ namo nārāyaṇāya bālasya
vyādhiṃ hana hana muñca muñca hrāsaya hrāsaya
svāhā” caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ sa-
mpadyate śubham || 90
23caturthadivase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti mukha-
muṇḍikā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa pra-
thamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | grīvāṃ nāmayati akṣiṇī
unmīlayati stanyaṃ na gṛhṇāti roditi svapiti
muṣṭiṃ badhnāti | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena sampadyate
śubham | nadyubhayataṭamṛttikāṃ gṛhītvā puttalikāṃ
kṛtvā utpalapuṣpaṃ gandhatāmbūlaṃ daśa dhvajāḥ ca-
tvāraḥ pradoṣāḥ trayodaśa svastikāḥ matsyamāṃsa-
surā agrabhaktaṃ ca uttarasyāṃ diśi aparāhṇe catu-
ṣpathe baliṃ dadyāt | ādyamāsiko dhūpaḥ “oṃṃ
namo nārāyaṇāya hana hana muñca muñca svāhā”
caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ sampadyate
śubham || 91
24pañcame divase māme varṣe vā gṛhṇāti ka-
ṭapūtanā nāma mātṛkā tayā gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ
bhavati jvaraḥ | gātramudvejayati stanyaṃ na gṛhṇāti
muṣṭiṃ ca bandhāti baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena sa-
mpadyate śubham | kumbhakāracakrasya mṛttikāṃ gṛ-
hītvā puttalikāṃ nirmāya gandhatāmbūlaṃ śuklaudanaṃ
śuklapuṣpaṃ pañcadhvajāḥ pañcapradīpāḥ pañcavaṭakāḥ
aiśānyāṃ diśi valirdātavyaḥ | śāntyudakena
snāpayecchivanirmālyasarpanirmokaguggulunimbapa-
trabālakaghṛtairdhūpaṃ dadyāt | “oṃṃ namo nārāyaṇāya
amukasya vyādhiṃ cūrṇaya cūrṇaya hana hana svāhā |”
caturthe divase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ sampadyate
śubham || 92
25ṣaṣṭhe divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti śaku-
nikā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ
bhavati jvaraḥ | gātrabhedaṃ ca darśayati divārātrā-
vutthānaṃ bhavati ūrdhvaṃ nirīkṣate | baliṃ tasya pra-
vakṣyāmi yena sampadyate śubham | piṣṭakena putta-
likāṃ kṛtvā śuklapuṣpaṃ raktapuṣpaṃ pītapuṣpaṃ gandha-
tāmbūlaṃ daśa pradīpāḥ daśadhvajāḥ daśasvastikāḥ
daśamuṣṭikāḥ daśavaṭakāḥ kṣīrajambūḍikā matsyamāṃ-
sasurā āgneyyāṃ diśi niṣkrānte madhyāhne baliṃ
dāpayet | śāntyudakena snāpayet | śivanirmā-
lyarasonaguggulusarpanirmokanimbapatraghṛtairdhūpaṃ da-
dyāt | “oṃṃnamo nārāyaṇāya cūrṇaya cūrṇaya
hana hana svāhā” caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojaye-
ttataḥ sampadyate śubham || 93
26saptame divase māse varṣe vā yadā gṛhṇāti
śuṣkarevatī nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa
prathamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | gātramudvejayati muṣṭiṃ ba-
dhnāti roditi | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena sa-
mpadyate śubham | raktapuṣpaṃ śuklapuṣpaṃ gandhatāmbūlaṃ
raktaudanaṃ kṛśarāstrayodaśa svastikāḥ matsyamāṃsa-
surāstrayodaśadhvajāḥ pañca pradīpāḥ paścimadigbhāge
grāmaniṣkāśe aparāhṇe vṛkṣamāśritya baliṃ da-
dyāt | śāntyudakena snānaṃ guggulumeṣaśṛṅgīsarṣa-
pośīrabālakaghṛtairdhūpayet | “oṃṃ namo nārāyaṇāya
dīptatejase hana hana muñca muñca svāhā” caturtha-
divase vrāṇahmaṃ bhojayettataḥ sampadyate śubham || 94
27aṣṭame divase māse vārṣe vā yadi gṛ-
hṇāti aryakā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamā-
treṇa prathamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | gṛdhragandhaḥ pūtigandha-
śca jāyate āhāraṃ ca na gṛhṇāti udvejayati gā-
trāṇi | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena sampadyate
śubham | raktapītadhvajāḥ candanaṃ puṣpaṃ śaṣkulyaḥ
parpaṭikāḥ matsyamāṃsasurājambuḍikāḥ pratyūṣe ba-
lirdeyaḥ prāntare | mantraḥ “oṃṃnamo nārāyaṇāya
caturdiḍmokṣaṇāya vyādhiṃ hana hana muñca muñca
oṃṃ hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā” caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhoja-
yettataḥ sampadyate śubham || 95
28navame divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti
bhūsūtikā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa
prathamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | nityaṃ chardirbhavati gātrabhedaṃ
darśayati muṣṭiṃ badhnāti | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi
yena sampadyate śubham | nadyubhayataṭamṛttikāṃ gṛ-
hītvā puttalikāṃ nirmāya śuklavastreṇa veṣṭayecchu-
klapuṣpaṃ gandhatāmbūlaṃ śuklatrayodaśadhvajāḥ trayo-
daśadīpāḥ trayodaśasvastikāḥ trayodaśaputtalikāḥ
trayodaśamatsyaputtalikāḥ matsyamāṃsasurāḥ uttara-
digbhāge grāmaniṣkāśe baliṃ dadyāt | śāntyu-
dakena snānaṃ guggulunimbapatragośṛṅgaśvetasarṣapa-
ghṛtairdhūpaṃ dadyāt | mantraḥ “oṃṃnamo nārāya-
ṇāya caturbhujāya hana hana muñca muñca svāhā |”
caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ sampadyate
śubham || 96
29daśame divase māse varṣe vā gṛhṇāti ni-
rṛtā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ
bhavati jvaraḥ | gātramudvejayati ātkāraṃ karoti
roditi mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca bhavati | baliṃ tasya pra-
vakṣyāmi yena sampadyate śubham | pārāvāramṛ-
ttikāṃ gṛhītvā puttalikāṃ nirmāya gandhatāmbūlaṃ
raktapuṣpaṃ raktacandanaṃ pañcavarṇadhvajāḥ pañcapradīpāḥ
pañcasvastikāḥ pañcaputtalikāḥ matsyamāṃsasurāḥ
vāyavyāṃ diśi baliṃ dadyāt | kākaviṣṭhāgomāṃ-
sagośṛṅgarasonamārjāralomanimbapatraghṛtairdhūpayet
“oṃṃnamo nārāyaṇāya cūrṇitahastāya muñca muñca
svāhā” caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ
sustho bhavati bālakaḥ || 97
30ekādaśe divase māse varṣe vā yadi gṛ-
hṇāti pilipicchikā nāma mātṛkā | tayā
gṛhītamātreṇa prathamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | āhāraṃ na
gṛhṇāti ūrdhvadṛṣṭirbhavati gātrabhaṅgo bhavati
baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena sampadyate śubham
piṣṭakena puttalikāṃ kṛtvā raktacandanaraktaṃ ca
tasyā mukhaṃ dugdhena siñcet | pītapuṣpaṃ gandhatā-
mbūlaṃ saptapītadhvajāḥ saptapradīpāḥ aṣṭau vaṭakāḥ
aṣṭau śaṣkulikāḥ aṣṭau pūrikāḥ matsyamāṃsasu-
rāḥ pūrvasyāṃ diśi balirdātavyaḥ | śāntyudakena
snānaṃ śivanirmālyaguggulugośṛṅgasarpanirmokaghṛtai-
rdhūpayet | “oṃṃnamo nārāyaṇāya muñca muñca
svāhā” caturthadivase brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ
sustho bhavati bālakaḥ || 98
31dvādaśe divase māse varṣe vā yadi gṛhṇāti
kāmukā nāma mātṛkā | tayā gṛhītamātreṇa pra-
thamaṃ bhavati jvaraḥ | vihasya vādayati kareṇa
tarjayati gṛhṇāti krāmati niḥśvasiti muhurmuhu-
rāhāraṃ na karoti | baliṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena
sampadyate śubham | kṣīreṇa puttalikāṃ kṛtvā gandhaṃ
tāmbūlaṃ śuklapuṣpaṃ śuklasaptadhvajāḥ saptapradīpāḥ
saptapūpikāḥ karasthena dadhibhaktena sarvakarmabaliṃ
dadyācchāntyudakena snāpayet | śivanirmālyagu-
ggulusarṣapaghṛtairdhūpayet | “oṃṃ namo nārāyaṇāya
muñca muñca hana hana svāhā” caturthadivase
brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayettataḥ sustho bhavati bālakaḥ || 99
Adhikāra 64
1ariṣṭānvadhanaṃ mantraṃ prayogaśca viṣāpahaḥ
daṃśanaṃ daṃśakasyāheḥ phalasya mṛduno'pi vā || 1
mūlaṃ taṇḍulavāriṇā pibati yaḥ
pratyaṅgirāsambhavaṃ
niṣpiṣṭaṃ śuci bhadrayogadivase
tasyāhibhītiḥ kutaḥ
darpādeva phaṇī yadā daśati taṃ
mohānvito mūlapaṃ
sthāne tatra sa eva yāti niyataṃ
vaktraṃ yamasyācirāt || 2
masūraṃ nimbapatrābhyāṃ khādenmeṣagate ravau
abdamekaṃ na bhītiḥ syādviṣāttasya na saṃśayaḥ || 3
dhavalapunarnavajaṭayā taṇḍulajalapītayā ca puṣyarkṣe
apaharati viṣadharaviṣopadravamāvatsaraṃ puṃsām || 4
gṛhadhūmo haridre dve samūlaṃ taṇḍulīyakam
api vāsakinādaṣṭaḥ pibeddadhi ghṛtāplutam
kūlikāmūlanasyena kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 5
śleṣmaṇaḥ karṇagūthasya vāmānāmikayā kṛtaḥ
lepo hanyādvipaṃ ghoraṃ nṛmūtrāsecanaṃ tathā || 6
śirīṣapuṣpasvarase bhāvitaṃ śvetasarṣapam
saptāhaṃ sarpadaṣṭānāṃ nasyapānāñjane hitam || 7
dvipalaṃ natakuṣṭābhyāṃ ghṛtakṣaudraṃ catuḥpalam
api takṣakadaṣṭānāṃ pānametatsukhapradam || 8
vandhyakarkoṭajaṃ mūlaṃ chāgamūtreṇa bhāvitam
nasyaṃ kāñjikasaṃyuktaṃ viṣopahatacetasaḥ || 9
2trivṛdviśālāmadhukaṃ haridre
mañjiṣṭhavargo lavaṇaṃ ca sarvam
kaṭutrikaṃ caiva vicūrṇitāni
śṛṅge nidadhyānmadhunā yutāni || 10
eṣo'gado hantyupayujyamānaḥ
pānāñjanābhyañjananasyayogaḥ
avāryavīryo viṣavegahantā
mahāgado nāma mahāprabhāvaḥ || 11
pīte viṣe syādvamanaṃ ca tvaksthe
pradehasekādi suśītalaṃ ca || 12
kapitthamāṃsaṃ sasitākṣaudraṃ kaṇṭhagate viṣe
lihyādāmāśayagate tābhyāṃ cūrṇapalaṃ natāt || 13
viṣe pakvāśayagate pippalīrajanīdvayam
mañjiṣṭhāṃ ca samaṃ piṣṭvā gopittena naraḥ pibet || 14
rajanīsaindhavakṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtamuttamam
pānaṃ mūlaviṣārtasya digdhaviddhasya ceṣyate || 15
sitāmadhuyutaṃ cūrṇaṃ tāmrasya kanakasya vā
lehaḥ praśamayatyugraṃ sarvasaṃyogajaṃ viṣam || 16
aṅkoṭamūlaniḥkvāthaṃ phāṇitaṃ saghṛtaṃ lihet
tailāktaḥ svinnasarvāṅgo garadoṣaviṣāpahaḥ || 17
kaṭabhyarjunaśairīyaśelukṣīridrumatvacaḥ
kaṣāyacūrṇakalkāḥ syuḥ kīṭalūtābraṇāpahāḥ || 18
agāradhūmamañjiṣṭhārajanīlavaṇottamaiḥ
lepo jayatyākhuviṣaṃ karṇikāyāśca pānataḥ || 19
3yaḥ kāsamardanapatraṃ vadane prakṣipya karṇe phūtkāram
manujo dadāti śīghraṃ jayati viṣaṃ vṛścikānāṃ saḥ || 20
daṃśebhrameṇavidhinā vṛścikaviṣahṛtkucairapadaguḍikā
puradhūpapūrvamarkacchadamiva piṣṭvā kṛtolepaḥ || 21
jīrakasya kṛtaḥ kalko ghṛtasaindhavasaṃyutaḥ
sukhoṇo vṛścikārtānāṃ suleṣo vedanāpahaḥ || 22
amalāgharṣaṇaṃ daṃśe kaṇṭakaṃ ca taduddharet
karaṇe viṣaje lepātphaṇijjakaraso'thavā || 23
kuṅkumakunaṭīkarkaṭaharitālaiḥ kusumbhasaṃmilitaiḥ
kṛtaguḍikābhrāmaṇato vidaṣṭagodhāsaraṭaviṣajit || 24
aṅkoṭapatradhūmo mīnaviṣaṃ jhaṭiti vighaṭayecchṛṅgī
godhāvaraṭīviṣamivālepena kuṭajakapālijaṭā || 25
kanakoduṃbaraphalamiva taṃḍulajalapiṣṭhaṃpītamapaharati
kanakadaladravaghṛtaguḍadugdhapalaikaṃ śunāṃ garalam || 26
lepa iva bhekagaralaṃ śirīṣabījaiḥ snuhīpayaḥsiktaiḥ
harati garalaṃ tryahamaśitāṅkoṭajaṭākuṣṭhasammilitā || 27
maricamahauṣadhabālakanāgāhvairmakṣikāviṣe lepaḥ
lālāviṣamapanayatomūlemilitepaṭolanīlikayoḥ || 28
4somavalko'śvakarṇaśca gojihvā haṃsapādyapi
rajanyau gairikaṃ lepo nakhadantaviṣāpahaḥ || 29
vacā hiṅgu viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ gajapippalī
pāṭhāprativiṣā vyoṣaṃ kāśyapena vinirmitam || 30
daśāṅgamagadaṃ pītvā sarvakīṭaviṣaṃ jayet
kīṭadaṣṭakriyāḥ sarvāḥ samānāḥ syurjalaukasām || 31
pṛkkāplavasthauṇeya-
kākṣī śaileyarocanātagaram
dhyāmakaṃ kuṅkumaṃ māṃsī
surasāgrailālakuṣṭhaghnam || 32
bṛhatīśirīṣapuṣpa-
śrīveṣṭakapadmacāraṭiviśālāḥ
suradārupadmakeśara-
śāravakamanaḥśilākauntyaḥ || 33
jātyarkapuṣpasarṣapa-
rajanīdvayahiṅgupippalīdrākṣāḥ
jalamudgaparṇīmadhuka-
madanakamathasindhuvārāśca || 34
sampākalodhramayūrakagandhaphalīlāṅgalīviḍaṅgāḥ
puṣye samuddhṛtya samaṃ piṣṭā guḍikā vidheyā syuḥ
sarvaviṣaghno jayakṛ-
dviṣamṛtasañjīvano jvaranihantā || 35
peyavilepanadhāraṇa-
dhūmagrahaṇairgṛhasthaśca || 36
bhūtaviṣajantvalakṣmī
kārmaṇamantrāgnyaśanyarīnhanyāt
duḥsvapnastrīdoṣā-
nakālamaraṇāmbucaurabhayam || 37
dhanadhānyakāryasiddhi-
śrīpuṣṭivarṇāyurvardhano dhanyaḥ
mṛtasañjīvana eva
prāgamṛtādbrahmaṇābhihitaḥ || 38
Adhikāra 65
1yajjarāvyādhividhvaṃsi bheṣajaṃ tadrasāyanam
pūrve vayasi madhye vā śuddhadehaḥ samācaret || 1
nāviśuddhaśarīrasya yukto rasāyano vidhiḥ
nābhāti vāsasi mliṣṭe raṅgayoga ivārpitaḥ || 2
guḍena madhunā śuṇṭhyā kṛṣṇayā lavaṇena vā
dve dve khādansadā pathye jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sukhī || 3
sindhūtthaśarkarāśuṇṭhīkaṇāmadhuguḍaḥ kramāt
varṣādiṣvabhayā sevyā rasāyanaguṇaiṣiṇā || 4
traiphalenāyasīṃ patrīṃ kalkenālepayennavām
tamahorātrikaṃ lepaṃ pibetkṣaudrodakāplutam || 5
prabhūtasnehamaśanaṃ jīrṇe tasminprayojayet
ajaro'rukasābhyāsājjīveccāpi samāḥ śatam || 6
2pañcāṣṭau saptadaśa vā pippalīḥ kṣaudrasarpiṣā
rasāyanaguṇānveṣī samāmekāṃ prayojayet || 7
tisrastisrastu pūrvāhṇe bhuktvāgre bhojanasya ca
pippalyaḥ kiṃśukakṣārabhāvitā ghṛtabharjitāḥ || 8
prayojyā madhusaṃmiśrā rasāyanaguṇaiṣiṇā
jetuṃ kāsaṃ kṣayaṃ śvāsaṃ śoṣaṃ hikkāṃ galāmayam || 9
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣaṃ pāṇḍutāṃ viṣamajvaram
vaisvaryaṃ pīnasaṃ śoṣaṃ gulmaṃ vātavalāsakam || 10
jaraṇānte'bhayāmekāṃ prāgbhakte dve bibhītake
bhuktvā tu madhusarpirbhyāṃ catvāryāmalakāni ca || 11
prayojayetsamāmekāṃ triphalāyā rasāyanam
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ pūrṇamajaro'vyādhireva ca || 12
3maṇḍūkaparṇyāḥ svarasaḥ prayojyaḥ
kṣīreṇa yaṣṭīmadhukasya cūrṇam
raso guḍūcyāstu samūlapūṣpyāḥ
kalkaḥ prayojyaḥ khalu śaṅkhapuṣpyāḥ || 13
āyuḥpradānyāmayanāśakāni
balāgnivarṇasvaravardhanāni
medhyāni caitāni rasāyanāni
medhyā viśeṣeṇa tu śaṅkhapuṣpī || 14
pītāśvagandhā payasārdhamāsaṃ
ghṛtena tailena sukhāmbunā vā
kṛśasya puṣṭiṃ vapuṣo vidhatte
bālasya śasyasya yathāmbuvṛṣṭiḥ || 15
dhātrītilānbhṛṅgarajovimiśrān
ye bhakṣayeyurmanujāḥ krameṇa
te kṛṣṇakeśā vimalendriyāśca
nirvyādhayo varṣaśataṃ bhaveyuḥ || 16
4vṛddhadārakamūlāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet
śatāvaryā rasenaiva saptarātrāṇi bhāvayet || 17
akṣamātraṃ tu tacūrṇaṃ sarpiṣā saha bhojayet
māsamātropayogena matimāñjāyate naraḥ || 18
medhāvī smṛtimāṃścaiva valīpalitavarjitaḥ
hastikarṇarajaḥ khādetprātarutthāya sarpiṣā || 19
yatheṣṭāhārācāro'pi sahasrāyurbhavennaraḥ
medhāvī balavānkāmī strīśatāni vrajatyasau || 20
madhunā tvaśvavegaḥ syādvaliṣṭhaḥ strīsahasragaḥ
mantraścāyaṃ prayoktavyo bhiṣajā cābhimantraṇe || 21
“oṃ namo mahāvināyakāya amṛtaṃ rakṣa rakṣa mama
phalasiddhiṃ dehi rudravacanena svāhā” || 22
5dhātrīcūrṇasya karṣaṃ svarasaparigataṃ
kṣaudrasarpiḥsamāṃśaṃ
kṛṣṇāmānīsitāṣṭaprasṛtayutamidaṃ
sthāpitaṃ bhasmarāśau
varṣānte tatsamaśnanbhavati vipalito
rūpavarṇaprabhāvai-
rnirvyādhirbuddhimedhā smṛtibalavacana-
sthairyasatvairupetaḥ || 23
guḍūcyapāmārgaviḍaṅgaśaṅkhinī-
vacābhayākuṣṭhaśatāvarīsamā
ghṛtena līḍhā prakaroti mānavaṃ
tribhirdinaiḥ ślokasahasradhāriṇam || 24
6samūlapatramādāya brahmīṃ prakṣālya vāriṇā
udūkhale kṣodayitvā rasaṃ vastreṇa gālayet || 25
rase caturguṇe tasminghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
auṣadhāni tu peṣyāṇi tānīmāni pradāpayet || 26
haridrā mālatī kuṣṭaṃ trivṛtā saharītakī
eteṣāṃ palikānbhāgāñśeṣāṇi kārṣikāṇi tu || 27
pippalyo'tha viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ śarkarā vacā
sarvametatsamāloḍya śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 28
etatprāśitamātreṇa vāgviśuddhiśca jāyate
saptarātraprayogeṇa kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 29
ardhamāsaprayogeṇa somarājīvapurbhavet
māsamātraprayogeṇa śrutamātraṃtu dhārayet || 30
hantyaṣṭādaśa kuṣṭhāni arśāṃsi vividhāni ca
pañca gulmān pramehāṃśca kāsaṃ pañcavidhaṃ jayet || 31
vandhyānāṃ caiva nārīṇāṃ narāṇāṃ cālparetasām
ghṛtaṃ sārasvataṃ nāma balavarṇāgnivardhanam || 32
7kāsaśvāsātisārajvarapiḍakakaṭī-
kuṣṭhakoṭhaprakārān
mūtrāghātodarārśaḥśvayathugalaśiraḥ
karṇaśūlākṣirogān
ye cānye vātapittakṣatajakaphakṛtā
vyādhayaḥ santi janto-
stāṃstānabhyāsayogādapanayati payaḥ
pītamante niśāyāḥ || 33
vyaṅgavalīpalitaghnaṃ pīnasavaisvaryakāsaśothaghnam
rajanīkṣaye'mbunasyaṃ rasāyanaṃ dṛṣṭijanana ca || 34
8nāgārjuno munīndraḥ
śaśāsa yallohaśāstramatigahanam
tasyārthasya smṛtaye
vayametadviśadākṣarairbrūmaḥ || 35
mene muniḥ svatantre
bhūyaḥ pākaṃ na palapañcakādarvāk
subahuprayogadoṣā-
dūrdhvaṃ na palatrayodaśakāt || 36
tatrāyasi pācanīye
pañcapalādau trayodaśapalakānte
lauhāddviguṇā triphalā
grāhyā ṣaḍbhiḥ palairadhikā || 37
māraṇapuṭanasthālī-
pākāḥ striphalaikabhāgasampādyaḥ
triphalāyā bhāgadvitayaṃ
grahaṇīyaṃ lauhapākārtham || 38
sarvatrāyastuṭanādyarthaikāṃśe śarāvasaṃkhyātam
pratipalameva triguṇaṃ pāthaḥ kvāthārthamādeyam || 39
saptapalādau bhāge pañcadaśānte'mbhamāṃ śarāvaiśca
dvyādyaikādaśakāntairadhikaṃ tadvāri kartavyam || 40
tatrāṣṭamo vibhāgaḥ śeṣaḥ kvāthasya yatnataḥ sthāpyaḥ
tena hi māraṇapuṭanasthālīpākā bhaviṣyanti || 41
9pākārthe tu triphalā
bhāgadvitaye śarāvasaṃkhyātam
pratipalamambusamaṃ syā-
dadhikaṃ dvābhyāṃ śarāvābhyām || 42
tatra caturtho bhāgaḥ
śeṣo nipuṇena yatnato grāhyaḥ
ayasaḥ pākārthatvā-
tsa ca sarvasmātpradhānatamaḥ || 43
pākārthamaśmasāre pañcapalādau trayodaśapalānte
dugdhaśarāvadvitayaṃ pādairekādikairadhikam || 44
pañcapalādikamātrā tadabhāve tadanusārato grāhyam
caturādikamekāntaṃ śaktāvadhikaṃ trayodaśakāt || 45
triphalātrikaṭukacitraka-
kāntakrāmakaviḍaṅgacūrṇāni
anyānyapi deyāni
palāśavṛkṣasya ca bījāni || 46
jātīphalajātīkoṣailākakkolakalavaṅgānām
sitakṛṣṇajīrakayorapi cūrṇānyayasaḥ samāni syuḥ
triphalātrikaṭuviḍaṅgā niyatā anye yathāprakṛti || 47
kālāyasadoṣahṛterjātīphalāderlavaṅgāntasya
kṣepaḥ prāptyanurūpaḥ sarvasyonasya caikādyaiḥ || 48
kāntakrāmakamekaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ doṣamapaharatyayasaḥ
dviguṇatriguṇacaturguṇamājyaṃ grāhyaṃ yathāprakṛti || 49
yadi bheṣajabhūyastvaṃ stokatvaṃ vāpi cūrṇānām
ayasā sāmyaṃ saṃkhyā bhūyo'lpatvena bhūyo'lpā || 50
evaṃ dhātvanusārāttattatkathitauṣadhasya bādhena
sarvatraiva vidheyastattadakathitasyauṣadhasyohaḥ || 51
10kāntādilauhamāraṇa-
vidhānasarvasvamucyate tāvat
yasya kṛte tallauhaṃ
paktavyaṃ tasya śubhe divase || 52
samṛdaṅgārakavālita
natabhūbhāge śivaṃ samabhyarcya
vaidikavidhinā vahniṃ
nidhāya hutvāhutīstatra || 53
dharmātsidhyati sarvaṃ
śreyastaddharmasiddhaye kimapi
śaktyanurūpaṃ dadyā-
ddvijāya santoṣiṇe guṇine || 54
santoṣya karmakāraṃ
prasādapūgādidānasammānaiḥ
ādau tadaśmasāraṃ
nirmalamekāntataḥ kuryāt || 55
tadanu kuṭhāracchinna-
triphalāgirikarṇikāsthisaṃhāraiḥ
karikarṇacchadamūlaka-
śatāvarīkeśarājākhyaiḥ || 56
śāliṃ ca mūlakakaśī-
mūlaprāvṛjjabhṛṅgarājaiśca
liptvā dagdhavyaṃ tad
duṣṭakriyalohakāreṇa || 57
cirajalabhāvitanirmala-
śālāṅgāreṇa parita ācchādya
kuśalādhmāpitabhastrā
navaratamuktena pavanena || 58
vahnerbāhyajvālā
boddhavyā jātu naiva kuñcikayā
mṛllavaṇasalilabhājā
kintu svacchāmbu saṃplutayā || 59
dravyāntarasaṃyogā-
tsvāṃ śaktiṃ bheṣajāni muñcanti
maladhūlīmatsarvaṃ
sarvatra vivarjayettasmāt || 60
sandaṃśena gṛhītvāntaḥ-
prajvālitāgnimadhyapamunīya
galati yathāyathamagre
tathaiva mṛdu vardhayennipuṇaḥ || 61
talanihitordhvamukhā-
ṅkuśalagnaṃ triphalājale
vinikṣipya nirvāpaye-
ccheṣaṃ triphalāmbu rakṣecca || 62
yallauhaṃ na mṛtaṃ ta-
tpunarapi paktavyamuktamārgeṇa
yanna mṛtaṃ tathāpi
tattyaktavyamalauhameva tataḥ || 63
tadanu ghanalauhapātre
kālāyaso mudgareṇa saṃcūrṇya
dattvā bahuśaḥ salilaṃ
prakṣālyāṅgāramuddhṛtya || 64

śuṣkīkṛtyāthavātape paścāt
lauhaśilāyāṃ piṃṣyā-
dasite'śmani vā tadaprāptau || 65
11atha kṛtvāyobhāṇḍe

śatāvarībhṛṅgakeśarājarasaiḥ
prāgvatsthālīpākaṃ
kuryātpratyekamekaikaṃ vā || 67
12hastapramāṇavadanaṃ
śvabhraṃ hastaikakhātasamamadhyam

śatāvarīkandamāṇasahajarasaiḥ
bhallātakakarikarṇa-
cchadamūlapunarnavāsvarasaiḥ || 70

cchādyānterandhramālipya || 71
tatpuṭapātram tatra
śvabhrajvalane nidhāya bhūyopiḥ
kāṣṭhakarīṣatuṣaista-
tsaṃcchādyāharniśaṃ dahetprājñaḥ || 72

rājaistu pacetsadaivapuṭapākam

rmilitairvā tricaturānvārān || 73

tādṛśadinaṃ piṃṣyāt
dvigaladrajasā tu yujyate yatra || 74
tadayaścūrṇaṃ piṣṭaṃ
ghṛṣṭaṃ ghanasūkṣmavāsasi ślakṣṇam
yadi rajasā sadṛśaṃ syāt
ketakyāstarhi tadbhadram || 75

dhikṛtapuruṣe svabhāvarugadhigamāt
kathitamapi heyamauṣadha-
mucitamupādeyamanyadapi || 76
13abhyastakarmavidhibhi-
rvālakuśāgrīyabuddhibhirlakṣyam

kaṭāhe dṛḍhamṛṇmaye praṇamya śivam
tadayaḥ pacedacapalaḥ
kāṣṭhendhanena vahninā mṛdunā || 78
nikṣipya triphalājala-
muditaṃ yattadghṛtaṃ ca dugdhaṃ ca
saṃcālya lauhamayyā
darvyā lagnaṃ samutpāṭya || 79
mṛdumadhyakharabhāvaiḥ
pākastrividho'tra vakṣyate puṃsām

prakṛtīnāṃ madhyamasya samaḥ || 80
abhyaktadarvilohaṃ
sukhaduḥkhaskhalanayogimṛdumadhyam

bhāṣante kecidācāryāḥ || 81

pralepamākhūtkarākṛtiṃ bruvate

sikatāpuñjopamaṃ tu kharam || 82
trividho'pi pāka īdṛk

prakṛtiviṣaye ca sūkṣmo
guṇadoṣau janayatītyalpam || 83

triphalādeḥ prakṣipeccūrṇam || 84

rbhavati tato vigalite taduṣṇatve

rghṛtamīkṣetādhikaṃ tato'nyasmin

14athavā vaktavyavidhiḥ

16nānāvidharukśāntyai

daśakṛṣṇalaparimāṇaṃ

idamadhikaṃ tadadhikatara-
miyadeva na mātṛmodakavat || 100

datvā madhvanurūpaṃ

bhavati phaḍantena lohavalarakṣā
sanamaskāreṇa vali-
rbhakṣaṇamayaso hūmantena || 103
“oṃ amṛtodbhavāya svāhā

mahāyakṣasenādhipataye

oṃ amṛte hrīm || 104 ||”
17jagdhvā tadamṛtasāraṃ
nīraṃ vā kṣīramevānu pibet
kāntakrāmakamamalaṃ
saṃcārya rasaṃ pibenna tu tat || 105

tapayānasnānavegarodhādīn

18prathamaṃ pītvā dugdhaṃ

19yasya na tathā sarati
sa yavakṣāraṃ jalaṃ pibetkoṣṇam

yāvaccāṣṭamamāsaṃ

pratilomaṃ hrāsayettadayaḥ || 120

kāntiṃ labhate cirasthiraṃ deham
saptāhatrayamātrāt-
sarvarujo hanti kiṃ bahunā || 122

saptavidhīnāṃ yathāvadākhyātam

mupakṛtaye ko'pi ṣaṭkarmā || 124
20yatra tatrodbhavaṃ lauhaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ māritaṃ yadi
triphalāvyoṣasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣayedvalināśanam || 125
sāmānyāddviguṇaṃ cauḍraṃ kaliṅgo'ṣṭaguṇastataḥ

21patrīkṛtasya gandhaka-

ākte dhmāpitatāmre
nirguṇḍīkalkakāñjikanimagne || 129

hilamocimūlapiṇḍe
kṣiptaṃ tadanu mārdasaṃpuṭe lipte
tīkṣṇaṃ dagdhaṃ piṣṭa-
mamlāmbhasā sādhucandrikārahitam || 131
recitatāmreṇa rasaḥ
svalpaśilāyāṃ ghṛṣya piṇḍikā kāryā

22tanu patrīkṛtaṃ tāmraṃ naipālaṃ gandhakaṃ samam

kṛtvā svalpavidhānena sthālīmadhye pidhāya ca

suliptāyāṃ ca yāmaikamadhojvālāṃ pradāpayet || 137

atha karṣaṃ gandhakasya vahnisthalohapātragam || 138
śilāputreṇa saṃmardya drutaṃ ghṛṣṭaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
kṛtvā deyaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ karṣamānaṃ tataḥ punaḥ || 139
raso'mlamathitaḥ śuddhastāvanmātraḥ pradīyate
tatastathaivaṃ saṃmardya punarājyaṃ pradāpayet || 140
aṣṭabindukamātraṃ ca mardayenmūrcchitaṃ tathā
sarvaṃ syāttasya ākṛṣya śilāputrādikaṃ dṛḍham || 141

tatpiṇḍaṃ vastravistīrṇaṃ piṇḍe trikaṭuje punaḥ
vasanāntarite dattvā poṭṭalīṃ kārayedbudhaḥ || 144
tatastāṃ poṭṭalīmājyamagnāṃ kṛtvā vidhāritām

yadā niṣphenatā cājye puṭitā ca dṛḍhā bhavet
23tadā pakvaṃ tamākṛṣya pañcaguñjātulāghṛtam || 146
trikaṭutriphalācūrṇaṃ tulyaṃ prātaḥ prayojayet

24hemādyāḥ sūryasantaptāḥ sravanti giridhātavaḥ
jatvābhaṃ mṛdumṛtsnācchaṃ yanmalaṃ tacchilājatu || 151

hemno'tha rajatāttāmrādvaraṃ kṛṣṇāyasādapi

25yathākramaṃ vātapitte śleṣmapitte kaphe triṣu || 157

lauhakiṭṭāyate vahnau vidhūmaṃ dahyate'mbhasi || 158

26pūrvoktena vidhānena lauhaiścūrṇīkṛtaiḥ saha

27carakoktaśilājatuno
vidhānaṃ sopaskaraṃ hyetat
kāle tu ravitāpāḍhye
kṛṣṇāyasajaṃ śilājatu pravaram || 169
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ
tryahaśca śuṣkaṃ punaḥ śuṣkam

rase balāyāstathā paṭolasya || 170
madhukarasairgomūtre
tryahaṃ tryahaṃ bhāvayetkramaśaḥ

tacca punarbhāvayecchuṣkam
saptāhaṃ bhāvyaṃ syā-
tkvāthenaiṣāṃ yathālābham || 171
kākolyau dve mede

dantībhakaṇākaliṅgacavyābdāḥ

śuddhasya palāni daśa ṣaṭ ca || 174

catvāri ghṛtasya mākṣikasyāṣṭau

cūrṇārdhapalāni pañcānām || 176
tvakkṣīrīpatratvak
nāgailānāṃ ca miśrayitvā tu
girijasya ṣoḍaśapalai-
rguḍikāḥ kāryāstato''kṣasamāḥ || 177
tāḥśuṣkānavakumbhe jātīpuṣpādhivāsite sthāpyāḥ
tāsāmekā kāle bhakṣyā peyāpi vā satatam || 178
28kṣīrarasadāḍimarasāḥ
surāsavaṃ madhu ca śiśiratoyāni

bradhnavamigulmapīnasahikkākāsāruciśvāsān || 183
jaṭharaṃ śvitraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ pāṇḍyaṃ klaibyaṃ madaṃ kṣayaṃ śoṣam
unmādāpasmārau vadanākṣiśirogadānsarvān || 184
ānāhamatīsāraṃ
sāsṛgdaraṃ kāmalāpramehāṃśca
yakṛdarbudāni vidradhiṃ
bhagandaraṃ raktapittaṃ ca || 185
atikārśyamatisthaulyaṃ
svedamatha ślīpadaṃ ca vinihanti
daṃṣṭrāviṣaṃ samaulaṃ
garāṇi ca bahuprakārāṇi || 186
mantrauṣadhiyogādīn
viprayutānbhautikānbhāvān
pāpālakṣmyau ceyaṃ
śamayedguḍikā śivā nāmnā || 187
balyā vṛṣyā dhanyā kāntiyaśaḥprajākarī ceyam
dadyānnṛpavallabhatāṃ jayaṃ vivāde sukhasthā ca || 188
śrīmānprakṛṣṭamedhaḥ
smṛtibuddhibalānvito'tulaśarīraḥ
puṣṭyojovarṇendriya-
tejobalasampadādisadupetaḥ || 189
valipalitarogarahito

dvābhyāṃ śatāni catvāri || 190
sarvāmayajitkathitaṃ
munigaṇabhakṣyaṃ rasāyanarahasyam || 191
samudbabhūvāmṛtamanthanotthaḥ
svedaḥ śilābhyo'mṛtavadgireḥ prāk

śivavadanavinirgatā yasmā-
nnāmnā tasmācchivāguḍiketi || 193
29supakvabhallātaphalāni samyak

vipācya toyena caturguṇena
caturthaśeṣe vyapanīya tāni || 194

ghṛtāṃśayuktena ghanaṃ yathā syāt

Adhikāra 66
1pippalīlavaṇopetau bastāṇḍau kṣīrasarpipā
sādhitau bhakṣayedyastu sa gacchetpramadāśatam || 1
bastāṇḍasiddhe payasi sādhitānasakṛttilān
yaḥ khādetsa naro gacchetstrīṇāṃ śatamapūrvavat || 2
cūrṇaṃ vidāryāḥ sukṛtaṃ svarasenaiva bhāvitam
sarpiḥkṣīrayutaṃ līḍhvā śataṃ gacchedvarāṅganāḥ || 3
evamāmalakaṃ cūrṇaṃ svarasenaiva bhāvitam
śarkarāmadhusarpirbhiryuktaṃ līḍhvā payaḥ pibet
etenāśītivarṣo'pi yuveva parihṛṣyate || 4
vidārīkandakalkaṃ tu ghṛtena payasā naraḥ
udumbarasamaṃ khādanvṛddho'pi taruṇāyate || 5
svayaṃguptāgokṣurayorbījacūrṇaṃ saśarkaram
dhāroṣṇena naraḥ pītvā payasā na kṣayaṃ vrajet || 6
uccaṭācūrṇamapyevaṃ kṣīreṇottamamucyate
śatāvaryuccaṭācūrṇaṃ peyamevaṃ sukhārthinā || 7
karṣaṃ madhukacūrṇasya ghṛtakṣaudrasamanvitam
payo'nupānaṃ yo lihyānnityavegaḥ sa nā bhavet || 8
gokṣurakaḥ kṣurakaḥ śatamūlī
vānari nāgabalātibalā ca
cūrṇamidaṃ payasā niśi peyaṃ
yasya gṛhe pramadāśatamasti || 9
2ghṛtabhṛṣṭo dugdhamāpapāyaso vṛṣya uttamaḥ
dadhnaḥ sāraṃ śaraccandrasannibhaṃ doṣavarjitam || 10
śarkarākṣaudramaricaistugākṣīryā ca buddhimān
yuktyā yuktaṃ sasūkṣmailaṃ nave kumbhe śucau paṭe || 11
mārjite prakṣipecchīte ghṛtāḍhyaṃ ṣaṣṭikaudanam
taddadyādupariṣṭācca rasānāṃ mātrayā pibet
varṇasvarabalopetaḥ pumāṃstena vṛṣāyate || 12
ārdrāṇi matsyamāṃsāni śapharīrvā subharjitāḥ
tapte sarpiṣi yaḥ khādetsa gacchetstrīṣu na kṣayam || 13
3śatāvarīrajaḥprasthaṃ prasthaṃ gokṣurakasya ca
vārāhyā viṃśatipalaṃ guḍūcyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
bhallātakānāṃ dvātriṃśaccitrakasya daśaiva tu || 14
tilānāṃ śodhitānāṃ ca prasthaṃ dadyātsucūrṇitam
tryūṣaṇasya palānyaṣṭau śarkarāyāśca saptatiḥ || 15
mākṣikaṃ śarkarārdhena mākṣikārdhena vai ghṛtam
śatāvarīsamaṃ deyaṃ vidārīkandajaṃ rajaḥ || 16
etadekīkṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ snigdhe bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
palārdhamupayuñjīta yatheṣṭaṃ cāpi bhojanam || 17
māsaikamupayogena jarāṃ hanti rujāmapi
balīpalitakhālityamehapāṇḍvādyapīnasān || 18
hantyaṣṭādaśa kuṣṭhāni tathāṣṭāvudarāṇi ca
bhagandaraṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ gṛdhrasīṃ sahalīmakam || 19
kṣayaṃ caiva mahāśvāsānpañcakāsānsudāruṇān
aśītiṃ vātajānrogāñcatvāriṃśacca paittikān || 20
viṃśatiṃ ślaiṣmikāṃścaiva saṃsṛṣṭānsānnipātikān
sarvānarśogadānhanti vṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā || 21
sa kāñcanābho mṛgarājavikrama-
sturaṅgamaṃ cāpyanuyāti vegataḥ
strīṇāṃ śataṃ gacchati so'tirekaṃ
prakṛṣṭadṛṣṭiśca yathā vihaṅgaḥ || 22
putrānsañjanayedvīrānnarasiṃhanibhāṃstathā
nārasiṃhamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvarogaharaṃ nṛṇām || 23
vārāhīkandasaṅgastu carmakārāluko mataḥ
paścime ghṛṣṭiśabdākhyo varāhalomavāniva || 24
4godhūmācca palaśataṃ niḥkvāthya salilāḍhake
pādāvaśeṣe pūte ca dravyāṇīmāni dāpayet || 25
godhūmaṃ yuñjātaphalaṃ māṣadrākṣāparūṣakam
kākolī kṣīrakākolī jīvantī saśatāvarī || 26
aśvagandhā sakharjūrā madhukaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ sitā
bhallātakamātmaguptā samabhāgāni kārayet || 27
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedekaṃ kṣīraṃ dattvā caturguṇam
mṛdvagninā ca siddhe ca dravyāṇyetāni niḥkṣipet || 28
tvagelāpippalīdhānyakarpūraṃ nāgakeśaram
yathālābhaṃ vinikṣipya sitākṣaudrapalāṣṭakam || 29
śattvekṣudaṇḍenāloḍya vidhivadviniyojayet
dālyodanena bhuñjīta pibenmāṃsarasena vā || 30
kevalasya pibedasya palamātrāṃ pramāṇataḥ
na tasya liṅgaśaithilyaṃ na ca śukrakṣayo bhavet || 31
balyaṃ paraṃ vātaharaṃ śukrasañjananaṃ param
mūtrakṛcchrapraśamanaṃ vṛddhānāṃ cāpi śasyate || 32
paladvayaṃ tadaśnīyāddaśarātramatandritaḥ
strīṇāṃ śataṃ ca bhajate pītvā cānupibetpayaḥ || 33
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaṃ caitadgodhūmādyaṃ rasāyanam
jaladroṇe tu godhūmakvāthe taccheṣamāḍhakam || 34
yuñjātakasya sthāne tu tadguṇaṃ tālamastakam
kalkadravyasamaṃ mānaṃ tvagādeḥ sāhacaryataḥ || 35
5ghṛtaṃ śatāvarīgarbhaṃ kṣīre daśaguṇe pacet
śarkarāpippalīkṣaudrayuktaṃ tadvṛṣyamucyate || 36
6kūṣmāṇḍakātpalaśataṃsusvinnaṃniṣkulīkṛtam
prasthaṃ ghṛtasya tailasya tasmiṃstapte pradāpayet || 37
tvakpatradhanyākavyoṣajīrakailādvayānalam
granthikaṃ cavyamānaṅgapippalīviśvabheṣajam || 38
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ kaśeruṃ ca pralambaṃ tālamastakam
cūrṇīkṛtaṃ palāṃśaṃ ca guḍasya ca tulāṃ pacet || 39
śītībhūte palānyaṣṭau madhunaḥ saṃpradāpayet
kaphapittānilaharaṃ mandāgnīnāṃ ca śasyate || 40
kṛśānāṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vājīkaraṇamuttamam
pramadāsu prasaktānāṃ ye ca syuḥ kṣīṇaretasaḥ || 41
kṣayeṇa ca gṛhītānāṃ parametadbhiṣagjitam
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ hikkāṃ hanti chardimarocakam || 42
guḍakūṣmāṇḍakaṃ khyātamaśvibhyāṃ samudāhṛtam
khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍavatpātraṃ svinnakūṣmāṇḍakaddravaḥ || 43
yatkiñcinmadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ jīvanaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ guru
harṣaṇaṃ manasaścaiva sarvaṃ tadvṛṣyamucyate || 44
bhallātakabṛhatīphala-
dāḍimaphalakalkasādhitaṃ kurute
liṅgaṃ mardanavidhinā
kaṭutailaṃ vājiliṅgābham || 45
kanakarasamasṛṇavartita-
hayagandhāmūlaviśvaparyuṣitam
māhiṣamiha navanītaṃ
gatabīje kanakaphalamadhye || 46
gomayagāḍhodvartitaṃ
pūrvaṃ paścādanena saṃliptam
bhavati hayaliṅgasadṛśaṃ
liṅgaṃ kaṭhināṅganādayitam || 47
7aśvagandhāvarīkuṣṭhamāṃsīsiṃhīphalānvitam
caturguṇena dugdhena tilatailaṃ vipācayet
stanaliṅgakarṇapālivardhanaṃ mrakṣaṇādidam || 48
8bhallātakabṛhatīphala-
nalinīdalasindhujalaśūkaiḥ
māhipanavanītena ca
karambitaiḥ saptadinamuṣitaiḥ || 49
mūlena hayagandhāyā māhiṣamalamarditapūrvamatha
liptaṃ bhavati laghukṛtarāsabhaliṅgaṃ dhruvaṃ puṃsām || 50
nīlotpalasitapaṅkajakeśaramadhuśarkarāvaliptena
surate suciraṃ ramate dṛḍhaliṅgo bhavati nābhivivareṇa || 51
siddhaṃ kusumbhatailaṃ bhūmilatācūrṇamiśritaṃ kurute
caraṇābhyaṅgena raterbījastambhāddṛḍhaṃ liṅgam || 52
saptāhaṃ chāgabhava-
salilasthaṃ karabhavāruṇīmūlam
gāḍhodvartanavidhinā
liṅgastambhaṃ tathā dṛḍhaṃ kurute || 53
gorekonnataśṛṅga-
tvagbhavacūrṇena dhūpitaṃ vastram
paridhāya bhajati lalanāṃ
naikāṇḍe? bhavati harṣārtaḥ || 54
samatilagokṣuracūrṇaṃ
chāgīkṣīreṇa sādhitaṃ samadhu
bhuktaṃ kṣapayati ṣāṇḍhyaṃ
yajjanitaṃ suprayogeṇa || 55
yogajavarāṅgabaddhaṃ
mathitena khālityaṃ harati
unmukhagośṛṅgodbhava-
mṛllepo? yogajadhvajabhaṅgaharaḥ || 56

mustakadhanyākamadhukajaḥ kavalaḥ

rasonamadirādijaṃ gandham || 57

tvaglīḍhādhovātagandhanut || 58
Adhikāra 67
1sarpistailaṃ vasā majjā sneheṣu pravaraṃ matam
tatrāpi cottamaṃ sarpiḥ saṃskārasyānuvartanāt || 1
kevalaṃ paittike sarpirvātike lavaṇānvitam
deyaṃ bahukaphe cāpi vyoṣakṣārasamāyutam || 2
tathā dhīsmṛtimedhāgnikāṅkṣiṇāṃ śasyate ghṛtam
granthināḍīkrimiśleṣmamedomārutarogiṣu || 3
tailaṃ lāghavadārḍhyārthaṃ krūrakoṣṭheṣu dehiṣu
vātātapādhvabhārastrīvyāyāmakṣīṇadhātuṣu || 4
rukṣakleśakṣayātyagnivātāvṛtapatheṣu ca
śeṣau vasante sandhyasthimarmakoṣṭharujāsu ca
tathā dagdhāhatabhraṣṭayonikarṇaśiroruji || 5
tailaṃ prāvṛṣi varṣānte sarpirantyau tu mādhave
sārādhaṇaṛtau snehaṃ pibetkāryavaśādiha || 6
2vātapittādhiko rātrāvuṣṇe cāpi pibennaraḥ
śleṣmādhiko divā śīte pibeccāmalabhāskare || 7
svedyasaṃśodhyamadyastrīvyāyāmāsakticintakāḥ
vṛddhā bālā balakṛśā rukṣakṣīṇāsraretasaḥ || 8
vātārtasyandatimiradāruṇapratirodhinaḥ
snehyā na tvatimandāgnitīkṣṇāgnisthūladurbalāḥ || 9
ūrustambhātisārāmagalarogagarodaraiḥ
mūrcchāchardyaruciśleṣmatṛṣṇāmadyaiśca pīḍitāḥ || 10
āmaprasūtā yukte ca nasye bastau virecane
snehasātmyaḥ kleśasaho dṛḍhaḥ kāle ca śītale || 11
acchameva pibetsnehamacchapānaṃ hi śobhanam
pivetsaṃśamanaṃ snehamannakāle prakāṅkṣitaḥ || 12
śuddhyarthaṃ punarāhāre naiśe jīrṇe pivennaraḥ
ahorātramahaḥ kṛtsnaṃ dinārdhaṃ ca pratīkṣate || 13
3uttamā madhyamā hrasvā snehamātrā jarāṃ prati
uttamasya palaṃ mātrā tribhiścākṣaiśca madhyame || 14
jaghanyasya palārdhena snehakvāthyauṣadheṣu ca
jalamuṣṇaṃ ghṛte peyaṃ yūṣastaile'nuśasyate || 15
vasāmajjñostu maṇḍaḥ syātsarveṣūṣṇamathāmbu vā
bhallātetaurave snehe śītameva jalaṃ pibet || 16
snehapītastu tṛṣṇāyāṃ pibeduṣṇodakaṃ naraḥ
evaṃ cānupraśāmyantaṃ snehamuṣṇāmbunoddharet || 17
mithyācārādvahutvādvā yasya sneho na jīryati
viṣṭabhya vāpi jīryettaṃ vāriṇoṣṇena vāmayet || 18
tataḥ snehaṃ punardadyāllaghukoṣṭhāya dehine
jīrṇājīrṇaviśaṅkāyāṃ pibeduṣṇodakaṃ naraḥ || 19
tenodgāro bhavecchuddho ruciścānnaṃ bhavetprati
bhojyānnaṃ mātrayā pāsyan śvaḥ pibanpītavānapi
dravoṣṇamanabhiṣyandi nātisnigdhamaśaṅkaram || 20
tryahāvaraṃ saptadinaṃ parantu
snigdho paraḥ svedayitavya iṣṭaḥ
nātaḥ paraṃ snehanamādiśanti
sātmī bhavet saptadinātparaṃ tu || 21
4mṛdukoṣṭhastrirātreṇa snihyatyacchopasevayā
snihyati krūrakoṣṭhastu saptarātreṇa mānavaḥ || 22
snigdhadravoṣṇadhanvottharasabhuksvedamācaret
snigdhastryahaṃ sthitaḥ kuryādvirekaṃ vamanaṃ punaḥ || 23
ekāhaṃ dinamanyacca kaphamutkleśya tatkaraiḥ
vātānulomyaṃ dīptāgnirvarcaḥ snigdhamasaṃhatam || 24
snehodvegaḥ klamaḥ samyak snigdhe rukṣe viparyayaḥ
atisnigdhe tu pāṇḍutvaṃ ghrāṇavaktragudasravāḥ || 25
rukṣasya snehanaṃ kāryamatisnigdhasya rukṣaṇam
śyāmākakoradūṣānnatakrapiṇyākaśaktubhiḥ || 26
bālavṛddhādiṣu snehaparihārāsahiṣṇuṣu
yogānimānanudvegānsadyaḥ snehānprayojayet || 27
bhṛṣṭe māṃsarase snigdhā yavāgūḥ svalpataṇḍulā
sakṣaudrā sevyamānā tu sadyaḥ snehanamucyate || 28
sarpistailavasāmajjātaṇḍulaprasṛtaiḥ śṛtā
pāñcaprasṛtikī peyā peyā snehanamicchatā || 29
sarpiṣmatī bahutilā tathaiva svalpataṇḍulā
sukhoṣṇā sevyamānā tu sadyaḥ snehanamucyate || 30
śarkarāghṛtasaṃsṛṣṭe duhyādgāṃ kalase'thavā
pāyayedakṣametaddhi sadyaḥ snehanamucyate || 31
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ guḍaṃ dadhi payastilān
kuṣṭhī śothī pramehī ca snehanena prayojayet || 32
snehairyathāsvaṃ tānsiddhaiḥ snehayedavikāribhiḥ
pippalībhirharītakyā siddhaistriphalayā saha || 33
snehamagre prayuñjīta tataḥ svedamanantaram
snehasvedopapannasya saṃśodhanamathāntaram || 34
Adhikāra 68
1vātaśleṣmaṇi vāte vā kaphe vā sveda iṣyate
snigdharukṣastathā snigdho rukṣaścātyupakalpitaḥ || 1
vyādhau śīte śarīre ca mahānsvedo mahābale
durbale durbalaḥ svedo madhyame madhyamo mataḥ || 2
āmāśayagate vāte kaphe pakvāśayāśraye
rakṣapūrvo hitaḥ svedaḥ snehapūrvastathaiva ca || 3
vṛṣaṇau hṛdayaṃ dṛṣṭī svedayenmṛdu vā na vā
madhyamaṃ vaṅkṣaṇau śeṣamaṅgāvayabamiṣṭataḥ
na svedayedatisthūlarukṣadurbalamūrcchitān || 4
stambhanīyakṣatakṣīṇaviṣamadyavikāriṇaḥ
timirodaravīsarpakuṣṭhaśoṣāḍhyarogiṇaḥ || 5
pītadugdhadadhisnehamadhūnkṛtavirecanān
bhraṣṭadagdhagudaglānikrodhaśokabhayārditān || 6
kṣuttṛṣṇākāmalāpāṇḍūmehinaḥ pittapīḍitān
garbhiṇīṃ puṣpitāṃ sūtāṃ mṛduvātyayike gade || 7
svedo hitastvanāgneyo vāte medaḥkaphāvṛte
nivātagṛhamāyāso guruprāvaraṇaṃ bhayam || 8
upanāhāhavakrodhabhūripānakṣudhātapāḥ
svedayanti daśaitāni naramagniguṇādṛte || 9
śītaśūlavyuparamaistambhagauravanigrahe
saṃjāte mārdave svede svedanādviratirmatā || 10
2sphoṭotpattiḥ pittaraktaprakopo
mado mūrcchābhramadāhau klamaśca
atisvede sandhipīḍā tṛṣā ca
kriyāḥ śītāstatra kuryādvidhijñaḥ || 11
sarvānsvedāgnivāte tu jīrṇānne cāvacārayet
yeṣāṃ nasyaṃ vidhātavyaṃ bastiścāpi hi dehinām || 12
śodhanīyāstu ye kecitpūrvaṃ svedyāstu te matāḥ
paścātsvedyā hṛte śalye mūḍhagarbhānupadravāḥ || 13
samyakprajātā kāle ca paścātsvedyā vijānatā
svedaḥ paścācca pūrvaṃ ca bhagandaryarśasastathā || 14
taptaiḥ saikatapāṇikāṃsyavasanaiḥ svedo'thavāṅgārakai-
rlepādvātaharaiḥ sahāmlalavaṇasnehaiḥ sukhoṣṇairbhavet
evaṃ taptapayo'mbu vātaśamanakvāthādisekādibhiḥ
taptaistoyanisecanodbhavabṛhadvāṣpaiḥ śilādyaiḥ kramāt || 15
tāpopanāhadravavāṣpapūrvāḥ
svedāstato'ntyaprathamau kaphe staḥ
vāyau dvitīyaḥ pavane kaphe ca
pittopasṛṣṭe vihitastṛtīyaḥ || 16
Adhikāra 69
1snigdhasvinnaṃ kaphesamyaksaṃyoge vā kapholbaṇe
śvovamyamutkliṣṭakaphaṃ matsyamāṃsatilādibhiḥ || 1
yathāvikāraṃ vihitāṃ madhusaindhavasaṃyutām
koṣṭhaṃ vibhajya bhaiṣajyamātrāṃ mantrābhimantritām || 2
“brahmadattāśvirudrendrabhūcandrārkānilānalāḥ
ṛṣayaḥ soṣadhigrāmā bhūtasaṅghāstu pāntu te || 3
rasāyanamivarṣīṇāṃ devānāmamṛtaṃ yathā
sudhevottamanāgānāṃ bhaiṣajyamidamastu te” || 4
pūrvāhṇe pāyapetpīto jānutulyāsane sthitaḥ
tanmanā jātahṛllāsaprasekaśchardayettataḥ || 5
aṅgulībhyāmanāyastanālena mṛdunāthavā
vaṣendrayavasindhūtthavacākalkayutaṃ pibet || 6
2yaṣṭīkaṣāyaṃ sakṣaudraṃ tena sādhu vamatyalam
taṇḍulasalilaniṣpiṣṭaṃ yaḥ pītvā vamati pūrvāhṇe || 7
phalinīvalkalamuṣṇaṃ harati garaṃ pittakaphajaṃ ca
kṣaudralīḍhaṃ tāmrarajo vamanaṃ garadoṣanut || 8
āṭarūṣaṃ vacā nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ phalinītvacam
kvāthayitvā pibettoyaṃ vāntikṛnmadanānvitam || 9
kvāthyadravyasya kuḍavaṃ sthāpayitvā jalāḍhake
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu vamaneṣvavacārayet || 10
nimbakaṣāyopetaṃ phalinīgadamadanamadhukasindhūtthaṃ
madhuyutametadvamanaṃ kaphataḥ pūrṇāśaye sadā śastam || 11
phalajīmūtakekṣvākukuṭajāḥ kṛtavedhanaḥ
dhāmārgavaśca saṃyojyāḥ sarvathā vamaneṣvamī || 12
3kramātkaphaḥ pittamathānilaśca
yasyeti samyagvamitaḥ sa iṣṭaḥ
hṛtpārśvamūrdhendriyamārgaśuddhau
tanorlaghutve'pi ca lakṣyamāṇe || 13
ducchardite sphoṭakakoṭhakaṇḍū-
kṛtsvāviśuddhirgurugātratā ca
tṛṇmohamūrcchānilakopanidrā-
balātihānirvamite'tividyāt || 14
tataḥ sāyaṃ prabhāte vā kṣudvānpeyādikaṃ bhajet || 15
peyāṃ vilepīmakṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ ca
yūṣaṃ rasaṃ dvistrirathaikaśaśca
krameṇa seveta viśuddhakāyaḥ
pradhānamadhyāvaraśuddhiśuddhaḥ || 16
jaghanyamadhyapravare tu vegā-
ścatvāra iṣṭā vamane ṣaḍaṣṭau
daśaiva te dvitriguṇā vireke
prasthastathā dvitricaturguṇaśca || 17
pittāntamiṣṭaṃ vamanaṃ virekā-
dardhaṃ kaphāntaṃ ca virekamāhuḥ
dvitrānsaviṭkāvapanīya vegān
meyaṃ vireke vamane tu pītam || 18
4vamane ca vireke ca tathā śoṇitamokṣaṇe
sārdhatrayodaśapalaṃ prasthamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 19
ayoge laṅghanaṃ kāryaṃ punarvāpi viśodhanam
ativāntaṃ ghṛtābhyaktamavagāhya hime jale || 20
upācaretsitākṣaudramiśrairlehaiścikitsakaḥ
vamane'tipravṛtte tu hṛdyaṃ kāryaṃ virecanam || 21
na vāmayettaimirikaṃ na gulminaṃ
na cāpi pāṇḍūdararogapīḍitam
sthūlakṣatakṣīṇakṛśātivṛddhā-
narśorditākṣepakapīḍitāṃśca || 22
rukṣe pramehe taruṇe ca garbhe
gacchatyathordhvaṃ rudhire ca tīvre
daṣṭe ca koṣṭhe krimibhirmanuṣyaṃ
na vāmayedvarcasi cātibaddhe || 23
ete'pyajīrṇavyathitā vāmyā ye ca viṣāturāḥ
atyulbaṇakaphā ye ca te ca syurmadhukāmbunā || 24
Adhikāra 70
1sigdhasvinnāyavāntāya dātavyaṃ tu virecanam
anyathā yojitaṃ hyetadgrahaṇīgadakṛnmatam || 1
mṛduḥ pittena koṣṭhaḥ syātkrūro vātakaphāśrayāt
madhyamaḥ samadopatvādyojyā mātrānurūpataḥ || 2
śarkarākṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ trivṛccūrṇāvacūrṇitam
recanaṃ sukumārāṇāṃ tvakpatramaricāṃśikam
trivṛccūrṇaṃ sitāyuktaṃ pibecchreṣṭhaṃ virecanam || 3
chittvā dvidhekṣuṃ parilipya kalkaiḥ
strimaṇḍijātaiḥ pariveṣṭya baddhvā
pakvaṃ tu samyakpuṭapākayuktyā
khādettu taṃ pittagadī suśītam || 4
pippalīnāgarakṣāraṃ śyāmātrivṛtayā saha
lehayenmadhunā sārdhaṃ kaphavyādhau virecanam || 5
harītakī viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ nāgaraṃ trivṛt
maricāni ca tatsarvaṃ gomūtreṇa virecanam || 6
trivṛcchāṇatrayasamā triphalā tatsamāni ca
kṣārakṛṣṇāviḍaṅgāni taccūrṇaṃ madhusarpiṣā || 7
lihyādguḍena guḍikāṃ kṛtvā vāpyupayojayet
kaphavātakṛtāngulmānplīhodarabhagandarān || 8
hantyanyānapi cāpyetannirapāyaṃ virecanam
2abhayā pippalīmūlaṃ maricaṃ nāgaraṃ tathā || 9
tvakpatrapippalīmustaviḍaṅgāmalakāni ca
karṣaḥ pratyekameṣāṃ tu dantyāḥ karṣatrayaṃ tathā || 10
paṭkarṣāśca sitāyāstu dvipalaṃ trivṛto bhavet
sarvaṃ sucūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā madhunā modakaṃ kṛtam || 11
khādetpratidinaṃ caikaṃ śīṃta cānu pibejjalam
tāvadviricyate janturyāvaduṣṇaṃ na sevate || 12
pāṇḍurogaṃ vipaṃ kāsaṃ jaṅghāpārśvarujau tathā
pṛṣṭhārtiṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca durnāma sabhagandaram || 13
aśmarīmehakuṣṭhāni ca dāhaśothodarāṇi ca
yakṣmāṇaṃ cakṣuṣo rogaṃ kramaṃ vaidyena jānatā
yojito'yaṃ nihantyāśu abhayādyo hi modakaḥ || 14
eraṇḍatailaṃ triphalākvāthena dviguṇena ca
yuktaṃ pītvā payobhirvā na cireṇa viricyate || 15
3srotoviśuddhīndriyasamprasādau
laghutvamūrjo'gniranāmayatvam
prāptiśca viṭpittakaphānilānāṃ
samyagviriktasya bhavet krameṇa || 16
syācchleṣmapittānilasaṃprakopaḥ
sādastathāgnergurutā pratiśyā
tandrā tathā chardirarocakaśca
vātānulomyaṃ na ca durvirikte || 17
kaphāsrapittakṣayajānilotthāḥ
suptyaṅgamardaklamavepanādyāḥ
nidrāvalābhāvatamaḥpraveśāḥ
sonmādahikkāśca virecite'ti || 18
mandāgnimakṣīṇamasaṃdviriktaṃ
na pāyayettaddivase yavāgūm
viparyaye taddivase tu sāyaṃ
peyākramo vāntavadiṣyate tu || 19
yathāṇuragnistṛṇagomayādyaiḥ
sandhukṣyamāṇo bhavati krameṇa
mahānsthiraḥ sarvasahastathaiva
śuddhasya peyādibhirantarāgniḥ || 20
4kaṣāyamadhuraiḥ pitte virekaḥ kaṭukaiḥ kaphe
snidhoṣṇalavaṇairvāyorapravṛtte ca pāyayet || 21
uṣṇāmbu svedayeccāsya pāṇitāpena codaram
utthāne'lpe dine tasminbhuktvānyedyuḥ punaḥ pibet || 22
adṛḍhasnehakoṣṭhastu pibedūrdhvaṃ daśāhataḥ
bhūyo'ṣyupaskṛtatanuḥ snehasvedairvirecanam || 23
yaugikaṃ samyagāloḍya smaranpūrvamanukramam
durvalaḥ śodhitaḥ pūrvamalpadoṣaḥ kṛśo naraḥ
aparijñātakoṣṭhastu pibenmṛdvalpamauṣadham || 24
rukṣavahvanilakrūrakoṣṭhavyāyāmasevinām
dīptāgnīnāṃ ca bhaipajyamavirecyaiva jīryati || 25
tebhyo vastiṃ purā dadyāttataḥ snigdhaṃ virecanam
asnigdhe recanaṃ snigdhaṃ rukṣaṃ snigdhe'tiśasyate || 26
virukṣya snehasātmyaṃ tu bhūyaḥ snigdhaṃ virecayet
padmakośīranāgāhvacandanāni prayojayet || 27
atiyoge virekasya pānālepanasecanaiḥ
sauvīrapiṣṭāmravalkalābhilepo'tisārahā || 28
avirecyā vālavṛddhaśrāntabhītanavajvarāḥ
alpāgnyadhogapittāsrakṣatapāyvatisāriṇaḥ || 29
saśalyā sthāpitakrūrakoṣṭhātisnigdhaśoṣiṇaḥ
garbhiṇī navasūtā ca tṛṣṇārto'jīrṇavānapi || 30
Adhikāra 71
1vāṃtolbaṇeṣu doṣeṣu vāte vā bastiriṣyate
yathocitātpādahīnaṃ bhojayitvānuvāsayet || 1
na cābhuktavate snehaḥ praṇidheyaḥ kathañcana
sūkṣmatvācchūnyakoṣṭhasya kṣipramūrdhvamathotpatet || 2
ṣaṭpalī ca bhavecchreṣṭhā madhyamā vipalī bhavet
kanīyasī sārdhapalā tridhā mātrānuvāsane || 3
prāgdeyamādye dvipalaṃ palārdha-
vṛddhirdvitīye palamakṣavṛddhiḥ
karṣadvayaṃ vā vasumāṣavṛddhi-
rbastau tṛtīye krama eṣa uktaḥ || 4
māṣamātraṃ pale snehe sindhujanmaśatāhvayoḥ
sa tu saindhavacūrṇena śatāhvena ca saṃyutaḥ || 5
bhavetsukhoṣṇaśca tathā nireti sahasā mukham
viriktaścānuvāsyaścetsaptarātrātparaṃ tadā || 6
2suvarṇarūpyatraputāmrarīti-
kāṃsyāyasāsthidrumaveṇudantaiḥ
nalairviṣāṇairmaṇibhiśca taistaiḥ
kāryāṇi netrāṇi sukarṇikāni || 7
paḍdvādaśāṣṭāṅgulasammitāni
ṣaḍviṃśatidvādaśavarṣajānām
syurmudgakarkandhusatīlavāhi
chidrāṇi vartyā pihitāni cāpi || 8
yathā yavo'ṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ
mūlāgrayoḥ syuḥ pariṇāhavanti
ṛjūni gopucchasamākṛtīni
ślakṣṇāni ca syurguḍikāmukhāni || 9
syātkarṇikaikāgracaturthabhāge
mūlāśrite bastinibandhane dve
jāradgavo māhiṣahāriṇau vā
syācchaukaro vastirajasya vāpi || 10
dṛḍhastanurnaṣṭaśirovibandhaḥ
kaṣāyaraktaḥ sumṛduḥ suśuddhaḥ
nṛṇāṃ vayo vīkṣya yathānurūpaṃ
netreṣu yojyastu suvaddhasūtraḥ || 11
3nirūhamātrā prathame prakuñco vatsare param
prakuñcavṛddhiḥ pratyabdaṃ yāvatpaṭ prasṛtāstataḥ || 12
prasṛtaṃ vardhayedūrdhvaṃ dvādaśāṣṭādaśasya tu
āsaptateridaṃ mānaṃ daśaiva prasṛtāḥ param || 13
yathāyathaṃ nirūhasya pādo mātrānuvāsane
kṛtacaṃkramaṇaṃ muktaviṇmūtraṃ śayane sukhe || 14
nātyucchrite na cocchīrṣe saṃviṣṭaṃ vāmapārśvataḥ
saṅkocya dakṣiṇaṃ sakthi prasārya ca tato'param
bastiṃ savye kare kṛtvā dakṣiṇenāvapīḍayet || 15
tathāsya netraṃ praṇayetsnigdhe snigdhamukhaṃ gude
ucchvāsya bastervadanaṃ baddhvā hastamakampayan || 16
pṛṣṭhavaṃśaṃ prati tato nātidrutavilambitam
nātivegaṃ na vā mandaṃ sakṛdeva prapīḍayet
sāvaśeṣaṃ prakurvīta vāyuḥ śeṣe hi tiṣṭhati || 17
nirūidāne'pi vidhirayameva samīritaḥ
4tataḥ praṇihite snehe uttāno vākśachataṃ bhavet
prasāritaiḥ sarvagātraistathā vīryaṃ prasarpati || 18
ākuñcayecchanaistristriḥ sakthibāhū tataḥ param
tāḍayettalayorenaṃ trīṃstrīnvārāñchanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 19
sphicoścainaṃ tataḥ śroṇiṃ śayyāṃ trirutkṣipecchanaiḥ
evaṃ praṇihite vastau mandāyāso'tha mandavāk || 20
āstīrṇe śayane kāmamāsītācārike rataḥ
yojyaḥ śīghraṃ nivṛtte'nyaḥ sneho'tiṣṭhanna kāryakṛt || 21
sānilaḥ sapurīṣaśca snehaḥ pratyeti yasya vai
vinā pīḍāṃ triyāmasthaḥ sa samyaganuvāsitaḥ || 22
kvāthārdhamātrayā prātardhānyaśuṇṭhījalaṃ pibet
pittottare kaduṣṇāmbhastāvanmātraṃ pibedanu || 23
tenāsya dīpyate vahnirbhaktākāṅkṣā ca jāyate
ahorātrādapi snehaḥ pratyāgacchanna duṣyati || 24
kuryādbastiguṇāṃścāpi jīrṇastvalpaguṇo bhavet
yasya nopadravaṃ kuryātsnehabastiraniḥsṛtaḥ || 25
sarvo'lpo vā vṛto raukṣyādupekṣyaḥ saṃvijānatā
anāyantamahorātrātsnehaṃ sopadravaṃ haret || 26
snehabastāvanāyāte nānyaḥ seko vidhīyate
5aśuddhasya malonmiśraḥ sneho naiti yadā punaḥ || 27
tadāṅgasadanādhvānaśūlāḥ śvāsaśca jāyate
pakvāśayagurutvaṃ ca tatra dadyānnirūhaṇam || 28
tīkṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇauṣadhaireva siddhaṃ cāpyanuvāsanam
snehabastirvidheyastu nāviśuddhasya dehinaḥ || 29
snehavīryaṃ tathādatte sneho nānuvisarpati
aśuddhamapi vātena kevalenābhipīḍitam || 30
ahorātrasya kāleṣu sarveṣvevānuvāsayet
anuvāsayettṛtīye'hni pañcame vā punaśca tam || 31
yathā vā snehapaktiḥ syādato'pyulvaṇamārutān
vyāyāmanityāndīptāgnīnrukṣāṃśca prativāsaram || 32
iti snehaistricaturaiḥ snigdhe srotoviśuddhaye
nirūhaṃ śodhanaṃ yuñjyādasnigdhe snehanaṃ tanoḥ || 33
viṣṭabdhānilaviṇmūtraḥ sneho hīne'nuvāsane
dāhajvarapipāsārtikaraścātyanuvāsane || 34
6snehabastiṃ nirūhaṃ vā naikamevātiśīlayet
snehātpittakaphotkledo nirūhātpavanādbhayam || 35
anāsthāpyā ye'bhidheyā nānuvāsyāśca te matāḥ
viśeṣatastvamī pāṇḍūkāmalāmehapīnasāḥ || 36
niramlaplīhaviḍbhedī gurukoṣṭhakaphodarāḥ
abhiṣyandabhṛśasthūlakrimikoṣṭhāḍhyamārutāḥ || 37
pīte viṣe gare'pacyāṃ ślīpadī galagaṇḍavān
anāsthāpyastvatisnigdhaḥ kṣatorasko bhṛśaṃ kṛśaḥ || 38
āmātisārī vamimānsaṃśuddho dattanāvanaḥ
śvāsakāsaprasekārśohikkādhmānālpavahnayaḥ || 39
śūlapāyuḥ kṛtāhāro baddhacchidradakodarī
kuṣṭhī ca madhumehī ca māsānsapta ca garbhiṇī || 40
na caikānte na nirdiṣṭe'pyatrābhiniviśedbudhaḥ
bhavetkadācitkāryāpi viruddhāpi matā kriyā || 41
chardihṛdrogagulmārte vamanaṃ sucikitsite
avasthāṃ prāpya nirdiṣṭaṃ kuṣṭhināṃ bastikarma ca || 42
Adhikāra 72
1anuvāsya snigdhatanuṃ tṛtīye'hni nirūhayet
madhyāhne kiñcidāvṛtte prayukte valimaṅgale || 1
abhyaktasveditotsṛṣṭamalaṃ nātibubhukṣitam
madhusnehanakalkākhyakaṣāyāvāpataḥ kramāt || 2
trīṇi ṣaḍ dve daśa trīṇi palānyanilarogiṣu
pitte catvāri catvāri dve dvipañcacatuṣṭayam || 3
ṣaṭ trīṇi dve daśa trīṇi kaphe cāpi nirūhaṇam
dattvādau saindhavasyākṣaṃ madhunaḥ prasṛtadvayam || 4
vinirmathya tato dadyātsnehasya prasṛtadvayam
ekībhūte tataḥ snehe kalkasya prasṛtaṃ kṣipet || 5
saṃmūrcchitaṃ kaṣāyaṃ taṃ pañcaprasṛtasaṃmitam
bitarettu yathāvāpamante dviprasṛtonmitam || 6
vastrapūtastathoṣṇāmbukumbhīvāṣpeṇa tāpitaḥ
evaṃ prakalpito vastirdvādaśaprasṛto bhavet || 7
na dhāvatyaupadhaṃ pāṇiṃ na tiṣṭhatyavalipya ca
na karoti ca sīmantaṃ sa nirūhaḥ suyojitaḥ || 8
2pūrvoktena vidhānena gudavastiṃ nidhāpayet
triṃśanmātrāsthito vastistatastūtkaṭako bhavet || 9
jānumaṇḍalamāveṣṭya dattaṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā
kṛṣṭanetracchaṭāśabdaśataṃ tiṣṭhedavegavān || 10
dvitīyaṃ vā tṛtīyaṃ vā caturthaṃ vā yathārthataḥ
samyak nirūhaliṅge tu prāpte vastiṃ nivārayet || 11
prasṛṣṭaviṇmūtrasamīraṇatvaṃ
rucyagnivṛddhyāśayalāghavāni
rogopaśāntiḥ prakṛtisthatā ca
valaṃ ca tatsyātsunirūḍhaliṅgam || 12
ayogaścātiyogaśca nirūhaśca viriktavat || 13
snigdhoṣṇa ekaḥ pavane samāṃso
dvau svāduśītau payasā ca pitte || 14
trayaḥ samūtrāḥ kaṭu koṣṇarukṣāḥ
kaphe nirūhā na paraṃ vidheyāḥ
eko'pakarṣatyanilaṃ svamārgāt
pittaṃ dvitīyastu kaphaṃ tṛtīyaḥ || 15
3āyāmāntaṃ muhūrtāntaṃ nirūhaṃ śodhanairharet
nirūhaireva matimānkṣāramūtrāmlasaṃyutaiḥ || 16
viguṇānilaviṣṭabdhaściraṃ tiṣṭhannirūhaṇaḥ
śūlāratijvarāṭopānmaraṇaṃ vā prayacchati || 17
na tu bhuktavate deyamāsthāpanamiti sthitiḥ
āmaṃ taddhi haredbhuktaṃ chardidopāṃśca kopayet || 18
āvasthikaḥ kramaścāpi matvā kāryo nirūhaṇe
atiprapīḍito bastiratikramyāśayaṃ tataḥ || 19
vāterito nāsikābhyāṃ mukhato vā prapadyate
chardihṛllāsamūrcchādīnprakuryāddāhameva ca || 20
tatra tūrṇaṃ galāpīḍaṃ kuryāccāpyavadhūnanam
śiraḥkāyavirekau ca tīkṣṇau sekāṃśca śītalān || 21
munirūḍhamathoṣṇāmbu snātaṃ bhuktarasaudanam
yathoktena vidhānena yojayetsnehavastinā || 22
tadahastasya pavanādbhayaṃ balavadiṣyate
rasaudanastena śastastadahaścānuvāsanam || 23
4daśamūlīkapāyeṇa śatāhvākṣaṃ prayojayet
saindhavākṣaṃ ca madhuno dvipalaṃ dvipalaṃ tathā || 24
tailasya palamekaṃ tu phalasyaikatra yojayet
ardhamātṛkasaṅgo'yaṃ bastirdeyo nirūhavat || 25
na ca sneho na ca svedaḥ parihāravidhirna ca
ātreyānumato hyeṣa sarvaroganivāraṇaḥ || 26
yakṣmaghnaśca śūlaghnaśca krimighnaśca viśepataḥ
śukrasañjanano hyeṣa vātaśoṇitanāśanaḥ
balavarṇakaro vṛṣyo vastiḥ puṃsavanaḥ paraḥ || 27
5snehaṃ guḍaṃ māṃsarasaṃ payaśca
amlāni mūtraṃ madhusaindhave ca
etānyanuktāni ca dāpayecca
nirūhayoge madanātphalaṃ ca || 28
lavaṇaṃ kārṣikaṃ dadyātpalamekaṃ tu mādanam
vāte guḍaḥ sitāpitte kaphe siddhārthakādayaḥ || 29
saindhavākṣaṃ samādāya śatāhvākṣaṃ tathaiva ca
gomūtrasya palānyaṣṭau vastikāyāḥ paladvayam || 30
guḍasya dve pale caiva sarvamāloḍya yatnataḥ
vastrapūtaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ ca bastiṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 31
śūlaṃ viṭsaṅgamānāhaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca dāruṇam
krimyudāvartagulmādīnsadyo hanyānniṣevitaḥ || 32
palaśuktikarṣakuḍavai-
ramlīguḍasindhujanmagomūtraiḥ
tailayuto'yaṃ bastiḥ
śūlānāhāmavātaharaḥ || 33
6vaitaraṇaḥ kṣārabastirbhukte cāpi pradīyate || 34
badaryairāvatīśeluśālmalīdhanvanāṅkurāḥ
kṣīrasiddhāḥ susiddhāḥsyuḥ sāsrāḥpicchilasaṃgatāḥ || 35
vārāhamāhiṣaurabhrabaiḍālaiṇeyakaukkuṭam
sadyaskamasṛgājaṃ vā deyaṃ picchilabastiṣu || 36
carakādau samuddiṣṭā bastayo ye sahasraśaḥ
vyavahāro na taiḥ prāyo nibaddhā nātra tena te || 37
bastirvayaḥsthāpayitā sukhāyu-
rbalāgnimedhāsvaravarṇakṛcca
sarvārthakārī śiśuvṛddhayūnāṃ
niratyayaḥ sarvagadāpahaśca || 38
Adhikāra 73
1pratimarśo'vapīḍaśca nasyaṃ pradhamanaṃ tathā
śirovirecanaṃ ceti nastaḥ karma ca pañcadhā || 1
īṣaducchiṅghanātsneho yāvānvaktraṃ prapadyate
nasto niṣiktaṃ taṃ vidyātpratimarśaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 2
pratimarśastu nasyārthaṃ karoti na ca doṣavān
nastaḥ snehāṅguliṃ dadyātprātarniśi ca sarvadā || 3
na cecchiṅghedarogāṇāṃ pratimarśaḥ sa dārḍhyakṛt
niśāharbhuktavantohaḥsvapnādhvaśramaretasām || 4
śirobhyañjanagaṇḍūṣaprasrāvāñjanavarcasām
dantakāṣṭhasya hāsyasya yojyo'nte'sau dvibindukaḥ || 5
śodhanaḥ stambhanaśca syādavapīḍo dvidhā mataḥ
avapaḍyi dīyate yasmādavapīḍastatastu saḥ || 6
snehārthaṃ śūnyaśirasāṃ grīvāskandhorasāṃ tathā
balārthaṃ dīyate sneho nastaḥ śabdo'tra vartate || 7
nasyasya snaihikasyātha deyāstvaṣṭau tu bindavaḥ
pratyekaśo nastakayornṛṇāmiti viniścayaḥ || 8
2śuktiśca pāṇiśuktiśca mātrāstisraḥprakīrtitāḥ
dvātriṃśadbindavaścātra śuktirityabhidhīyate || 9
dve śuktī pāṇiśuktiśca deyātra kuśalairnaraiḥ
tailaṃ kaphe ca vāte ca kevale pavane vasām || 10
dadyānnastaḥ sadā pitte sarpirmajjā samārute
dhmāpanaṃ recanaścūrṇo yuñjyāttaṃ mukhavāyunā || 11
ṣaḍaṅguladvimukhayā nāḍyā bheṣajagarbhayā
sa hi bhūritaraṃ doṣaṃ cūrṇatvādapakarṣati || 12
3śirovirecanadravyaiḥ snehairvātaiḥ prasādhitaiḥ
śirovirecanaṃ dadyādrogeṣu teṣu buddhimān || 13
gaurave śirasaḥ śūle jāḍye syande galāmaye
śoṣagaṇḍakrimigranthikuṣṭhāpasmārapīnase || 14
snigdhasvinnottamāṅgasya prākkṛtāvaśyakasya ca
nivātaśayanasthasya jatrūrdhvaṃ svedayetpunaḥ || 15
athottānārdhadehasya pāṇipāde prasārite
kiñcidunnatapādasya kiñcinmūrdhani nāmite || 16
nāsāpuṭaṃ pidhāyaikaṃ paryāyeṇa niṣecayet
uṣṇāmbutaptaṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ praṇāḍyā picunā tathā || 17
datte pādatalaskandhahastakarṇādi mardayet
śanairucchiṅghya niṣṭhīvetpārśvayorubhayostataḥ || 18
ābheṣajakṣayādevaṃ dvistrirvā nasyamācaret
snehaṃ virecanasyānte dadyāddoṣādyapekṣayā || 19
4tryahāttryahācca saptāhaṃ snehakarma samācaret
ekāhāntaritaṃ kuryādrecanaṃ śirasastathā || 20
samyaksnigdhe sukhocchvāsasvapnabodhākṣipāṭavam
rūkṣe'kṣistabdhatā śoṣo nāsāsye mūrdhaśūnyatā || 21
snigdhetikaṇḍūrgurutāprasekārucipīnasāḥ
suvirikte'kṣilaghutāvaktrasvaraviśuddhayaḥ || 22
durvirikte gadodrekaḥ kṣāmatātivirecite
toyamadyagarasnehapītānāṃ pātumicchatām || 23
bhuktabhaktaśiraḥsnātasnātukāmasrutāsṛjām
navapīnasarogārtasūtikāśvāsakāsinām || 24
śuddhānāṃ dattabastīnāṃ tathācārtavadurdine
anyatrātyayike vyādhau naiṣāṃ nasyaṃ prayojayet || 25
na nasyamūnasaptābde nātītāśītibatsare
na conadvādaśe dhūmaḥ kavalo nonapañcame || 26
na śuddhirūnadaśame na cātikrāntasaptatau
ājanmamaraṇaṃ śastaḥ pratimarśastu bastivat || 27
iti nasyādhikāraḥ | samāptaśca pañcakarmādhikāraḥ
Adhikāra 74
1prāyogikaḥ snaihikaśca dhūmo vairecanastathā
kāsaharo vāmanaśca dhūmaḥ pañcavidho mataḥ || 1
ṛjutrikopaphalitaṃ kolāsthyagrapramāṇitam
bastinetrasamadravyaṃ dhūmanetraṃ praśasyate || 2
sārdhastryaṃśayutaḥ pūrṇo hastaḥ prāyogikādiṣu
netre kāsahare tryaṃśahīnaḥ śeṣe daśāṅgulaḥ || 6
auṣadhairvartikāṃ kṛtvā śaragarbhāṃ viśoṣitām
vigarbhāmagnisaṃpluṣṭāṃ kṛtvā dhūmaṃ pibennaraḥ || 4
vaktreṇaiva vameddhūmaṃ nasto vaktreṇa vā piban
uraḥkaṇṭhagate doṣe vaktreṇa dhūmamāpibet || 5
nāsayā tu pibeddoṣe śiroghrāṇākṣisaṃśraye
gandhairakuṣṭhatagarairvartiḥ prāyogike matā || 6
snaihike tu madhūcchiṣṭasnehaguggulusarjakaiḥ
śirovirecanadravyairvartirvairecane matā || 7
kāsaghnaireva kāsaghnī vāmanairvāmanī matā
yojyā na pittaraktārttiviriktodaramehiṣu || 8
timirordhvanilādhmānarohiṇīdattabastiṣu
matsyamadyadadhikṣīrakṣaudrasnehaviṣāśiṣu || 9
śirasyabhihate pāṇḍuroge jāgarite niśi
raktapittāndhyabādhiryatṛṇmūrcchāmadamohakṛt || 10
dhūmo'kāle'tipīto vā tatra śīto vidhirhitaḥ
etaddhūmavidhānaṃ tu leśataḥ samprakāśitam || 11
Adhikāra 75
1snindhoṣṇaiḥ snehiko vāte svāduśītaiḥprasādanaḥ
pitte kaṭvamlalavaṇai rūkṣaiḥ saṃśodhanaḥ kaphe || 1
kaṣāyasvādutiktaiśca kavalo ropaṇo vraṇe
sukhaṃ sañcāryate yā tu sā mātrā kavale hitā || 2
asañcāryā tu yā mātrā gaṇḍūṣe sā prakīrtitā
tāvacca dhāraṇīyo'yaṃ yāvaddoṣapravartanam || 3
punaścānyo'pi dātavyastathā kṣaudraghṛtādibhiḥ
vyādherapacayastuṣṭirvaiśadyaṃ vaktralāghavam || 4
indriyāṇāṃ prasādaśca kavale śuddhilakṣaṇam
dāhatṛṣṇāvraṇān hanti madhugaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam || 5
dhānyāmlamāsyavairasyamaladaurgandhyanāśanam
tadīṣallavaṇaṃ śītaṃ mukhaśoṣaharaṃ param || 6
āśu kṣārāmlagaṇḍūṣo bhinatti śleṣmaṇaścayam
susthe hitaṃ vātaharaṃ tailagaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam || 7
Adhikāra 76
1sarveṣāmakṣirogāṇāmādāvāścyotanaṃ hitam
ruktodakaṇḍūgharṣādāsruharāganibarhaṇam || 1
uṣṇaṃ vāte kaphe coṣṇaṃ tacchītaṃ raktapittayoḥ
nivātasthasya vāmena pāṇinonmīlya locanam || 2
śuktau pralambayānyena picuvartyā kanīnike
daśa dvādaśa vā bindūn dvyaṅgulādavasecayet || 3
tataḥ pramṛjya mṛdunā celena kaphavātayoḥ
anyena koṣṇapānīyaplutena svedayenmṛdu || 4
atyuṣṇātīkṣṇaṃ rugrāgadṛṅnāśāyākṣisecanam
atiśītaṃ tu kurute nistodastambhavedanāḥ || 5
kaṣāyavartmatāṃ gharṣaṃ kṛcchrāttanmehanaṃ bahu
vikāravṛddhimatyalpaṃ saṃrambhamaparisrutam || 6
2athāñjanaṃ śuddhatanornetramātrāśraye male
pakvaliṅge'lpaśothārtikaṇḍūpaicchilyalakṣite || 7
mandagharṣāsrurāge'kṣṇi prayojyaṃ ghanadūṣike
lekhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ dṛṣṭiprasādanamiti tridhā || 8
añjanaṃ lekhanaṃ tatra kaṣāyāmlapaṭūṣaṇaiḥ
ropaṇaṃ tiktakairdravyaiḥ svāduśītaiḥ prasādanam || 9
daśāṅgulā tanurmadhye śalākā mukulānanā
praśastā lekhane tāmrī ropaṇe kālalohajā || 10
aṅgulā ca suvarṇotthā rūpajā ca prasādane
piṇḍo rasakriyā cūrṇaṃ tridhaivāñjanakalpanā || 11
gurau madhyalaghau doṣe tāṃ krameṇa prayojayet
athānūnmīlayan dṛṣṭī antaḥ sañcārayecchanaiḥ || 12
añjite vartmanī kiñciccālayeccaivamañjanam
apetauṣadhasambandhaṃ nirvṛtaṃ nayanaṃ yadā || 13
vyādhidoṣaṃ tu yogyābhiradbhiḥ prakṣālayettadā
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhakenākṣi tato vāmaṃ savāsasā || 14
ūrdhvavartmani saṃgṛhya śodhyaṃ vāmena cetarat
niśi svapnena madhyāhnapānānnoṣṇagabhastibhiḥ || 15
akṣirogāya doṣāḥ syurvardhitotpīḍitadrutāḥ
prātaḥ sāyaṃ ca tacchāntyai vyabhrake'to'ñjayetsadā || 16
kaṇḍūjāḍye'ñjanaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ dhūmaṃ vā yojayet punaḥ
tīkṣṇāñjanābhitapte tu tūrṇaṃ pratyañjanaṃ hitam || 17
3nāñjayedbhītavamitaviriktāśitavegite
kruddhajvaritabhrāntākṣaśirorukśoṣajāgare || 18
adṛṣṭe'rke śiraḥsnāte pītayordhūmamadyayoḥ
ajīrṇe'pyarkasaṃtapte divāsvapne pipāsite || 19
nirvāte tarpaṇaṃ yojyaṃ śuddhayormūrdhakāyayoḥ
kāle sādhāraṇe prātaḥ sāyaṃ vottānaśāyinaḥ || 20
yavamāṣamayīṃ pālīṃ netrakoṇādbahiḥ samām
dvyaṅguloccāṃ dṛḍhāṃ kṛtvā yathāsvaṃ siddhamāvapet || 21
sarpirnimīlite netre taptāmbu pravilāyitam
naktāndhyavātatimirakṛcchrarodhādike vasām || 22
āpakṣmāgrādathonmeṣaṃ śanakaistasya kurvataḥ
mātrāṃ vigaṇayet tatra vartma sandhisitāsite || 23
dṛṣṭau ca kramaśo vyādhau śataṃ trīṇi ca pañca ca
śatāni sapta cāṣṭau ca daśa manthe'nile daśa || 24
pitte ṣaṭ svasthavṛtte ca balāse pañca dhārayet
kṛtvāpāṅge tato dvāraṃ snehaṃ pātre nigālayet || 25
pibecca dhūmaṃ nekṣeta vyomarūpaṃ ca bhāsvaram
itthaṃ pratidinaṃ vāte pitte tvekāntaraṃ kaphe || 26
svasthe ca dvyantaraṃ dadyādātṛpteriti yojayet
prakāśakṣamatā svāsthyaṃ viśadaṃ laghu locanam || 27
4tṛpte viparyayo'tṛpte tṛpte'tiśleṣmajā rujaḥ
puṭapākaṃ prayuñjīta pūrvokteṣveva pakṣmasu || 28
savāte snehanaḥ śleṣmasahite lekhano mataḥ
tṛṭdaurbalye'nile pitte rakte svasthe prasādanaḥ || 29
bilvamātraṃ pṛthak piṇḍaṃ māṃsabheṣajakalkayoḥ
urubūkavaṭāmbhojapatraiḥ snigdhādiṣu kramāt || 30
beṣṭayitvā mṛdāliptaṃ dhavadhanvanagomayaiḥ
pacet pradīptairagnyābhaṃ pakvaṃ niṣpīḍya tadrasam || 31
netre tarpaṇavat yuñjyācchataṃ dve trīṇi dhārayet
lekhanasnehanāntyeṣu koṣṇau pūrvau himo'paraḥ || 32
thūmapo'nte tayoreva yogāstatra ca tṛptivat
tarpaṇaṃ puṭapākaṃ ca nasyānarhe na yojayet || 33
yāvantyahāni yuñjīta dviguṇo hitabhāgbhavet || 34
Adhikāra 77
1atha snigdhatanuḥ snigdharasānnapratibhojitaḥ
pratyādityamukhaṃ svinno jānūccāsanasaṃsthitaḥ || 1
mṛdupaṭṭāttakośānto jānusthāpitakūrparaḥ
aṅguṣṭhagarbhamuṣṭibhyāṃ manye gāḍhaṃ nipīḍayet || 2
dantasampīḍanotkāsagaṇḍādhmānāni cācaret
pṛṣṭhato yantrayeccainaṃ vastramāveṣṭayennaraḥ || 3
kandharāyāṃ parikṣipya nasyāntarvāmatarjanīm
evamutthāpya vidhinā śirāṃ vidhyecchirogatām || 4
vidhyeddhastaśirāṃ bāhāvanākuñcitakūrpare
baddhvā sukhopaviṣṭasya muṣṭimaṅguṣṭhagarbhiṇam || 5
ūrdhvaṃ vedhyapradeśācca paṭṭikāṃ caturaṅgule
pāde tu susthite'dhastājjānusandhernipīḍite || 6
gāḍhaṃ karābhyāmāgulphaṃ caraṇe tasya copari
dvitīye kuñcite kiñcidārūḍhe hastavattataḥ || 7
baddhvā vidhyecchirāmitthamanukteṣvapi kalpayet
teṣu teṣu pradeśeṣu tattadyantramupāyavit || 8
tato vrīhimukhaṃ vyadhyapradeśe nyasya pīḍayet
aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ tu talapracchāditaṃ bhiṣak || 9
vāmahastena vinyasya kuṭhārīmitareṇa tu
tāḍayenmadhyamāṅgulyāṅguṣṭhaviṣṭabdhamuktayā || 10
māṃsale nikṣipeddeśe vrīhyāsyaṃ brīhimātrakam
yavārdhamasthnāmupari śirāṃ vidhyankuṭhārikām || 11
2asamyagasre sravati bailvavyoṣaniśānataiḥ
sāgāradhūmalavaṇatailairdihyācchirāmukham
samyak pravṛtte koṣṇena tailena lavaṇena ca || 12
aśuddho balino'pyasraṃ na prasthātsrāvayetparam
atisrutau hi mṛtyuḥ syāddāruṇā vānilāmayāḥ || 13
tatrābhyaṅgarasakṣīraraktapānāni bheṣajam
srute rakte śanairyantramapanīya himāmbunā || 14
prakṣālya tailaprotāktaṃ bandhanīyaṃ śirāmukham
aśuddhaṃ srāvayedbhūyaḥ sāyamahnyapare'pi vā || 15
rakte tvatiṣṭhati kṣipraṃ stambhanīmācaretkriyām
lodhrapriyaṅgupattuṅgamāṣayaṣṭyāhvagairikaiḥ || 16
mṛtkapālāñjanakṣaumamasīkṣīritvagaṅkuraiḥ
vicūrṇayedvraṇamukhaṃ padmakādihimaṃ pibet || 17
tāmeva vā śirāṃ vidhyedvyadhāttasmādanantaram
śirāmukhaṃ vā tvaritaṃ dahettaptaśalākayā || 18
saśeṣamapyasṛgdhāryaṃ na cātisrutimācaret
harecchuṅgādinā śeṣaṃ prasādamathavā nayet || 19
3marmahīne yathāsannapradeśe vyadhayecchirām
natūnaṣoḍaśātītasaptatyabdasrutāsṛjām || 20
asnigdhāsveditātyarthasveditānilarogiṇām
garbhiṇīsūtikājīrṇapittāsraśvāsakāsinām || 21
atisārodaracchardipāṇḍusarvāṅgaśoṣiṇām
snehapītaṃ prayukteṣu tathā pañcasu karmasu || 22
nāyantritāṃ śirāṃ vidhyenna tiryaṅnāpyanutthitām
nātiśītoṣṇavātābhreṣvanyatrātyayikādgadāt || 23
nātyuṣṇaśītaṃ laghudīpanīyaṃ
rakte'panīte hitamannapānam
tadā śarīraṃ hyanavasthitāsṛk
vahnirviśeṣeṇa ca rakṣaṇīyaḥ || 24
naro hitāhāravihārasevī
māsaṃ bhavedābalalābhato vā
prasannavarṇendriyamindriyārthā-
nicchantamavyāhataśāktivegam
sukhānvitaṃ puṣṭibalopapannaṃ
viśuddharaktaṃ puruṣaṃ vadanti || 25
Adhikāra 78
1brāhme muhūrte uttiṣṭhetsustho rakṣārthamāyuṣaḥ
śarīracintāṃ nirvartya kṛtaśaucavidhistataḥ || 1
prātarbhuktvā ca mṛdvagraṃ kaṣāyakaṭutiktakam
bhakṣayeddantapavanaṃ dantamāṃsānyabādhayan || 2
nādyādajīrṇavamathuśvāsakāsajvarārditaḥ
tṛṣṇāsyapākahṛnnetraśiraḥkarṇāmayī ca tat
sauvīramañjanaṃ nityaṃ hitamakṣṇoḥ prayojayet || 3
saptarātre'ṣṭarātre vā srāvaṇārthaṃ rasāñjanam
tato nāvanagaṇḍūṣadhūmatāmbūlabhāgbhavet || 4
tāmbūlaṃ kṣatapittāsrarūkṣotkupitacakṣuṣām
viṣamūrcchāmadārtānāmapathyaṃ cāpi śoṣiṇām || 5
2abhyaṅgamācarennityaṃ sajarāśramavātahā
śiraḥśravaṇapādeṣu taṃ viśeṣeṇa śīlayet || 6
bāhyābhyaṅgaḥ kaphagrastakṛtasaṃśuddhyajīrṇibhiḥ
śarīraceṣṭā yā ceṣṭā sthairyārthā balavardhanī || 7
dehavyāyāmasaṃkhyātā mātrayā tāṃ samācaret
vātapittāmayī bālo vṛddho'jīrṇī ca tāṃ tyajet || 8
udvartanaṃ tathā kāryaṃ tataḥ snānaṃ samācaret
uṣṇāmbunādhaḥkāyasya pariṣeko balāvahaḥ || 9
tenaiva tūttamāṅgasya balakṛt keśacakṣuṣoḥ
snānamarditanetrāsyakarṇarogātisāriṣu || 10
ādhmānapīnasājīrṇabhuktavatsu ca garhitam
nīcaromanakhaśmaśrurnirmalāṅghrimalāyanaḥ || 11
snānaśīlaḥ sasurabhiḥ suveṣo nirmalāmbaraḥ
dhārayetsatataṃ ratnaṃ siddhamantramahauṣadhīḥ || 12
3sātapatrapadatrāṇo vicaredyugamātradṛk
niśi cātyayike kārye daṇḍī maunī sahāyavān || 13
jīrṇe hitaṃ mitaṃ cādyānna vegāndhārayedbalāt
na vegito'nyakāryaḥ syānnājitvāsādhyamāmayam || 14
daśadhā pāpakarmāṇi kāyavāṅmānasaistyajet
kāle hitaṃ mitaṃ brūyādavisaṃvādi peśalam || 15
ātmavatsatataṃ paśyedapi kīṭapipīlikām
ātmanaḥ pratikūlāni pareṣāṃ na samācaret || 16
naktaṃdināni me yānti kathaṃbhūtasya saṃprati
duḥkhabhāk na bhavatyevaṃ nityaṃ sannihitasmṛtiḥ || 17
4māsairdvisaṃkhyairmāghādyaiḥ kramātṣaḍṛtavaḥ smṛtāḥ
valinaḥ śītasaṃrodhāddhemante pravalo'nalaḥ || 18
sevetāto hime snigdhisvādvamlalavaṇān rasān
godhūmapiṣṭamāṃsekṣukṣīrotthavikṛtīḥ surām || 19
navamannaṃ vasāṃ tailaṃ śaucakārye sukhodakam
yuktyārkakiraṇān svedaṃ pādatrāṇaṃ ca sarvadā || 20
prāvārājinakauśeyapraveṇīkuthakāstṛtam
upṇasvabhāvairlaghubhiḥ prāvṛtaḥ śayanaṃ bhajet || 21
aṅgāratāpasaṃtaptagarbhabhūveśmani priyām
pīvarorustanaśroṇīmāliṅgyāgurucarcitām || 22
ayameva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ śiśire'pi viśepataḥ
tadā hi śītamādhvīkaṃ raukṣaṃ cādanakālajam || 23
kaphaścito hi śiśire vasante'rkāṃśutāpitaḥ
hatvāgniṃ kurute rogāṃstatastatra prayojayet || 24
tīkṣṇaṃ vamananasyādikavaḍagrahamañjanam
vyāyāmodvartanaṃ dhūmaṃ śaucakārye sukhodakam || 25
snāto'nuliptaḥ karpūracandanāgurukuṅkumaiḥ
purāṇayavagodhūmakṣaudrajāṅgalaśūlyabhuk
prapibedāsavāriṣṭasīdhumādhvīkamādhavān || 26
5vasante'nubhavetstrīṇāṃ kānanānāṃ ca yauvanam
gurūṣṇasnigdhamadhuraṃ divāsvapnaṃ ca varjayet || 27
mayūkhairjagataḥ snehaṃ grīṣme pepīyate raviḥ
svādu śītaṃ dravaṃ snigdhamannapānaṃ tadā hitam || 28
śītaṃ saśarkaraṃ manthaṃ jāṅgalānmṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
ghṛtaṃ payaḥ saśālyannaṃ bhajangrīṣme na sīdati || 29
madyamalpaṃ na vā peyamathavā subahūdakam
madhyāhne candanārdrāṅgaḥ svapyāddhārāgṛhe niśi || 30
niśākarakarākīrṇe pravāte saudhamastake
nivṛttakāmo vyajanaiḥ pāṇisparśaiḥ sacandanaiḥ || 31
sevyamāno bhajetāsyāṃ muktāmaṇivibhūṣitaḥ
lavaṇāmlakaṭūṣṇāni vyāyāmaṃ cātra varjayet || 32
bhūbāṣpānmeghanisyandātpākādamlājjalasya ca
varṣāsvagnibale kṣīṇe kupyanti pavanādayaḥ || 33
bhajetsādhāraṇaṃ sarvamūṣmaṇastejanaṃ ca yat
āsthāpanaṃ śuddhatanurjīrṇaṃ dhānyaṃ kṛtānrasān || 34
jāṅgalaṃ piśitaṃ yūṣānmadhvariṣṭaṃ cirantanam
divyaṃ kaupaṃ śṛtaṃ vāmbho bhojanaṃ tvatidurdine || 35
vyaktāmlalavaṇasnehaṃ saṃśuṣkaṃ kṣaudravallaghu
nadījalodamanthāhaḥsvapnāyāsātapāṃstyajet || 36
varṣāśītocitāṅgānāṃ sahasaivārkaraśmibhiḥ
taptānāmācitaṃ pittaṃ prāyaḥ śaradi kupyati || 37
tajjayāya ghṛtaṃ tiktaṃ vireko raktamokṣaṇam
tiktasvādukapāyaṃ ca kṣudhito'nnaṃ bhajellaghu || 38
6ikṣavaḥ śālayo mudgāḥ saro'mbhaḥ kvathitaṃ payaḥ
śaradyetāni pathyāni pradoṣe cenduraśmayaḥ || 39
śāradāni ca mālyāni vāsāṃsi vimalāni ca
tuṣārakṣārasauhityadadhitailarasātapān || 40
tīkṣṇamadyadivāsvapnapurovātātapāṃstyajet
śīte varpāsu cādyāṃstrīnvasante'ntyānrasānbhajet || 41
svādūnnidāghe śaradi svādutiktakapāyakān
śaradvasantayo rūkṣaṃ śītaṃ gharmaghanāntayoḥ || 42
annapānaṃ samāsena viparītamato'nyathā
nityaṃ sarvarasābhyāsaḥ svasvādhikyamṛtāvṛtau || 43
ṛtorādyantasaptāhāvṛtusandhiriti smṛtaḥ
tatra pūrvo vidhistyājyaḥ sevanīyo'paraḥ kramaḥ || 44
ityuktamṛtusātmyaṃ yacceṣṭāhāravyapāśrayam
upaśete yadaucityādokasātmyaṃ taducyate || 45
deśānāmāmayānāṃ ca viparītaṃ guṇaṃ guṇaiḥ
sātmyamicchanti sātmajñāśceṣṭitaṃ cādyameva ca || 46
tacca nityaṃ prayuñjīta svāsthyaṃ yenānuvartate
ajātānāṃ vikārāṇāmanutpattikaraṃ ca yat || 47
nagarī nagarasyeva rathasyeva rathī yathā
svaśarīrasya medhāvī kṛtyeṣvavahito bhavet || 48
7gauḍādhinātharasavatyadhikāripātra-
nārāyaṇasya tanayaḥ sunayo'ntaraṅgāt
bhānoranuprathitalodhravalīkulīnaḥ
śrīcakradatta iha kartṛpadādhikārī || 1
yaḥ siddhayogalikhitādhikasiddhayogā-
natraiva nikṣipati kevalamuddharedvā
bhaṭṭatrayatripathavedavidā janena
dattaḥ patetsapadi mūrdhani tasya śāpaḥ || 2
vasuvedāṃkabhūmyabde 1948 kārtike māsi dhīmatā
ravidattena vaidyena bhāṣāṭīkā sukalpitā || 1
